// backup.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function syncActiveSessionBeforeExport(){
  try { if(activeClaim && document.getElementById('reviewModal') && document.getElementById('reviewModal').classList.contains('open')) upsertCurrentSession(); } catch(e){}
}
function getSessionState(){
  return {
    currentPage,
    viewState: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(viewState)),
    currentTimelineClaimId,
    filters: { currentFilter, currentSearch, dashSearchTerm, reportsSearchTerm, intakeMonthFilter, intakeStatusFilter, intakeSearch, providerStatusFilter, providerSearch, contractStatusFilter, contractSearch, tariffFilter, tariffSearch, drFilter, drSearch, trashFilter, trashSearch, srchTerm, srchTariffId, srchFilter, srchPage, srchPerPage, sessionsFilter, sessionsSearch },
    activeSessionId: activeSessionId || null,
    capturedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}
function restoreSessionState(state){
  if(!state) return;
  try {
    const f = state.filters || {};
    if(f.currentFilter !== undefined) currentFilter = f.currentFilter;
    if(typeof f.currentSearch === 'string') currentSearch = f.currentSearch;
    if(typeof f.dashSearchTerm === 'string') dashSearchTerm = f.dashSearchTerm;
    if(typeof f.reportsSearchTerm === 'string') reportsSearchTerm = f.reportsSearchTerm;
    if(f.intakeMonthFilter !== undefined) intakeMonthFilter = f.intakeMonthFilter;
    if(f.intakeStatusFilter !== undefined) intakeStatusFilter = f.intakeStatusFilter;
    if(typeof f.intakeSearch === 'string') intakeSearch = f.intakeSearch;
    if(f.providerStatusFilter !== undefined) providerStatusFilter = f.providerStatusFilter;
    if(typeof f.providerSearch === 'string') providerSearch = f.providerSearch;
    if(f.contractStatusFilter !== undefined) contractStatusFilter = f.contractStatusFilter;
    if(typeof f.contractSearch === 'string') contractSearch = f.contractSearch;
    if(f.tariffFilter !== undefined) tariffFilter = f.tariffFilter;
    if(typeof f.tariffSearch === 'string') tariffSearch = f.tariffSearch;
    if(f.drFilter !== undefined) drFilter = f.drFilter;
    if(typeof f.drSearch === 'string') drSearch = f.drSearch;
    if(f.trashFilter !== undefined) trashFilter = f.trashFilter;
    if(typeof f.trashSearch === 'string') trashSearch = f.trashSearch;
    if(typeof f.srchTerm === 'string') srchTerm = f.srchTerm;
    if(f.srchTariffId !== undefined) srchTariffId = f.srchTariffId;
    if(f.srchFilter !== undefined) srchFilter = f.srchFilter;
    if(typeof f.srchPage === 'number') srchPage = f.srchPage;
    if(typeof f.srchPerPage === 'number') srchPerPage = f.srchPerPage;
    if(f.sessionsFilter !== undefined) sessionsFilter = f.sessionsFilter;
    if(typeof f.sessionsSearch === 'string') sessionsSearch = f.sessionsSearch;
    if(state.currentTimelineClaimId) currentTimelineClaimId = state.currentTimelineClaimId;
    if(state.viewState && state.viewState.type) viewState = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.viewState));
    if(state.activeSessionId && workSessions.some(s => s.id === state.activeSessionId)) activeSessionId = state.activeSessionId;
    const syncChips = (sel, attr, val) => { const c = document.querySelector(sel); if(!c) return; c.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(ch => ch.classList.toggle('active', ch.dataset[attr] === val)); };
    syncChips('#filterChips','filter',currentFilter); syncChips('#intakeStatusChips','istatus',intakeStatusFilter);
    syncChips('#providerFilterChips','pfilter',providerStatusFilter); syncChips('#contractFilterChips','cfilter',contractStatusFilter);
    syncChips('#tariffFilterChips','tfilter',tariffFilter); syncChips('#drFilterChips','drf',drFilter);
    syncChips('#trashFilterChips','trf',trashFilter); syncChips('#srchChips','srch',srchFilter);
    syncChips('#sessionsFilterChips','sfilter',sessionsFilter);
    const mc = document.querySelector('#monthChips'); if(mc) mc.querySelectorAll('.month-chip').forEach(ch => ch.classList.toggle('active', ch.dataset.month === intakeMonthFilter));
    const setVal = (sel, v) => { const el = document.querySelector(sel); if(el) el.value = v; };
    setVal('#searchInput',currentSearch||''); setVal('#dashSearch',dashSearchTerm||''); setVal('#reportsSearch',reportsSearchTerm||'');
    setVal('#intakeSearch',intakeSearch||''); setVal('#providerSearch',providerSearch||''); setVal('#contractSearch',contractSearch||'');
    setVal('#tariffSearch',tariffSearch||''); setVal('#drSearch',drSearch||''); setVal('#trashSearch',trashSearch||'');
    setVal('#srchQuery',srchTerm||''); setVal('#srchTariff',srchTariffId||'all'); setVal('#srchPerPage',String(srchPerPage));
    setVal('#sessionsSearch',sessionsSearch||'');
    if(state.currentPage === 'view' && viewState.type && viewState.id){ applyPage('view'); renderView(); }
    else if(state.currentPage === 'timeline' && currentTimelineClaimId){ applyPage('timeline'); renderTimeline(); }
    else if(state.currentPage) applyPage(state.currentPage);
    if(typeof renderAll === 'function') renderAll();
    renderSessions(); updateSessionsBadge();
  } catch(e){ console.warn('restoreSessionState', e); }
}
function getBackupData(){
  syncActiveSessionBeforeExport();
  return {
    app:'TPA Pro', version:'1.0', type:'full-backup', exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    settings: { reviewer:{...appSettings.reviewer}, company:{...appSettings.company} },
    sessionsMeta: { totalCount:workSessions.length, activeCount:workSessions.filter(s=>s.status==='active').length, pausedCount:workSessions.filter(s=>s.status==='paused').length, activeSessionId:activeSessionId||null, sessionsFilter, sessionsSearch },
    data: { providers:providers.map(p=>({...p})), contracts:contracts.map(c=>({...c})), tariffs:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tariffs)), batches:batches.map(b=>({...b})), claims:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(claims)), trash:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(trash)), deductionRules:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(deductionRules)), paProcedures:paProcedures.map(p=>({...p})), workSessions:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(workSessions)) },
    session: getSessionState()
  };
}
function getSettingsData(){
  return { app:'TPA Pro', version:'1.0', type:'settings', exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(), settings:{ reviewer:{...appSettings.reviewer}, company:{...appSettings.company}, deductionRules:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(deductionRules)), paProcedures:paProcedures.map(p=>({...p})) } };
}
function getSessionsData(){
  syncActiveSessionBeforeExport();
  return { app:'TPA Pro', version:'1.0', type:'sessions', exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(), sessionsMeta:{ totalCount:workSessions.length, activeCount:workSessions.filter(s=>s.status==='active').length, pausedCount:workSessions.filter(s=>s.status==='paused').length, activeSessionId:activeSessionId||null }, sessions:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(workSessions)) };
}
function exportSessions(){
  try {
    const now = new Date(), date = now.toISOString().slice(0,10), time = now.toTimeString().slice(0,5).replace(':','');
    downloadJSON(getSessionsData(), `TPA_Pro_Sessions_${date}_${time}.json`);
    trackBackup();
    showSuccess('تم', `${workSessions.length} جلسة`);
  } catch(err){ showWarning('خطأ', err.message); }
}
function doExportBackup(silent){
  syncActiveSessionBeforeExport();
  try {
    const now = new Date(), date = now.toISOString().slice(0,10), time = now.toTimeString().slice(0,5).replace(':','');
    downloadJSON(getBackupData(), `TPA_Pro_Backup_${date}_${time}.json`);
    trackBackup();
    if(!silent) showSuccess('تم', `${workSessions.length} جلسة`);
    return true;
  } catch(err){ if(!silent) showWarning('خطأ', err.message); return false; }
}
function exportBackup(){ return doExportBackup(false); }
function exportSettings(){
  try { const d = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10); downloadJSON(getSettingsData(), `TPA_Pro_Settings_${d}.json`); trackBackup(); showSuccess('تم','تم'); }
  catch(err){ showWarning('خطأ', err.message); }
}
// Validate imported JSON before any confirmation or state mutation.
function validateBackupPayload(p){
  const fail = message => ({ok:false, message});
  if(!p || typeof p !== 'object' || Array.isArray(p)) return fail('بنية الملف غير صحيحة');
  if(p.app !== 'TPA Pro') return fail('الملف لا ينتمي إلى TPA Pro');
  if(!['full-backup','settings','sessions'].includes(p.type)) return fail('نوع النسخة غير مدعوم');
  let nodes = 0;
  const inspect = (value, depth) => {
    if(++nodes > 250000 || depth > 30) throw new Error('الملف كبير أو متداخل بشكل غير مسموح');
    if(!value || typeof value !== 'object') return;
    if(Array.isArray(value)) { if(value.length > 100000) throw new Error('عدد عناصر غير مسموح'); value.forEach(v=>inspect(v,depth+1)); return; }
    for(const key of Object.keys(value)) {
      if(['__proto__','prototype','constructor'].includes(key)) throw new Error('مفتاح غير آمن في الملف');
      inspect(value[key],depth+1);
    }
  };
  try { inspect(p,0); } catch(e) { return fail(e.message); }
  const isArray = (v,max=50000) => Array.isArray(v) && v.length <= max;
  if(p.type === 'full-backup') {
    if(!p.data || typeof p.data !== 'object') return fail('بيانات النسخة الكاملة مفقودة');
    for(const key of ['providers','contracts','tariffs','batches','claims','trash','deductionRules','paProcedures','workSessions']) {
      if(!isArray(p.data[key])) return fail('قائمة غير صالحة: '+key);
    }
    if(typeof inspectDataRelationships === 'function'){
      const integrity = inspectDataRelationships(p.data, p.data.workSessions);
      if(!integrity.ok) return fail('تعارض في معرفات النسخة الاحتياطية: '+integrity.issues.filter(x=>x.severity==='error').map(x=>x.code).join(', '));
    }
  } else if(p.type === 'sessions') {
    if(!isArray(p.sessions,10000)) return fail('قائمة الجلسات غير صالحة');
    if(typeof inspectDataRelationships === 'function'){
      const integrity = inspectDataRelationships({claims}, p.sessions);
      if(!integrity.ok) return fail('تعارض في معرفات جلسات المراجعة: '+integrity.issues.filter(x=>x.severity==='error').map(x=>x.code).join(', '));
    }
  } else if(p.type === 'settings') {
    if(!p.settings || typeof p.settings !== 'object' || Array.isArray(p.settings)) return fail('الإعدادات غير صالحة');
  }
  return {ok:true};
}
function importBackupFile(file){
  if(!file) return;
  if(!Number.isFinite(file.size) || file.size <= 0 || file.size > 20 * 1024 * 1024){ showWarning('ملف غير صالح','يجب أن يكون حجم الملف بين 1 بايت و20MB'); return; }
  if(!/\.json$/i.test(file.name || '')){ showWarning('ملف غير صالح','يسمح باستيراد ملفات JSON فقط'); return; }
  const r = new FileReader();
  r.onload = e => {
    try {
      const p = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
      const validation = validateBackupPayload(p);
      if(!validation.ok){ showWarning('نسخة غير صالحة', validation.message); return; }
      if(p.type === 'sessions') confirmSessionsImport(p);
      else if(p.type === 'settings') confirmSettingsImport(p);
      else if(p.type === 'full-backup' || p.data) confirmFullImport(p);
      else showWarning('غير معروف','—');
    } catch(err){ showWarning('خطأ', err.message); }
  };
  r.onerror = () => showWarning('خطأ','تعذر');
  r.readAsText(file);
}

// claims-view-2.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function applyMaxLabsRule(pat){
  markSessionDirty();
  const max = REVIEW_RULES.maxLabsPerVisit;
  const idxs = [];
  (pat.lines||[]).forEach((l,i) => { if(REVIEW_RULES.labCategories.includes(l.category)) idxs.push(i); });
  if(idxs.length <= max){ showWarning('لا حاجة', `عدد التحاليل ${idxs.length} ≤ ${max}.`); return; }
  const excess = idxs.length - max;
  // الاحتفاظ بأعلى التحاليل تكلفة وخصم الأقل تكلفة عند تجاوز الحد.
  const sorted = idxs.slice().sort((a,b) =>
    ((Number(pat.lines[a].qty)||0)*(Number(pat.lines[a].billedUnit)||0)) -
    ((Number(pat.lines[b].qty)||0)*(Number(pat.lines[b].billedUnit)||0))
  );
  sorted.slice(0, excess).forEach(i => {
    const l = pat.lines[i];
    l.approved = 0;
    l.note = 'تجاوز حد التحاليل';
    l._maxlabsForced = true;
    l._overrideMode = 'auto';
    l._autoApproved = 0;
    l._autoRuleId = '_maxlabs';
    l._autoRuleName = `حد التحاليل (${max})`;
    l._appliedRuleIds = ['_maxlabs'];
  });
  showSuccess('تم', `تم خصم ${excess} تحليل`);
}
function saveReview(){
  if(!activeClaim) return;
  const missing = [];
  workingInvoices.forEach((inv,i) => { DOC_ITEMS.forEach(d => { if(!inv.documents[d.key]) missing.push(`فاتورة ${i+1}: ${d.label}`); }); });
  if(missing.length){ showWarning('مستندات ناقصة', missing.join('<br>')+'<br><br>متابعة؟', () => { if(!activeClaim._overrideChecklist) activeClaim._overrideChecklist=[]; activeClaim._overrideChecklist.push({ by:'reviewer', at:new Date().toISOString(), reason:'missing-docs', missing:missing.slice() }); finalizeReview(); }); return; }
  if(activeClaim.priorApproval && activeClaim.priorApproval.required && activeClaim.priorApproval.status !== 'approved'){ showWarning('موافقة ناقصة','متابعة؟', () => { if(!activeClaim._overrideChecklist) activeClaim._overrideChecklist=[]; activeClaim._overrideChecklist.push({ by:'reviewer', at:new Date().toISOString(), reason:'prior-approval', missing:['pa'] }); finalizeReview(); }); return; }
  finalizeReview();
}
function finalizeReview(){
  if(!activeClaim) return;
  if (typeof collectExceptions === 'function') {
    const excs = collectExceptions();
    if (excs.length > 0) { showWarning('استثناءات معلّقة', `بقي ${excs.length} استثناء. عالجها أو تجاوز الفحوصات.`); return; }
  }
  const cid = activeClaim.id;
  activeClaim.invoices = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(workingInvoices));
  activeClaim.invoices.forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => computePatient(p, activeClaim, inv.date, inv)));
  activeClaim.reason = $('#rvReason').value;
  activeClaim.notes = $('#rvNotes').value.trim();
  if(activeClaim.priorApproval && activeClaim.priorApproval.required){
    activeClaim.priorApproval.status = $('#paStatus').value;
    activeClaim.priorApproval.refNumber = $('#paRefNumber').value.trim();
    activeClaim.priorApproval.date = $('#paDate').value;
  }
  recalcClaim(activeClaim);
  if(activeClaim.status === 'pending') activeClaim.reviewedDate = daysFromNow(0);
  if(activeClaim.approved <= 0) activeClaim.status = 'rejected';
  else if(activeClaim.approved >= activeClaim.billed) activeClaim.status = 'approved';
  else activeClaim.status = 'partial';
  if (typeof appendClaimAuditEvent === 'function') {
    appendClaimAuditEvent(activeClaim, {
      action: activeClaim._signedOff ? 'review.signed-off' : 'review.saved',
      details: {
        decision: activeClaim._decision || activeClaim.status,
        status: activeClaim.status,
        submittedBilled: Number.isFinite(Number(activeClaim.submittedBilled)) ? Number(activeClaim.submittedBilled) : null,
        billed: Number(activeClaim.billed) || 0,
        approved: Number(activeClaim.approved) || 0,
        overrideCount: Array.isArray(activeClaim._overrideChecklist) ? activeClaim._overrideChecklist.length : 0,
        reason: String(activeClaim.reason || '').slice(0,500)
      }
    });
  }
  workSessions = workSessions.filter(x => x.claimId !== activeClaim.id);
  persistSessions();
  if (typeof persistData === 'function') persistData(true);
  clearSessionDirty();
  closeReview({ discard:true });
  renderAll();
  if(currentTimelineClaimId === cid) renderTimeline();
  showSuccess('تم','تم حفظ المراجعة');
  // Phase 4: انتقال للجلسة التالية
  const next = workSessions.find(s => s.status === 'paused');
  if (next) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (confirm(`هناك ${workSessions.length} جلسة موقوفة. فتح التالية (${next.claimId})؟`)) {
        resumeSession(next.id);
      }
    }, 400);
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: /mnt/data/phase23work/TPA-Pro-main/js/claims-view.js
================================================================================
// claims-view.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)

    const billed = $('#calcBilled')||$('#rvPageCalcBilled');
    const ded = $('#calcDed')||$('#rvPageCalcDed');
    const rate = $('#calcRate')||$('#rvPageCalcRate');
    // recalcReview already updates
  } catch(e){}
}
function renderReviewInvoices(){
  const tabs = (currentPage === 'review' ? document.getElementById('rvPageInvoiceTabs') : null) || document.getElementById('rvInvoiceTabs') || document.getElementById('rvPageInvoiceTabs');
  const panel = (currentPage === 'review' ? document.getElementById('rvPageInvoicePanel') : null) || document.getElementById('rvInvoicePanel') || document.getElementById('rvPageInvoicePanel');
  if(!tabs || !panel){
    recalcReview(); return;
  }
  if(!workingInvoices.length){
    tabs.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="rv-add-tab" data-add-invoice="1">+ إضافة مذكرة خصم</button>';
    panel.innerHTML = '<div class="rv-empty">لا مذكرات خصم</div>';
    recalcReview(); return;
  }
  tabs.innerHTML = workingInvoices.map((inv,i) => {
    const t = INVOICE_TYPES[inv.type] || {label:inv.type};
    const tot = invoiceTotals(inv);
    const name = inv.debitNoteNumber || inv.number || inv.id;
    const ct = inv.claimType === 'retiree' ? 'معاشات' : 'عاملين';
    return `<button type="button" class="rv-inv-tab ${i===rvActiveInvoiceIdx?'active':''}" data-inv-idx="${i}"><span>مذكرة خصم ${escapeHtml(name)} • ${ct}</span><span class="cnt">${(inv.patients||[]).length} مريض</span><span class="amt">${fmt(tot.billed)}</span></button>`;
  }).join('') + '<button type="button" class="rv-add-tab" data-add-invoice="1">+ مذكرة خصم</button>';
  renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview();
}
function reviewLineRow(ln, i){
  const billed = ln.qty*ln.billedUnit;
  const hasT = ln.tariffUnit !== null && ln.tariffUnit !== undefined;
  const tt = hasT ? ln.qty*ln.tariffUnit : null;
  const lesser = ln.lineBase !== undefined ? ln.lineBase : (hasT ? Math.min(billed, tt) : billed);
  const appr = Number(ln.approved)||0;
  const diff = billed-appr;
  const mode = ln._overrideMode || 'auto';
  const flags = [];
  if(hasT && ln.billedUnit > ln.tariffUnit) flags.push('أعلى من اللائحة');
  if(hasT && ln.tariffUnit === 0) flags.push('غير مغطاة');
  if(ln.note) flags.push(ln.note);
  let status = '';
  if(ln._maxlabsForced) status = '<span class="rv-auto-badge auto">حد تحاليل</span>';
  else if(ln.paRejected) status = '<span class="rv-auto-badge rejected">مرفوض PA</span>';
  else if(mode === 'manual') status = '<span class="rv-auto-badge manual">يدوي</span>';
  else if(mode === 'reverted') status = '<span class="rv-auto-badge reverted">معاد</span>';
  else if(ln._compositeApplied && ln._compositeApplied.length) status = `<span class="rv-auto-badge auto">🎁 ${escapeHtml(ln._compositeApplied[0].name)}</span>`;
  else if(ln._autoRuleId) status = `<span class="rv-auto-badge auto">${ln._autoRuleRate}%${ln._appliedRuleIds&&ln._appliedRuleIds.length>1?' 🔗':''}</span>`;
  let rowCls = '';
  if(appr === 0) rowCls = 'rejected';
  else if(mode === 'manual') rowCls = 'flagged';
  else if(ln._autoRuleId && ln._autoApproved < ln.lineBase) rowCls = 'flagged';
  const hasAuto = (ln._appliedRuleIds && ln._appliedRuleIds.length > 0) && ln._autoApproved < ln.lineBase;
  const btns = `
    ${mode==='auto' && hasAuto ? `<button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn undo" data-revert-line="${i}">↺</button>` : ''}
    ${mode!=='auto' ? `<button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn reset" data-reset-line="${i}">⟲</button>` : ''}
    <button type="button" class="rv-line-act edit" data-edit-line="${i}">✎</button>
    <button type="button" class="rv-line-act del" data-del-line="${i}">×</button>`;
  const flagsHtml = flags.length ? ' · <span style="color:var(--warn);font-weight:700">' + flags.map(escapeHtml).join(' · ') + '</span>' : '';
  const autoRuleHtml = ln._autoRuleName ? ' · <span style="color:var(--info);font-weight:700">📋 ' + escapeHtml(ln._autoRuleName) + '</span>' : '';
  return `<tr class="${rowCls}">
    <td>${i+1}</td>
    <td><span class="mono" style="color:var(--navy-400);font-weight:700">${escapeHtml(ln.code)}</span></td>
    <td><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:12.5px">${escapeHtml(ln.name)}</div>
        <div style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:1px">${escapeHtml(ln.category)}${flagsHtml}${autoRuleHtml}</div></td>
    <td class="mono">${ln.qty}</td>
    <td class="mono">${fmt(billed)}</td>
    <td class="mono">${hasT?fmt(tt):'—'}</td>
    <td class="mono" style="color:var(--teal);font-weight:800">${fmt(lesser)}</td>
    <td><input type="number" class="rv-line-approved" data-line-idx="${i}" value="${appr}" min="0" max="${billed}"></td>
    <td class="mono rv-diff" style="color:${diff>0?'var(--bad)':'var(--muted)'};font-weight:800">${fmt(diff)}</td>
    <td style="text-align:center">${status}</td>
    <td style="white-space:nowrap"><div style="display:flex;gap:2px">${btns}</div></td>
  </tr>`;
}
function _renderDeductionReasons(p){
  if(!p.deductionReasons || !p.deductionReasons.length) return '';
  const rows = p.deductionReasons.map(dr => {
    const amt = Number(dr.amount)||0;
    if(amt <= 0) return '';
    const src = dr.source === 'manual' ? 'يدوي' : dr.source === 'rule' ? 'قاعدة' : 'تلقائي';
    const lvl = dr.level === 'patient' ? 'المريض' : dr.level === 'line' ? 'البند' : dr.level;
    return `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(dr.reason||'—')}${dr.note?'<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:10.5px">'+escapeHtml(dr.note)+'</div>':''}</td><td style="text-align:center">${escapeHtml(src)}</td><td style="text-align:center">${escapeHtml(lvl)}</td><td class="mono" style="text-align:left;color:var(--bad);font-weight:700">${fmtC(amt)}</td></tr>`;
  }).filter(Boolean).join('');
  if(!rows) return '';
  return `<div class="rv-section-title" style="margin-top:10px">أسباب الخصم لكل مبلغ</div><div class="rv-lines-wrap"><table class="rv-lines-table"><thead><tr><th>السبب</th><th>المصدر</th><th>المستوى</th><th>المبلغ</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div>`;
}
function renderActiveInvoice(){
  const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx];
  const box = (currentPage === 'review' ? document.getElementById('rvPageInvoicePanel') : null) || document.getElementById('rvInvoicePanel') || document.getElementById('rvPageInvoicePanel');
  if(!box) return;
  if(!inv){ box.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  if(!inv.patients) inv.patients = [];
  if(rvActivePatientIdx >= inv.patients.length) rvActivePatientIdx = 0;
  const t = INVOICE_TYPES[inv.type] || {label:inv.type};
  const tot = invoiceTotals(inv);
  const invDocs = DOC_ITEMS.map(d => { const on = !!inv.documents[d.key]; return `<label class="rv-doc ${on?'ok':'miss'}"><input type="checkbox" data-doc="${d.key}" ${on?'checked':''}>${d.label}</label>`; }).join('');
  const rules = `<div class="rv-rule-bar"><button type="button" class="rv-rule-btn" data-rule="lesser">اعتماد الأقل</button><button type="button" class="rv-rule-btn" data-rule="billed">اعتماد كامل</button><button type="button" class="rv-rule-btn danger" data-rule="reject">رفض الكل</button><button type="button" class="rv-rule-btn warn" data-rule="maxlabs">حد التحاليل (${REVIEW_RULES.maxLabsPerVisit})</button></div>`;

  const _isExt = isExternalInvoice(inv);
  const _lineLabel = _isExt ? 'إجراءات المريض' : 'بنود المريض';
  const sections = (inv.patients||[]).map((p, pi) => {
    computePatient(p, activeClaim, inv.date, inv);
    const benType = patientBeneficiaryType(p, activeClaim);
    const rej = (p.lines||[]).filter(l=>l.paRejected).length;
    const capMode = p._capMode || 'auto';
    const capLabel = capMode==='manual'?'يدوي':capMode==='reverted'?'معاد':(p._autoCapRule?'تلقائي':'—');
    const capRow = p._autoCapRule || capMode !== 'auto' ? `<div class="rv-pat-auto-row ${capMode==='manual'?'manual':(capMode==='reverted'?'reverted':'')}"><span class="lbl">🛡️ سقف (${p._autoCapRule?p._autoCapRule.rate+'%':''}) <span class="rv-auto-badge ${capMode==='manual'?'manual':capMode==='reverted'?'reverted':'auto'}">${capLabel}</span></span>${capMode==='manual'?`<input type="number" class="rv-manual-input" data-manual-cap="1" data-pat-idx="${pi}" value="${p._manualCapAmount||0}" min="0" max="${p._subtotal}">`:`<span class="amt">${p._capAmt?fmtC(p._capAmt):'—'}</span><button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn" data-manual-cap-toggle="1" data-pat-idx="${pi}" title="يدوي">✎</button>`}${p._autoCapRule?`<span class="rule-name">${escapeHtml(p._autoCapRule.name)}</span>`:''}${capMode==='auto'&&p._autoCapAmt>0?`<button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn undo" data-revert-cap="1" data-pat-idx="${pi}">↺</button>`:''}${capMode!=='auto'?`<button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn reset" data-reset-cap="1" data-pat-idx="${pi}">⟲</button>`:''}</div>` : '';
    const copayMode = p._copayMode || 'auto';
    const copayLabel = copayMode==='manual'?'يدوي':copayMode==='reverted'?'معاد':(p._autoCopayRule?'تلقائي':'—');
    const copayRow = p._autoCopayRule || copayMode !== 'auto' ? `<div class="rv-pat-auto-row ${copayMode==='manual'?'manual':(copayMode==='reverted'?'reverted':'')}"><span class="lbl">💰 تحمل (${p._autoCopayRule?p._autoCopayRule.rate+'%':''}) <span class="rv-auto-badge ${copayMode==='manual'?'manual':copayMode==='reverted'?'reverted':'auto'}">${copayLabel}</span></span>${copayMode==='manual'?`<input type="number" class="rv-manual-input" data-manual-copay="1" data-pat-idx="${pi}" value="${p._manualCopayAmount||0}" min="0" max="${p._subtotal-p._capAmt}">`:`<span class="amt">${p._copayAmt?fmtC(p._copayAmt):'—'}</span><button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn" data-manual-copay-toggle="1" data-pat-idx="${pi}" title="يدوي">✎</button>`}${p._autoCopayRule?`<span class="rule-name">${escapeHtml(p._autoCopayRule.name)}</span>`:''}${copayMode==='auto'&&p._autoCopayAmt>0?`<button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn undo" data-revert-copay="1" data-pat-idx="${pi}">↺</button>`:''}${copayMode!=='auto'?`<button type="button" class="rv-ov-btn reset" data-reset-copay="1" data-pat-idx="${pi}">⟲</button>`:''}</div>` : '';
    const autoBlock = (capRow||copayRow) ? `<div class="rv-pat-auto ${capMode==='manual'?'has-manual':p._autoCapAmt>0?'has-auto':''}">${capRow}${copayRow}</div>` : '';
    const patBilled = (p.lines||[]).reduce((s,l)=>s+(Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0),0);

        <div class="rv-patient-calc"><div class="rv-pcalc-item"><span>إجمالي المريض</span><b>${fmtC(p._subtotal)}</b></div><div class="rv-pcalc-item ded"><span>سقف</span><b>${p._capAmt?fmtC(p._capAmt):'—'}</b></div><div class="rv-pcalc-item ded"><span>تحمل</span><b>${p._copayAmt?fmtC(p._copayAmt):'—'}</b></div><div class="rv-pcalc-item ok"><span>المعتمد</span><b>${fmtC(p._final)}</b></div></div>
        ${autoBlock}
        ${rej>0?`<div class="rv-pa-reject-note">⛔ ${rej} بند مرفوض — متقاعد بدون موافقة</div>`:''}
        <div class="rv-section-title">مستندات المريض</div>
        <div class="rv-docs">${pDocsHtml}</div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin:8px 0;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <button type="button" class="rv-act-btn primary" data-edit-patient="1" data-pat-idx="${pi}">تعديل المريض</button>
          <button type="button" class="rv-act-btn primary" data-add-line="1" data-pat-idx="${pi}">+ بند</button>
        </div>
        <div class="rv-section-title">${_lineLabel}</div>
        ${rules}
        <div class="rv-lines-wrap"><table class="rv-lines-table"><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>الكود</th><th>البند</th><th>كمية</th><th>فاتورة</th><th>لائحة</th><th>الأقل</th><th>معتمد</th><th>فرق</th><th>حالة</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody data-pat-idx="${pi}">${linesHtml}</tbody></table></div>
        ${_renderDeductionReasons(p)}
      </div>
    </details>`;
  }).join('');

  const totalBilled = (inv.patients||[]).reduce((s,p)=>s+((p.lines||[]).reduce((a,l)=>a+(Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0),0)),0);
  const totalNet = (inv.patients||[]).reduce((s,p)=>s+(p._final||0),0);

  const _catLabel = isExternalInvoice(inv) ? 'خارجية' : 'داخلية';
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="rv-inv-meta">
      <div class="rv-meta-item"><span>نوع الفاتورة</span><b>${_catLabel}</b></div>
      <div class="rv-meta-item"><span>نوع مذكرة الخصم</span><b>${t.label}</b></div>
      <div class="rv-meta-item"><span>نوع المطالبة</span><b>${inv.claimType === 'retiree' ? 'معاشات' : 'عاملين'}</b></div>
      <div class="rv-meta-item"><span>رقم مذكرة الخصم</span><b>${escapeHtml(inv.debitNoteNumber||inv.number||inv.id)}</b></div>
      <div class="rv-meta-item"><span>رقم الفاتورة المرتبطة</span><b>${escapeHtml(inv.linkedInvoiceNumber||inv.invoiceNumber||'—')}</b></div>
      <div class="rv-meta-item"><span>تاريخ</span><b>${inv.debitNoteDate||inv.date||'—'}</b></div>
      <div class="rv-meta-item"><span>${inv.type==='inpatient'?'الإقامة':'عدد المرضى'}</span><b>${inv.type==='inpatient'?(inv.admissionDate||'—')+' ← '+(inv.dischargeDate||'—'):inv.patients.length}</b></div>
    </div>
    <div class="rv-inv-actions">
      <button type="button" class="rv-act-btn primary" data-add-patient="1">+ مريض</button>
      <button type="button" class="rv-act-btn ghost" data-edit-invoice="1">تعديل مذكرة الخصم</button>
      <button type="button" class="rv-act-btn danger" data-delete-invoice="1">حذف مذكرة الخصم</button>
    </div>
    <div class="rv-inv-total"><span>مطالب: <b>${fmtC(tot.billed)}</b></span><span>معتمد: <b style="color:var(--ok)">${fmtC(tot.approved)}</b></span><span>استقطاع: <b style="color:var(--bad)">${fmtC(tot.deduction)}</b></span></div>
    ${!_isExt ? (() => { const checks=[['surgery','الإجراء الجراحي ونوع العملية'],['surgeon','أتعاب الجراح'],['assistant','أتعاب مساعد الجراح'],['anesthesia','أتعاب التخدير وطبيب التخدير'],['or','استخدام وتجهيز غرفة العمليات'],['implants','الدعامات والمسامير والمستلزمات المزروعة'],['consumables','المستهلكات الطبية'],['admission','مبرر الدخول وقرار الحجز'],['stay','عدد أيام الإقامة ومطابقتها'],['room','فئة الغرفة وسعرها'],['icu','العناية المركزة ومبرراتها'],['nursing','خدمات التمريض'],['meds','الأدوية والجرعات'],['labs','التحاليل والفحوص'],['imaging','الأشعة والتقارير'],['blood','نقل الدم ومشتقاته'],['physio','العلاج الطبيعي والتأهيل'],['discharge','ملخص الخروج وخطة العلاج']]; const saved=inv.internalReview||{}; return `<details class="rv-internal-review"><summary>قائمة تدقيق الفاتورة الداخلية والعمليات والإقامة</summary><div class="rv-internal-check-grid">${checks.map(([k,label])=>`<label><input type="checkbox" data-internal-review="${k}" ${saved[k]===true?'checked':''}> ${label}</label>`).join('')}</div><div class="fg" style="margin-top:8px"><label>ملاحظات المراجع على الفاتورة الداخلية</label><textarea data-internal-review-note rows="2">${escapeHtml(saved.note||'')}</textarea></div></details>`; })() : ''}
    <div class="rv-section-title">مستندات مذكرة الخصم</div>
    <div class="rv-docs">${invDocs}</div>
    <div class="rv-section-title">المرضى (${(inv.patients||[]).length})</div>
    <div class="rv-patients-accordion">${sections || '<div class="rv-empty">لا مرضى</div>'}</div>
    <div class="rv-patients-footer">
      <span>عدد المرضى: <b>${(inv.patients||[]).length}</b></span>
      <span>إجمالي: <b>${fmtC(totalBilled)}</b></span>
      <span>مستقطع: <b style="color:var(--bad)">${fmtC(totalBilled-totalNet)}</b></span>
      <span>صافي: <b style="color:var(--ok)">${fmtC(totalNet)}</b></span>
    </div>
    ${(() => {
      const _ext = isExternalInvoice(inv);
      const _totalDed = totalBilled - totalNet;
      if (_ext) {
        const _reasons = (inv.patients||[]).flatMap(p => (p.deductionReasons||[])).map(d => d.reason).filter(Boolean);
        const _reason = _reasons.length ? _reasons[0] : '—';
        return `<div class="rv-section-title">مذكرة الخصم (خارجية — بند واحد)</div>
        <div class="rv-lines-wrap"><table class="rv-lines-table"><thead><tr><th>البند</th><th>المبلغ</th><th>السبب</th></tr></thead><tbody>
          <tr><td>إجمالي الخصم</td><td class="mono" style="color:var(--bad);font-weight:700">${fmtC(_totalDed)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(_reason)}</td></tr>
        </tbody></table></div>`;
      }
      // internal: per-line discounts
      const _rows = (inv.patients||[]).flatMap(p => (p.lines||[]).map(l => {
        const _ded = (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0) - (Number(l.approved)||0);
        if (_ded <= 0) return '';
        const _r = (l._legalDeduction && l._legalDeduction.title) || l.note || '—';
        return `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(l.code)} — ${escapeHtml(l.name)}</td><td class="mono" style="color:var(--bad);font-weight:700">${fmtC(_ded)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(_r)}</td></tr>`;
      })).filter(Boolean).join('');
      return `<div class="rv-section-title">مذكرة الخصم (داخلية — عدة بنود)</div>
      <div class="rv-lines-wrap"><table class="rv-lines-table"><thead><tr><th>البند</th><th>الخصم</th><th>السبب</th></tr></thead><tbody>
        ${_rows || '<tr><td colspan="3" style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted)">لا خصومات</td></tr>'}
        <tr style="font-weight:800"><td>الإجمالي</td><td class="mono" style="color:var(--bad)">${fmtC(_totalDed)}</td><td></td></tr>
      </tbody></table></div>`;
    })()}
    <div class="rv-reapply-bar"><span>📋 القواعد نشطة</span><button type="button" data-reapply-all="1">⟲ إعادة تطبيق</button></div>`;
  if (typeof renderExceptions === 'function') { clearTimeout(window._excDebounce); window._excDebounce = setTimeout(renderExceptions, 120); }
}

function collectExceptions(){
  const pending = [], processed = [], pa = [];
  (workingInvoices||[]).forEach((inv, ii) => (inv.patients||[]).forEach((pat, pi) => (pat.lines||[]).forEach((ln, li) => {
    const item = { inv, ii, pat, pi, ln, li };
    const d = ln._decision;
    if (d === 'needs-attention') pending.push(item);
    else if (d === 'pa-pending') pa.push(item);
    else processed.push(item);
  })));
  return { pending, processed, pa };
}
function _excFilter(){ return (window._excFilterVal || 'all'); }
function setExcFilter(v){ window._excFilterVal = v; renderExceptions(); }
function renderExceptions(){
  const box = document.getElementById('rvExceptions') || document.getElementById('rvPageExceptions');
  if (!box) return;
  const data = collectExceptions();
  const pending = data.pending || [], processed = data.processed || [], pa = data.pa || [];
  const cnt = document.getElementById('rvExcCount') || document.getElementById('rvPageExcCount');
  const totalPending = pending.length + pa.length;
  if (cnt) cnt.textContent = totalPending;
  const filter = _excFilter();
  const showPending = (filter === 'all' || filter === 'pending');
  const showProcessed = (filter === 'all' || filter === 'processed');
  const showPA = (filter === 'all' || filter === 'pa');

  let html = '';
  html += `<div class="rv-exc-summary">${pending.length} معلّقة · ${pa.length} بانتظار PA · ${processed.length} معالجة</div>`;
  html += `<div class="rv-exc-filter" style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin:8px 0;flex-wrap:wrap">
    <button type="button" class="btn-sm ${filter==='all'?'solid':'ghost'}" onclick="setExcFilter('all')">الكل</button>
    <button type="button" class="btn-sm ${filter==='pending'?'solid':'ghost'}" onclick="setExcFilter('pending')">معلّقة (${pending.length})</button>
    <button type="button" class="btn-sm ${filter==='processed'?'solid':'ghost'}" onclick="setExcFilter('processed')">معالجة (${processed.length})</button>
    <button type="button" class="btn-sm ${filter==='pa'?'solid':'ghost'}" onclick="setExcFilter('pa')">بانتظار PA (${pa.length})</button>
  </div>`;

  function cardPending(e){
    const ln = e.ln;
    const reasons = (ln._checksFailed||[]).map(r => reasonLabel(r)).join('، ') || '—';
    const billed = (Number(ln.qty)||0)*(Number(ln.billedUnit)||0);
    const tariffTotal = ln.tariffUnit !== null && ln.tariffUnit !== undefined ? (Number(ln.qty)||0)*Number(ln.tariffUnit) : null;
    const lesser = ln.lineBase !== undefined ? ln.lineBase : (tariffTotal !== null ? Math.min(billed, tariffTotal) : billed);
    const rate = ln._contractRate || 0;
    const after = ln._contractCovered ? Math.round(lesser*(1-rate/100)) : 0;
    const sev = (ln._checksFailed||[]).some(r => isRejectReason(r)) ? 'reject' : 'attention';
    const color = sev === 'reject' ? '#FEF2F2' : '#FFFBEB';
    return `<div class="rv-exc-card" data-exc="${e.ii}-${e.pi}-${e.li}" style="background:${color};border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:10px">
      <div style="font-weight:800;color:var(--navy)">${escapeHtml(ln.code)} · ${escapeHtml(ln.name)}</div>
      <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);margin:4px 0">المريض: ${escapeHtml(e.pat.name)} · فاتورة ${e.inv.id||e.ii+1}</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;margin:6px 0">فاتورة ${fmt(billed)} · لائحة ${tariffTotal!==null?fmt(tariffTotal):'—'} · الأقل ${fmt(lesser)} · خصم ${rate}% → معتمد مقترح ${fmt(after)}</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--warn)">⚠ ${escapeHtml(reasons)}</div>
      <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);margin:4px 0">الاقتراح: ${escapeHtml(ln._suggestedAction?.type||'review')}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-top:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <button type="button" class="btn-sm" data-exc-act="approve" data-exc-key="${e.ii}-${e.pi}-${e.li}">اعتماد</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn-sm" data-exc-act="reduce" data-exc-key="${e.ii}-${e.pi}-${e.li}">تخفيض</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn-sm danger" data-exc-act="reject" data-exc-key="${e.ii}-${e.pi}-${e.li}">رفض</button>
        ${(ln._checksFailed||[]).some(r=>String(r).startsWith('pa')) ? `<button type="button" class="btn-sm" data-exc-act="pa" data-exc-key="${e.ii}-${e.pi}-${e.li}">طلب موافقة</button>` : ''}
      </div>
      <div class="rv-exc-note-wrap" style="margin-top:8px">
        <input type="text" class="rv-exc-note" data-exc-note="${e.ii}-${e.pi}-${e.li}" placeholder="ملاحظة (إلزامية للرفض/التخفيض)">
      </div>
      <div class="rv-exc-reduce-wrap" style="display:none;margin-top:6px" data-exc-reduce="${e.ii}-${e.pi}-${e.li}">
        <input type="number" class="rv-exc-reduce-val" data-exc-reduce-val="${e.ii}-${e.pi}-${e.li}" min="0" max="${after}" value="${after}" placeholder="مبلغ معتمد">
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }

  function cardProcessed(e){
    const ln = e.ln;
    const billed = (Number(ln.qty)||0)*(Number(ln.billedUnit)||0);
    const status = ln._decision || 'auto-approved';
    const icon = status.includes('reject') ? '⛔' : (status.includes('reduce') ? '🔻' : '✅');
    const color = status.includes('reject') ? '#FEF2F2' : (status.includes('reduce') ? '#FFFBEB' : '#ECFDF5');
    const decided = ln._decidedAt ? new Date(ln._decidedAt).toLocaleString() : 'تلقائي';
    const by = ln._decidedBy || 'المحرك';
    const note = ln._decisionNote || ln.note || '';
    const reasons = (ln._checksFailed||[]).map(r=>reasonLabel(r)).join('، ') || '—';
    return `<div class="rv-exc-card" style="background:${color};border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:10px;opacity:.85">
      <div style="font-weight:800;color:var(--navy)">${icon} ${escapeHtml(ln.code)} · ${escapeHtml(ln.name)}</div>
      <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);margin:4px 0">المريض: ${escapeHtml(e.pat.name)} · فاتورة ${e.inv.id||e.ii+1} · ${fmt(billed)}</div>
      <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);margin:4px 0">سبب: ${escapeHtml(reasons)}</div>
      ${note?`<div style="font-size:11.5px;margin:4px 0">ملاحظة: ${escapeHtml(note)}</div>`:''}
      <div style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--muted);margin:4px 0">بواسطة: ${escapeHtml(by)} · ${escapeHtml(decided)}</div>
      <div style="margin-top:6px"><button type="button" class="btn-sm ghost" data-exc-reopen="${e.ii}-${e.pi}-${e.li}">إعادة فتح</button></div>
    </div>`;
  }

  function cardPA(e){
    const ln = e.ln;
    const billed = (Number(ln.qty)||0)*(Number(ln.billedUnit)||0);
    return `<div class="rv-exc-card" style="background:#EFF6FF;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:10px">
      <div style="font-weight:800;color:var(--navy)">⏳ ${escapeHtml(ln.code)} · ${escapeHtml(ln.name)}</div>
      <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);margin:4px 0">المريض: ${escapeHtml(e.pat.name)} · فاتورة ${e.inv.id||e.ii+1} · ${fmt(billed)}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-top:8px">
        <button type="button" class="btn-sm" data-exc-act="approve" data-exc-key="${e.ii}-${e.pi}-${e.li}">الموافقة صادرة</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn-sm danger" data-exc-act="reject" data-exc-key="${e.ii}-${e.pi}-${e.li}">رفض</button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }

  if (showPending && pending.length) {
    html += `<div class="rv-exc-section-title">معلّقة (${pending.length})</div>` + pending.map(cardPending).join('');
  } else if (showPending) {
    html += `<div class="rv-empty" style="padding:12px;color:var(--ok);font-weight:700">✅ لا استثناءات معلّقة</div>`;
  }
  if (showProcessed && processed.length) {
    html += `<div class="rv-exc-section-title">معالجة (${processed.length})</div>` + processed.map(cardProcessed).join('');
  }
  if (showPA && pa.length) {
    html += `<div class="rv-exc-section-title">بانتظار PA (${pa.length})</div>` + pa.map(cardPA).join('');
  }

  if (!pending.length && !processed.length && !pa.length) {
    html = '<div class="rv-empty" style="text-align:center;padding:24px;color:var(--muted)">لا بنود بعد — أضف مذكرة خصم أولًا</div>';
  }

  box.innerHTML = html;
  const btn = document.getElementById('finalizeReviewBtn');
  if (btn) btn.disabled = (pending.length + pa.length) > 0;
}
function renderFinalSummary(){
  // numbers
  const nb = document.getElementById('rvFinalNumbers') || document.getElementById('rvPageFinalNumbers');
  if (nb) {
    const billed = (activeClaim && activeClaim.billed) ? activeClaim.billed : 0;
    let approved = 0, ded = 0;
    (workingInvoices||[]).forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => { if(typeof computePatient === 'function') computePatient(p, activeClaim, inv.date, inv); approved += (p._final||0); }));
    ded = billed - approved;
    nb.innerHTML = `<div class="calc-box"><div class="calc-item"><span>المطالب به</span><b>${fmt(billed)}</b></div><div class="calc-item ded"><span>الاستقطاع</span><b>${fmt(ded)}</b></div><div class="calc-item rate"><span>النسبة</span><b>${billed?Math.round(ded/billed*100):0}%</b></div><div class="calc-item ok"><span>المعتمد</span><b>${fmt(approved)}</b></div></div>`;
  }
  // reasons breakdown - per patient with all deduction reasons
  const rb = document.getElementById('rvFinalReasons') || document.getElementById('rvPageFinalReasons');
  if (rb) {
    let html = '<div class="rv-section-title">أسباب الخصم لكل مريض (كل مبلغ له سبب)</div>';
    (workingInvoices||[]).forEach((inv, ii) => {
      (inv.patients||[]).forEach((p, pi) => {
        if(typeof computePatient === 'function') computePatient(p, activeClaim, inv.date, inv);
        const drs = p.deductionReasons || [];
        if(!drs.length) return;
        const pBilled = (p.lines||[]).reduce((s,l)=>s+(Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0),0);
        const pDed = drs.reduce((s,d)=>s+(Number(d.amount)||0),0);
        html += `<div style="border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:10px;margin-bottom:10px;background:#fff">`;
        html += `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:6px"><div><b>${escapeHtml(p.name)}</b> <span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11px">مذكرة خصم ${escapeHtml(inv.debitNoteNumber||inv.number||inv.id)} | فاتورة مرتبطة ${escapeHtml(inv.linkedInvoiceNumber||inv.invoiceNumber||'—')}</span></div><div style="font-size:12px">مطالب: <b>${fmtC(pBilled)}</b> · مستقطع: <b style="color:var(--bad)">${fmtC(pDed)}</b> · معتمد: <b style="color:var(--ok)">${fmtC(p._final||0)}</b></div></div>`;
        html += `<table style="width:100%;font-size:11.5px;border-collapse:collapse"><thead><tr style="background:#F8FAFC"><th style="text-align:right;padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--line)">السبب</th><th style="text-align:center;padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--line)">المصدر</th><th style="text-align:center;padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--line)">المستوى</th><th style="text-align:center;padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--line)">مرجع</th><th style="text-align:left;padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--line)">المبلغ</th></tr></thead><tbody>`;
        drs.forEach(dr => {
          const amt = Number(dr.amount)||0;
          if(amt <= 0) return;
          const src = dr.source === 'manual' ? 'يدوي' : dr.source === 'rule' ? 'قاعدة' : 'تلقائي';
          const lvl = dr.level === 'patient' ? 'المريض' : dr.level === 'line' ? 'البند' : dr.level;
          const ref = dr.ref && (dr.ref.lineCode || dr.ref.ruleId) ? (dr.ref.lineCode || dr.ref.ruleId) : '—';
          html += `<tr><td style="padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--line)">${escapeHtml(dr.reason||'—')} ${dr.note?'<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:10.5px">'+escapeHtml(dr.note)+'</div>':''}</td><td style="text-align:center;padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--line)">${escapeHtml(src)}</td><td style="text-align:center;padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--line)">${escapeHtml(lvl)}</td><td style="text-align:center;padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--line);font-family:monospace">${escapeHtml(ref)}</td><td style="text-align:left;padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--bad);font-weight:700" class="mono">${fmtC(amt)}</td></tr>`;
        });
        html += `</tbody></table></div>`;
      });
    });
    if(html.indexOf('<table') === -1) html += '<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted)">لا توجد استقطاعات مسجلة</div>';
    rb.innerHTML = html;
  }
  // checklist
  const cb = document.getElementById('rvFinalChecklist') || document.getElementById('rvPageFinalChecklist');
  if (cb) {
    const _ce = typeof collectExceptions === 'function' ? collectExceptions() : {pending:[],processed:[],pa:[]};
    const excs = (_ce.pending||[]).concat(_ce.pa||[]);
    const billed = activeClaim ? (Number(activeClaim.billed)||0) : 0;
    let entered = 0;
    (workingInvoices||[]).forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => { if(typeof computePatient === 'function') computePatient(p, activeClaim, inv.date, inv); (p.lines||[]).forEach(l => { entered += (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0); }); }));
    const mismatch = Math.abs(billed - entered) > 1;
    const paPending = (workingInvoices||[]).reduce((s,inv)=>s+(inv.patients||[]).reduce((a,p)=>a+(p.lines||[]).filter(l=>l._decision==='pa-pending').length,0),0);
    const missingDocsInv = (workingInvoices||[]).reduce((s,inv)=>s+DOC_ITEMS.filter(d=>!inv.documents||!inv.documents[d.key]).length,0);
    const missingDocsPat = (workingInvoices||[]).reduce((s,inv)=>s+(inv.patients||[]).reduce((a,p)=>a+DOC_ITEMS.filter(d=>!(p.documents&&p.documents[d.key])).length,0),0);
    const missingDocs = missingDocsInv + missingDocsPat;
    const items = [
      { key:'match', ok: !mismatch, text: `الإجمالي مُطابق (${fmt(billed)} = ${fmt(entered)})` },
      { key:'exc', ok: excs.length === 0, text: `كل الاستثناءات معالجة (${excs.length} متبقٍ)` },
      { key:'pa', ok: paPending === 0, text: `لا توجد بنود بانتظار موافقة PA (${paPending})` },
      { key:'docs', ok: missingDocs === 0, text: `المستندات مكتملة (${missingDocs} ناقصة)` },
    ];
    cb.innerHTML = '<div class="rv-section-title">قائمة التحقق الإلزامية</div>' + items.map(it => `<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:12px;padding:4px 0;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" class="final-check" data-final-check="${it.key}" ${it.ok?'checked':''} ${it.ok?'':'disabled'}> <span style="color:${it.ok?'var(--text,#111)':'var(--bad,#dc2626)'}">${escapeHtml(it.text)}</span></label>`).join('');
    // gate finalize button
    const finBtn = document.getElementById('finalizeReviewBtn') || document.getElementById('rvPageFinalizeBtn');
    if (finBtn) {
      const allChecked = items.every(it => it.ok);
      finBtn.disabled = !allChecked;
    }
  }
  // default decision
  const approved = (workingInvoices||[]).reduce((s,inv)=>s+(inv.patients||[]).reduce((a,p)=>{ if(typeof computePatient === 'function') computePatient(p, activeClaim, inv.date, inv); return a+(p._final||0); },0),0);
  const billed2 = activeClaim ? (Number(activeClaim.billed)||0) : 0;
  let def = 'partial';
  if (approved === 0) def = 'rejected';
  else if (approved >= billed2 && billed2 > 0) def = 'approved';
  const r = document.querySelector(`input[name="rvDecision"][value="${def}"]`) || document.querySelector(`input[name="rvPageDecision"][value="${def}"]`);
  if (r) r.checked = true;
}
function recalcReview(){
  if(typeof clearValidationCache === 'function') clearValidationCache();
  let billed = 0, eligible = 0, finalApproved = 0;
  workingInvoices.forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(pat => {
    computePatient(pat, activeClaim, inv.date, inv);
    billed += (pat.lines||[]).reduce((s,l)=>s + (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0), 0);
    eligible += pat._subtotal || 0;
    finalApproved += pat._final || 0;
  }));
  const ded = Math.max(0, billed-finalApproved);
  const rate = billed ? (ded/billed*100) : 0;
  const b1 = document.getElementById('calcBilled') || document.getElementById('rvPageCalcBilled');
  const d1 = document.getElementById('calcDed') || document.getElementById('rvPageCalcDed');
  const r1 = document.getElementById('calcRate') || document.getElementById('rvPageCalcRate');
  if(b1) b1.textContent = fmtC(billed);
  if(d1) d1.textContent = fmtC(ded);
  if(r1) r1.textContent = rate.toFixed(1)+'%';
}
function applyReviewRule(rule){
  markSessionDirty();
  const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx]; if(!inv||!inv.patients||!inv.patients.length) return;
  const pat = inv.patients[rvActivePatientIdx]; if(!pat) return;
  if(rule === 'maxlabs'){ applyMaxLabsRule(pat); renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview(); return; }
  pat.lines.forEach(l => {
    const billed = l.qty*l.billedUnit;
    const hasT = l.tariffUnit !== null && l.tariffUnit !== undefined;
    const tt = hasT ? l.qty*l.tariffUnit : billed;
    if(rule === 'lesser'){ l.approved = Math.min(billed, tt); l._overrideMode='manual'; l._manualValue = l.approved; l.note=''; l._maxlabsForced = false; }
    else if(rule === 'billed'){ l.approved = billed; l._overrideMode='manual'; l._manualValue = billed; l.note=''; l._maxlabsForced = false; }
    else if(rule === 'reject'){ l.approved = 0; l._overrideMode='manual'; l._manualValue = 0; l._maxlabsForced = false; }
  });
  renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview();
}

================================================================================
FILE: /mnt/data/phase23work/TPA-Pro-main/js/compute-patient.js
================================================================================

/** Remove only deductions generated by a previous calculation; retain reviewer-entered reasons. */
function resetComputedDeductionReasons(reasons){
  return (Array.isArray(reasons) ? reasons : []).filter(d => d && !d._generatedByCompute && !['auto', 'rule'].includes(d.source));
}
/**
 * حساب مريض واحد: تطبيق التعرفة والعقد والقواعد والأسقف/التحمل.
 * @param {Object} pat - بيانات المريض (بما في ذلك lines)
 * @param {Object} claim - المطالبة
 * @param {string} [refDate] - تاريخ المرجع
 * @param {Object} [invoice] - الفاتورة
 * @returns {Object} pat بعد التحديث
 */
function computePatient(pat, claim, refDate, invoice){
  refDate = refDate || (invoice && invoice.date) || claim.date || claim.receivedDate || daysFromNow(0);
  const allRules = deductionRules.filter(r => r.enabled !== false && r.active !== false);
  const ctx = { claim, patient: pat, invoice: invoice || null };
  const _ctx = getContractContext(claim);
  if (!Array.isArray(pat.deductionReasons)) pat.deductionReasons = [];
  // أعد بناء الأسباب التي ينشئها الحساب في كل مرة، بما فيها التخفيضات اليدوية
  // الناتجة عن override، مع الإبقاء على أسباب المراجع اليدوية المدخلة خارج الحساب.
  pat.deductionReasons = resetComputedDeductionReasons(pat.deductionReasons);
  // دعم مستندات المريض مع توافق خلفي
  if (!pat.documents && invoice && invoice.documents) {
    pat.documents = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(invoice.documents));
  } else if (!pat.documents) {
    pat.documents = { patientId: false, referral: false, medicalReport: false, priorApprovalDoc: false };
  }
  const _addDed = (entry) => {
    if (!entry || !entry.amount || entry.amount <= 0) return;
    pat.deductionReasons.push(Object.assign({
      id: 'dr_' + Date.now().toString(36) + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6),
      source: 'auto',
      _generatedByCompute: true,
      level: 'line',
      decidedBy: 'system',
      decidedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    }, entry));
  };
  (pat.lines||[]).forEach(l => {
    const billed = (Number(l.qty)||0) * (Number(l.billedUnit)||0);
    // re-read tariff price from current tariff (source of truth)
    let tariffUnit = (l.tariffUnit !== null && l.tariffUnit !== undefined) ? Number(l.tariffUnit) : null;
    if (l.tariffId && typeof tariffs !== 'undefined') {
      const t = tariffs.find(x => x.id === l.tariffId);
      const svc = t && (t.services||[]).find(s => String(s.code) === String(l.code));
      if (svc && svc.currentPrice !== undefined && svc.currentPrice !== null) tariffUnit = Number(svc.currentPrice);
    }
    l.tariffUnit = tariffUnit;
    const hasT = (tariffUnit !== null && tariffUnit !== undefined);
    const tariffTotal = hasT ? (Number(l.qty)||0) * tariffUnit : billed;
    const lesser = hasT ? Math.min(billed, tariffTotal) : billed;
    l.lineBase = lesser;
    if(l._overrideMode === undefined) l._overrideMode = 'auto';

    const covered = !l._manualEntry && isServiceCovered(_ctx, l.code) && hasT;
    if(!covered){
      l._autoApproved = 0;
      l._contractRejected = true;
      l._contractRate = 0;
      l.lineBaseAfterContract = 0;
      const _key = l._manualEntry ? 'not-in-tariff' : (isServiceCovered(_ctx, l.code) ? 'not-in-tariff' : 'not-covered');
      l._legalDeduction = (typeof generateLegalDeductionReason === 'function') ? generateLegalDeductionReason(_key, { claim, patient: pat, invoice: invoice || null, line: l }) : null;
      l._legalReason = l._legalDeduction;
      l.legalDeduction = l._legalDeduction;
      const _billedLine = (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0);
      if (_billedLine > 0) {
        _addDed({ amount: _billedLine, reason: l._legalDeduction ? l._legalDeduction.title : (_key==='not-covered'?'غير مغطاة':'غير مدرجة'), reasonCode: _key, source: 'auto', level: 'line', ref: { lineCode: l.code, lineName: l.name }, note: l.note || '' });
      }
      return;
    }
    const rate = getContractRateForService(_ctx, l.code);
    l._contractRate = rate;
    l.lineBaseAfterContract = Math.round(lesser * (1 - rate / 100));
    l._contractRejected = false;
  });
  const compositeChildren = new Set((allRules.filter(r => r.isComposite)).flatMap(r => r.compositeConfig?.childRuleIds || []));
  const lineRules = topoSortRules(allRules.filter(r => r.isComposite || (isLineLevelAction(r.action) && !compositeChildren.has(r.id))));
  pat.lines.forEach(l => {
    const lctx = { ...ctx, line: l };
    const appliedIds = new Set();
    const compositeLog = [];
    let current = (l.lineBaseAfterContract !== undefined ? l.lineBaseAfterContract : l.lineBase);
    let lastRuleId = null, lastRuleName = '', lastRuleRate = 0;
    for(const rule of lineRules){
      if(!checkRequires(rule, appliedIds)) continue;
      if(!ruleMatchesContext(rule, lctx, refDate)) continue;
      if(rule.isComposite){
        const cctx = { ...lctx, line: { ...l, lineBase: current } };
        const res = evaluateCompositeRule(rule, cctx, refDate);
        if(res && res.newApproved !== undefined && res.newApproved < current){
          const _dedAmt = current - res.newApproved;
          current = Math.max(0, Math.min(current, res.newApproved));
          appliedIds.add(rule.id);
          (res.appliedChildRules||[]).forEach(cr => appliedIds.add(cr.id));
          compositeLog.push({ id: rule.id, name: rule.name, children: (res.appliedChildRules||[]).map(c=>c.id) });
          lastRuleId = rule.id; lastRuleName = rule.name; lastRuleRate = 0;
          _addDed({ amount: _dedAmt, reason: rule.name || 'قاعدة مركبة', reasonCode: 'composite-rule', source: 'rule', level: 'line', ref: { lineCode: l.code, ruleId: rule.id }, note: (res.appliedChildRules||[]).map(c=>c.name).join('، ') });
        }
      } else {
        const res = evaluateRuleAction(rule, lctx, current);
        if(res.newApproved !== undefined && res.newApproved < current){
          const _dedAmt = current - res.newApproved;
          current = Math.max(0, Math.min(current, res.newApproved));
          appliedIds.add(rule.id);
          lastRuleId = rule.id; lastRuleName = rule.name; lastRuleRate = rule.rate;
          _addDed({ amount: _dedAmt, reason: rule.name || 'قاعدة خصم', reasonCode: rule.action || 'rule', source: 'rule', level: 'line', ref: { lineCode: l.code, ruleId: rule.id }, note: rule.rate ? `نسبة ${rule.rate}%` : '' });
        }
      }
    }
    l._autoApproved = current;
    l._appliedRuleIds = Array.from(appliedIds);
    l._autoRuleId = lastRuleId; l._autoRuleName = lastRuleName; l._autoRuleRate = lastRuleRate;
    l._compositeApplied = compositeLog;
    l.paRejected = false;
  });
  // ✅ F6: الأولوية لـ _maxlabsForced قبل القواعد العادية
  pat.lines.forEach(l => {
    if(l._contractRejected){
      l.approved = 0;
      l._overrideMode = 'manual';
      l._manualValue = 0;
      if(!l.note) l.note = l._manualEntry ? 'إدخال يدوي غير مدرج' : 'غير مغطاة/غير مدرجة';
      return;
    }
    if(l._maxlabsForced){
      l.approved = 0;
      l._autoApproved = 0;
      l._autoRuleId = '_maxlabs';
      l._autoRuleName = `حد التحاليل (${REVIEW_RULES.maxLabsPerVisit})`;
      l._autoRuleRate = 0;
      l._appliedRuleIds = ['_maxlabs'];
      l.note = 'تجاوز حد التحاليل';
       l._legalDeduction = (typeof generateLegalDeductionReason === 'function') ? generateLegalDeductionReason('max-labs-exceeded', { claim, patient: pat, invoice: invoice || null, line: l, maxLabs: (typeof REVIEW_RULES !== 'undefined' ? REVIEW_RULES.maxLabsPerVisit : 0) }) : null;
       l._legalReason = l._legalDeduction;
       l.legalDeduction = l._legalDeduction;
       const _billedLine = (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0);
       if (_billedLine > 0) _addDed({ amount: _billedLine, reason: l._legalDeduction ? l._legalDeduction.title : 'تجاوز حد التحاليل', reasonCode: 'max-labs-exceeded', source: 'rule', level: 'line', ref: { lineCode: l.code, ruleId: '_maxlabs' }, note: `الحد ${REVIEW_RULES&&REVIEW_RULES.maxLabsPerVisit?REVIEW_RULES.maxLabsPerVisit:''}` });
      return;
    }
    const _billedBefore = (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0);
    const _baseForDed = (l.lineBaseAfterContract !== undefined ? l.lineBaseAfterContract : (l.lineBase !== undefined ? l.lineBase : _billedBefore));
    if(l._overrideMode === 'manual' && l._manualValue !== null && l._manualValue !== undefined){
      const _prevAuto = l._autoApproved !== undefined ? l._autoApproved : _baseForDed;
      l.approved = Math.max(0, Math.min(_billedBefore, Number(l._manualValue)||0));
      if (l.approved < _prevAuto) {
        _addDed({ amount: _prevAuto - l.approved, reason: 'تخفيض يدوي من المراجع', reasonCode: 'manual-reduction', source: 'manual', level: 'line', ref: { lineCode: l.code }, note: l.note || '' });
      } else if (l.approved < _baseForDed && _prevAuto >= _baseForDed) {
        _addDed({ amount: _baseForDed - l.approved, reason: 'تخفيض يدوي من المراجع', reasonCode: 'manual-reduction', source: 'manual', level: 'line', ref: { lineCode: l.code }, note: l.note || '' });
      }
    } else if(l._overrideMode === 'reverted'){ l.approved = l.lineBase; }
    else { l.approved = l._autoApproved; }
    if (!l._contractRejected && !l._maxlabsForced && !l.paRejected && !l._legalDeduction) {
      const _billed = (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0);
      const _tt = (l.tariffUnit !== null && l.tariffUnit !== undefined) ? (Number(l.qty)||0)*Number(l.tariffUnit) : null;
      if (_tt !== null && _billed > _tt + 0.01) {
        l._legalDeduction = (typeof generateLegalDeductionReason === 'function') ? generateLegalDeductionReason('price-exceeds', { claim, patient: pat, invoice: invoice || null, line: l, diff: _billed - _tt }) : null;
      } else if ((Number(l._contractRate)||0) > 0) {
        l._legalDeduction = (typeof generateLegalDeductionReason === 'function') ? generateLegalDeductionReason('contract-discount', { claim, patient: pat, invoice: invoice || null, line: l, rate: l._contractRate }) : null;
      }
      if (l._legalDeduction) { l._legalReason = l._legalDeduction; l.legalDeduction = l._legalDeduction; }
    }
  });
  if(!pat.beneficiaryType) pat.beneficiaryType = claim.type || 'employee';
  const benType = patientBeneficiaryType(pat, claim);
  const paValid = hasValidPriorApproval(claim);
  if(benType === 'retiree' && !paValid){
    pat.lines.forEach(l => { if(lineRequiresPA(l)){ l.paRejected = true; l.approved = 0; l._legalDeduction = (typeof generateLegalDeductionReason === 'function') ? generateLegalDeductionReason('pa-required', { claim, patient: pat, invoice: invoice || null, line: l }) : null; l._legalReason = l._legalDeduction; l.legalDeduction = l._legalDeduction; const _billedLine = (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0); if (_billedLine > 0) _addDed({ amount: _billedLine, reason: l._legalDeduction ? l._legalDeduction.title : 'غياب الموافقة المسبقة', reasonCode: 'pa-required', source: 'auto', level: 'line', ref: { lineCode: l.code }, note: '' }); } });
  }
  const subtotal = pat.lines.reduce((s,l)=>s+(Number(l.approved)||0), 0);
  const pctx = { ...ctx, patient: pat };
  const patientRules = topoSortRules(allRules.filter(r => ['apply_cap','apply_copay'].includes(r.action)));
  let autoCap = 0, capRule = null;
  let autoCopay = 0, copayRule = null;
  const appliedPids = new Set();
  for(const rule of patientRules.filter(r => r.action === 'apply_cap')){
    if(!checkRequires(rule, appliedPids)) continue;
    if(!ruleMatchesContext(rule, pctx, refDate)) continue;
    const r = evaluateRuleAction(rule, pctx, subtotal);
    if(r.deduct !== undefined && r.deduct > autoCap){ autoCap = r.deduct; capRule = rule; }
    appliedPids.add(rule.id);
  }
  for(const rule of patientRules.filter(r => r.action === 'apply_copay')){
    if(!checkRequires(rule, appliedPids)) continue;
    if(!ruleMatchesContext(rule, pctx, refDate)) continue;
    const r = evaluateRuleAction(rule, pctx, subtotal-autoCap);
    if(r.deduct !== undefined && r.deduct > autoCopay){ autoCopay = r.deduct; copayRule = rule; }
    appliedPids.add(rule.id);
  }
  let finalCap = autoCap, finalCopay = autoCopay;
  if(pat._capMode === 'manual' && pat._manualCapAmount !== null && pat._manualCapAmount !== undefined) finalCap = Number(pat._manualCapAmount)||0;
  else if(pat._capMode === 'reverted') finalCap = 0;
  if(pat._copayMode === 'manual' && pat._manualCopayAmount !== null && pat._manualCopayAmount !== undefined) finalCopay = Number(pat._manualCopayAmount)||0;
  else if(pat._copayMode === 'reverted') finalCopay = 0;
  pat._subtotal = subtotal;
  pat._autoCapAmt = autoCap; pat._autoCapRule = capRule;
  pat._autoCopayAmt = autoCopay; pat._autoCopayRule = copayRule;
  pat._capAmt = Math.max(0, Math.min(subtotal, Math.round(finalCap)));
  pat._copayAmt = Math.max(0, Math.min(subtotal-pat._capAmt, Math.round(finalCopay)));
  pat._final = Math.max(0, subtotal-pat._capAmt-pat._copayAmt);
  pat._beneficiaryPays = pat._capAmt + pat._copayAmt;
  if (pat._capAmt > 0) {
    pat._capLegal = (typeof generateLegalDeductionReason === 'function') ? generateLegalDeductionReason('coverage-cap', { claim, patient: pat, capRate: capRule ? capRule.rate : 0, capAmount: pat._capAmt }) : null;
    _addDed({ amount: pat._capAmt, reason: pat._capLegal ? pat._capLegal.title : 'تجاوز سقف التغطية المالي', reasonCode: 'coverage-cap', source: pat._capMode === 'manual' ? 'manual' : (capRule ? 'rule' : 'auto'), level: 'patient', ref: { ruleId: capRule ? capRule.id : null }, note: capRule ? capRule.name : '' });
  }
  if (pat._copayAmt > 0) {
    pat._copayLegal = (typeof generateLegalDeductionReason === 'function') ? generateLegalDeductionReason('retiree-copay', { claim, patient: pat, copayRate: copayRule ? copayRule.rate : 0, copayAmount: pat._copayAmt }) : null;
    _addDed({ amount: pat._copayAmt, reason: pat._copayLegal ? pat._copayLegal.title : 'تحمل المتقاعد/المعاشات', reasonCode: 'retiree-copay', source: pat._copayMode === 'manual' ? 'manual' : (copayRule ? 'rule' : 'auto'), level: 'patient', ref: { ruleId: copayRule ? copayRule.id : null }, note: copayRule ? copayRule.name : '' });
  }
  pat._refDate = refDate;
  if(pat._capMode === undefined) pat._capMode = 'auto';
  if(pat._copayMode === undefined) pat._copayMode = 'auto';
  return pat;
}
function recalcClaim(c){
  if(typeof clearValidationCache === 'function') clearValidationCache();
  // Preserve the amount submitted by the source system before recalculation overwrites billed.
  // Existing records are migrated on first calculation; newly created claims should set this explicitly.
  if (c.submittedBilled === undefined || c.submittedBilled === null || c.submittedBilled === '') {
    const priorBilled = Number(c.billed);
    c.submittedBilled = Number.isFinite(priorBilled) && priorBilled >= 0 ? priorBilled : null;
    c._submittedBilledCapturedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  }
  if(!c.invoices) c.invoices = [];
  let billed = 0, eligible = 0, approved = 0, cap = 0, copay = 0;
  c.invoices.forEach(inv => {
    (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => {
      computePatient(p, c, inv.date, inv);
      billed += (p.lines||[]).reduce((s,l)=>s + (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0), 0);
      eligible += p._subtotal||0; approved += p._final||0;
      cap += p._capAmt||0; copay += p._copayAmt||0;
    });
  });
  c.billed = billed; c.eligible = eligible;
  // قيمة الاستحقاق المحسوبة مستقلة عن حالة سير العمل؛ لا تصفّر المطالبة لمجرد أنها قيد المراجعة.
  c.approved = approved;
  c.violationDeduction = cap + copay;
  c.capDeduction = cap; c.copayDeduction = copay;
}
claims.forEach(c => { if(!c.type) c.type = 'employee'; recalcClaim(c); });

// data-models.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function mkLine(code, name, category, qty, billedUnit, tariffUnit, approved, note){
  const billed = qty * billedUnit;
  const hasT = (tariffUnit !== undefined && tariffUnit !== null);
  const tariffTotal = hasT ? qty * tariffUnit : null;
  const lesser = hasT ? Math.min(billed, tariffTotal) : billed;
  return { code, name, category, qty, billedUnit, tariffUnit: hasT ? tariffUnit : null,
           approved: (approved === undefined ? lesser : approved), note: note || '' };
}
function mkPatient(id, name, nationalId, lines){
  return { id, name: name || '—', nationalId: nationalId || '', lines: lines || [] };
}
function mkInvoice(id, type, date, patients, extra){
  return Object.assign({
    id, number: id, type, date,
    documents: { patientId:true, referral:true, medicalReport:true, priorApprovalDoc:true },
    admissionDate:'', dischargeDate:'', roomClass:'',
    patients: patients || []
  }, extra || {});
}
function allInvoiceLines(inv){
  if(!inv || !inv.patients) return [];
  return inv.patients.reduce((acc,p)=> acc.concat(p.lines || []), []);
}
function invoiceTotals(inv){
  const lines = allInvoiceLines(inv);
  const billed = lines.reduce((a,l)=> a + l.qty*l.billedUnit, 0);
  const approved = lines.reduce((a,l)=> a + (Number(l.approved)||0), 0);
  return { billed, approved, deduction: Math.max(0, billed-approved) };
}

// deduction-rules-2.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function renderPaProcedures(){
  const body = $('#paProcBody'); if(!body) return;
  if(!paProcedures.length){ body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align:center;padding:22px;color:var(--muted)">لا إجراءات</td></tr>'; return; }
  body.innerHTML = paProcedures.map(p => `<tr><td><span class="mono" style="color:var(--navy-400);font-weight:700">${escapeHtml(p.code)}</span></td><td>${escapeHtml(p.name)}</td><td><span class="badge info">${escapeHtml(p.category)}</span></td><td style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted)">${escapeHtml(p.notes||'—')}</td><td class="act"><div style="display:flex;gap:5px;align-items:center"><button class="btn-xs" data-edit-paproc="${escapeHtml(p.code)}">تحرير</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-del-paproc="${escapeHtml(p.code)}">×</button></div></td></tr>`).join('');
}
function openDrModal(id){
  editingDrId = id || null;
  const r = id ? deductionRules.find(x => x.id === id) : null;
  $('#drModalTitle').textContent = r ? 'تعديل' : 'إضافة';
  $('#drModalSub').textContent = r ? r.id : '—';
  $('#drName').value = r ? r.name : ''; $('#drType').value = r ? r.type : 'coverage_cap'; $('#drRate').value = r ? r.rate : 20;
  $('#drFrom').value = r ? r.effectiveFrom : daysFromNow(0); $('#drTo').value = r ? (r.effectiveTo||'') : daysFromNow(365);
  $('#drActive').checked = r ? r.active : true; $('#drNotes').value = r ? r.notes||'' : '';
  const ct = r ? (r.scope.claimTypes||[]) : ['employee'];
  $$('.dr-ctype').forEach(cb => cb.checked = ct.includes(cb.value));
  (el => { if (el) el.style.display = r ? 'block' : 'none'; })($('#deleteDrBtn'));
  $('#drModal').classList.add('open');
}
function closeDrModal(){ $('#drModal').classList.remove('open'); editingDrId = null; }
function saveDr(){
  const name = $('#drName').value.trim(), type = $('#drType').value, rate = Number($('#drRate').value);
  const from = $('#drFrom').value, to = $('#drTo').value;
  if(!name){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الاسم'); return; }
  if(isNaN(rate) || rate < 0 || rate > 100){ showWarning('تنبيه','النسبة'); return; }
  if(!from){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل السريان'); return; }
  const cts = $$('.dr-ctype').filter(cb=>cb.checked).map(cb=>cb.value);
  if(!cts.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','حدد نوع'); return; }
  const data = { name, type, action: type==='coverage_cap'?'apply_cap':'apply_copay', rate, effectiveFrom:from, effectiveTo:to||'', active:$('#drActive').checked, notes:$('#drNotes').value.trim(), scope:{ providerIds:[], contractIds:[], claimTypes:cts } };
  if(editingDrId){ const r = deductionRules.find(x=>x.id===editingDrId); if(r) Object.assign(r, data); showSuccess('تم','تم'); }
  else {
    const y = new Date(from).getFullYear(), pre = 'DR-'+y+'-';
    let m = 0;
    deductionRules.forEach(x => { if(x.id.startsWith(pre)){ const n = parseInt(x.id.split('-').pop(), 10); if(!isNaN(n)) m = Math.max(m,n); } });
    deductionRules.push({ id:pre+String(m+1).padStart(3,'0'), ...data });
    showSuccess('تم','تمت');
  }
  closeDrModal(); renderDeductionRules();
  if (typeof claims !== 'undefined') { claims.forEach(c => { if (typeof recalcClaim === 'function') recalcClaim(c); }); }
  if (typeof persistData === 'function') persistData(true);
  if (typeof renderAll === 'function') renderAll();
}
function deleteDr(id){
  const r = deductionRules.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!r) return;
  showDelete('حذف', `"${r.name}".`, () => {
    deductionRules = deductionRules.filter(x=>x.id!==id);
    // clean up dependencies
    deductionRules.forEach(x => {
      if (x.requires && Array.isArray(x.requires.ruleIds)) x.requires.ruleIds = x.requires.ruleIds.filter(rid => rid !== id);
      if (x.compositeConfig && Array.isArray(x.compositeConfig.childRuleIds)) x.compositeConfig.childRuleIds = x.compositeConfig.childRuleIds.filter(rid => rid !== id);
    });
    closeDrModal(); renderDeductionRules(); showSuccess('تم','تم');
    if (typeof claims !== 'undefined') { claims.forEach(c => { if (typeof recalcClaim === 'function') recalcClaim(c); }); }
    if (typeof persistData === 'function') persistData(true);
    if (typeof renderAll === 'function') renderAll();
  });
}
function openPaProcModal(code){
  editingPaProcCode = code || null;
  const p = code ? paProcedures.find(x=>x.code===code) : null;
  $('#paProcModalTitle').textContent = p ? 'تعديل' : 'إضافة';
  $('#paProcCode').value = p ? p.code : ''; $('#paProcName').value = p ? p.name : '';
  $('#paProcCat').value = p ? p.category : 'عمليات'; $('#paProcNotes').value = p ? p.notes||'' : '';
  $('#paProcModal').classList.add('open');
}
function savePaProc(){
  const code = $('#paProcCode').value.trim(), name = $('#paProcName').value.trim();
  if(!code || !name){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الكود والاسم'); return; }
  const data = { code, name, category:$('#paProcCat').value, notes:$('#paProcNotes').value.trim() };
  if(editingPaProcCode){ const i = paProcedures.findIndex(x=>x.code===editingPaProcCode); if(i>=0) paProcedures[i] = data; }
  else { if(paProcedures.find(x=>x.code===code)){ showWarning('تنبيه','مستخدم'); return; } paProcedures.push(data); }
  $('#paProcModal').classList.remove('open'); editingPaProcCode = null;
  renderPaProcedures(); renderDeductionRules(); showSuccess('تم','تم');
}

function testDeductionRule(id){
  const rule = deductionRules.find(r => r.id === id);
  if(!rule){ showWarning('تنبيه','القاعدة غير موجودة'); return; }
  if(!claims.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','لا مطالبات للاختبار'); return; }
  const claim = claims[0];
  const inv0 = claim.invoices && claim.invoices[0];
  const pat = inv0 && inv0.patients && inv0.patients[0];
  if(!pat || !pat.lines || !pat.lines.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','لا بنود'); return; }
  const line = pat.lines[0];
  const inv = claim.invoices && claim.invoices[0];
  const ctx = { claim, patient: pat, invoice: inv, line: { ...line, lineBase: line.qty * line.billedUnit } };
  ctx.line.lineBase = line.qty * (line.tariffUnit != null ? Math.min(line.billedUnit, line.tariffUnit) : line.billedUnit);
  const refDate = claim.date || claim.receivedDate || daysFromNow(0);
  const matched = ruleMatchesContext(rule, ctx, refDate);
  const applied = new Set();
  const res = rule.isComposite
    ? evaluateCompositeRule(rule, ctx, refDate)
    : evaluateRuleAction(rule, ctx, ctx.line.lineBase);
  const requiresOk = checkRequires(rule, applied);
  const msg = `القاعدة: ${escapeHtml(rule.name)}<br>مطابقة: ${matched ? '✅ نعم' : '❌ لا'}<br>الأساس: ${fmt(ctx.line.lineBase)}<br>النتيجة: ${res.newApproved !== undefined ? fmt(res.newApproved) : fmt(ctx.line.lineBase - (res.deduct||0))}<br>الخصم: ${fmt(res.deduct || (ctx.line.lineBase - (res.newApproved||ctx.line.lineBase)))}`;
  showInfo('اختبار قاعدة', msg);
}


function simulateRule(id){
  const rule = deductionRules.find(r => r.id === id);
  if(!rule){ showWarning('تنبيه','القاعدة غير موجودة'); return; }
  if(!claims.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','لا مطالبات للاختبار'); return; }
  const sample = claims.slice(0, 50);
  let totalLines = 0, affected = 0;
  let totalBefore = 0, totalAfter = 0, totalExtraDed = 0;
  const affectedSamples = [];
  const isPatientLevel = ['apply_cap','apply_copay'].includes(rule.action);

  sample.forEach(claim => {
    const refDate = claim.date || claim.receivedDate || daysFromNow(0);
    if(isPatientLevel){
      (claim.invoices||[]).forEach(inv => {
        (inv.patients||[]).forEach(pat => {
          const subtotal = (pat.lines||[]).reduce((s,l) => s + (Number(l.approved)|| (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0)), 0);
          const ctx = { claim, patient: pat, invoice: inv };
          const matched = ruleMatchesContext(rule, ctx, refDate);
          if(!matched){ totalBefore += subtotal; totalAfter += subtotal; return; }
          const res = evaluateRuleAction(rule, ctx, subtotal);
          const deduct = res.deduct !== undefined ? res.deduct : (subtotal - (res.newApproved !== undefined ? res.newApproved : subtotal));
          const after = Math.max(0, subtotal - deduct);
          totalLines += (pat.lines||[]).length;
          totalBefore += subtotal; totalAfter += after;
          if(deduct > 0){ affected++; totalExtraDed += deduct; if(affectedSamples.length < 8) affectedSamples.push({ claim: claim.id, service: (pat.lines||[]).map(l=>l.name||l.code).join(', ').slice(0,40), before: subtotal, after }); }
        });
      });
    } else {
      (claim.invoices||[]).forEach(inv => {
        (inv.patients||[]).forEach(pat => {
          (pat.lines||[]).forEach(line => {
            totalLines++;
            const base = line.qty * (line.tariffUnit != null ? Math.min(line.billedUnit, line.tariffUnit) : line.billedUnit);
            const ctx = { claim, patient: pat, invoice: inv, line: { ...line, lineBase: base } };
            const matched = ruleMatchesContext(rule, ctx, refDate);
            if(!matched){ totalBefore += base; totalAfter += base; return; }
            const res = rule.isComposite ? evaluateCompositeRule(rule, ctx, refDate) : evaluateRuleAction(rule, ctx, base);
            const newVal = res && res.newApproved !== undefined ? res.newApproved : (base - (res && res.deduct ? res.deduct : 0));
            totalBefore += base; totalAfter += newVal;
            if(newVal < base){ affected++; totalExtraDed += (base - newVal); if(affectedSamples.length < 8) affectedSamples.push({ claim: claim.id, service: line.name || line.code, before: base, after: newVal }); }
          });
        });
      });
    }
  });

  const pct = totalBefore ? ((totalExtraDed/totalBefore)*100).toFixed(1) : '0';
  let msg = `القاعدة: ${rule.name}<br>المطالبات المختبرة: ${sample.length}<br>البنود: ${totalLines}<br>البنود/المرضى المتأثرون: ${affected}<br>إجمالي قبل: ${fmt(totalBefore)}<br>إجمالي بعد: ${fmt(totalAfter)}<br>استقطاع إضافي: ${fmt(totalExtraDed)} (${pct}%)`;
  if(affectedSamples.length){
    msg += '<br><br><b>عينة من البنود المتأثرة:</b><br>';
    affectedSamples.forEach(s => { msg += `${escapeHtml(s.claim)} — ${escapeHtml(s.service)}: ${fmt(s.before)} → ${fmt(s.after)}<br>`; });
  }
  showInfo('محاكاة قاعدة الخصم', msg);
}
