// deduction-rules.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let drFilter = 'all', drSearch = '', editingDrId = null;
let editingPaProcCode = null;
function bindDeductionRulesControls(){
  $$('.dr-tab').forEach(t => t.addEventListener('click', () => {
    $$('.dr-tab').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
    t.classList.add('active');
    $$('.dr-panel').forEach(p => p.classList.toggle('active', p.dataset.drPanel === t.dataset.drTab));
  }));
  $('#drSearch').addEventListener('input', e => { drSearch = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase(); renderDeductionRules(); });
  $('#drFilterChips').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const chip = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!chip) return;
    $$('#drFilterChips .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    chip.classList.add('active'); drFilter = chip.dataset.drf; renderDeductionRules();
  });
  $('#addDrBtn').addEventListener('click', () => openDrWizard());
  $('#drBody').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const ed = e.target.closest('[data-edit-dr]'); if(ed) openDrModal(ed.dataset.editDr);
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-del-dr]'); if(del) deleteDr(del.dataset.delDr);
    const ts = e.target.closest('[data-test-dr]'); if(ts) testDeductionRule(ts.dataset.testDr);
    const sm = e.target.closest('[data-sim-dr]'); if(sm) simulateRule(sm.dataset.simDr);
  });
  $$('[data-close-dr]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', closeDrModal));
  $('#saveDrBtn').addEventListener('click', saveDr);
  $('#deleteDrBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { if(editingDrId) deleteDr(editingDrId); });
  $('#addPaProcBtn').addEventListener('click', () => openPaProcModal());
  $('#paProcBody').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const ed = e.target.closest('[data-edit-paproc]'); if(ed) openPaProcModal(ed.dataset.editPaproc);
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-del-paproc]'); if(del){ const code=del.dataset.delPaproc; const proc=paProcedures.find(x=>x.code===code); if(!proc)return; showDelete('حذف إجراء موافقة مسبقة', `سيتم حذف الإجراء ${proc.name||code}.`, () => { paProcedures = paProcedures.filter(x => x.code !== code); renderPaProcedures(); showSuccess('تم','تم'); }); }
  });
  $$('[data-close-paproc]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', () => $('#paProcModal').classList.remove('open')));
  $('#savePaProcBtn').addEventListener('click', savePaProc);
  bindDrWizard();
}
function drStatus(r){
  const today = daysFromNow(0);
  if(!r.active) return { label:'معطّلة', cls:'inactive' };
  if(r.effectiveFrom && today < r.effectiveFrom) return { label:'لم تبدأ', cls:'upcoming' };
  if(r.effectiveTo && today > r.effectiveTo) return { label:'منتهية', cls:'expired' };
  return { label:'نشطة', cls:'active' };
}
function renderDeductionRules(){
  const activeN = deductionRules.filter(r => drStatus(r).cls === 'active').length;
  const capN = deductionRules.filter(r => r.type === 'coverage_cap').length;
  const copayN = deductionRules.filter(r => r.type === 'retiree_copay').length;
  $('#drKpi').innerHTML = `
    <div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">القواعد</div><div class="kpi-val">${deductionRules.length} <small>قاعدة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('shield')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${activeN} نشطة</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k2"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">سقف</div><div class="kpi-val">${capN} <small>قاعدة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('file')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">للعاملين</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k3"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">تحمل</div><div class="kpi-val">${copayN} <small>قاعدة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('money')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">للمعاشات</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k4"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">إجراءات</div><div class="kpi-val">${paProcedures.length} <small>إجراء</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('contract')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">الموافقة</div></div>`;
  const list = deductionRules.filter(r => {
    if(drFilter === 'active' && drStatus(r).cls !== 'active') return false;
    if(drFilter === 'expired' && drStatus(r).cls !== 'expired') return false;
    if((drFilter === 'coverage_cap' || drFilter === 'retiree_copay') && r.type !== drFilter) return false;
    if(drSearch){ const q = drSearch; return r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.type.includes(q); }
    return true;
  });
  const body = $('#drBody');
  if(!list.length) body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;padding:26px;color:var(--muted)">لا قواعد</td></tr>';
  else body.innerHTML = list.map(r => {
    const st = drStatus(r);
    const tl = r.type === 'coverage_cap' ? 'سقف' : r.type === 'retiree_copay' ? 'تحمل' : r.isComposite ? 'سوبر' : 'مخصص';
    const tc = r.type === 'coverage_cap' ? 'cap' : 'copay';
    const tags = [];
    if(r.scope?.providerIds?.length) tags.push(`<span class="dr-scope-tag">${r.scope.providerIds.length} جهة</span>`);
    (r.scope?.claimTypes||[]).forEach(ct => tags.push(`<span class="dr-scope-tag ${ct==='retiree'?'warn':''}">${ct==='employee'?'عاملين':'معاشات'}</span>`));
    if(!r.scope?.providerIds?.length) tags.unshift('<span class="dr-scope-tag bad">كل</span>');
    return `<tr>
      <td data-label="الرقم"><span class="clm-id">${escapeHtml(r.id)}</span></td>
      <td data-label="الاسم"><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:12.5px">${escapeHtml(r.name)}</div>${r.notes?`<div style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px">${escapeHtml(r.notes)}</div>`:''}</td>
      <td data-label="النوع"><span class="dr-type-badge ${tc}">${tl}</span></td>
      <td data-label="النسبة"><span class="dr-rate-badge">${r.rate}%</span></td>
      <td data-label="النطاق"><div class="dr-scope-tags">${tags.join('')}</div></td>
      <td data-label="السريان" style="font-size:11px;line-height:1.6"><div>${escapeHtml(r.effectiveFrom||'—')}</div><div style="color:var(--muted)">${escapeHtml(r.effectiveTo||'—')}</div></td>
      <td data-label="الحالة"><span class="dr-status-pill ${st.cls}">${st.label}</span></td>
      <td class="act"><div style="display:flex;gap:5px;align-items:center"><button class="btn-xs" data-test-dr="${escapeHtml(r.id)}">اختبار</button><button class="btn-xs" data-sim-dr="${escapeHtml(r.id)}">محاكاة</button><button class="btn-xs" data-edit-dr="${escapeHtml(r.id)}">تحرير</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-del-dr="${escapeHtml(r.id)}">×</button></div></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
  renderPaProcedures();
}

// dialog.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let dialogState = { open:false, onConfirm:null, onCancel:null, interval:null };
function _unlockBodyScroll(){ if(!document.querySelector('.modal.open, .dialog-overlay.open')) document.body.style.overflow = ''; }
const DIALOG_ICONS = {
  info: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="M12 16v-4M12 8h.01"/></svg>',
  success: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M22 11.08V12a10 10 0 1 1-5.93-9.14"/><path d="M22 4 12 14.01l-3-3"/></svg>',
  warning: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M10.29 3.86 1.82 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.71 3h16.94a2 2 0 0 0 1.71-3L13.71 3.86a2 2 0 0 0-3.42 0z"/><path d="M12 9v4M12 17h.01"/></svg>',
  danger: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M10.29 3.86 1.82 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.71 3h16.94a2 2 0 0 0 1.71-3L13.71 3.86a2 2 0 0 0-3.42 0z"/><path d="M12 9v4M12 17h.01"/></svg>',
  delete: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M3 6h18M8 6V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h4a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v2"/><path d="M19 6v14a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H7a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V6"/><path d="M10 11v6M14 11v6"/></svg>'
};
// Render only a small HTML subset in dialog details. Imported text is never
// allowed to introduce scripts, event handlers, URLs, forms, or executable nodes.
function sanitizeDialogBody(markup){
  const template = document.createElement('template');
  template.innerHTML = String(markup || '');
  const allowedTags = new Set(['DIV','SPAN','B','STRONG','P','BR','LABEL','INPUT']);
  const allowedAttrs = new Set(['class','style','type','id','name','value','checked','disabled','for']);
  const clean = node => {
    Array.from(node.childNodes).forEach(child => {
      if(child.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE){
        if(!allowedTags.has(child.tagName)){
          child.replaceWith(document.createTextNode(child.textContent || ''));
          return;
        }
        Array.from(child.attributes).forEach(attr => {
          const name = attr.name.toLowerCase();
          if(!allowedAttrs.has(name)) child.removeAttribute(attr.name);
          else if(name === 'style'){
            const safe = attr.value.split(';').filter(rule => {
              const lower = rule.toLowerCase();
              return !/(url\s*\(|expression\s*\(|@import|behavior\s*:|-moz-binding)/i.test(lower);
            }).join(';');
            if(safe) child.setAttribute('style', safe); else child.removeAttribute('style');
          }
        });
        if(child.tagName === 'INPUT'){
          const type = (child.getAttribute('type') || 'text').toLowerCase();
          if(!['checkbox','radio'].includes(type)) child.replaceWith(document.createTextNode(''));
        }
        clean(child);
      } else if(child.nodeType !== Node.TEXT_NODE){ child.remove(); }
    });
  };
  clean(template.content);
  return template.content;
}
function setDialogButtonLabel(button, label, countdown){
  button.textContent = String(label || 'حسنًا');
  if(countdown !== undefined){
    const span = document.createElement('span');
    span.className = 'countdown-num'; span.textContent = ` (${countdown})`;
    button.appendChild(span);
  }
}
function openDialog(opts){
  if(dialogState.interval){ clearInterval(dialogState.interval); dialogState.interval = null; }
  const type = opts.type || 'info';
  const iconType = type === 'delete' ? 'delete' : type;
  $('#dialogIcon').className = 'dialog-icon-wrap ' + (iconType === 'delete' ? 'danger' : type);
  $('#dialogIcon').innerHTML = DIALOG_ICONS[iconType] || DIALOG_ICONS.info;
  $('#dialogTitle').textContent = opts.title || '';
  $('#dialogMessage').innerHTML = escapeHtml(opts.message || '');
  (el => { if (el) el.style.display = opts.message ? 'block' : 'none'; })($('#dialogMessage'));
  const dialogBody = $('#dialogBody'); dialogBody.replaceChildren(sanitizeDialogBody(opts.body || '')); 
  (el => { if (el) el.style.display = opts.body ? 'block' : 'none'; })($('#dialogBody'));
  const actions = $('#dialogActions'); actions.innerHTML = '';
  if(opts.requireCountdown){
    let rem = 5;
    const cBtn = document.createElement('button');
    cBtn.className = 'dialog-btn primary ' + (iconType === 'delete' || type === 'danger' ? 'danger' : (type === 'warning' ? 'warning' : 'info'));
    cBtn.disabled = true;
    setDialogButtonLabel(cBtn, opts.confirmText || 'حذف', rem);
    const xBtn = document.createElement('button');
    xBtn.className = 'dialog-btn secondary'; xBtn.textContent = opts.cancelText || 'إلغاء';
    xBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { closeDialog(); if(opts.onCancel) opts.onCancel(); });
    actions.appendChild(cBtn); actions.appendChild(xBtn);
    dialogState.interval = setInterval(() => {
      rem--;
      if(rem > 0) setDialogButtonLabel(cBtn, opts.confirmText || 'حذف', rem);
      else { clearInterval(dialogState.interval); dialogState.interval = null; cBtn.disabled = false; setDialogButtonLabel(cBtn, opts.confirmText || 'حذف'); }
    }, 1000);
    cBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { if(cBtn.disabled) return; clearInterval(dialogState.interval); dialogState.interval = null; closeDialog(); if(opts.onConfirm) opts.onConfirm(); });
  } else {
    if(opts.cancelText !== null){
      const xBtn = document.createElement('button');
      xBtn.className = 'dialog-btn secondary'; xBtn.textContent = opts.cancelText || 'إلغاء';
      xBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { closeDialog(); if(opts.onCancel) opts.onCancel(); });
      actions.appendChild(xBtn);
    }
    const cBtn = document.createElement('button');
    cBtn.className = 'dialog-btn primary ' + (iconType === 'delete' || type === 'danger' ? 'danger' : (type === 'warning' ? 'warning' : (type === 'info' ? 'info' : '')));
    cBtn.textContent = String(opts.confirmText || 'حسنًا');
    cBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { closeDialog(); if(opts.onConfirm) opts.onConfirm(); });
    actions.appendChild(cBtn);
  }
  dialogState.onConfirm = opts.onConfirm; dialogState.onCancel = opts.onCancel; dialogState.open = true;
  $('#unifiedDialog').classList.add('open'); document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
}
function closeDialog(){
  if(dialogState.interval){ clearInterval(dialogState.interval); dialogState.interval = null; }
  $('#unifiedDialog').classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); dialogState.open = false;
}
function showInfo(t,m,oc){ openDialog({type:'info', title:t, message:m, confirmText:'حسنًا', cancelText:null, onConfirm:oc}); }
function showSuccess(t,m,oc){ openDialog({type:'success', title:t, message:m, confirmText:'تم', cancelText:null, onConfirm:oc}); }
function showWarning(t,m,oc,xc){ openDialog({type:'warning', title:t, message:m, confirmText:'متابعة', cancelText:'إلغاء', onConfirm:oc, onCancel:xc}); }
function showDelete(t,m,oc){ openDialog({type:'delete', title:t, message:m, confirmText:'حذف نهائي', cancelText:'إلغاء', requireCountdown:true, onConfirm:oc}); }
$$('[data-dialog-close]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', () => { if(dialogState.interval) clearInterval(dialogState.interval); closeDialog(); if(dialogState.onCancel) dialogState.onCancel(); }));

function openInvoiceEditor(idx){

  $('#invModalSub').textContent = inv ? (inv.debitNoteNumber||inv.number||inv.id) : '—';
  const ctype = activeClaim ? (activeClaim.type || 'employee') : 'employee';
  if(document.getElementById('invClaimType')) document.getElementById('invClaimType').value = inv && inv.claimType ? inv.claimType : ctype;
  if(document.getElementById('invCategory')) document.getElementById('invCategory').value = inv && (inv.category||inv.invoiceCategory) ? (inv.category||inv.invoiceCategory) : (inv && inv.type==='inpatient' ? 'internal' : 'external');
  if(inv){
    $('#invType').value = inv.type; $('#invNumber').value = inv.debitNoteNumber||inv.number||inv.id; $('#invDate').value = inv.debitNoteDate||inv.date||'';

}
function saveInvoiceEditor(confirmed){

  }

    return;
  }

  const inpData = { category: invoiceCategory, invoiceCategory, admissionDate: type==='inpatient'?($('#invAdmission').value||''):'', dischargeDate: type==='inpatient'?($('#invDischarge').value||''):'', roomClass: type==='inpatient'?$('#invRoomClass').value.trim():'' };
  if(rvEditInvoiceIdx >= 0){

    if(inv.patients && inv.patients.length) inv.patients.forEach(p => { if(p.beneficiaryType === undefined) p.beneficiaryType = claimType; });
    else inv.patients = [mkPatient(newPatientId(inv), pName, pNat, [])];
    if(inv.patients[0]) { inv.patients[0].name = pName; inv.patients[0].nationalId = pNat; if(!inv.patients[0].beneficiaryType) inv.patients[0].beneficiaryType = claimType; }
    rvActiveInvoiceIdx = rvEditInvoiceIdx;
  } else {
    const id = newInvoiceId();
    const inv = mkInvoice(id, type, date, [mkPatient(id+'-P1', pName, pNat, [])]);
    inv.claimType = claimType; inv.debitNoteNumber = num || id; inv.number = inv.debitNoteNumber; inv.debitNoteDate = date; inv.date = date; inv.linkedInvoiceNumber = linked; inv.invoiceNumber = linked;
    if(inv.patients[0]) { inv.patients[0].beneficiaryType = claimType; inv.patients[0].documents = {}; }
    Object.assign(inv, inpData); workingInvoices.push(inv); rvActiveInvoiceIdx = workingInvoices.length-1;
  }
  rvActivePatientIdx = 0; closeInvoiceEditor(); renderReviewInvoices();
}

// engine-fields-2.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function compareValues(actual, op, exp, ctx){
  ctx = ctx || {};
  if(isDateOperator(op)) return compareDates(actual, op, exp, ctx);
  if(op === 'len_eq') return String(actual||'').length === parseNumber(exp);
  if(op === 'len_gt') return String(actual||'').length > parseNumber(exp);
  if(op === 'len_lt') return String(actual||'').length < parseNumber(exp);
  if(op === 'normalize_eq') return normalizeArabic(actual) === normalizeArabic(exp);
  if(op === 'normalize_contains') return normalizeArabic(actual).includes(normalizeArabic(exp));
  if(op === 'regex_match'){ try { return new RegExp(String(exp)).test(String(actual||'')); } catch(e){ return false; } }
  if(op === 'similar_to'){ const [t, th] = String(exp||'').split('|'); return levenshteinSimilarity(String(actual||''), t||'') >= (parseNumber(th)||80); }
  if(op === 'not_contains') return !String(actual||'').includes(String(exp||''));
  if(op === 'num_between'){ const [lo, hi] = String(exp||'').split(',').map(parseNumber); return parseNumber(actual)>=lo && parseNumber(actual)<=hi; }
  if(op === 'approx_eq'){ const [t, tol] = String(exp||'').split('|'); return Math.abs(parseNumber(actual)-parseNumber(t)) <= (parseNumber(tol)||0.5); }
  if(['gt','gte','lt','lte'].includes(op)){
    const a = parseNumber(actual), b = parseNumber(exp);
    if(String(actual).trim()!=='' && String(exp).trim()!==''){
      if(op === 'gt') return a > b;
      if(op === 'gte') return a >= b;
      if(op === 'lt') return a < b;
      if(op === 'lte') return a <= b;
    }
  }
  switch(op){
    case 'eq': return String(actual) === String(exp);
    case 'ne': return String(actual) !== String(exp);
    case 'in': { const arr = String(exp||'').split(',').map(s=>s.trim()); return arr.includes(String(actual)); }
    case 'not_in': { const arr = String(exp||'').split(',').map(s=>s.trim()); return !arr.includes(String(actual)); }
    case 'contains': return String(actual||'').includes(String(exp||''));
    case 'starts_with': return String(actual||'').startsWith(String(exp||''));
    case 'exists': return actual !== undefined && actual !== null;
    case 'is_empty': return !actual || String(actual).trim() === '';
    case 'is_not_empty': return actual !== undefined && actual !== null && String(actual).trim() !== '';
    case 'is_true': return actual === true || actual === 'true';
    case 'is_false': return actual === false || actual === 'false';
    // عامل مقارنة غير معروف: افشل بشكل مغلق بدل تمرير القاعدة.
    default: return false;
  }
}
function getScopeItems(scope, ctx){
  if(scope === 'self' || scope === 'line') return ctx.line ? [{line:ctx.line, patient:ctx.patient, invoice:ctx.invoice, claim:ctx.claim}] : [];
  if(scope === 'patient') return (ctx.patient?.lines||[]).map(l=>({line:l, patient:ctx.patient, invoice:ctx.invoice, claim:ctx.claim}));
  if(scope === 'invoice') return (ctx.invoice?.patients||[]).flatMap(pt=>(pt.lines||[]).map(l=>({line:l, patient:pt, invoice:ctx.invoice, claim:ctx.claim})));
  if(scope === 'claim') return (ctx.claim?.invoices||[]).flatMap(iv=>(iv.patients||[]).flatMap(pt=>(pt.lines||[]).map(l=>({line:l, patient:pt, invoice:iv, claim:ctx.claim}))));
  return [];
}
function evaluateCondition(cond, ctx){
  if(!cond) return true;
  const mode = cond.mode || 'expr';
  const scope = cond.scope || 'self';
  if(mode === 'expr'){ if(!cond.field || !cond.op) return false; return compareValues(resolveFieldPath(cond.field, ctx), cond.op, cond.value, ctx); }
  if(mode === 'duplicate' || mode === 'unique'){
    const f = cond.field || 'line.code';
    const items = getScopeItems(scope, ctx);
    const my = resolveFieldPath(f, ctx);
    const cnt = items.filter(it => String(resolveFieldPath(f, it)) === String(my)).length;
    return mode === 'duplicate' ? cnt > 1 : cnt === 1;
  }
  const items = getScopeItems(scope, ctx);
  if(mode === 'any'){ if(!cond.subField || !cond.subOp) return false; return items.some(it => compareValues(resolveFieldPath(cond.subField, it), cond.subOp, cond.subValue, it)); }
  if(mode === 'all'){ if(!cond.subField || !cond.subOp) return false; if(!items.length) return false; return items.every(it => compareValues(resolveFieldPath(cond.subField, it), cond.subOp, cond.subValue, it)); }
  if(mode === 'count'){ if(!cond.subField || !cond.subOp) return false; const n = items.filter(it => compareValues(resolveFieldPath(cond.subField, it), cond.subOp, cond.subValue, it)).length; return compareValues(n, cond.compareOp||'gt', cond.compareValue||0, ctx); }
  if(mode === 'frequency'){
    // count occurrences of same field value within windowDays in the given scope
    const f = cond.field || 'line.code';
    const windowDays = Number(cond.windowDays) || 0;
    const items = getScopeItems(scope, ctx);
    const myVal = resolveFieldPath(f, ctx);
    const myDate = ctx.invoice?.date || ctx.claim?.date || ctx.line?.date;
    let cnt = 0;
    items.forEach(it => {
      if(String(resolveFieldPath(f, it)) !== String(myVal)) return;
      const d = it.invoice?.date || it.claim?.date || it.line?.date;
      if(windowDays > 0 && myDate && d){
        const diff = Math.abs(new Date(myDate) - new Date(d)) / (1000*60*60*24);
        if(diff <= windowDays) cnt++;
      } else { cnt++; }
    });
    return compareValues(cnt, cond.compareOp || 'gt', cond.compareValue || 1, ctx);
  }
  if(mode === 'bundle'){
    // true if all component codes are present in the scope
    const comps = Array.isArray(cond.components) ? cond.components : String(cond.components||'').split(',').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if(!comps.length) return false;
    const items = getScopeItems(scope, ctx);
    const codes = items.map(it => String(resolveFieldPath(cond.field || 'line.code', it)));
    return comps.every(c => codes.includes(String(c)));
  }
  if(mode === 'diagnosis_match'){
    // verify claim diagnosis is compatible with the service (allowed list)
    const allowed = String(cond.value || '').split(',').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    const dx = resolveFieldPath(cond.field || 'claim.diagnosis', ctx);
    if(!allowed.length) return false;
    return allowed.includes(String(dx));
  }
  if(['sum','avg','max','min'].includes(mode)){
    const vals = items.map(it => Number(resolveFieldPath(cond.aggField||'line.billedTotal', it))||0);
    let res = 0;
    if(mode === 'sum') res = vals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
    else if(mode === 'avg') res = vals.length ? vals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/vals.length : 0;
    else if(mode === 'max') res = vals.length ? Math.max(...vals) : 0;
    else if(mode === 'min') res = vals.length ? Math.min(...vals) : 0;
    return compareValues(Math.round(res), cond.compareOp||'gt', cond.compareValue||0, ctx);
  }
  return false;
}
function evaluateConditionTree(node, ctx){
  if(!node || !Array.isArray(node.rules)) return true;
  if(!node.rules.length) return node.logic === 'OR' ? false : true;
  const res = node.rules.map(item => {
    if(item.logic && Array.isArray(item.rules)) return evaluateConditionTree(item, ctx);
    return evaluateCondition(item, ctx);
  });
  return node.logic === 'OR' ? res.some(Boolean) : res.every(Boolean);
}

FILE: /mnt/data/phase23work/TPA-Pro-main/js/engine-fields.js

// engine-fields.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function resolveFieldPath(path, ctx){
  if(!path) return null;
  if(path === 'line.billedTotal') return ctx.line ? ctx.line.qty * ctx.line.billedUnit : null;
  if(path === 'line.tariffTotal'){
    if(!ctx.line) return null;
    return (ctx.line.tariffUnit !== null && ctx.line.tariffUnit !== undefined) ? ctx.line.qty * ctx.line.tariffUnit : null;
  }
  if(path === 'line.diff'){
    if(!ctx.line) return null;
    const b = ctx.line.qty * ctx.line.billedUnit;
    return b - (ctx.line.lineBase !== undefined ? ctx.line.lineBase : b);
  }
  if(path === 'line.index'){ if(!ctx.patient || !ctx.line) return null; return (ctx.patient.lines||[]).indexOf(ctx.line)+1; }
  if(path === 'line.nameNormalized') return ctx.line ? normalizeArabic(ctx.line.name) : null;
  if(path === 'line.nameLength') return ctx.line ? String(ctx.line.name||'').length : null;
  if(path === 'line.billedRounded100') return ctx.line ? roundToNearest(ctx.line.qty*ctx.line.billedUnit, 100) : null;
  if(path === 'claim.invoiceCount') return (ctx.claim?.invoices||[]).length;
  if(path === 'claim.patientCount') return (ctx.claim?.invoices||[]).reduce((s,iv)=>s+(iv.patients||[]).length,0);
  if(path === 'claim.lineCount') return (ctx.claim?.invoices||[]).reduce((s,iv)=>s+(iv.patients||[]).reduce((a,p)=>a+(p.lines||[]).length,0),0);
  if(path === 'invoice.patientCount') return (ctx.invoice?.patients||[]).length;
  if(path === 'invoice.lineCount') return (ctx.invoice?.patients||[]).reduce((a,p)=>a+(p.lines||[]).length,0);
  if(path === 'patient.lineCount') return (ctx.patient?.lines||[]).length;
  if(path === 'claim.daysSinceReceived'){
    const d = toDate(ctx.claim?.receivedDate); if(!d) return null;
    const now = new Date(); now.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    return Math.round((now-d)/86400000);
  }
  if(path === 'claim.daysBetweenReceivedAndDate') return daysBetween(ctx.claim?.receivedDate, ctx.claim?.date);
  if(path === 'invoice.daysSince'){
    const d = toDate(ctx.invoice?.date); if(!d) return null;
    const now = new Date(); now.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    return Math.round((now-d)/86400000);
  }
  if(path === 'invoice.stayDays'){
    if(ctx.invoice?.type !== 'inpatient') return null;
    return daysBetween(ctx.invoice?.admissionDate, ctx.invoice?.dischargeDate);
  }
  const cm = path.match(/^(.+)\.(year|month|day|dayOfWeek|quarter|dayName|dayOfYear)$/);
  if(cm){
    const base = resolveFieldPath(cm[1], ctx);
    const d = toDate(base);
    if(!d) return null;
    const c = cm[2];
    if(c === 'year') return d.getFullYear();
    if(c === 'month') return d.getMonth()+1;
    if(c === 'day') return d.getDate();
    if(c === 'dayOfWeek') return d.getDay();
    if(c === 'quarter') return Math.floor(d.getMonth()/3)+1;
    if(c === 'dayName') return ['الأحد','الإثنين','الثلاثاء','الأربعاء','الخميس','الجمعة','السبت'][d.getDay()];
    if(c === 'dayOfYear') return dayOfYear(d);
  }
  if(path === 'patient.beneficiaryType') return (ctx.patient && ctx.patient.beneficiaryType) || (ctx.claim && ctx.claim.type) || null;
  const parts = path.split('.');
  let v = ctx;
  for(const p of parts){ if(v === null || v === undefined) return null; v = v[p]; }
  return v;
}
function resolveFieldRef(exp, ctx){
  if(typeof exp !== 'string') return exp;
  const prefixes = ['claim.', 'line.', 'patient.', 'invoice.'];
  if(!prefixes.some(p=>exp.startsWith(p))) return exp;
  const v = resolveFieldPath(exp, ctx || {});
  return (v !== null && v !== undefined) ? v : exp;
}
const DATE_OPS = ['before','after','on_or_before','on_or_after','between_dates','not_between_dates','same_day','same_month','same_year','same_quarter','is_today','is_weekend','is_weekday','in_last_n_days','in_next_n_days','is_this_month','is_this_quarter','is_this_year','is_last_month','is_last_quarter','is_last_year','before_field','after_field','on_same_day_as','same_week_as','before_field_days','after_field_days','between_fields','working_days_since','within_days','within_days_of','is_business_day','is_not_business_day','is_first_day_of_month','is_last_day_of_month'];
function isDateOperator(op){ return DATE_OPS.includes(op); }
function compareDates(actual, op, exp, ctx){
  ctx = ctx || {};
  const a = toDate(resolveDateKeyword(actual));
  const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  if(op === 'is_today') return a && a.toDateString() === today.toDateString();
  if(op === 'is_weekend'){ if(!a) return false; const d=a.getDay(); return d===0||d===6; }
  if(op === 'is_weekday'){ if(!a) return false; const d=a.getDay(); return d!==0&&d!==6; }
  if(op === 'is_business_day') return isBusinessDay(a);
  if(op === 'is_not_business_day') return a && !isBusinessDay(a);
  if(op === 'is_first_day_of_month') return a && a.getDate() === 1;
  if(op === 'is_last_day_of_month') return a && a.getDate() === getDaysInMonth(a);
  if(!a) return false;
  if(op === 'before_field' || op === 'after_field' || op === 'on_same_day_as' || op === 'same_week_as'){
    const ref = toDate(resolveDateKeyword(resolveFieldRef(exp, ctx)));
    if(!ref) return false;
    if(op === 'before_field') return a < ref;
    if(op === 'after_field') return a > ref;
    if(op === 'on_same_day_as') return a.toDateString() === ref.toDateString();
    if(op === 'same_week_as') return isSameWeek(a, ref);
  }
  if(op === 'before_field_days' || op === 'after_field_days'){
    const [fr, nr] = String(exp||'').split('|');
    const ref = toDate(resolveDateKeyword(resolveFieldRef(fr, ctx)));
    const n = parseNumber(nr)||0;
    if(!ref) return false;
    const b = new Date(ref.getTime() + (op==='before_field_days'?-n:n)*86400000);
    return op === 'before_field_days' ? a < b : a > b;
  }
  if(op === 'between_fields'){
    const parts = String(exp||'').split(',').map(x=>toDate(resolveDateKeyword(resolveFieldRef(x.trim(), ctx))));
    const [f1,f2] = parts;
    if(!f1||!f2) return false;
    const lo = f1<f2?f1:f2, hi = f1<f2?f2:f1;
    return a >= lo && a <= hi;
  }
  if(op === 'working_days_since'){
    const [fr, nr] = String(exp||'').split('|');
    const ref = toDate(resolveDateKeyword(resolveFieldRef(fr, ctx)));
    if(!ref) return false;
    return countWorkingDays(ref, today) >= (parseNumber(nr)||0);
  }
  if(op === 'within_days'){ const n = parseNumber(exp)||0; return Math.abs(Math.round((today-a)/86400000)) <= n; }
  if(op === 'within_days_of'){
    const [rr, nr] = String(exp||'').split('|');
    const ref = toDate(resolveDateKeyword(rr));
    const n = parseNumber(nr)||0;
    return ref && Math.abs(Math.round((a-ref)/86400000)) <= n;
  }
  if(op === 'in_last_n_days'){ const n = parseNumber(exp)||0; const d = Math.floor((today-a)/86400000); return d>=0 && d<=n; }
  if(op === 'in_next_n_days'){ const n = parseNumber(exp)||0; const d = Math.floor((a-today)/86400000); return d>=0 && d<=n; }
  if(op === 'is_this_month') return a.getFullYear()===today.getFullYear() && a.getMonth()===today.getMonth();
  if(op === 'is_this_quarter') return a.getFullYear()===today.getFullYear() && Math.floor(a.getMonth()/3)===Math.floor(today.getMonth()/3);
  if(op === 'is_this_year') return a.getFullYear()===today.getFullYear();
  if(op === 'is_last_month'){ const t = new Date(today); t.setMonth(t.getMonth()-1); return a.getFullYear()===t.getFullYear() && a.getMonth()===t.getMonth(); }
  if(op === 'is_last_quarter'){ const t = new Date(today); t.setMonth(t.getMonth()-3); return a.getFullYear()===t.getFullYear() && Math.floor(a.getMonth()/3)===Math.floor(t.getMonth()/3); }
  if(op === 'is_last_year') return a.getFullYear()===today.getFullYear()-1;
  if(op === 'between_dates' || op === 'not_between_dates'){
    const [e1, e2] = String(exp||'').split(',').map(x=>toDate(resolveDateKeyword(x.trim())));
    if(!e1||!e2) return false;
    const within = a>=e1 && a<=e2;
    return op === 'between_dates' ? within : !within;
  }
  const b = toDate(resolveDateKeyword(exp));
  if(!b) return false;
  if(op === 'before') return a < b;
  if(op === 'after') return a > b;
  if(op === 'on_or_before') return a <= b;
  if(op === 'on_or_after') return a >= b;
  if(op === 'same_day') return a.toDateString() === b.toDateString();
  if(op === 'same_month') return a.getFullYear()===b.getFullYear() && a.getMonth()===b.getMonth();
  if(op === 'same_year') return a.getFullYear()===b.getFullYear();
  if(op === 'same_quarter') return a.getFullYear()===b.getFullYear() && Math.floor(a.getMonth()/3)===Math.floor(b.getMonth()/3);
  return false;
}

FILE: /mnt/data/phase23work/TPA-Pro-main/js/engine-rules.js

// engine-rules.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function isLineLevelAction(a){ return ['reject_line','reduce_by_percent','reduce_by_fixed','cap_to_amount','set_to_percent'].includes(a); }
function topoSortRules(rules){
  const byId = {}; rules.forEach(r => byId[r.id] = r);
  const visited = {}, sorted = [], stack = new Set(), cycles = [];
  function visit(id, path){
    if(visited[id]) return;
    if(stack.has(id)){ const cs = path.indexOf(id); if(cs >= 0) cycles.push(path.slice(cs).concat([id]).join(' → ')); return; }
    stack.add(id);
    const r = byId[id];
    if(!r){ stack.delete(id); return; }
    (r.requires?.ruleIds||[]).forEach(dep => { if(byId[dep]) visit(dep, path.concat([id])); });
    stack.delete(id);
    visited[id] = true; sorted.push(r);
  }
  rules.forEach(r => visit(r.id, []));
  if(cycles.length){
    console.warn('⚠️ حلقات دائرية:', cycles);
  }
  const cyclicIds = new Set(cycles.flatMap(c => c.split(' → ')));
  return sorted.filter(r => !cyclicIds.has(r.id));
}
function checkRequires(rule, appliedIds){
  if(!rule.requires || !rule.requires.ruleIds || !rule.requires.ruleIds.length) return true;
  const mode = rule.requires.mode || 'any';
  if(mode === 'any') return rule.requires.ruleIds.some(id => appliedIds.has(id));
  if(mode === 'all') return rule.requires.ruleIds.every(id => appliedIds.has(id));
  if(mode === 'none') return !rule.requires.ruleIds.some(id => appliedIds.has(id));
  // Fail closed: an unknown dependency mode must never silently bypass prerequisites.
  return false;
}
function ruleMatchesContext(rule, ctx, refDate){
  if(rule.enabled === false || rule.active === false) return false;
  const rDate = (typeof toDate === 'function') ? toDate(refDate) : new Date(refDate);
  if(rule.effectiveFrom && rDate < toDate(rule.effectiveFrom)) return false;
  if(rule.effectiveTo && rDate > toDate(rule.effectiveTo)) return false;
  const claim = ctx.claim;
  if(rule.scope?.claimTypes?.length && !rule.scope.claimTypes.includes(claim.type || 'employee')) return false;
  if(rule.scope?.providerIds?.length){
    const prov = providers.find(p => p.name === claim.provider);
    if(!prov || !rule.scope.providerIds.includes(prov.id)) return false;
  }
  if(rule.scope?.contractIds?.length){
    const prov = providers.find(p => p.name === claim.provider);
    const cids = prov ? contracts.filter(c => c.providerId === prov.id).map(c => c.id) : [];
    if(!rule.scope.contractIds.some(cid => cids.includes(cid))) return false;
  }
  if(rule.conditions && rule.conditions.rules && rule.conditions.rules.length){
    if(!evaluateConditionTree(rule.conditions, ctx)) return false;
  }
  return true;
}
function evaluateRuleAction(rule, ctx, baseAmount){
  const amt = baseAmount !== undefined ? baseAmount : (ctx.line ? ctx.line.lineBase : 0);
  let rate = Number(rule.rate) || 0;
  if(rule.tiersEnabled && Array.isArray(rule.tiers) && rule.tiers.length){
    const tier = rule.tiers.find(t => (Number(t.from)||0) <= amt && (t.to === null || t.to === undefined || t.to === '' || amt <= Number(t.to)));
    if(tier) rate = Number(tier.rate) || rate;
  }
  switch(rule.action){
    case 'reject_line': return { newApproved: 0 };
    case 'reduce_by_percent': return { newApproved: Math.max(0, Math.round(amt*(1-Math.min(100,rate)/100))) };
    case 'reduce_by_fixed': return { newApproved: Math.max(0, amt-rate) };
    case 'cap_to_amount': return { newApproved: Math.min(amt, Math.max(0, rate)) };
    case 'set_to_percent': return { newApproved: Math.min(amt, Math.max(0, Math.round(amt*Math.min(100,rate)/100))) };
    case 'apply_cap': return { deduct: Math.round(amt*rate/100) };
    case 'apply_copay': return { deduct: Math.round(amt*rate/100) };
    default: return { newApproved: amt };
  }
}
function evaluateCompositeRule(rule, ctx, refDate){
  const cfg = rule.compositeConfig || {};
  const childRules = (cfg.childRuleIds||[]).map(id => deductionRules.find(r => r.id === id)).filter(r => r && r.enabled !== false && r.active !== false && isLineLevelAction(r.action));
  if(!childRules.length) return null;
  const requireAll = cfg.requireAllMatch !== false;
  const matched = childRules.map(child => ({ rule:child, matched: ruleMatchesContext(child, ctx, refDate) })).filter(r => r.matched);
  if(requireAll && matched.length !== childRules.length) return null;
  if(!matched.length) return null;
  const base = ctx.line ? (ctx.line.lineBase||0) : 0;
  const mode = cfg.applyMode || 'atomic';
  if(mode === 'atomic'){
    let cur = base, applied = [];
    for(const c of matched){
      const cctx = { ...ctx, line: ctx.line ? { ...ctx.line, lineBase: cur } : null };
      const r = evaluateRuleAction(c.rule, cctx, cur);
      if(r.newApproved !== undefined){ cur = Math.max(0, Math.min(cur, r.newApproved)); applied.push(c.rule); }
    }
    return { newApproved: cur, appliedChildRules: applied };
  }
  if(mode === 'best'){
    let best = base, bestR = null;
    for(const c of matched){ const r = evaluateRuleAction(c.rule, ctx, base); const v = r.newApproved !== undefined ? r.newApproved : base; if(v < best){ best = v; bestR = c.rule; } }
    return { newApproved: best, appliedChildRules: bestR ? [bestR] : [] };
  }
  if(mode === 'sum'){
    let total = 0, applied = [];
    for(const c of matched){ const r = evaluateRuleAction(c.rule, ctx, base); const d = r.deduct !== undefined ? r.deduct : (r.newApproved !== undefined ? (base-r.newApproved) : 0); total += Math.max(0, d); applied.push(c.rule); }
    return { newApproved: Math.max(0, base-total), appliedChildRules: applied };
  }
  if(mode === 'first_match'){
    const c = matched[0]; const r = evaluateRuleAction(c.rule, ctx, base);
    return { newApproved: r.newApproved !== undefined ? r.newApproved : base, appliedChildRules: [c.rule] };
  }
  return null;
}
function patientBeneficiaryType(pat, claim){ return pat.beneficiaryType || claim.type || 'employee'; }
function hasValidPriorApproval(claim){ return claim.priorApproval && claim.priorApproval.required && claim.priorApproval.status === 'approved'; }
function lineRequiresPA(line){ if(line.requiresPriorApproval) return true; return paProcedures.some(p => (p.code || p.id) === line.code); }

/* ---------- كشف التعارضات (Conflict Detection) ---------- */
function _scopeOverlaps(a, b){
  const aProv = (a.scope?.providerIds||[]), bProv = (b.scope?.providerIds||[]);
  const aCon = (a.scope?.contractIds||[]), bCon = (b.scope?.contractIds||[]);
  const aCt = (a.scope?.claimTypes||[]), bCt = (b.scope?.claimTypes||[]);
  const provOverlap = (!aProv.length || !bProv.length) || aProv.some(x => bProv.includes(x));
  const conOverlap = (!aCon.length || !bCon.length) || aCon.some(x => bCon.includes(x));
  const ctOverlap = (!aCt.length || !bCt.length) || aCt.some(x => bCt.includes(x));
  return provOverlap && conOverlap && ctOverlap;
}
function _conditionsEqual(a, b){
  try { return JSON.stringify(a||{}) === JSON.stringify(b||{}); } catch(e){ return false; }
}
function detectRuleConflicts(newRule, existingRules){
  existingRules = existingRules || (typeof deductionRules !== 'undefined' ? deductionRules : []);
  const conflicts = [];
  // 1. broken dependencies
  const reqIds = (newRule.requires && newRule.requires.ruleIds) ? newRule.requires.ruleIds : [];
  reqIds.forEach(rid => {
    if(!existingRules.some(r => r.id === rid)){
      conflicts.push({ type:'REQUIRES_MISSING', severity:'error', message:`القاعدة تعتمد على قاعدة غير موجودة: ${rid}` });
    }
  });
  // 2. circular dependencies
  reqIds.forEach(rid => {
    const dep = existingRules.find(r => r.id === rid);
    if(dep && dep.requires && Array.isArray(dep.requires.ruleIds)){
      if(dep.requires.ruleIds.includes(newRule.id)){
        conflicts.push({ type:'CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY', severity:'error', message:`حلقة دائرية محتملة بين القاعدة الجديدة والقاعدة ${rid}` });
      }
      // mutual among required
      if(dep.requires.ruleIds.some(x => reqIds.includes(x) && x !== rid)){
        conflicts.push({ type:'CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY', severity:'warning', message:`اعتماد متبادل بين القواعد المطلوبة (${rid})` });
      }
    }
  });
  existingRules.forEach(ex => {
    // 3. redundant exact duplicate
    if(ex.id !== newRule.id && ex.action && newRule.action && ex.action === newRule.action && ex.rate === newRule.rate && _scopeOverlaps(ex, newRule) && _conditionsEqual(ex.conditions, newRule.conditions)){
      conflicts.push({ type:'REDUNDANT', severity:'warning', message:`القاعدة مطابقة (أو شبه مطابقة) للقاعدة السارية: ${ex.id} — ${ex.name}` });
    }
    // 4. contradictory intent on same scope+conditions
    const rejectIntent = (r) => r.action === 'reject_line' || (['reduce_by_percent','cap_to_amount','set_to_percent'].includes(r.action) && (Number(r.rate)||0) >= 99);
    const allowIntent = (r) => r.action === 'cap_to_amount' && (Number(r.rate)||0) > 0 && (Number(r.rate)||0) < 99;
    if(_scopeOverlaps(ex, newRule) && _conditionsEqual(ex.conditions, newRule.conditions)){
      if(rejectIntent(newRule) && allowIntent(ex)){
        conflicts.push({ type:'CONTRADICTORY', severity:'warning', message:`القاعدة الجديدة (رفض) تتعارض مع القاعدة السارية ${ex.id} (سماح)` });
      }
      if(allowIntent(newRule) && rejectIntent(ex)){
        conflicts.push({ type:'CONTRADICTORY', severity:'warning', message:`القاعدة الجديدة (سماح) تتعارض مع القاعدة السارية ${ex.id} (رفض)` });
      }
    }
    const exHasCond = !!(ex.conditions && Array.isArray(ex.conditions.rules) && ex.conditions.rules.length);
    const newHasCond = !!(newRule.conditions && Array.isArray(newRule.conditions.rules) && newRule.conditions.rules.length);
    // 5. double-deduction risk: same scope+conditions, both line-level, different rules
    if(ex.id !== newRule.id && exHasCond && newHasCond && _scopeOverlaps(ex, newRule) && _conditionsEqual(ex.conditions, newRule.conditions) && isLineLevelAction(ex.action) && isLineLevelAction(newRule.action) && ex.action !== newRule.action){
      conflicts.push({ type:'OVERLAP', severity:'info', message:`خطر استقطاع مزدوج: ${ex.id} و القاعدة الجديدة تطبقان على نفس الشروط` });
    }
  });
  // de-duplicate by message
  const seen = new Set();
  const uniq = conflicts.filter(c => { if(seen.has(c.message)) return false; seen.add(c.message); return true; });
  return { hasConflicts: uniq.length > 0, conflicts: uniq };
}

/**
 * توليد سبب الخصم القانوني (عنوان/سند/تفسير) لمفتاح قاعدة معين.
 * @param {string} ruleKey - مفتاح القاعدة
 * @param {Object} [context] - سياق (claim, patient, invoice, line, ...)
 * @returns {Object} كائن {title, legalBasis, explanation, fullText, formattedText}
 */
function generateLegalDeductionReason(ruleKey, context) {
  context = context || {};
  const template = LEGAL_DEDUCTION_REASONS[ruleKey];
  if (!template) {
    const fallbackExpl = context.note || 'تم تطبيق الاستقطاع بناءً على قواعد التدقيق الآلي.';
    const fallback = {
      title: 'استقطاع مالي وتعديل تعاقدي',
      legalBasis: 'بناءً على أحكام المراجعة المالية والتدقيق التعاقدي.',
      explanation: fallbackExpl,
      fullText: `[سبب الخصم]: استقطاع مالي وتعديل تعاقدي\n[المستند القانوني]: بناءً على أحكام المراجعة المالية والتدقيق التعاقدي.\n[التفسير الإجرائي]: ${fallbackExpl}`,
      formattedText: `[السبب]: استقطاع مالي وتعديل تعاقدي | [السند]: بناءً على أحكام المراجعة المالية والتدقيق التعاقدي. | [التفسير]: ${fallbackExpl}`
    };
    return fallback;
  }
  let explanationText;
  try { explanationText = template.explanation(context); }
  catch (e) { explanationText = context.note || 'تم تطبيق الاستقطاع بناءً على قواعد التدقيق الآلي.'; }
  return {
    title: template.title,
    legalBasis: template.legalBasis,
    explanation: explanationText,
    fullText: `[سبب الخصم]: ${template.title}\n[المستند القانوني]: ${template.legalBasis}\n[التفسير الإجرائي]: ${explanationText}`,
    formattedText: `[السبب]: ${template.title} | [السند]: ${template.legalBasis} | [التفسير]: ${explanationText}`
  };
}

  'tariff-limit':       { label: 'تجاوز سعر اللائحة',            severity: 'reduce',    category: 'pricing' },
  'contract-discount':  { label: 'خصم العقد',                    severity: 'reduce',    category: 'contract' },
};

function getContractContext(claim) {
  const empty = { provider: null, contract: null, tariff: null, serviceOverrides: {} };
  if (!claim) return empty;

  const provider = (typeof providers !== 'undefined' ? providers : []).find(p => p.name === claim.provider || p.id === claim.provider || (claim.providerId && p.id === claim.providerId)) || null;
  if (!provider) return { ...empty, provider };

  const claimDate = claim.date || claim.receivedDate || new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  const contract = (typeof contracts !== 'undefined' ? contracts : []).find(c => { const start=String(c.startDate||''); const end=String(c.endDate||''); return c.providerId===provider.id && c.status!=='terminated' && (!start || start<=claimDate) && (!end || claimDate<=end); }) || null;

  let tariffBase = null;
  const applicable = t => (typeof isTariffApplicableForDate === 'function') ? isTariffApplicableForDate(t, claimDate) : effectiveTariffStatus(t) === 'active';
  if (contract?.tariffId) {
    tariffBase = (typeof tariffs !== 'undefined' ? tariffs : []).find(t => t.id === contract.tariffId && applicable(t)) || null;
  }
  if (!tariffBase) {
    tariffBase = (typeof tariffs !== 'undefined' ? tariffs : []).find(t =>
      ((contract && (t.linkedContractIds || []).includes(contract.id)) ||
       (t.linkedProviderIds || []).includes(provider.id)) && applicable(t)
    ) || null;
  }
  const resolvedVersion = tariffBase && typeof getTariffVersionForDate==='function' ? getTariffVersionForDate(tariffBase,claimDate) : null;
  const tariff = tariffBase ? { ...tariffBase, services: (typeof getTariffServicesForDate === 'function') ? getTariffServicesForDate(tariffBase, claimDate) : (tariffBase.services||[]), _resolvedVersionEffectiveDate: resolvedVersion && resolvedVersion.effectiveDate || tariffBase.effectiveDate } : null;

  const serviceOverrides = contract?.serviceOverrides || {};

  return { provider, contract, tariff, serviceOverrides };
}

function getContractRateForService(ctx, code) {
  const ov = ctx?.serviceOverrides?.[code];
  if (ov && ov.discountRate !== null && ov.discountRate !== undefined) {
    return Number(ov.discountRate) || 0;
  }
  return Number(ctx?.contract?.discountRate) || 0;
}

function isServiceCovered(ctx, code) {
  const ov = ctx?.serviceOverrides?.[code];
  if (ov && ov.covered === false) return false;
  const svc = (ctx?.tariff?.services || []).find(s => String(s.code) === String(code));
  if (svc && svc.covered === false) return false;
  return true;
}

/* ---------- التخزين المؤقت لنتائج الفحص ---------- */
const VALIDATION_CACHE = new Map();
function _validationCacheKey(line, ctx){
  try {
    return JSON.stringify({
      code: line.code, qty: line.qty, billedUnit: line.billedUnit, manual: !!line._manualEntry,
      claim: ctx.claim && ctx.claim.id, inv: ctx.invoice && ctx.invoice.id, pat: ctx.patient && ctx.patient.id,
      priorApproval: ctx.claim && ctx.claim.priorApproval ? {
        required: !!ctx.claim.priorApproval.required,
        status: ctx.claim.priorApproval.status || '',
        date: ctx.claim.priorApproval.date || ''
      } : null,
      contract: ctx.contract && ctx.contract.id, tariff: ctx.tariff && ctx.tariff.id, tariffVersion: ctx.tariff && ctx.tariff._resolvedVersionEffectiveDate,
      serviceOverride: (ctx.serviceOverrides || ctx.contract?.serviceOverrides || {})[String(line.code)] || null,
    });
  } catch(e){ return null; }
}
function clearValidationCache(){ VALIDATION_CACHE.clear(); }

/**
 * التحقق من بند واحد وتطبيق التعرفة والعقد والقواعد.
 * @param {Object} line - البند
 * @param {Object} ctx - السياق (claim, patient, invoice, contract, tariff, ...)
 * @returns {Object} نتيجة التحقق
 */
function validateLine(line, ctx) {
  const _ck = _validationCacheKey(line, ctx);
  if (_ck && VALIDATION_CACHE.has(_ck)) { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(VALIDATION_CACHE.get(_ck))); }
  const { claim, patient, invoice, contract, tariff, serviceOverrides } = ctx;
  const ctxFull = { claim, patient, invoice, contract, tariff, serviceOverrides: serviceOverrides || (contract?.serviceOverrides || {}) };
  const passed = [];
  const failed = [];
  const reductions = [];

  const billedTotal = (Number(line.qty) || 0) * (Number(line.billedUnit) || 0);
  const code = String(line.code || '').trim();

  const tariffSvc = (tariff?.services || []).find(s => String(s.code) === code) || null;
  if (!tariffSvc || line._manualEntry) {
    failed.push(line._manualEntry ? 'manual-entry' : 'not-in-tariff');
  } else {
    passed.push('in-tariff');
  }

  if (tariffSvc && !line._manualEntry) {
    if (!isServiceCovered(ctxFull, code)) {
      failed.push('not-covered');
    } else {
      passed.push('covered');
    }
  }

  if (tariffSvc && !line._manualEntry) {
    const ov = (ctxFull.serviceOverrides || {})[code] || {};
    const requiresPA = ov.requiresPA === true || tariffSvc.requiresPriorApproval === true;
    if (requiresPA) {
      const pa = claim?.priorApproval;
      if (!pa || !pa.required) {
        failed.push('pa-required');
      } else if (pa.status === 'rejected') {
        failed.push('pa-rejected');
      } else if (pa.status === 'pending' || pa.status === 'not-requested') {
        failed.push('pa-pending');
      } else if (pa.status === 'approved') {
        const lineDate = invoice?.date || claim?.date;
        if (pa.date && lineDate && new Date(pa.date) > new Date(lineDate)) {
          failed.push('pa-expired');
        } else {
          passed.push('pa-valid');
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const tariffUnit = tariffSvc?.currentPrice ?? null;
  const tariffTotal = tariffUnit !== null ? (Number(line.qty) || 0) * tariffUnit : null;

  if (tariffSvc && tariffTotal !== null) {
    if (billedTotal > tariffTotal + 0.01) {
      passed.push('price-exceeds');
      reductions.push('tariff-limit');
    } else {
      passed.push('price-ok');
    }
  }

  if (patient && invoice) {
    const siblings = (patient.lines || []).filter(l => l !== line && String(l.code) === code);
    if (siblings.length > 0) {
      failed.push('duplicate');
    } else {
      passed.push('no-duplicate');
    }
  }

  let decision = 'auto-approved';
  let suggestedAction = null;

  const hasReject = failed.some(f => VALIDATION_REASONS[f]?.severity === 'reject');
  const hasAttention = failed.some(f => VALIDATION_REASONS[f]?.severity === 'attention');

  if (hasReject) {
    decision = 'auto-rejected';
    suggestedAction = { type: 'reject', reason: failed.find(f => VALIDATION_REASONS[f]?.severity === 'reject') };
  } else if (hasAttention) {
    decision = 'needs-attention';
    const first = failed.find(f => VALIDATION_REASONS[f]?.severity === 'attention');
    suggestedAction = {
      type: first?.startsWith('pa') ? 'request-pa' : 'review',
      reason: first
    };
  } else if (reductions.length > 0) {
    decision = 'auto-reduced';
    suggestedAction = { type: 'reduce', reason: reductions[0] };
  }

  const contractRate = getContractRateForService(ctxFull, code);
  const covered = !line._manualEntry && isServiceCovered(ctxFull, code) && !!tariffSvc;
  const lesser = tariffTotal !== null ? Math.min(billedTotal, tariffTotal) : billedTotal;
  const afterContract = covered ? Math.round(lesser * (1 - contractRate / 100)) : 0;

  function _mapLegalKey(r){ 
    if(r === 'not-in-tariff') return 'not-in-tariff';
    if(r === 'not-covered') return 'not-covered';
    if(r === 'manual-entry') return 'not-in-tariff';
    if(r && String(r).startsWith('pa')) return 'pa-required';
    if(r === 'tariff-limit') return 'price-exceeds';
    if(r === 'contract-discount') return 'contract-discount';
    return r;
  }
  const _primaryReason = hasReject ? failed.find(f => VALIDATION_REASONS[f]?.severity === 'reject') : (hasAttention ? failed.find(f => VALIDATION_REASONS[f]?.severity === 'attention') : (reductions.length ? reductions[0] : null));
  const _legalKey = _primaryReason ? _mapLegalKey(_primaryReason) : null;
  const _legalDeduction = (_legalKey && typeof generateLegalDeductionReason === 'function') ? generateLegalDeductionReason(_legalKey, { claim, patient, invoice, line, diff: billedTotal - (tariffTotal !== null ? tariffTotal : billedTotal), maxLabs: null }) : null;
  const _legalReasons = [...failed, ...(reductions||[])].map(_mapLegalKey).map(k => (typeof generateLegalDeductionReason === 'function') ? generateLegalDeductionReason(k, { claim, patient, invoice, line }) : null).filter(Boolean);

  const _res = {
    passed,
    failed,
    reductions,
    decision,
    suggestedAction,
    tariffSvc,
    tariffUnit,
    tariffTotal,
    billedTotal,
    lesser,
    contractRate,
    covered,
    afterContract,
    legalKey: _legalKey,
    legalReason: _legalDeduction,
    legalDeduction: _legalDeduction,
    legalReasons: _legalReasons,
  };
  if (_ck) VALIDATION_CACHE.set(_ck, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(_res)));
  return _res;
}

function validateInvoice(inv, ctx) {
  const _ck = inv && inv.id ? ('VINV:'+JSON.stringify({ id: inv.id, contract: ctx.contract&&ctx.contract.id, documents: inv.documents || {} })) : null;
  if (_ck && VALIDATION_CACHE.has(_ck)) { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(VALIDATION_CACHE.get(_ck))); }
  const passed = [];
  const failed = [];
  const missingDocs = [];
  const DOC_ITEMS_LOCAL = (typeof DOC_ITEMS !== 'undefined' ? DOC_ITEMS : []);
  DOC_ITEMS_LOCAL.forEach(d => {
    if (!inv.documents || !inv.documents[d.key]) missingDocs.push(d.label);
  });
  if (missingDocs.length) {
    failed.push('missing-docs');
  } else {
    passed.push('docs-present');
  }
  const _vires = { passed, failed, missingDocs };
  if (_ck) VALIDATION_CACHE.set(_ck, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(_vires)));
  return _vires;
}

function getClaimReconciliationTarget(claim) {
  if (!claim) return { amount: 0, source: 'missing-claim' };
  const submitted = claim.submittedBilled;
  if (submitted !== undefined && submitted !== null && submitted !== '' && Number.isFinite(Number(submitted)) && Number(submitted) >= 0) {
    return { amount: Number(submitted), source: 'submittedBilled' };
  }
  return { amount: Number(claim.billed) || 0, source: 'legacy-billed' };
}

function validateClaim(claim) {
  const failed = [];
  const entered = (claim.invoices || []).reduce((s, inv) =>
    s + (inv.patients || []).reduce((a, p) =>
      a + (p.lines || []).reduce((c, l) => c + (Number(l.qty) || 0) * (Number(l.billedUnit) || 0), 0), 0), 0);

  // Compare invoice lines with the immutable submitted amount, not the recalculated billed total.
  const reconciliationTarget = getClaimReconciliationTarget(claim);
  const target = reconciliationTarget.amount;
  const remaining = target - entered;

  if (Math.abs(remaining) > 1) failed.push('invoice-mismatch');

  const pendingExceptions = [];
  (claim.invoices || []).forEach(inv => (inv.patients || []).forEach(p => (p.lines || []).forEach(l => {
    if (l._decision === 'needs-attention' && l._overrideMode === 'auto') {
      pendingExceptions.push({ code: l.code, reasons: l._checksFailed || [] });
    }
  })));

  return { failed, entered, target, remaining, pendingExceptions, targetSource: reconciliationTarget.source };
}

function isRejectReason(code) {
  return VALIDATION_REASONS[code]?.severity === 'reject';
}
function isAttentionReason(code) {
  return VALIDATION_REASONS[code]?.severity === 'attention';
}
function reasonLabel(code) {
  return VALIDATION_REASONS[code]?.label || code;
}

// entities.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function getProviderById(id){ return providers.find(p=>p.id===id); }
function getContractById(id){ return contracts.find(c=>c.id===id); }
function getTariffById(id){ return tariffs.find(t=>t.id===id); }

// Immutable, effective-dated tariff snapshots. Older claim dates resolve to the
// price list that was in force on that date instead of today's edited prices.
function shiftTariffIsoDate(date,days){ const m=String(date||'').match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/); if(!m)return ''; const d=new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]),Number(m[2])-1,Number(m[3])+Number(days||0))); return d.toISOString().slice(0,10); }
function tariffDateMinusOne(date){ return shiftTariffIsoDate(date,-1); }
function tariffDatePlusOne(date){ return shiftTariffIsoDate(date,1); }
function getTariffVersionForDate(t, date){
  if(!t) return null;
  const target = String(date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)).slice(0,10);
  const versions = [...(Array.isArray(t.priceVersions)?t.priceVersions:[])];
  versions.push({effectiveDate:t.effectiveDate||'', expiryDate:t.expiryDate||'', services:t.services||[], basePriceLevel:t.basePriceLevel, _current:true});
  const matches = versions.filter(v => v.effectiveDate && v.effectiveDate <= target && (!v.expiryDate || target <= v.expiryDate));
  matches.sort((a,b)=>String(b.effectiveDate).localeCompare(String(a.effectiveDate)));
  return matches[0] || null;
}
function getTariffServicesForDate(t, date){ const v=getTariffVersionForDate(t,date); return v ? (v.services||[]) : (t&&Array.isArray(t.services)?t.services:[]); }
function isTariffApplicableForDate(t,date){ return !!(t && t.status!=='cancelled' && getTariffVersionForDate(t,date)); }
function archiveTariffVersion(t, newEffectiveDate, action, changes){
  if(!t) throw new Error('اللائحة غير موجودة');
  const next=String(newEffectiveDate||'').slice(0,10), current=String(t.effectiveDate||'').slice(0,10);
  if(!next) throw new Error('حدد تاريخ السريان');
  if(current && next < current) throw new Error('تاريخ السريان لا يمكن أن يسبق النسخة الحالية');
  if(current && next > current){
    const end=tariffDateMinusOne(next);
    if(t.expiryDate && t.expiryDate < next) throw new Error('تاريخ السريان الجديد يأتي بعد انتهاء اللائحة');
    if(!Array.isArray(t.priceVersions)) t.priceVersions=[];
    t.priceVersions.push({effectiveDate:current,expiryDate:end,services:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.services||[])),basePriceLevel:Number(t.basePriceLevel)||100,archivedAt:new Date().toISOString()});
  }
  if(!Array.isArray(t.changeLog)) t.changeLog=[];
  t.changeLog.push({id:'TCH-'+Date.now().toString(36)+'-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),at:new Date().toISOString(),effectiveDate:next,action:action||'update',actor:(typeof appSettings!=='undefined'&&appSettings.reviewer&&appSettings.reviewer.name)||'المستخدم',changes:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(changes||[]))});
  t.effectiveDate=next;
  return true;
}

function getBatchById(id){ return batches.find(b=>b.id===id); }
function getClaimById(id){ return claims.find(c=>c.id===id); }
function getContractByProvider(pid){ return contracts.find(c=>c.providerId===pid); }
function effectiveContractStatus(c){
  if(!c) return 'pending';
  if(c.status === 'terminated') return 'terminated';
  if(!c.startDate || !c.endDate) return 'upcoming';
  const now = new Date(), s = new Date(c.startDate), e = new Date(c.endDate);
  if(now < s) return 'upcoming';
  if(now > e) return 'expired';
  const d = Math.ceil((e-now)/(1000*60*60*24));
  if(d <= 60) return 'expiring';
  return 'active';
}
function effectiveTariffStatus(t){
  if(!t) return 'pending';
  if(t.status === 'cancelled') return 'cancelled';
  if(t.status === 'pending') return 'pending';
  if(!t.effectiveDate) return 'pending';
  const now = new Date(), e = new Date(t.effectiveDate), x = t.expiryDate ? new Date(t.expiryDate) : null;
  if(now < e) return 'pending';
  if(x && now > x) return 'expired';
  return 'active';
}
function getEffectiveProviderStatus(p){
  const c = getContractByProvider(p.id);
  return c ? effectiveContractStatus(c) : 'upcoming';
}
function syncContractToProvider(c){
  const p = getProviderById(c.providerId);
  if(!p) return;
  p.contractStart = c.startDate;
  p.contractEnd = c.endDate;
  p.discountRate = c.discountRate;
}
function getProviderStats(name){
  const l = claims.filter(c => c.provider === name);
  const b = l.reduce((s,c)=>s+c.billed,0);
  const a = l.reduce((s,c)=>s+c.approved,0);
  const d = Math.max(0,b-a);
  const r = b ? (d/b*100) : 0;
  return { claims:l.length, billed:b, approved:a, deduction:d, rate:r };
}
function providerHasClaims(name){ return claims.some(c => c.provider === name); }
function renameProviderClaims(oldName, newName){
  claims.forEach(c => { if(c.provider === oldName) c.provider = newName; });
  workSessions.forEach(s => { if(s.claimSnapshot && s.claimSnapshot.provider === oldName) s.claimSnapshot.provider = newName; });
  if(typeof activeClaim !== 'undefined' && activeClaim && activeClaim.provider === oldName){
    activeClaim.provider = newName;
    if($('#rvProvider')) $('#rvProvider').value = newName;
  }
  persistSessions(); persistData();
}
function nextContractNumber(sd){
  const y = sd ? new Date(sd).getFullYear() : new Date().getFullYear();
  const pre = 'CTR-'+y+'-';
  let m = 0;
  contracts.forEach(c=>{ if(c.id.startsWith(pre)){ const n = parseInt(c.id.split('-').pop(), 10); if(!isNaN(n)) m = Math.max(m,n); } });
  return pre + String(m+1).padStart(3,'0');
}
function nextTariffNumber(ed){
  const y = ed ? new Date(ed).getFullYear() : new Date().getFullYear();
  const pre = 'TAR-'+y+'-';
  let m = 0;
  tariffs.forEach(t=>{ if(t.id.startsWith(pre)){ const n = parseInt(t.id.split('-').pop(), 10); if(!isNaN(n)) m = Math.max(m,n); } });
  return pre + String(m+1).padStart(3,'0');
}
function nextBatchNumber(month){
  const pre = 'BTH-'+month+'-';
  let m = 0;
  batches.forEach(b=>{ if(b.id.startsWith(pre)){ const n = parseInt(b.id.split('-').pop(), 10); if(!isNaN(n)) m = Math.max(m,n); } });
  return pre + String(m+1).padStart(3,'0');
}
function nextReceivedNumber(){
  const y = new Date().getFullYear();
  let m = 0;
  claims.forEach(c => {
    const rd = c.receivedDate || c.date;
    if(!rd || new Date(rd).getFullYear() !== y) return;
    const n = parseInt(String(c.receivedNumber), 10);
    if(!isNaN(n)) m = Math.max(m, n);
  });
  return String(m + 1);
}
function generateClaimId(){
  const maxNum = claims.reduce((m,c)=>{ const n = parseInt(c.id.split('-').pop(), 10); return Math.max(m, isNaN(n)?0:n); }, 0);
  return 'CLM-2501-' + String(maxNum+1).padStart(4,'0');
}
function stats(){ return statsFor(claims); }
function statsFor(customClaims){
  const t = customClaims.length;
  const b = customClaims.reduce((s,c)=>s+c.billed,0);
  const a = customClaims.reduce((s,c)=>s+c.approved,0);
  const d = b-a;
  const reviewed = customClaims.filter(c => c.status !== 'pending');
  const rb = reviewed.reduce((s,c)=>s+c.billed,0);
  const ra = reviewed.reduce((s,c)=>s+c.approved,0);
  const r = rb ? ((rb-ra)/rb*100) : 0;
  return {
    total:t, billed:b, approved:a, deduction:d, rate:r,
    pending: customClaims.filter(c=>c.status==='pending').length,
    approvedN: customClaims.filter(c=>c.status==='approved').length,
    partialN: customClaims.filter(c=>c.status==='partial').length,
    rejectedN: customClaims.filter(c=>c.status==='rejected').length
  };
}
function reasonsBreakdown(){ return reasonsBreakdownFor(claims); }
function reasonsBreakdownFor(list){
  const m = {};
  list.forEach(c=>{
    if(!c.reason) return;
    const d = c.billed - c.approved;
    if(d <= 0) return;
    m[c.reason] = (m[c.reason]||0) + d;
  });
  return Object.entries(m).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
}
function addToTrash(type, typeLabel, name, data){
  trash.push({ id:'TRS-'+Date.now()+'-'+Math.floor(Math.random()*10000), type, typeLabel, name, data, deletedAt:new Date().toISOString() });
}

function buildExportHTML(data){
  const now = new Date();
  const dateStr = now.toLocaleDateString('ar-SA');
  const timeStr = now.toLocaleTimeString('ar-SA', { hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit' });
  const generatedAt = `${dateStr} - ${timeStr}`;
  const esc = v => String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#39;');
  const kpiHTML = data.kpis && data.kpis.length ? `<div class="kpi-grid">${data.kpis.map(k=>`<div class="kpi-box"><div class="kpi-label">${esc(k.label)}</div><div class="kpi-value">${esc(k.value)}</div></div>`).join('')}</div>` : '';
  const tableHTML = data.columns && data.rows && data.rows.length ? `<table><thead><tr>${data.columns.map(c=>`<th>${esc(c)}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${data.rows.map(r=>`<tr>${r.map(c=>`<td>${esc(c)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>` : '<p style="text-align:center;color:#64748B;padding:20px">لا بيانات</p>';
  return `
    <!DOCTYPE html><html lang="ar" dir="rtl"><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>${esc(data.title)}</title>
    <style>${getExportDocStyles()}</style></head><body>
    <div class="header"><div class="logo"><div class="logo-icon">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none"><path d="M24 4 L42 11 V24 C42 34.5 34 42.5 24 45.5 C14 42.5 6 34.5 6 24 V11 Z" fill="rgba(255,255,255,.1)" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="2.6" stroke-linejoin="round"/><path d="M16 24 L21.5 29.5 L33 18" stroke="#C8A24A" stroke-width="3.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>
      </div><div><div class="brand-name">${esc(EXPORT_BRAND.name)}</div><div class="brand-sub">${esc(EXPORT_BRAND.sub)} — ${esc(EXPORT_BRAND.version)}</div></div></div>
      <div class="doc-info"><div>الإنشاء: <strong>${generatedAt}</strong></div><div>المستخدم: <strong>${esc(appSettings.reviewer.name)}</strong></div><div>التقرير: <strong>${esc(data.title)}</strong></div></div></div>
    <div class="doc-title">${esc(data.title)}</div>
    <div class="doc-sub">${esc(data.subtitle||'')}${data.count !== undefined ? ` — السجلات: ${esc(data.count)}` : ''}</div>
    ${kpiHTML}${tableHTML}
    <div class="footer"><div>${esc(EXPORT_BRAND.name)} © ${now.getFullYear()}</div><div>${generatedAt}</div></div>
    </body></html>
  `;
}
function downloadExcel(htmlContent, filename){
  const matchBody = htmlContent.match(/<body[^>]*>([\s\S]*)<\/body>/i);
  const body = matchBody ? matchBody[1] : htmlContent;
  const styles = (htmlContent.match(/<style>([\s\S]*?)<\/style>/g) || []).join('\n');
  const template = `<html xmlns:o="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:office" xmlns:x="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:excel"><head><meta charset="UTF-8">${styles}</head><body>${body}</body></html>`;
  const blob = new Blob(['\ufeff', template], { type:'application/vnd.ms-excel;charset=utf-8' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  const safe = filename.replace(/[^\u0600-\u06FFa-zA-Z0-9\-_\s]/g,'').trim().replace(/\s+/g,'_');
  a.download = `${safe}_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.xls`;
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
}
function openPrintWindow(html, mode){
  const win = window.open('', '_blank');
  if(!win){ showWarning('تعذّر','يرجى السماح للنوافذ المنبثقة.'); return; }
  win.document.write(html); win.document.close();
  setTimeout(() => { try { win.focus(); win.print(); } catch(e){} }, 600);
  if(mode === 'pdf') showInfo('PDF','اختر <b>"حفظ كـ PDF"</b>.');
}
const screenDataCollectors = {
  dashboard: () => { const s = stats(); return { title:'لوحة التحكم', subtitle:'ملخص', count:claims.length,
    kpis:[{label:'المطالبات', value:`${s.total}`},{label:'المطالب به', value:fmtC(s.billed)},{label:'الاستقطاع', value:fmtC(s.deduction)},{label:'المعتمد', value:fmtC(s.approved)}],
    columns:['#','رقم','وارد','المريض','الجهة','المطالب','المعتمد','الحالة'],
    rows: claims.slice(0,20).map((c,i)=>[i+1,c.id,c.receivedNumber||'—',c.patient,c.provider,fmt(c.billed),fmt(c.approved),(STATUS[c.status] || STATUS.pending).label]) }; },
  claims: () => { const s = statsFor(claims); return { title:'تقرير المطالبات', subtitle:'القائمة', count:claims.length,
    kpis:[{label:'المطالبات', value:`${s.total}`},{label:'المطالب', value:fmtC(s.billed)},{label:'الاستقطاع', value:fmtC(s.deduction)},{label:'المعتمد', value:fmtC(s.approved)}],
    columns:['#','رقم','وارد','المريض','الجهة','التاريخ','المطالب','المعتمد','الحالة'],
    rows: claims.map((c,i)=>[i+1,c.id,c.receivedNumber||'—',c.patient,c.provider,c.date,fmt(c.billed),fmt(c.approved),(STATUS[c.status] || STATUS.pending).label]) }; },
  intake: () => ({ title:'تقرير الوارد', subtitle:'الدفعات', count:batches.length, kpis:[], columns:['#','رقم','الجهة','الشهر','الاستلام','العدد','القيمة','الحالة'], rows: batches.map((b,i)=>{ const p=getProviderById(b.providerId); return [i+1,b.id,p?p.name:'—',formatMonth(b.month),b.receivedDate,b.claimsCount,fmt(b.totalBilled),BATCH_STATUS[b.status].label]; }) }),
  providers: () => ({ title:'تقرير الجهات', subtitle:'القائمة', count:providers.length, kpis:[], columns:['#','رقم','الجهة','النوع','المدينة','التواصل','الهاتف','الحالة','المطالبات'], rows: providers.map((p,i)=>{ const cs=CONTRACT_STATUS[getEffectiveProviderStatus(p)]; const st=getProviderStats(p.name); return [i+1,p.id,p.name,p.type,p.city,p.contact||'—',p.phone||'—',cs.label,st.claims]; }) }),
  contracts: () => ({ title:'تقرير العقود', subtitle:'القائمة', count:contracts.length, kpis:[], columns:['#','رقم','الجهة','البداية','النهاية','المتبقي','الخصم','الحالة'], rows: contracts.map((c,i)=>{ const p=getProviderById(c.providerId); const cs=CONTRACT_STATUS[effectiveContractStatus(c)]; return [i+1,c.id,p?p.name:'—',c.startDate,c.endDate,daysRemainingText(c.endDate),`${c.discountRate}%`,cs.label]; }) }),
  tariffs: () => ({ title:'تقرير اللوائح', subtitle:'القائمة', count:tariffs.length, kpis:[], columns:['#','رقم','الاسم','النوع','السريان','الانتهاء','المستوى','الخدمات'], rows: tariffs.map((t,i)=>{ const adj=ADJUST_TYPES[t.adjustmentType]||ADJUST_TYPES.none; return [i+1,t.id,t.name,TARIFF_TYPES[t.type].label,t.effectiveDate,t.expiryDate||'—',`${t.basePriceLevel}%`,(t.services||[]).length]; }) }),
  reports: () => { let base=claims; if(reportsSearchTerm){ const q=reportsSearchTerm.toLowerCase(); base=claims.filter(c=>c.id.toLowerCase().includes(q)||c.patient.toLowerCase().includes(q)||c.provider.toLowerCase().includes(q)); } const s=statsFor(base); return { title:'تقرير مالي', subtitle: reportsSearchTerm?`فلترة: ${reportsSearchTerm}`:'شامل', count:base.length,
    kpis:[{label:'المطالبات', value:`${s.total}`},{label:'المطالب', value:fmtC(s.billed)},{label:'الاستقطاع', value:fmtC(s.deduction)},{label:'النسبة', value:`${s.rate.toFixed(1)}%`}],
    columns:['#','رقم','المريض','الجهة','المطالب','المعتمد','الاستقطاع','السبب','الحالة'],
    rows: base.map((c,i)=>[i+1,c.id,c.patient,c.provider,fmt(c.billed),fmt(c.approved),fmt(c.billed-c.approved),c.reason||'—',(STATUS[c.status] || STATUS.pending).label]) }; },
  search: () => { const results=getSearchResults(); return { title:'نتائج البحث', subtitle: srchTerm?`"${srchTerm}"`:'الكل', count:results.length, kpis:[], columns:['#','الكود','الخدمة','الفئة','الأساسي','الحالي','التغطية','اللائحة'], rows: results.map((r,i)=>[i+1,r.code,r.name,r.category,fmt(r.basePrice),fmt(r.currentPrice),r.covered?'مغطاة':'غير مغطاة',r.tariffName]) }; },
  trash: () => ({ title:'سلة المحذوفات', subtitle:'العناصر', count:trash.length, kpis:[], columns:['#','النوع','الاسم','التفاصيل','تاريخ الحذف'], rows: trash.map((item,i)=>[i+1,item.typeLabel,item.name,getTrashItemDetails(item),new Date(item.deletedAt).toISOString().slice(0,10)]) }),
  timeline: () => {
    const claim = getClaimById(currentTimelineClaimId);
    if(!claim) return null;
    const events = buildClaimTimeline(claim);
    return { title:`الخط الزمني — ${claim.id}`, subtitle:`${claim.patient}`, count:events.length, kpis:[], columns:['#','التاريخ','الحدث','التفاصيل','مرجع'], rows: events.map((e,i)=>[i+1,e.date,e.title,e.description||'—',e.refNumber||'—']) };
  },
  viewClaim: () => {
    const { type, id } = viewState;
    if(type !== 'claim') return null;
    const c = getClaimById(id); if(!c) return null;
    const rows = [];
    (c.invoices||[]).forEach((inv, ii) => {
      const t = INVOICE_TYPES[inv.type] || {label:inv.type};
      (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => {
        (p.lines||[]).forEach(l => {
          rows.push([`${ii+1}`, t.label, p.name, l.code, l.name, l.category, l.qty, fmt(l.qty*l.billedUnit), fmt(Number(l.approved)||0), fmt((l.qty*l.billedUnit)-(Number(l.approved)||0))]);
        });
      });
    });
    return { title:`المطالبة — ${c.id}`, subtitle:`${c.patient}`, count:rows.length,
      kpis:[{label:'المطالب به', value:fmtC(c.billed)},{label:'المعتمد', value:fmtC(c.approved)},{label:'الاستقطاع', value:fmtC(c.billed-c.approved)},{label:'الحالة', value:(STATUS[c.status] || STATUS.pending).label}],
      columns:['فاتورة','نوع','مريض','كود','بند','فئة','كمية','مطالب','معتمد','استقطاع'], rows };
  }
};
function exportSessionSummary(){
  if(!workSessions || !workSessions.length){ showWarning('لا جلسات','لا توجد جلسات للتصدير'); return; }
  const rows = [['ID','Claim','Status','LastActive']];
  workSessions.forEach(s => rows.push([s.id, s.claimId, s.status, s.lastActiveAt]));
  const csv = rows.map(r => r.map(safeCsvCell).join(',')).join('\n');
  const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = 'sessions-summary.csv'; a.click(); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}
function exportScreen(screen, format){
  const collector = screenDataCollectors[screen];
  if(!collector){ showWarning('خطأ','لا بيانات'); return; }
  const data = collector();
  if(!data){ showWarning('خطأ','لا بيانات'); return; }
  const html = buildExportHTML(data);
  if(format === 'excel'){ downloadExcel(html, data.title); showSuccess('تم', `${data.title} إلى Excel`); }
  else if(format === 'pdf'){ openPrintWindow(html, 'pdf'); }
  else if(format === 'print'){ openPrintWindow(html, 'print'); }
}
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const btn = e.target.closest('[data-export]');
  if(!btn) return;
  exportScreen(btn.dataset.export, btn.dataset.format);
});

================================================================================
FILE: /mnt/data/phase23work/TPA-Pro-main/js/i18n-core.js
================================================================================
// i18n-core.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let currentLang = (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') ? (localStorage.getItem('tpa_lang') || 'ar') : 'ar';
function setLang(lang){
  if(!I18N[lang]) return;
  currentLang = lang;
  try { localStorage.setItem('tpa_lang', lang); } catch(e){}
  document.documentElement.lang = lang;
  document.documentElement.dir = (lang === 'ar') ? 'rtl' : 'ltr';
}
function t(key){ const d = I18N[currentLang] || I18N.ar; return d[key] !== undefined ? d[key] : key; }
function isRTL(){ return currentLang === 'ar'; }
