// init.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
const LOAD_STEPS = ['تهيئة محرك المراجعة...','تحميل المطالبات...','تحميل اللوائح...','تجهيز التصدير...'];
let loadingDone = false;
function startLoading(){
  let p = 0;
  const fill = $('#ldFill'), pctEl = $('#ldPct'), statEl = $('#ldStatus'), steps = $$('#ldSteps .ld-step');
  const tick = setInterval(() => {
    p += Math.random()*11+5;
    if(p >= 100) p = 100;
    fill.style.width = p+'%'; pctEl.textContent = Math.floor(p)+'%';
    const idx = Math.min(3, Math.floor(p/25));
    statEl.textContent = LOAD_STEPS[idx];
    steps.forEach((s,i) => { s.classList.remove('now','done'); if(i < idx) s.classList.add('done'); else if(i === idx && p < 100) s.classList.add('now'); else if(i === idx && p >= 100) s.classList.add('done'); });
    if(p >= 100 && !loadingDone){ loadingDone = true; clearInterval(tick); steps.forEach(s => { s.classList.remove('now'); s.classList.add('done'); }); statEl.textContent = 'اكتمل التحميل'; setTimeout(afterLoading, 480); }
  }, 170);
}
function afterLoading(){
  applyAppSettingsToUI();
  if(localStorage.getItem(ONBOARDING_KEY) !== '1'){
    go('onboarding');
    setTimeout(() => { try { if(typeof bindOnboarding === 'function') bindOnboarding(); } catch(e){} const el = document.getElementById('onbReviewerName'); if(el) el.focus(); if(typeof translateDOMMulti === 'function') translateDOMMulti(); }, 350);
    return;
  }
  // Remove legacy persistent auth tokens; authentication is scoped to this tab session.
  try { localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_KEY); } catch(e){}
  if(sessionStorage.getItem(AUTH_KEY) === '1'){ go('app'); initApp(); setTimeout(() => { if(typeof translateDOM === 'function') translateDOM(document.body); }, 100); }
  else { go('lock'); setTimeout(()=>$('#pwd').focus(), 350); }
}
$('#eyeBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { const i = $('#pwd'); i.type = i.type === 'password' ? 'text' : 'password'; });
let _loginInProgress = false;
$('#lockForm').addEventListener('submit', async e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  if(_loginInProgress) return;
  const v = $('#pwd').value.trim(), err = $('#lockErr'), card = $('#lockCard');
  const wait = getAuthenticationLockoutMs();
  if(wait > 0){ err.textContent = `محاولات كثيرة. حاول بعد ${Math.ceil(wait/1000)} ثانية.`; err.classList.add('show'); return; }
  _loginInProgress = true;
  const submit = $('#lockForm').querySelector('[type="submit"]');
  if(submit) submit.disabled = true;
  try {
    const ok = await verifyPassword(v);
    if(ok){
      resetAuthenticationFailures(); err.classList.remove('show'); sessionStorage.setItem(AUTH_KEY,'1'); $('#pwd').value = ''; go('app'); initApp();
    } else {
      const remaining = recordAuthenticationFailure();
      err.textContent = remaining > 0 ? `كلمة المرور غير صحيحة. تم إيقاف المحاولات مؤقتًا ${Math.ceil(remaining/1000)} ثانية.` : 'كلمة المرور غير صحيحة';
      err.classList.add('show'); card.classList.remove('shake'); void card.offsetWidth; card.classList.add('shake'); $('#pwd').select();
    }
  } finally { _loginInProgress = false; if(submit) submit.disabled = false; }
});
$('#pwd').addEventListener('input', () => $('#lockErr').classList.remove('show'));
setTimeout(() => { if(typeof applyAllTranslations === 'function') applyAllTranslations(); if(typeof translateDOMMulti === 'function') translateDOMMulti(); else if(typeof translateDOM === 'function') translateDOM(document.body); go('loading'); startLoading(); }, 2400);
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const btn = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#tbBackupBtn');
  if(!btn) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  if(typeof doExportBackup === 'function') doExportBackup(false);
});
// Focus management for modals (accessibility)
(function(){
  let lastFocus = null;
  const observer = new MutationObserver(mutations => {
    mutations.forEach(m => {
      m.addedNodes && m.addedNodes.forEach(node => {
        if(node && node.classList && node.classList.contains && node.classList.contains('modal') && node.classList.contains('open')){
          lastFocus = document.activeElement;
          const first = node.querySelector('input, select, textarea, button');
          if(first) setTimeout(() => first.focus(), 50);
        }
      });
      if(m.type === 'attributes' && m.attributeName === 'class' && m.target && m.target.classList){
        if(m.target.classList.contains('open') && (m.target.classList.contains('modal') || m.target.classList.contains('dialog-overlay'))){
          if(!m.target._focused){ m.target._focused = true; lastFocus = document.activeElement; const first = m.target.querySelector('input, select, textarea, button'); if(first) setTimeout(() => first.focus(), 50); }
        } else if(m.target._focused && !m.target.classList.contains('open')){
          m.target._focused = false;
          if(lastFocus && typeof lastFocus.focus === 'function') setTimeout(() => lastFocus.focus(), 50);
        }
      }
    });
  });
  observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });
})();
// Topbar scrolled state
(function(){
  const main = document.querySelector('.main') || window;
  const handler = () => {
    const topbar = document.querySelector('.topbar');
    if(!topbar) return;
    const y = (main === window) ? window.scrollY : main.scrollTop;
    topbar.classList.toggle('scrolled', y > 8);
  };
  (main === window ? window : main).addEventListener('scroll', handler, {passive:true});
})();
// Collapsible review sections
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const t = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.rv-section-title');
  if(!t) return;
  t.classList.toggle('collapsed');
});
// Theme toggle (light/dark)
(function(){
  try {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('tpa_theme');
    if(saved === 'dark') document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme','dark');
  } catch(e){}
})();
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const btn = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#tbThemeBtn');
  if(!btn) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const html = document.documentElement;
  const dark = html.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark';
  if(dark){ html.removeAttribute('data-theme'); try { localStorage.setItem('tpa_theme','light'); } catch(e){} }
  else { html.setAttribute('data-theme','dark'); try { localStorage.setItem('tpa_theme','dark'); } catch(e){} }
});
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  if(e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#smartBackBtn')){ if(typeof goBack === 'function') goBack(); }
});
document.addEventListener('touchstart', e => {
  if(e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#smartBackBtn')){ if(typeof goBack === 'function') { e.preventDefault(); goBack(); } }
}, {passive:false});
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const btn = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#tbTrashBtn');
  if(!btn) return;
  if(currentPage && currentPage !== 'trash') navHistory.push({ page: currentPage, viewType: viewState.type, viewId: viewState.id });
  if(typeof navigateTo === 'function') navigateTo('trash', {noPush:true});
});
function toggleUserMenu(e){
  const btn = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#tbUserBtn');
  if(!btn) return;
  if(e) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); }
  const m = document.getElementById('tbUserMenu');
  if(m) m.style.display = m.style.display === 'none' ? 'flex' : 'none';
}
document.addEventListener('click', toggleUserMenu);
document.addEventListener('touchstart', toggleUserMenu, {passive:false});
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const m = document.getElementById('tbUserMenu');
  if(m && !e.target.closest('#tbUserWrap')) m.style.display = 'none';
});
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const item = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.tb-menu-item');
  if(!item) return;
  const action = item.dataset.action;
  const m = document.getElementById('tbUserMenu'); if(m) m.style.display = 'none';
  if(action === 'help' && typeof navigateTo === 'function'){ if(currentPage && currentPage !== 'help') navHistory.push({ page: currentPage, viewType: viewState.type, viewId: viewState.id }); navigateTo('help', {noPush:true}); }
  if(action === 'settings' && typeof navigateTo === 'function'){ if(currentPage && currentPage !== 'settings') navHistory.push({ page: currentPage, viewType: viewState.type, viewId: viewState.id }); navigateTo('settings', {noPush:true}); }
  if(action === 'lang' && typeof setLang === 'function'){ setLang(currentLang === 'ar' ? 'en' : 'ar'); item.textContent = currentLang === 'ar' ? 'اللغة: EN' : 'Language: AR'; if(typeof translateDOMMulti === 'function') translateDOMMulti(); }
});
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const btn = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.onb-eye');
  if(!btn) return;
  const inp = document.getElementById(btn.dataset.target);
  if(!inp) return;
  const show = inp.type === 'password';

});

// intake.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let intakeMonthFilter = 'all', intakeStatusFilter = 'all', intakeSearch = '', editingBatchId = null;
let workingBatchClaim = null;
function renderProviderOptions(filter){
  const sel = $('#btProvider'); if(!sel) return;
  const q = (filter||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = q ? providers.filter(p => (p.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (p.city||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)) : providers;
  sel.innerHTML = list.map(p => `<option value="${escapeHtml(p.id)}">${escapeHtml(p.name)}${p.city?' — '+escapeHtml(p.city):''}</option>`).join('');
  if(!list.length) sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">لا نتائج</option>';
}
function bindIntakeControls(){
  $('#intakeSearch').addEventListener('input', e => { intakeSearch = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase(); renderIntake(); });
  $('#intakeStatusChips').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const chip = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!chip) return;
    $$('#intakeStatusChips .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    chip.classList.add('active'); intakeStatusFilter = chip.dataset.istatus; renderIntake();
  });
  $('#monthChips').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const chip = e.target.closest('.month-chip'); if(!chip) return;
    $$('#monthChips .month-chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    chip.classList.add('active'); intakeMonthFilter = chip.dataset.month; renderIntake();
  });
  $('#addBatchBtn').addEventListener('click', () => openBatchModal());
  $('#batchesBody').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const ed = e.target.closest('[data-edit-batch]'); if(ed){ openBatchModal(ed.dataset.editBatch); return; }
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-delete-batch]'); if(del){ deleteBatchWithDialog(del.dataset.deleteBatch); return; }
  });
  $$('[data-close-batch]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', closeBatchModal));
  $('#saveBatch').addEventListener('click', () => saveBatch(false));
  $('#saveBatchAnother').addEventListener('click', () => saveBatch(true));
  $('#deleteBatch').addEventListener('click', () => { if(editingBatchId) deleteBatchWithDialog(editingBatchId); });
  $('#btProviderSearch').addEventListener('input', e => renderProviderOptions(e.target.value));
}
function renderIntake(){
  const total = batches.length;
  const tc = batches.reduce((s,b)=>s+b.claimsCount,0);
  const tv = batches.reduce((s,b)=>s+b.totalBilled,0);
  const rc = batches.filter(b=>b.status==='received' || b.status==='new').length;
  const uc = batches.filter(b=>b.status==='under-review').length;
  const cc = batches.filter(b=>b.status==='completed').length;
  $('#intakeKpi').innerHTML = `
    <div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">الدفعات</div><div class="kpi-val">${total} <small>دفعة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('inbox')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${rc} وارد · ${uc} قيد · ${cc} مكتمل</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k2"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">المطالبات</div><div class="kpi-val">${fmt(tc)} <small>مطالبة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('file')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${total} دفعة</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k3"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">القيمة</div><div class="kpi-val">${fmt(tv)} <small>ج.م</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('money')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">مجموع</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k4"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">بحاجة إجراء</div><div class="kpi-val">${rc+uc} <small>دفعة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('shield')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">لم تكتمل</div></div>`;
  const months = [...new Set(batches.map(b=>b.month))].sort().reverse();
  const mHtml = ['<button class="month-chip '+(intakeMonthFilter==='all'?'active':'')+'" data-month="all">الكل <span class="mc-count">'+total+'</span></button>']
    .concat(months.map(m => { const cnt = batches.filter(b=>b.month===m).length; return `<button class="month-chip ${intakeMonthFilter===m?'active':''}" data-month="${m}">${formatMonth(String(m).split(',')[0])} <span class="mc-count">${cnt}</span></button>`; }));
  $('#monthChips').innerHTML = mHtml.join('');
  const list = batches.filter(b => {
    if(intakeMonthFilter !== 'all' && String(b.month) !== intakeMonthFilter && !(String(b.month).split(',').includes(intakeMonthFilter))) return false;
    if(intakeStatusFilter !== 'all' && b.status !== intakeStatusFilter) return false;
    if(intakeSearch){ const p = getProviderById(b.providerId); const q = intakeSearch; return b.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (p && p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)); }
    return true;
  }).sort((a,b) => (b.receivedDate||'').localeCompare(a.receivedDate||''));
  const body = $('#batchesBody');
  if(!list.length){ body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;padding:36px;color:var(--muted)">لا دفعات</td></tr>'; return; }
  body.innerHTML = list.map(b => {
    const p = getProviderById(b.providerId);
    const pName = p ? p.name : '—';
    const bs = BATCH_STATUS[b.status] || BATCH_STATUS.received;
    return `<tr>
      <td data-label="رقم"><span class="clm-id">${escapeHtml(b.id)}</span></td>
      <td data-label="الجهة"><div class="prov-cell"><div class="prov-avatar alt">${pName.charAt(0)}</div><div><div class="prov-name">${escapeHtml(pName)}</div><div class="prov-city">${escapeHtml(p?p.city:'' )}</div></div></div></td>
      <td data-label="الشهر"><span class="mono">${escapeHtml(String(b.month))}</span></td>
      <td data-label="الاستلام" class="hide-sm mono">${formatDate(b.receivedDate)}</td>
      <td data-label="المطالبات" class="mono">${fmt(b.claimsCount)}</td>
      <td data-label="القيمة" class="mono big">${fmt(b.totalBilled)}</td>
      <td data-label="الحالة"><span class="badge ${bs.cls}">${bs.label}</span></td>
      <td class="act"><div style="display:flex;gap:5px;flex-wrap:nowrap;align-items:center"><button class="btn-xs" data-edit-batch="${b.id}">تعديل</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-delete-batch="${b.id}">×</button></div></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
}
function openBatchModal(id){
  editingBatchId = id || null; const isEdit = !!id;
  const b = isEdit ? getBatchById(id) : null; if(isEdit && !b) return;
  $('#btModalTitle').textContent = isEdit ? 'تعديل الدفعة' : 'استلام مطالبة';
  $('#btModalSub').textContent = isEdit ? b.id : 'بيانات جديدة';
  renderProviderOptions('');
  $('#btProviderSearch').value = '';
  workingBatchClaim = null;
  if(isEdit){
    $('#btProvider').value = b.providerId;
    $('#btPeriodType').value = b.periodType || 'single';
    $('#btMonth').value = b.month;
    $('#btStatus').value = b.status;
    $('#btNumber').value = b.id;
    $('#btNotes').value = b.notes||'';
    $('#btClaimReceivedDate').value = b.claimReceivedDate||'';
    $('#btBilled').value = b.totalBilled||0;
    (el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'block'; })($('#deleteBatch'));
  } else {
    const today = daysFromNow(0);
    $('#btPeriodType').value = 'single';
    $('#btMonth').value = today.slice(0,7);
    $('#btStatus').value = 'new';
    $('#btNumber').value = nextReceivedNumber();
    $('#btNotes').value = '';
    $('#btClaimReceivedDate').value = today;
    $('#btBilled').value = '';
    (el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'none'; })($('#deleteBatch'));
  }
  $('#btMonth').onchange = () => { if(!isEdit) $('#btNumber').value = nextReceivedNumber(); };
  $('#batchModal').classList.add('open'); document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
  setTimeout(() => $('#btMonth')?.focus(), 200);}
function closeBatchModal(){ if(!$('#batchModal').classList.contains('open')) return; $('#batchModal').classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); editingBatchId = null; workingBatchClaim = null; }
function saveBatch(another){
  const providerId = $('#btProvider').value;
  const periodType = $('#btPeriodType').value;
  const month = $('#btMonth').value.trim();
  const claimReceivedDate = $('#btClaimReceivedDate').value || daysFromNow(0);
  const receivedDate = claimReceivedDate;
  const status = $('#btStatus').value;
  const claimDate = receivedDate;
  const billed = Number($('#btBilled').value);
  const notes = $('#btNotes').value.trim();
  if(!providerId){ showWarning('تنبيه','اختر الجهة'); return; }
  if(!month){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الشهر'); return; }
  if(!billed || billed <= 0){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل القيمة الإجمالية'); return; }
  const p = getProviderById(providerId);
  const batchId = editingBatchId || ('BTH-'+month.split(',')[0]+'-'+$('#btNumber').value);
  if(editingBatchId){
    const b = getBatchById(editingBatchId);
    if(b){ b.providerId = providerId; b.month = month; b.periodType = periodType; b.receivedDate = receivedDate; b.status = status; b.notes = notes; b.claimDate = claimDate; b.claimReceivedDate = claimReceivedDate; b.totalBilled = billed; b.claimsCount = 1; }
  } else {
    batches.push({ id: batchId, providerId, month, periodType, receivedDate, status, notes, patient: '', claimDate, claimReceivedDate, claimsCount:1, totalBilled:billed });
    // create a claim with status 'new' and total billed
    const claimId = generateClaimId();
    claims.push({ id: claimId, patient: '', provider: p?p.name:'', type:'employee', date: claimDate, receivedDate: claimReceivedDate||receivedDate, receivedNumber: $('#btNumber').value, status: status==='new'?'new':status, reason:'', notes, priorApproval:{required:false,status:'not-requested',refNumber:'',date:''}, batchId, invoices: [] , billed });
    // set billed on claim
    const c = claims[claims.length-1]; c.billed = billed; c.approved = 0; c.eligible = billed;
  }
  persistData(true); renderIntake(); updateBackupStats();
  if(another){ openBatchModal(null); }
  else { closeBatchModal(); showSuccess('تم','تم الحفظ'); }
}
function deleteBatchWithDialog(id){
  const b = getBatchById(id); if(!b) return;
  showDelete('حذف الدفعة', `سيتم حذف ${escapeHtml(b.id)}.`, () => {
    batches = batches.filter(x => x.id !== id);
    claims = claims.filter(c => c.batchId !== id);
    persistData(true); renderIntake(); updateBackupStats(); showSuccess('تم','تم');
  });
}

FILE: /mnt/data/phase23work/TPA-Pro-main/js/item-editor.js

// item-editor.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function getApplicableTariffs(providerName){
  const provider = providers.find(p => p.name === providerName);
  let list = [], fallback = false;
  if(provider){
    const cids = contracts.filter(c => c.providerId === provider.id).map(c => c.id);
    list = tariffs.filter(t => (t.linkedProviderIds||[]).includes(provider.id) || (t.linkedContractIds||[]).some(cid => cids.includes(cid)));
    if(!list.length){ list = [...tariffs]; fallback = true; }
  } else { list = [...tariffs]; fallback = true; }
  const order = { active:0, pending:1, expired:2, cancelled:3 };
  const claimDate = (typeof activeClaim!=='undefined' && activeClaim && (activeClaim.date||activeClaim.receivedDate)) || new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  list = list.filter(t => (typeof isTariffApplicableForDate==='function') ? isTariffApplicableForDate(t, claimDate) : effectiveTariffStatus(t)==='active');
  list.sort((a,b) => (order[effectiveTariffStatus(a)]||9) - (order[effectiveTariffStatus(b)]||9));
  Object.defineProperty(list, '_fallback', { value: fallback, enumerable: false });
  return list;
}
function searchTariffServices(query, appl){
  const q = (query||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  if(!q) return [];
  const out = [];
  const claimDate = (typeof activeClaim!=='undefined' && activeClaim && (activeClaim.date||activeClaim.receivedDate)) || new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  appl.forEach(t => ((typeof getTariffServicesForDate==='function') ? getTariffServicesForDate(t,claimDate) : (t.services||[])).forEach(s => {
    if(!s.code.toLowerCase().includes(q) && !s.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) && !(s.nameEn||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) && !s.category.toLowerCase().includes(q)) return;
    out.push({ ...s, tariffId:t.id, tariffName:t.name, tariffType:t.type });
  }));
  out.sort((a,b) => { const ae = a.code.toLowerCase()===q?0:1, be = b.code.toLowerCase()===q?0:1; if(ae!==be) return ae-be; return a.name.localeCompare(b.name); });
  return out.slice(0, 12);
}
function renderLineSourceBanner(){
  const banner = $('#lnSourceBanner'); if(!banner) return;
  if(lnApplicableTariffs._fallback){
    banner.className = 'ln-source-banner warn';
    banner.innerHTML = `⚠ <b>لا لائحة مرتبطة بالجهة</b> — البحث في كل اللوائح النشطة كبديل.`;
    return;
  }
  const active = lnApplicableTariffs.filter(t => effectiveTariffStatus(t) === 'active');
  const shown = active.length ? active : lnApplicableTariffs;
  if(shown.length){
    banner.className = 'ln-source-banner';
    const names = shown.slice(0,3).map(t=>escapeHtml(t.name)).join(' · ');
    banner.innerHTML = `📋 اللوائح: <b>${names}${shown.length>3?' (+'+(shown.length-3)+')':''}</b>`;
  } else { banner.className = 'ln-source-banner warn'; banner.innerHTML = '⚠ لا لائحة نشطة'; }
}
function applyServiceToLine(svc) {
  const ctx = getContractContext(activeClaim);
  const override = ctx.serviceOverrides?.[svc.code] || {};
  const contractRate = override.discountRate ?? (Number(ctx.contract?.discountRate) || 0);
  const covered = override.covered !== false;

  $('#lnCode').value = svc.code;
  $('#lnName').value = svc.name;
  $('#lnCategory').value = svc.category || 'أخرى';
  $('#lnTariffUnit').value = svc.currentPrice;
  $('#lnBilledUnit').value = svc.currentPrice;
  $('#lnBilledTotal').value = (Math.max(1, Number($('#lnQty').value)||1) * Number(svc.currentPrice||0)).toFixed(2);
  $('#lnContractDiscount').value = covered ? contractRate : '—';

  lnSelectedTariffId = svc.tariffId || ctx.contract?.tariffId || ctx.tariff?.id || '';

  const banner = $('#lnSourceBanner');
  if (!covered) {
    banner.className = 'ln-source-banner warn';
    banner.innerHTML = '⛔ <b>غير مغطاة بالعقد</b> — سيُرفض البند تلقائيًا';
  } else {
    banner.className = 'ln-source-banner';
    banner.innerHTML = `✅ مغطاة · اللائحة <b>${escapeHtml(ctx.tariff?.name || '—')}</b> · خصم العقد <b>${contractRate}%</b>`;
  }

  updateComputedPreview();
  setTimeout(() => $('#lnBilledUnit').focus(), 50);
}
function bindLineAutocomplete(){
  const codeInp = $('#lnCode'), nameInp = $('#lnName'), suggest = $('#lnSuggest');
  if(!codeInp || !nameInp || !suggest) return;
  const run = val => {
    if(!val || val.length < 1){ suggest.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    const results = searchTariffServices(val, lnApplicableTariffs);
    if(!results.length){ suggest.innerHTML = '<div class="ln-sug-empty">لا نتائج</div>'; return; }
    suggest.innerHTML = `<div class="ln-suggest-list">${results.map((s,i)=>`<div class="ln-sug-item" data-sug-idx="${i}"><span class="s-code">${escapeHtml(s.code)}</span><div class="s-info"><div class="s-name">${escapeHtml(s.name)}${s.requiresPriorApproval?' ⚠':''}</div>${s.nameEn?`<div class="s-cat" style="direction:ltr;text-align:right">${escapeHtml(s.nameEn)}</div>`:''}<div class="s-cat">${escapeHtml(s.category)} · ${escapeHtml(s.tariffName)}</div></div><div class="s-price">${fmt(s.currentPrice)}<small>ج.م</small></div></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
    suggest.querySelectorAll('[data-sug-idx]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', () => { const s = results[Number(el.dataset.sugIdx)]; if(s) applyServiceToLine(s); }));
  };
  const deb = val => { clearTimeout(lnDebounceTimer); lnDebounceTimer = setTimeout(()=>run(val), 160); };
  [codeInp, nameInp].forEach(inp => {
    inp.addEventListener('input', e => { lnSelectedTariffId = null; deb(e.target.value); });
    inp.addEventListener('focus', e => { if(e.target.value) run(e.target.value); });
    inp.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if(e.key==='Enter'){ const f = suggest.querySelector('[data-sug-idx]'); if(f){ e.preventDefault(); f.click(); } } else if(e.key==='Escape'){ suggest.innerHTML = ''; } });
  });
  codeInp.addEventListener('change', () => { const v = codeInp.value.trim().toLowerCase(); if(!v||lnSelectedTariffId) return; const all = searchTariffServices(v, lnApplicableTariffs); const exact = all.find(s => s.code.toLowerCase()===v); if(exact) applyServiceToLine(exact); });
  document.addEventListener('click', e => { if(!e.target.closest('#lineModal') || e.target.closest('.ln-suggest') || e.target.closest('#lnCode') || e.target.closest('#lnName')) return; suggest.innerHTML = ''; });
}
function openLineEditor(patIdx, lineIdx) {
  if (activeClaim && activeClaim._locked) { showWarning('مقفل','المطالبة موقوفة/موقعة — لا يمكن التعديل'); return; }
  const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx];
  const pat = inv && inv.patients ? inv.patients[patIdx] : null;
  if (!pat) return;

  rvEditLineIdx = (lineIdx === undefined || lineIdx === null) ? -1 : lineIdx;
  const l = rvEditLineIdx >= 0 ? pat.lines[rvEditLineIdx] : null;

  $('#lnModalTitle').textContent = l ? 'تعديل البند' : 'إضافة بند';
  $('#lnModalSub').textContent = `${inv.id} · ${pat.name}`;

  const ctx = getContractContext(activeClaim);
  const services = ctx.tariff?.services || [];

  const sel = $('#lnServiceSelect');
  if (!services.length) {
    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">— لا خدمات في اللائحة —</option>' +
      '<option value="__manual__">— إدخال يدوي (سيُرفض) —</option>';
  } else {
    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">— اختر من لائحة العقد —</option>' +
      services.map(s => `<option value="${escapeHtml(s.code)}">${escapeHtml(s.code)} — ${escapeHtml(s.name)} (${fmt(s.currentPrice)} ج.م)</option>`).join('') +
      '<option value="__manual__">— إدخال يدوي (سيُرفض تلقائيًا) —</option>';
  }

  if (l) {
    const inTariff = services.some(s => String(s.code) === String(l.code));
    if (inTariff) {
      sel.value = l.code;
      const svc = services.find(s => String(s.code) === String(l.code));
      applyServiceToLine(svc);
      $('#lnQty').value = l.qty;
      if (l.billedUnit !== null && l.billedUnit !== undefined) $('#lnBilledUnit').value = l.billedUnit;
      $('#lnBilledTotal').value = (Number(l.qty||1) * Number(l.billedUnit||0)).toFixed(2);
    } else {
      sel.value = '__manual__';
      $('#lnCode').value = l.code || '';
      $('#lnName').value = l.name || '';
      $('#lnCategory').value = l.category || 'أخرى';
      $('#lnTariffUnit').value = '';
      $('#lnContractDiscount').value = '—';
      toggleManualFields(true);
      $('#lnQty').value = l.qty;
      $('#lnBilledUnit').value = l.billedUnit;
      $('#lnBilledTotal').value = (Number(l.qty||1) * Number(l.billedUnit||0)).toFixed(2);
    }
  } else {
    sel.value = '';
    $('#lnCode').value = '';
    $('#lnName').value = '';
    $('#lnCategory').value = '';
    $('#lnTariffUnit').value = '';
    $('#lnContractDiscount').value = '';
    $('#lnQty').value = 1;
    $('#lnBilledUnit').value = '';
    $('#lnBilledTotal').value = '';
    toggleManualFields(false);
  }

  if (!ctx.contract || !ctx.tariff) {
    $('#lnSourceBanner').className = 'ln-source-banner warn';
    $('#lnSourceBanner').innerHTML = '⚠ <b>لا عقد/لائحة مرتبطة بالجهة</b> — اربط العقد بلائحة أولًا، أو استخدم الإدخال اليدوي (سيُرفض).';
  } else {
    $('#lnSourceBanner').className = 'ln-source-banner';
    $('#lnSourceBanner').innerHTML = `📋 <b>${escapeHtml(ctx.contract.id)}</b> · ${escapeHtml(ctx.tariff.name)} · خصم عام ${Number(ctx.contract.discountRate) || 0}%`;
  }

  updateComputedPreview();
  $('#lineModal').classList.add('open');
  setTimeout(() => sel.focus(), 120);
}
function saveLineEditor() {
  if (activeClaim && activeClaim._locked) { showWarning('مقفل','المطالبة موقوفة/موقعة — لا يمكن التعديل'); return; }
  markSessionDirty();
  const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx];
  const pat = inv && inv.patients ? inv.patients[rvActivePatientIdx] : null;
  if (!pat) return;

  const isManual = $('#lnServiceSelect').value === '__manual__';
  const code = $('#lnCode').value.trim();
  const name = $('#lnName').value.trim();
  const cat = $('#lnCategory').value.trim() || 'أخرى';
  const qty = Math.max(1, Number($('#lnQty').value) || 1);
  const totalInput = document.getElementById('lnBilledTotal');
  const qtyInput = document.getElementById('lnQty');
  const totalBilled = Number(totalInput && totalInput.value) || 0;
  const bu = totalBilled > 0 && Number(qtyInput.value) > 0 ? totalBilled / Number(qtyInput.value) : (Number($('#lnBilledUnit').value) || 0);
  const tuRaw = $('#lnTariffUnit').value.trim();
  const tu = (tuRaw === '' || tuRaw === '—') ? null : Number(tuRaw);

  if (!code) { showWarning('تنبيه', 'أدخل الكود'); return; }
  if (!name) { showWarning('تنبيه', 'أدخل الاسم'); return; }
  if (bu <= 0) { showWarning('تنبيه', 'أدخل السعر'); return; }
  if (qty > 10000) { showWarning('تنبيه', 'الكمية كبيرة'); return; }
  if (bu > 10000000) { showWarning('تنبيه', 'السعر مرتفع'); return; }
  if (tu !== null && tu < 0) { showWarning('تنبيه', 'سعر اللائحة سالب'); return; }

  const ctx = getContractContext(activeClaim);
  const tariffSvc = (ctx.tariff?.services || []).find(s => String(s.code) === String(code)) || null;

  const data = {
    code, name,
    category: cat,
    qty,
    billedUnit: bu,
    tariffUnit: tu,
    tariffId: lnSelectedTariffId || ctx.contract?.tariffId || ctx.tariff?.id || '',
    _manualEntry: isManual,
    _contractRate: 0,
    _contractCovered: true,
    note: '',
    approved: 0,
    _overrideMode: 'auto',
    requiresPriorApproval: !!(tariffSvc && tariffSvc.requiresPriorApproval),
  };

  const validation = validateLine(data, {
    claim: activeClaim,
    patient: pat,
    invoice: inv,
    contract: ctx.contract,
    tariff: ctx.tariff,
    serviceOverrides: ctx.serviceOverrides,
  });

  data._decision = validation.decision;
  data._checksPassed = validation.passed;
  data._checksFailed = validation.failed;
  data._flaggedReasons = [...validation.failed, ...(validation.reductions || [])];
  data._suggestedAction = validation.suggestedAction;
  data._legalDeduction = validation.legalDeduction || validation.legalReason || null;
  data._legalReason = data._legalDeduction;
  data.legalDeduction = data._legalDeduction;
  data._contractRate = validation.contractRate;
  data._contractCovered = validation.covered;
  data.approved = validation.afterContract;

  if (validation.decision === 'auto-rejected') {
    data.approved = 0;
    data._overrideMode = 'manual';
    data._manualValue = 0;
    data.note = reasonLabel(validation.suggestedAction?.reason || 'not-in-tariff');
  } else if (validation.decision === 'auto-reduced') {
    data.note = reasonLabel('tariff-limit');
  } else if (validation.decision === 'needs-attention') {
    data.note = reasonLabel(validation.suggestedAction?.reason || '');
  }

  if (rvEditLineIdx >= 0 && pat.lines[rvEditLineIdx]) {
    pat.lines[rvEditLineIdx] = data;
  } else {
    if (!pat.lines) pat.lines = [];
    pat.lines.push(data);
  }

  closeLineEditor();
  renderActiveInvoice();
  recalcReview();

  if (validation.decision === 'auto-rejected') {
    showToast(`⛔ رُفض البند — ${data.note}`);
  } else if (validation.decision === 'auto-reduced') {
    showToast(`🔻 خُفّض البند — ${data.note}`);
  } else if (validation.decision === 'needs-attention') {
    showToast(`⚠️ يحتاج مراجعة — ${data.note}`);
  }
}
function closeLineEditor(){ if(!$('#lineModal').classList.contains('open')) return; $('#lineModal').classList.remove('open'); rvEditLineIdx = -1; }

function syncLineBilledAmount(source){
  const qty = Math.max(1, Number($('#lnQty').value)||1);
  const unit = document.getElementById('lnBilledUnit');
  const total = document.getElementById('lnBilledTotal');
  if(!unit || !total) return;
  if(source === 'total'){ const amount = Math.max(0, Number(total.value)||0); unit.value = (amount/qty).toFixed(2); }
  else { total.value = (qty * Math.max(0, Number(unit.value)||0)).toFixed(2); }
  updateComputedPreview();
}

function updateComputedPreview() {
  const box = $('#lnComputedPreview');
  if (!box) return;

  const qty = Math.max(0, Number($('#lnQty').value) || 0);
  const totalEl = document.getElementById('lnBilledTotal');
  const billed = totalEl && totalEl.value !== '' ? (Number(totalEl.value)||0) : qty * (Number($('#lnBilledUnit').value) || 0);
  const isManual = $('#lnServiceSelect').value === '__manual__';

  if (!qty || !billed) {
    box.classList.remove('show');
    box.innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }

  box.classList.add('show');

  if (isManual) {
    box.className = 'ln-source-result show';
    box.style.background = '#FEF2F2';
    box.style.borderColor = '#FECACA';
    box.style.color = '#B91C1C';
    box.innerHTML = `⛔ <b>غير مدرجة في اللائحة</b> — المعتمد: <b>0</b> (رفض تلقائي)`;
    return;
  }

  const ctx = getContractContext(activeClaim);
  const code = $('#lnCode').value.trim();
  const tariffUnit = Number($('#lnTariffUnit').value) || 0;
  const tariffTotal = qty * tariffUnit;
  const lesser = Math.min(billed, tariffTotal);
  const rate = getContractRateForService(ctx, code);
  const covered = isServiceCovered(ctx, code);
  const after = covered ? Math.round(lesser * (1 - rate / 100)) : 0;

  if (!covered) {
    box.className = 'ln-source-result show';
    box.style.background = '#FEF2F2';
    box.style.borderColor = '#FECACA';
    box.style.color = '#B91C1C';
    box.innerHTML = `⛔ <b>غير مغطاة بالعقد</b> — المعتمد: <b>0</b>`;
    return;
  }

  box.className = 'ln-source-result show';
  box.style.background = '';
  box.style.borderColor = '';
  box.style.color = '';

  const exceeds = billed > tariffTotal + 0.01;
  const exceedsBadge = exceeds ? ' · <span style="color:#B45309;font-weight:800">تجاوز اللائحة</span>' : '';

  box.innerHTML = `
    فاتورة <b>${fmt(billed)}</b>
    · لائحة <b>${fmt(tariffTotal)}</b>
    · الأقل <b>${fmt(lesser)}</b>
    · خصم ${rate}%${exceedsBadge}
    → المعتمد <b style="color:#047857;font-size:14px">${fmt(after)}</b>
  `;
}

function toggleManualFields(enabled) {
  ['lnCode', 'lnName', 'lnCategory'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.readOnly = !enabled;
  });
  if (enabled) {
    const tu = $('#lnTariffUnit'); if (tu) tu.value = '';
    const cd = $('#lnContractDiscount'); if (cd) cd.value = '—';
    const banner = $('#lnSourceBanner');
    if (banner) {
      banner.className = 'ln-source-banner warn';
      banner.innerHTML = '⚠ <b>إدخال يدوي</b> — البند غير مدرج في اللائحة وسيُرفض تلقائيًا';
    }
  }
}

function bindLineSmartForm() {
  const sel = $('#lnServiceSelect');
  const qty = $('#lnQty');
  const bu = $('#lnBilledUnit');

  if (sel && !sel.dataset.bound) {
    sel.dataset.bound = '1';
    sel.addEventListener('change', () => {
      const code = sel.value;
      if (code === '__manual__') {
        toggleManualFields(true);
        $('#lnCode').value = '';
        $('#lnName').value = '';
        $('#lnCategory').value = 'أخرى';
        updateComputedPreview();
        setTimeout(() => $('#lnCode').focus(), 50);
        return;
      }
      if (!code) {
        toggleManualFields(false);
        $('#lnCode').value = '';
        $('#lnName').value = '';
        $('#lnCategory').value = '';
        $('#lnTariffUnit').value = '';
        $('#lnContractDiscount').value = '';
        updateComputedPreview();
        return;
      }
      const ctx = getContractContext(activeClaim);
      const svc = (ctx.tariff?.services || []).find(s => String(s.code) === code);
      if (svc) {
        toggleManualFields(false);
        applyServiceToLine(svc);
      }
    });
  }

  if (qty && !qty.dataset.bound) {
    qty.dataset.bound = '1';
    qty.addEventListener('input', () => syncLineBilledAmount('unit'));
  }
  if (bu && !bu.dataset.bound) {
    bu.dataset.bound = '1';
    bu.addEventListener('input', () => syncLineBilledAmount('unit'));
    const total = document.getElementById('lnBilledTotal');
    if(total && !total.dataset.bound){ total.dataset.bound='1'; total.addEventListener('input', () => syncLineBilledAmount('total')); }
  }
}
function updatePAWarning(){
  const status = $('#paStatus').value;
  const box = $('#priorApprovalBox'), w = $('#paWarningText');
  const msgs = { 'not-requested':'⚠️ لم يتم تقديم طلب موافقة.', 'pending':'⏳ طلب قيد الدراسة.', 'rejected':'❌ تم رفض الموافقة.', 'approved':'✅ الموافقة سارية.' };
  w.textContent = msgs[status] || '';
  box.className = 'pa-box ' + (status === 'approved' ? 'ok' : 'warn');
}

// lang.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function applyTranslations(){
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el => {
    const key = el.getAttribute('data-i18n');
    const val = t(key);
    if(val && val !== key){ el.textContent = val; }
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-placeholder]').forEach(el => {
    const key = el.getAttribute('data-i18n-placeholder');
    const val = t(key);
    if(val && val !== key){ el.setAttribute('placeholder', val); }
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-html]').forEach(el => {
    const key = el.getAttribute('data-i18n-html');
    const val = t(key);
    if(val && val !== key){ el.innerHTML = val; }
  });
}
function applyNavTranslations(){
  const map = {
    dashboard:'nav.dashboard', claims:'nav.claims', intake:'nav.intake',
    sessions:'nav.sessions', providers:'nav.providers', contracts:'nav.contracts',
    tariffs:'nav.tariffs', search:'nav.search', 'deduction-rules':'nav.deduction',
    reports:'nav.reports', trash:'nav.trash', settings:'nav.settings', help:'nav.help'
  };
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(btn => {
    const page = btn.dataset.page;
    const key = map[page];
    if(!key) return;
    const label = btn.querySelector('.nav-label');
    const sub = btn.querySelector('.nav-sub');
    if(label) label.textContent = t(key);
    if(sub) sub.textContent = t(key+'.sub');
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-section-title').forEach((el,i) => {
    const keys = ['nav.main','nav.basic','nav.reports'];
    if(keys[i]) el.textContent = t(keys[i]);
  });
}
function applyTopbarTranslations(){
  const sb = document.getElementById('smartBackBtn'); if(sb) sb.title = t('topbar.back');
  const ts = document.getElementById('tbSearchBtn'); if(ts) ts.title = t('topbar.search');
  const tb = document.getElementById('tbBackupBtn'); if(tb) tb.title = t('topbar.backup');
  const tu = document.getElementById('tbUserBtn'); if(tu) tu.title = t('topbar.settings');
  const pill = document.querySelector('.pill'); if(pill) pill.textContent = t('topbar.connected');
}
function applyPageTitleTranslations(){
  if(!currentPage) return;
  const el = document.getElementById('pageTitle');
  const sub = document.getElementById('pageDesc');
  const k = 'page.'+currentPage;
  const kt = t(k);
  if(el && kt && kt !== k) el.textContent = kt;
  const ks = t(k+'.sub');
  if(sub && ks && ks !== k+'.sub') sub.textContent = ks;
}
function applyAllTranslations(){
  applyNavTranslations();
  applyTopbarTranslations();
  applyPageTitleTranslations();
  applyTranslations();
}
// override setLang to also update nav/topbar/page
const _origSetLang = setLang;
setLang = function(lang){
  _origSetLang(lang);
  applyAllTranslations();
  if(lang === 'ar'){ if(typeof restoreDOM === 'function') restoreDOM(document.body); if(typeof renderAll === 'function') renderAll(); }
  else { if(typeof translateDOMMulti === 'function') translateDOMMulti(); else if(typeof translateDOM === 'function') translateDOM(document.body); }
  // update splash/loading/lock text (best effort)
  const spSub = document.querySelector('.sp-sub'); if(spSub) spSub.textContent = t('splash.sub');
  const spBadge = document.querySelector('.sp-badge'); if(spBadge) spBadge.textContent = 'v1.0';
  const ldHead = document.querySelector('.ld-head h2'); if(ldHead) ldHead.textContent = t('splash.title');
  const ldP = document.querySelector('.ld-head p'); if(ldP) ldP.textContent = t('loading');
  const lockH = document.querySelector('#lock h1'); if(lockH) lockH.textContent = t('auth.title');
  const lockHint = document.querySelector('#lock .hint'); if(lockHint) lockHint.textContent = t('auth.password');
  const lockBtn = document.querySelector('#lock .btn-primary'); if(lockBtn) lockBtn.lastChild && (lockBtn.lastChild.textContent = ' '+t('auth.login'));
};

// mobile.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function openSidebar(){
  const sb = document.querySelector('.sidebar');
  const ov = document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay');
  if(sb) sb.classList.add('open');
  if(ov) ov.classList.add('show');
  document.body.classList.add('sidebar-open');
  document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
}
function closeSidebar(){
  const sb = document.querySelector('.sidebar');
  const ov = document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay');
  if(sb) sb.classList.remove('open');
  if(ov) ov.classList.remove('show');
  document.body.classList.remove('sidebar-open');
  _unlockBodyScroll();
}
// Let the browser display its native install UI. Intercepting beforeinstallprompt
// without promptly calling prompt() produces a misleading console banner warning.
let deferredInstallPrompt = null;
function updateInstallBtn(){ const b = document.getElementById('installAppBtn'); if(b) b.style.display = 'none'; }
function installApp(){ return false; }
window.installApp = installApp;
function toggleSidebar(){
  if(window.innerWidth <= 900){ openSidebar(); return; }
  const shell = document.querySelector('.app-shell');
  const sb = document.querySelector('.sidebar');
  if(!shell || !sb) return;
  const collapsed = shell.classList.toggle('collapsed');
  sb.classList.toggle('collapsed', collapsed);
  try { localStorage.setItem('tpa_sidebar_collapsed', collapsed ? '1' : '0'); } catch(e){}
}
function bindMobile(){
  const hb = document.getElementById('hamburgerBtn');
  const ov = document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay');
  if(hb) hb.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if(window.innerWidth <= 900) openSidebar(); else toggleSidebar();
  });
  if(ov) ov.addEventListener('click', closeSidebar);
  $$('.nav-item').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if(window.innerWidth <= 900) closeSidebar();
  }));
  window.addEventListener('resize', () => { if(window.innerWidth > 900) closeSidebar(); });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if(e.key === 'Escape') closeSidebar(); });
  // restore collapsed state on desktop
  try {
    if(localStorage.getItem('tpa_sidebar_collapsed') === '1' && window.innerWidth > 900){
      const shell = document.querySelector('.app-shell'); const sb = document.querySelector('.sidebar');
      if(shell && sb){ shell.classList.add('collapsed'); sb.classList.add('collapsed'); }
    }
  } catch(e){}
}

// modals-build.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function buildModals(){
  $('#modalsContainer').innerHTML = MODAL_REVIEW + MODAL_INVOICE + MODAL_PATIENT + MODAL_LINE + MODAL_PROVIDER + MODAL_CONTRACT + MODAL_TARIFF + MODAL_TARIFF_IMPORT + MODAL_BATCH + MODAL_DR + MODAL_PAPROC + MODAL_DRWIZARD;
  // Accessibility: expose modal dialogs to assistive tech
  $$('#modalsContainer .modal').forEach((m, i) => {
    m.setAttribute('role', 'dialog');
    m.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
    const title = m.querySelector('.modal-head h3, h3');
    if (title && !title.id) title.id = 'modalTitle' + i;
    if (title) m.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', title.id);
  });
}
buildModals();

// navigation.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let navHistory = [];
let currentPage = 'dashboard';
let viewState = { type:null, id:null };
const PAGE_META = {
  dashboard:['لوحة التحكم','نظرة عامة على الأداء المالي'],
  claims:['إدارة المطالبات','عرض، مراجعة، وتصدير جميع المطالبات'],
  timeline:['الخط الزمني','تتبع جميع أحداث مراجعة المطالبة'],
  view:['تفاصيل المطالبة','عرض تفاصيل المطالبة وفواتيرها'],
  search:['بحث اللوائح','بحث متقدم في لوائح الأسعار والخدمات'],
  review:['مراجعة المطالبة','مراجعة مالية مفصلة للمطالبة'],
  intake:['استلام الدفعات','استلام وإدارة الدفعات الشهرية من الجهات'],
  providers:['مقدمو الخدمات','إدارة جهات تقديم الخدمات الطبية وعقودها'],
  contracts:['العقود','متابعة وإدارة عقود الجهات الطبية'],
  services:['الخدمات','إدارة خدمات اللوائح الطبية'],
  tariffs:['اللوائح','إدارة لوائح الأسعار والخدمات الطبية'],
  'deduction-rules':['قواعد الخصم','إنشاء وإدارة قواعد الخصم (سقف، تحمل، مخصص، سوبر)'],
  reports:['التقارير والتحليلات','تحليل مالي شامل للمطالبات'],
  trash:['سلة المحذوفات','استعادة أو حذف نهائي للعناصر المحذوفة'],
  sessions:['جلسات المراجعة','متابعة وإدارة مراجعات المطالبات المفتوحة'],
  settings:['الإعدادات والحساب','إدارة الحساب، الهوية، والأمان'],
  help:['المساعدة والتوثيق','دليل المستخدم، الاختصارات، FAQ']
};
function go(screenId){
  $$('.screen').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active'));
  const el = document.getElementById(screenId);
  if(el) el.classList.add('active');
  window.scrollTo(0,0);
}
function navigateTo(page, opts){
  opts = opts || {};
  if(page === currentPage && !opts.force) return;
  if(!opts.noPush && currentPage !== 'splash' && currentPage !== 'lock' && currentPage){
    navHistory.push({ page: currentPage, viewType: viewState.type, viewId: viewState.id });
  }
  applyPage(page);
}
function applyPage(page){
  currentPage = page;
  const activeNav = ['timeline','view','review'].includes(page) ? (navHistory.length ? navHistory[navHistory.length-1].page : 'claims') : page;
  $$('.nav-item').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.page === activeNav));
  $$('.page').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
  const el = document.getElementById('page-'+page); if(el) el.classList.add('active');
  const meta = PAGE_META[page] || ['',''];
  const tTitle = (typeof t === 'function') ? t('page.'+page) : null;
  const tSub = (typeof t === 'function') ? t('page.'+page+'.sub') : null;
  $('#pageTitle').textContent = (tTitle && tTitle !== 'page.'+page) ? tTitle : meta[0];
  const pd = document.getElementById('pageDesc'); if(pd) pd.textContent = (tSub && tSub !== 'page.'+page+'.sub') ? tSub : meta[1];
  const backBtn = $('#smartBackBtn');
  if(page === 'dashboard') backBtn.classList.remove('show');
  else backBtn.classList.add('show');
  window.scrollTo({top:0, behavior:'smooth'});
  setTimeout(() => { if(typeof translateDOM === 'function') translateDOM(document.body); }, 0);
  if(page === 'search') renderSearch();
  if(page === 'services') renderServices();
  if(page === 'trash') renderTrash();
  if(page === 'deduction-rules') renderDeductionRules();
  if(page === 'reports') renderReports();
  if(page === 'sessions') renderSessions();
  if(page === 'services') { if(typeof bindServicesControls === 'function') bindServicesControls(); renderServices(); }
  if(page === 'settings') { if(typeof bindSettingsPassword === 'function') bindSettingsPassword(); }
}
function goBack(){
  if(navHistory.length === 0){ navigateTo('dashboard', {noPush:true}); return; }
  const prev = navHistory.pop();
  if(prev.viewType && prev.viewId && prev.page === 'view'){ viewState.type = prev.viewType; viewState.id = prev.viewId; applyPage('view'); renderView(); return; }
  if(prev.page === 'view'){ return goBack(); }
  applyPage(prev.page);
}
function bindNav(){
  $$('.nav-item').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if(currentPage && currentPage !== b.dataset.page){
      navHistory.push({ page: currentPage, viewType: viewState.type, viewId: viewState.id });
    }
    navigateTo(b.dataset.page, {noPush:true});
  }));
}

// notifications-v2.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let toastTimer;
function showToast(msg){
  const tm = $('#toastMsg'); if(tm) tm.textContent = msg;
  const t = $('#toast'); if(t) t.classList.add('show');
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2600);
}
function updateTrashBadge(){
  const items = Array.isArray(trash) ? trash : [];
  const b = $('#trashCount');
  if(b){ b.textContent = items.length; b.style.display = items.length > 0 ? 'grid' : 'none'; }
  const tb = $('#tbTrashCount');
  if(tb){ tb.textContent = items.length; tb.style.display = items.length > 0 ? 'inline-flex' : 'none'; }
  const st = $('#settingsTrashCount'); if(st) st.textContent = items.length;
}

// onboarding.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function showOnboardingError(msg){ const e = document.getElementById('onbErr'); if(e){ e.querySelector('span').textContent = msg; e.classList.add('show'); } }
function clearOnboardingError(){ const e = document.getElementById('onbErr'); if(e){ e.classList.remove('show'); } }
function bindOnboarding(){
  const root = document.getElementById('onboarding');
  if(root && root.dataset.bound === '1') return;
  if(root) root.dataset.bound = '1';
  const next = document.getElementById('onbNextBtn');
  const langBtn = document.getElementById('onbLangBtn');
  if(langBtn){ langBtn.textContent = currentLang === 'ar' ? 'EN' : 'AR'; langBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { const newLang = currentLang === 'ar' ? 'en' : 'ar'; setLang(newLang); langBtn.textContent = newLang === 'ar' ? 'EN' : 'AR'; if(typeof applyAllTranslations === 'function') applyAllTranslations(); if(typeof translateDOMMulti === 'function') translateDOMMulti(); else if(typeof translateDOM === 'function') translateDOM(document.body); }); }
  setTimeout(() => { document.getElementById('onbReviewerName')?.focus(); }, 50);
  // eye toggles
  document.querySelectorAll('.onb-eye').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const inp = document.getElementById(btn.dataset.target);
      if(!inp) return;
      const show = inp.type === 'password';
      inp.type = show ? 'text' : 'password';
      btn.setAttribute('aria-label', show ? 'إخفاء' : 'إظهار');
    });
  });
  // strength meter
  const pwdIn = document.getElementById('onbPwd');
  const bar = document.getElementById('onbPwdBar');
  if(pwdIn && bar){ pwdIn.addEventListener('input', () => {
    const v = pwdIn.value;
    let score = 0;
    if(v.length > 0) score = 1;
    if(v.length >= 6) score = 2;
    if(v.length >= 10) score = 3;
    if(/[A-Za-z\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(v) && /\d/.test(v)) score = Math.max(score, 3);
    if(v.length >= 6 && /[A-Za-z\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(v) && /\d/.test(v)) score = 4;
    if(/[^A-Za-z0-9\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(v)) score = Math.min(4, score+1);
    bar.style.width = (score*25)+'%';
    bar.className = 'pw-bar s'+score;
  }); }
  // submit on Enter
  ['onbReviewerName','onbReviewerRole','onbCompanyName','onbCompanyBrand','onbPwd','onbPwdConfirm'].forEach(id=>{
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el) el.addEventListener('keydown', e=>{ if(e.key==='Enter'){ e.preventDefault(); next?.click(); } });
  });
  const confIn = document.getElementById('onbPwdConfirm');
  if(confIn){ confIn.addEventListener('input', () => {
    if(confIn.value && confIn.value !== pwdIn.value){ showOnboardingError('التأكيد لا يطابق'); } else { clearOnboardingError(); }
  }); }
  if(next) next.addEventListener('click', () => {
    clearOnboardingError();
    const name = (document.getElementById('onbReviewerName')?.value||'').trim();
    const role = (document.getElementById('onbReviewerRole')?.value||'').trim() || 'مراجع مالي معتمد';
    const cn = (document.getElementById('onbCompanyName')?.value||'').trim() || 'TPA';
    const cb = (document.getElementById('onbCompanyBrand')?.value||'').trim() || 'TPA Pro';
    const pwd = document.getElementById('onbPwd')?.value || '';
    const conf = document.getElementById('onbPwdConfirm')?.value || '';
    if(!name){ showOnboardingError('أدخل اسم المراجع'); return; }
    if(!cb){ showOnboardingError('أدخل العلامة التجارية'); return; }
    if(!pwd || pwd.length < 6){ showOnboardingError('كلمة المرور 6 أحرف على الأقل'); return; }
    if(!/[A-Za-z\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(pwd) || !/\d/.test(pwd)){ showOnboardingError('كلمة المرور يجب أن تحتوي على أحرف وأرقام'); return; }
    if(pwd !== conf){ showOnboardingError('التأكيد لا يطابق'); return; }
    appSettings.reviewer.name = name; appSettings.reviewer.role = role;
    appSettings.company.name = cn; appSettings.company.brand = cb;
    appSettings.company.tagline = appSettings.company.tagline || 'نظام المراجعة المالية للمطالبات الطبية';
    saveAppSettings();
    next.disabled = true; next.textContent = '...';
    (async () => {
      try {
        const h = await hashPassword(pwd);
        localStorage.setItem(PWD_HASH_KEY, h);
        if(localStorage.getItem(PWD_HASH_KEY) !== h) throw new Error('تعذر حفظ كلمة المرور');
        localStorage.setItem(ONBOARDING_KEY, '1');
        sessionStorage.removeItem(AUTH_KEY); localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_KEY);
        document.getElementById('onbStep1').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('onbStep2').style.display = 'block';
        document.querySelectorAll('.onb-dot').forEach((d,i)=>{ d.classList.toggle('active', i===1); });
      } catch(e) {
        showOnboardingError(e && e.message ? e.message : 'تعذر إعداد كلمة المرور بأمان. تحقق من التخزين واتصال آمن ثم أعد المحاولة.');
        next.disabled = false; next.textContent = 'متابعة';
      }
    })();
  });
  const impBtn = document.getElementById('onbImportBtn');
  const fileIn = document.getElementById('onbImportFile');
  if(impBtn && fileIn){ impBtn.addEventListener('click', () => fileIn.click()); }
  if(fileIn){ fileIn.addEventListener('change', e => {
    const f = e.target.files && e.target.files[0];
    if(f){ importBackupFile(f); }
    e.target.value = '';
  }); }
  const skip = document.getElementById('onbSkipBtn');
  if(skip) skip.addEventListener('click', () => {
    // Password and onboarding flag were saved in step 1; continue to authentication without a reload.
    try { localStorage.setItem(ONBOARDING_KEY, '1'); sessionStorage.removeItem(AUTH_KEY); localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_KEY); } catch(e) { showOnboardingError('تعذر إكمال الإعداد بسبب مشكلة في التخزين.'); return; }
    const screen = document.getElementById('onboarding'); if(screen) screen.classList.remove('active');
    if(typeof go === 'function') go('lock');
    setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('pwd')?.focus(), 80);
  });
}
// Patch importBackupFile success: after import, complete onboarding
// Wrap applyFullBackup to complete onboarding after a successful import
if(typeof applyFullBackup === 'function'){
  const _origApply = applyFullBackup;
  applyFullBackup = function(parsed, opts){
    const r = _origApply(parsed, opts);
    try { localStorage.setItem(ONBOARDING_KEY, '1'); sessionStorage.removeItem(AUTH_KEY); localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_KEY); } catch(e){}
    setTimeout(() => { try { location.reload(); } catch(e){} }, 800);
    return r;
  };
}
// delegated language toggle (works even if bindOnboarding ran before DOM ready)
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const btn = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#onbLangBtn');
  if(!btn) return;
  const newLang = currentLang === 'ar' ? 'en' : 'ar';
  setLang(newLang);
  btn.textContent = newLang === 'ar' ? 'EN' : 'AR';
  if(typeof applyAllTranslations === 'function') applyAllTranslations();
  if(typeof translateDOMMulti === 'function') translateDOMMulti(); else if(typeof translateDOM === 'function') translateDOM(document.body);
});
// Bind when DOM ready
setTimeout(() => { try { bindOnboarding(); } catch(e){ console.warn('bindOnboarding', e); } }, 0);

// providers-2.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let selectedContractServices = [];
function getAllServiceNames(){
  const set = new Set();
  (typeof tariffs !== 'undefined' ? tariffs : []).forEach(t => (t.services||[]).forEach(s => { if(s && s.name) set.add(s.name); }));
  if(!set.size){ ['جميع الخدمات','الخدمات الأساسية','الطوارئ','العلاج الطبيعي'].forEach(x => set.add(x)); }
  return [...set];
}
function renderContractServicesOptions(q){
  const box = $('#ctrServicesList'); if(!box) return;
  const term = (q||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = getAllServiceNames().filter(s => !term || s.toLowerCase().includes(term));
  box.innerHTML = list.map(s => `<label style="display:block;margin:2px 0"><input type="checkbox" value="${escapeHtml(s)}" ${selectedContractServices.includes(s)?'checked':''}> ${escapeHtml(s)}</label>`).join('');
  box.querySelectorAll('input[type=checkbox]').forEach(cb => {
    cb.addEventListener('change', () => {
      if(cb.checked){ if(!selectedContractServices.includes(cb.value)) selectedContractServices.push(cb.value); }
      else selectedContractServices = selectedContractServices.filter(x => x !== cb.value);
    });
  });
}
function renderContracts(){
  const activeN = contracts.filter(c=>effectiveContractStatus(c)==='active').length;
  const expN = contracts.filter(c=>effectiveContractStatus(c)==='expiring').length;
  const expdN = contracts.filter(c=>effectiveContractStatus(c)==='expired').length;
  let tm = 0, td = 0;
  contracts.forEach(c => { td += (c.discountRate||0); });
  const avg = contracts.length ? td/contracts.length : 0;
  $('#contractKpi').innerHTML = `
    <div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">العقود</div><div class="kpi-val">${contracts.length} <small>عقد</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('contract')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${providers.length} جهة</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k2"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">السارية</div><div class="kpi-val">${activeN} <small>عقد</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('check')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${expN} قريب · ${expdN} منتهي</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k4"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">متوسط الخصم</div><div class="kpi-val">${avg.toFixed(1)}<small>%</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('cut')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">عبر العقود</div></div>`;
  const urgent = contracts.filter(c => ['expiring','expired'].includes(effectiveContractStatus(c)));
  const al = $('#contractAlert');
  if(urgent.length){
    al.classList.add('show');
    const en = urgent.filter(c=>effectiveContractStatus(c)==='expiring').length;
    const xn = urgent.filter(c=>effectiveContractStatus(c)==='expired').length;
    const parts = []; if(en) parts.push(`${en} تنتهي خلال 60 يوم`); if(xn) parts.push(`${xn} منتهي`);
    $('#contractAlertText').textContent = `لديك ${parts.join(' و ')}.`;
  } else al.classList.remove('show');
  const list = contracts.filter(c => {
    if(contractStatusFilter !== 'all' && effectiveContractStatus(c) !== contractStatusFilter) return false;
    if(contractSearch){ const q = contractSearch, p = getProviderById(c.providerId); return c.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (p && p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)); }
    return true;
  });
  const body = $('#contractsBody');
  if(!list.length){ body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;padding:36px;color:var(--muted)">لا عقود</td></tr>'; return; }
  body.innerHTML = list.map(c => {
    const eff = effectiveContractStatus(c), cs = CONTRACT_STATUS[eff] || CONTRACT_STATUS.active;
    const p = getProviderById(c.providerId);
    return `<tr>
      <td data-label="رقم"><span class="clm-id">${escapeHtml(c.id)}</span></td>
      <td data-label="الجهة"><div class="prov-cell"><div class="prov-avatar alt">${escapeHtml((p?p.name:'—').charAt(0))}</div><div><div class="prov-name">${escapeHtml(p?p.name:'—')}</div><div class="prov-city">${escapeHtml(p?p.city:'' )}</div></div></div></td>
      <td data-label="البداية" class="hide-sm mono">${formatDate(c.startDate)}</td>
      <td data-label="النهاية" class="hide-sm mono">${formatDate(c.endDate)}</td>
      <td data-label="المتبقي"><span class="days-cell ${daysRemainingClass(c.endDate)}">${daysRemainingText(c.endDate)}</span></td>
      <td data-label="الخصم" class="mono">${c.discountRate}%</td>
      <td data-label="الحالة"><span class="badge ${cs.cls}">${cs.label}</span></td>
      <td class="act"><div style="display:flex;gap:5px;flex-wrap:nowrap;align-items:center"><button class="btn-xs" data-edit-contract="${c.id}">تعديل</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-delete-contract="${c.id}">×</button></div></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
}
function openContractModal(id){
  editingContractId = id || null; const isEdit = !!id;
  const c = isEdit ? getContractById(id) : null; if(isEdit && !c) return;
  if(isEdit){ $('#ctrProvider').innerHTML = `<option value="${escapeHtml(c.providerId)}">${escapeHtml((getProviderById(c.providerId)||{}).name||'—')}</option>`; $('#ctrProvider').disabled = true; }
  else {
    const used = contracts.map(x => x.providerId);
    const avail = providers.filter(p => !used.includes(p.id));
    if(!avail.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','جميع الجهات لديها عقود'); return; }
    $('#ctrProvider').disabled = false;
    $('#ctrProvider').innerHTML = avail.map(p => `<option value="${escapeHtml(p.id)}">${escapeHtml(p.name)}</option>`).join('');
  }
  $('#ctrModalTitle').textContent = isEdit ? 'تعديل العقد' : 'إضافة عقد';
  $('#ctrModalSub').textContent = isEdit ? c.id : 'بيانات جديدة';
  if(isEdit){
    $('#ctrNumber').value = c.id; $('#ctrStatus').value = c.status||'active';
    $('#ctrStart').value = c.startDate; $('#ctrEnd').value = c.endDate;
    $('#ctrDiscount').value = c.discountRate; $('#ctrPayment').value = c.paymentTerms||'30 يوم';
    selectedContractServices = isEdit ? (c.coveredServices ? String(c.coveredServices).split('، ').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean) : []) : ['جميع الخدمات'];
    $('#ctrNotes').value = c.notes||''; (el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'block'; })($('#deleteContract'));
  } else {
    const today = daysFromNow(0);
    selectedContractServices = ['جميع الخدمات'];
    $('#ctrStatus').value = 'active'; $('#ctrStart').value = today; $('#ctrEnd').value = daysFromNow(365);
    $('#ctrDiscount').value = 10; $('#ctrPayment').value = '30 يوم';
    $('#ctrNotes').value = ''; $('#ctrNumber').value = nextContractNumber(today);
    (el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'none'; })($('#deleteContract'));
  }
  const ss = $('#ctrServicesSearch'); if(ss) ss.value = '';
  renderContractServicesOptions('');
  if(!isEdit) $('#ctrStart').onchange = function(){ $('#ctrNumber').value = nextContractNumber(this.value); };
  else $('#ctrStart').onchange = null;
  $('#contractModal').classList.add('open'); document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
}
function closeContractModal(){ if(!$('#contractModal').classList.contains('open')) return; $('#contractModal').classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); editingContractId = null; }
function saveContract(){
  const pid = $('#ctrProvider').value; if(!pid){ showWarning('تنبيه','اختر جهة'); return; }
  const s = $('#ctrStart').value, e = $('#ctrEnd').value;
  if(!s || !e){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل التواريخ'); return; }
  if(new Date(e) <= new Date(s)){ showWarning('تنبيه','النهاية بعد البداية'); return; }
  const data = { providerId:pid, startDate:s, endDate:e, discountRate:Math.min(100,Math.max(0,Number($('#ctrDiscount').value)||0)), paymentTerms:$('#ctrPayment').value, coveredServices:selectedContractServices.join('، '), autoRenew:true, status:$('#ctrStatus').value||'active', notes:$('#ctrNotes').value.trim() };
  if(editingContractId){ const c = getContractById(editingContractId); if(!c) return; Object.assign(c, data); syncContractToProvider(c); showSuccess('تم','تم التحديث'); }
  else { const nid = nextContractNumber(s); contracts.push({ id:nid, ...data }); syncContractToProvider({ id:nid, ...data }); showSuccess('تم','تمت الإضافة'); }
  closeContractModal(); renderAll();
}
function deleteContractWithDialog(id){
  const c = getContractById(id); if(!c) return;
  showDelete('حذف العقد', `سيتم نقل ${c.id}.`, () => {
    addToTrash('contract','عقد', c.id, c);
    contracts = contracts.filter(x => x.id !== id);
    tariffs.forEach(t => { t.linkedContractIds = (t.linkedContractIds||[]).filter(cid => cid !== id); });
    if($('#contractModal').classList.contains('open')) closeContractModal();
    renderAll(); showSuccess('تم','تم النقل');
  });
}
function bindTariffsControls(){
  $('#tariffSearch').addEventListener('input', e => { tariffSearch = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase(); renderTariffs(); });
  $('#tariffFilterChips').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const chip = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!chip) return;
    $$('#tariffFilterChips .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    chip.classList.add('active'); tariffFilter = chip.dataset.tfilter; renderTariffs();
  });
  $('#addTariffBtn').addEventListener('click', () => openTariffModal());
  const importTariffBtn=$('#importTariffExcelBtn'); if(importTariffBtn&&!importTariffBtn.dataset.bound){importTariffBtn.dataset.bound='1';importTariffBtn.addEventListener('click',openTariffImportModal);}
  $$('[data-close-tariff-import]').forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('click',()=>$('#tariffImportModal').classList.remove('open')));
  const confirmImport=$('#confirmTariffImport'); if(confirmImport&&!confirmImport.dataset.bound){confirmImport.dataset.bound='1';confirmImport.addEventListener('click',confirmTariffImport);}
  $('#tariffsBody').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const ed = e.target.closest('[data-edit-tariff]'); if(ed) openTariffModal(ed.dataset.editTariff);
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-delete-tariff]'); if(del) deleteTariffWithDialog(del.dataset.deleteTariff);
  });
  $$('.tf-tab').forEach(tab => tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
    $$('.tf-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
    tab.classList.add('active');
    $$('.tf-panel').forEach(p => p.classList.toggle('active', p.dataset.tfPanel === tab.dataset.tfTab));
  }));
  $$('.link-tab').forEach(tab => tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
    $$('.link-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
    tab.classList.add('active');
    (el => { if (el) el.style.display = tab.dataset.linkTab === 'providers' ? 'flex' : 'none'; })($('#linkProvidersList'));
    (el => { if (el) el.style.display = tab.dataset.linkTab === 'contracts' ? 'flex' : 'none'; })($('#linkContractsList'));
  }));
  const lpl = $('#linkProvidersList'); if(lpl) lpl.addEventListener('change', e => { if(e.target.type === 'checkbox') e.target.closest('.link-item').classList.toggle('checked', e.target.checked); });
  const lcl = $('#linkContractsList'); if(lcl) lcl.addEventListener('change', e => { if(e.target.type === 'checkbox') e.target.closest('.link-item').classList.toggle('checked', e.target.checked); });
  const tss = $('#tfServicesSearch'); if(tss && !tss.dataset.bound){ tss.dataset.bound='1'; tss.addEventListener('input', e => renderTariffServicesChecklist(e.target.value)); }
  $('#tfApplyAnnual').addEventListener('click', applyAnnualIncrease);


  $$('[data-close-tariff]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', closeTariffModal));
  $('#saveTariff').addEventListener('click', saveTariff);
  $('#deleteTariff').addEventListener('click', () => { if(editingTariffId) deleteTariffWithDialog(editingTariffId); });
}
