// providers-3.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let editingServiceTariffId = null;
let workingLinkedServices = [];
function renderTariffs(){
  const activeN = tariffs.filter(t=>effectiveTariffStatus(t)==='active').length;
  const generalN = tariffs.filter(t=>t.type==='general').length;
  const privateN = tariffs.filter(t=>t.type==='private').length;
  const suppN = tariffs.filter(t=>t.type==='supplementary').length;
  const totalLinks = tariffs.reduce((s,t)=>s+(t.linkedContractIds||[]).length,0);
  const totalServices = tariffs.reduce((s,t)=>s+(t.services?t.services.length:0),0);
  $('#tariffKpi').innerHTML = `
    <div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">اللوائح</div><div class="kpi-val">${tariffs.length} <small>لائحة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('tariff')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${activeN} نشطة · ${totalServices} خدمة</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k2"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">التوزيع</div><div class="kpi-val" style="font-size:15px">عامة ${generalN} · خاصة ${privateN} · مكملة ${suppN}</div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('file')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${generalN+privateN+suppN}</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k3"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">خدمات</div><div class="kpi-val">${totalServices}</div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('shield')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">عبر اللوائح</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k4"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">الروابط</div><div class="kpi-val">${totalLinks}</div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('link')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">عقود</div></div>`;
  const list = tariffs.filter(t => {
    if(tariffFilter === 'discount'){ if(t.adjustmentType !== 'discount') return false; }
    else if(tariffFilter === 'increase'){ if(t.adjustmentType !== 'increase') return false; }
    else if(tariffFilter !== 'all' && t.type !== tariffFilter) return false;
    if(tariffSearch){ const q = tariffSearch; return t.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) || t.name.toLowerCase().includes(q); }
    return true;
  });
  const body = $('#tariffsBody');
  if(!list.length){ body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center;padding:36px;color:var(--muted)">لا لوائح</td></tr>'; return; }
  body.innerHTML = list.map(t => {
    const eff = effectiveTariffStatus(t), ts = TARIFF_STATUS[eff] || TARIFF_STATUS.active;
    const adj = ADJUST_TYPES[t.adjustmentType] || ADJUST_TYPES.none;
    const adjHtml = t.adjustmentType === 'none' ? '<span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">—</span>' : `<span class="badge ${adj.cls}" style="font-weight:800">${adj.label} ${t.adjustmentRate}%</span>`;
    return `<tr>
      <td data-label="رقم"><span class="clm-id">${escapeHtml(t.id)}</span></td>
      <td data-label="الاسم"><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px">${escapeHtml(t.name)}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px">${t.basePriceLevel}% · ${(t.services||[]).length} خدمة</div></td>
      <td data-label="النوع"><span class="badge ${TARIFF_TYPES[t.type].cls}">${TARIFF_TYPES[t.type].label}</span></td>
      <td data-label="التسوية">${adjHtml}</td>
      <td data-label="الربط"><div style="font-size:12px;white-space:nowrap"><span style="color:var(--teal);font-weight:800">${(t.linkedProviderIds||[]).length}</span> جهة · <span style="color:var(--gold);font-weight:800">${(t.linkedContractIds||[]).length}</span> عقد</div></td>
      <td data-label="الحالة"><span class="badge ${ts.cls}">${ts.label}</span></td>
      <td class="act"><div style="display:flex;gap:5px;flex-wrap:nowrap;align-items:center"><button class="btn-xs" data-edit-tariff="${t.id}">تحرير</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-delete-tariff="${t.id}">×</button></div></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
}
function openTariffModal(id){
  editingTariffId = id || null; const isEdit = !!id;
  const t = isEdit ? getTariffById(id) : null; if(isEdit && !t) return;
  $('#tfModalTitle').textContent = isEdit ? 'تحرير اللائحة' : 'إضافة لائحة';
  $('#tfModalSub').textContent = isEdit ? `${t.id} — ${t.name}` : 'بيانات جديدة';
  if(isEdit){
    $('#tfNumber').value = t.id; $('#tfName').value = t.name; $('#tfType').value = t.type; $('#tfStatus').value = t.status||'active';
    $('#tfEffective').value = t.effectiveDate||''; $('#tfExpiry').value = t.expiryDate||'';
    $('#tfPriceLevel').value = t.basePriceLevel; $('#tfDescription').value = t.description||'';
    $('#tfIncreaseRate').value = t.annualIncreaseRate||0;
    $('#tfLastIncrease').value = t.lastIncreaseDate||''; $('#tfApprovedBy').value = t.lastIncreaseApprovedBy||'';
    workingServices = (t.services||[]).map(s => ({...s}));
    workingIncreaseHistory = (t.increaseHistory||[]).map(h => ({...h}));
    workingLinkedServices = [...(t.linkedServiceNames||[])];
    (el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'block'; })($('#deleteTariff'));
  } else {
    const today = daysFromNow(0);
    $('#tfNumber').value = nextTariffNumber(today); $('#tfName').value = ''; $('#tfType').value = 'general'; $('#tfStatus').value = 'active';
    $('#tfEffective').value = today; $('#tfExpiry').value = daysFromNow(365); $('#tfPriceLevel').value = 100; $('#tfDescription').value = '';
    $('#tfIncreaseRate').value = 0; $('#tfLastIncrease').value = ''; $('#tfApprovedBy').value = '';

    workingServices = []; workingIncreaseHistory = []; workingLinkedServices = []; (el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'none'; })($('#deleteTariff'));
  }
  $$('.tf-tab').forEach((tab,i) => tab.classList.toggle('active', i === 0));
  $$('.tf-panel').forEach((p,i) => p.classList.toggle('active', i === 0));
  renderIncreaseHistory(); renderLinkLists(isEdit ? t : null); renderTariffServicesChecklist();
  if(!isEdit) $('#tfEffective').onchange = function(){ $('#tfNumber').value = nextTariffNumber(this.value); };
  else $('#tfEffective').onchange = null;
  $('#tariffModal').classList.add('open'); document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
}
function closeTariffModal(){ if(!$('#tariffModal').classList.contains('open')) return; $('#tariffModal').classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); editingTariffId = null; workingServices = []; workingIncreaseHistory = []; }
function openServiceModal(i, tariffId){
  editingServiceIndex = i;
  editingServiceTariffId = tariffId || editingServiceTariffId;
  // populate tariff selector
  const sel = $('#svcTariff');
  if(sel){
    sel.innerHTML = (tariffs||[]).map(t => `<option value="${escapeHtml(t.id)}">${escapeHtml(t.name)}</option>`).join('');
    if(editingServiceTariffId) sel.value = editingServiceTariffId;
    else if(tariffs && tariffs.length) { editingServiceTariffId = tariffs[0].id; sel.value = tariffs[0].id; }
  }
  const t = tariffs.find(x=>x.id===editingServiceTariffId);
  const svc = (i !== null && t && t.services && t.services[i]) ? t.services[i] : null;
  const svcEff=$('#svcEffectiveDate'); if(svcEff){ svcEff.value=tariffDatePlusOne(t&&t.effectiveDate||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)); }
  if(svc){ $('#svcModalTitle').textContent = 'تعديل'; $('#svcModalSub').textContent = svc.code+' — '+svc.name; $('#svcCode').value = svc.code; $('#svcCategory').value = svc.category; $('#svcName').value = svc.name; $('#svcBasePrice').value = svc.basePrice; $('#svcCurrentPrice').value = svc.currentPrice; $('#svcCovered').checked = !!svc.covered; $('#svcRequiresPriorApproval').checked = !!svc.requiresPriorApproval; }
  else { $('#svcModalTitle').textContent = 'إضافة'; $('#svcModalSub').textContent = '—'; $('#svcCode').value = ''; $('#svcCategory').value = 'استشارة'; $('#svcName').value = ''; $('#svcBasePrice').value = ''; $('#svcCurrentPrice').value = ''; $('#svcCovered').checked = true; $('#svcRequiresPriorApproval').checked = false; }
  $('#serviceModal').classList.add('open');
}
function closeServiceModal(){ if(!$('#serviceModal').classList.contains('open')) return; $('#serviceModal').classList.remove('open'); editingServiceIndex = null; }
function saveService(){
  const tid = $('#svcTariff') ? $('#svcTariff').value : editingServiceTariffId;
  const t = tariffs.find(x=>x.id===tid); if(!t){ showWarning('تنبيه','اختر اللائحة'); return; }
  if(!t.services) t.services = [];
  const code = $('#svcCode').value.trim(), name = $('#svcName').value.trim(), cat = $('#svcCategory').value;
  const bp = Number($('#svcBasePrice').value)||0, cp = Number($('#svcCurrentPrice').value)||0;
  if(!code){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الكود'); return; }
  if(!name){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الاسم'); return; }
  if(bp <= 0){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل سعراً'); return; }
  const data = { code, name, category:cat, basePrice:bp, currentPrice:cp||bp, covered:$('#svcCovered').checked, requiresPriorApproval:$('#svcRequiresPriorApproval').checked };
  const eff=$('#svcEffectiveDate')?.value||''; if(!eff){showWarning('تنبيه','حدد تاريخ السريان');return;}
  if(t.effectiveDate&&eff<=t.effectiveDate){showWarning('تاريخ السريان','يجب أن يكون بعد تاريخ سريان النسخة الحالية');return;}
  const before=editingServiceIndex!==null&&t.services[editingServiceIndex]?{...t.services[editingServiceIndex]}:null;
  if(t.services.some((s,i)=>s.code===code && i!==editingServiceIndex)){showWarning('تنبيه','الكود مستخدم');return;}
  const snap={services:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.services)),priceVersions:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.priceVersions||[])),changeLog:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.changeLog||[])),effectiveDate:t.effectiveDate};
  archiveTariffVersion(t,eff,before?'service-update':'service-add',[{code,action:before?'update':'add',before,after:{...data}}]);
  if(before)t.services[editingServiceIndex]=data;else t.services.push(data);
  if(typeof persistData==='function'&&persistData(true)===false){t.services=snap.services;t.priceVersions=snap.priceVersions;t.changeLog=snap.changeLog;t.effectiveDate=snap.effectiveDate;showWarning('خطأ','تعذر الحفظ وتم التراجع');return;}
  closeServiceModal(); renderServices(); renderTariffs(); renderTariffChangeHistory(); showSuccess('تم','تم الحفظ مع حفظ تاريخ الأسعار');
}
function renderIncreaseHistory(){ const box = $('#tfHistoryList'); if(!workingIncreaseHistory.length){ box.innerHTML = '<p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);text-align:center;padding:12px;background:#F8FAFC;border-radius:9px">لا سجل</p>'; return; } box.innerHTML = workingIncreaseHistory.map(h => `<div class="history-item"><div><div class="hi-date">${escapeHtml(h.date)}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px">${escapeHtml(h.approvedBy)}</div></div><div style="text-align:left"><div class="hi-rate">+${escapeHtml(h.rate)}%</div></div></div>`).join(''); }
function applyAnnualIncrease(){
  const rate = Number($('#tfIncreaseRate').value)||0;
  if(rate <= 0 || rate > 100){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل نسبة'); return; }
  const cur = Number($('#tfPriceLevel').value)||100;
  showWarning('تأكيد', `تطبيق ${rate}%؟`, () => {
    workingServices.forEach(s => { s.basePrice = Math.round(s.basePrice*(1+rate/100)); s.currentPrice = Math.round(s.currentPrice*(1+rate/100)); });
    const date = daysFromNow(0), by = $('#tfApprovedBy').value.trim()||'الإدارة';
    workingIncreaseHistory.push({ date, rate, approvedBy:by, fromLevel:cur, toLevel:cur+rate });
    $('#tfPriceLevel').value = cur+rate; $('#tfLastIncrease').value = date;
    renderIncreaseHistory(); showSuccess('تم',`${rate}%`);
  });
}
function renderLinkLists(t){
  const sc = t ? (t.linkedContractIds||[]) : [];
  const box = $('#linkContractsList'); if(box) box.innerHTML = contracts.map(c => { const p = getProviderById(c.providerId); return `<label class="link-item ${sc.includes(c.id)?'checked':''}"><input type="checkbox" value="${escapeHtml(c.id)}" ${sc.includes(c.id)?'checked':''}><div><div class="li-name">${escapeHtml(c.id)}</div><div class="li-sub">${escapeHtml(p?p.name:'—')}</div></div></label>`; }).join('');
}
function getAllServiceNamesDistinct(){
  const set = new Set();
  (typeof tariffs !== 'undefined' ? tariffs : []).forEach(t => (t.services||[]).forEach(s => { if(s && s.name) set.add(s.name); }));
  return [...set];
}
function renderTariffServicesChecklist(q){
  const box = $('#tfServicesChecklist'); if(!box) return;
  const term = (q||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = getAllServiceNamesDistinct().filter(n => !term || n.toLowerCase().includes(term));
  box.innerHTML = list.map(n => `<label style="display:block;margin:2px 0"><input type="checkbox" value="${escapeHtml(n)}" ${workingLinkedServices.includes(n)?'checked':''}> ${escapeHtml(n)}</label>`).join('');
  box.querySelectorAll('input[type=checkbox]').forEach(cb => {
    cb.addEventListener('change', () => {
      if(cb.checked){ if(!workingLinkedServices.includes(cb.value)) workingLinkedServices.push(cb.value); }
      else workingLinkedServices = workingLinkedServices.filter(x => x !== cb.value);
    });
  });
}
function saveTariff(){
  const name = $('#tfName').value.trim(); if(!name){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الاسم'); return; }
  const eff = $('#tfEffective').value; if(!eff){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل السريان'); return; }
  const lp = [];
  const lc = $$('#linkContractsList input:checked').map(i => i.value);
  const data = { name, type:$('#tfType').value, description:$('#tfDescription').value.trim(), effectiveDate:eff, expiryDate:$('#tfExpiry').value||'', status:$('#tfStatus').value||'active', basePriceLevel:Number($('#tfPriceLevel').value)||100, annualIncreaseEnabled:false, annualIncreaseRate:Number($('#tfIncreaseRate').value)||0, lastIncreaseDate:$('#tfLastIncrease').value||'', lastIncreaseApprovedBy:$('#tfApprovedBy').value.trim(), adjustmentType:'none', adjustmentRate:0, linkedProviderIds:lp, linkedContractIds:lc, linkedServiceNames:workingLinkedServices, increaseHistory:workingIncreaseHistory, services:workingServices.map(s=>({...s})) };
  if(editingTariffId){
    const t=getTariffById(editingTariffId); if(!t)return;
    const oldServices=JSON.stringify(t.services||[]), newServices=JSON.stringify(data.services||[]);
    const dateChanged=String(t.effectiveDate||'')!==String(eff||'');
    if(oldServices!==newServices || dateChanged){
      if(t.effectiveDate && eff<=t.effectiveDate){showWarning('تاريخ السريان','عند تغيير الأسعار أو تاريخ سريان النسخة يجب أن يكون التاريخ الجديد بعد النسخة الحالية');return;}
      const before=JSON.parse(oldServices), after=JSON.parse(newServices), oldBy=new Map(before.map(x=>[String(x.code),x])), newBy=new Map(after.map(x=>[String(x.code),x]));
      const changes=[]; for(const [code,svc] of oldBy){const n=newBy.get(code);if(!n)changes.push({code,action:'delete',before:svc,after:null});else if(JSON.stringify(svc)!==JSON.stringify(n))changes.push({code,action:'update',before:svc,after:n});}
      for(const [code,svc] of newBy)if(!oldBy.has(code))changes.push({code,action:'add',before:null,after:svc});
      archiveTariffVersion(t,eff,'tariff-edit',changes);
    } else if(!Array.isArray(t.changeLog)) t.changeLog=[];
    Object.assign(t,data); if(oldServices===newServices && !dateChanged){t.changeLog.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),effectiveDate:eff,action:'tariff-metadata-edit',actor:(appSettings.reviewer&&appSettings.reviewer.name)||'المستخدم',changes:[]});}
    showSuccess('تم','تم التحديث مع الاحتفاظ بتاريخ الأسعار');
  }
  else { const nid = nextTariffNumber(eff); tariffs.push({ id:nid, ...data, priceVersions:[], changeLog:[{at:new Date().toISOString(),effectiveDate:eff,action:'create',actor:(appSettings.reviewer&&appSettings.reviewer.name)||'المستخدم',changes:(data.services||[]).map(x=>({code:x.code,action:'add',before:null,after:x}))}] }); showSuccess('تم','تمت الإضافة'); }
  closeTariffModal(); renderAll();
}

// providers-4.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function deleteTariffWithDialog(id){
  const t = getTariffById(id); if(!t) return;
  showDelete('حذف اللائحة', `سيتم نقل "${t.name}".`, () => {
    addToTrash('tariff','لائحة', t.name, t);
    tariffs = tariffs.filter(x => x.id !== id);
    if($('#tariffModal').classList.contains('open')) closeTariffModal();
    renderAll(); showSuccess('تم','تم النقل');
  });
}
function deleteClaimWithDialog(id){
  const c = getClaimById(id); if(!c) return;
  const linkedSession = findSessionByClaim(id);
  const claimToTrash = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(c));
  if(linkedSession && Array.isArray(linkedSession.workingInvoices) && linkedSession.workingInvoices.length){
    claimToTrash.invoices = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(linkedSession.workingInvoices));
    claimToTrash.invoices.forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => computePatient(p, claimToTrash, inv.date, inv)));
    claimToTrash.billed = claimToTrash.invoices.reduce((s,inv) => s + (inv.patients||[]).reduce((a,p) => a + (p._subtotal||0), 0), 0);
    claimToTrash.approved = claimToTrash.invoices.reduce((s,inv) => s + (inv.patients||[]).reduce((a,p) => a + (p._final||0), 0), 0);
    if(linkedSession.reviewFormState){
      if(linkedSession.reviewFormState.reason) claimToTrash.reason = linkedSession.reviewFormState.reason;
      if(linkedSession.reviewFormState.notes) claimToTrash.notes = linkedSession.reviewFormState.notes;
      if(claimToTrash.priorApproval && linkedSession.reviewFormState.paStatus){
        claimToTrash.priorApproval.status = linkedSession.reviewFormState.paStatus;
        claimToTrash.priorApproval.refNumber = linkedSession.reviewFormState.paRefNumber || '';
        claimToTrash.priorApproval.date = linkedSession.reviewFormState.paDate || '';
      }
    }
  }
  const msg = linkedSession ? `سيتم نقل "${c.id}" مع تعديلات الجلسة وحذفها.` : `سيتم نقل "${c.id}".`;
  showDelete('حذف', msg, () => {
    addToTrash('claim', 'مطالبة', c.id, claimToTrash);
    claims = claims.filter(x => x.id !== id);
    if(linkedSession){
      workSessions = workSessions.filter(x => x.id !== linkedSession.id);
      if(activeSessionId === linkedSession.id){
        activeSessionId = null;
        $('#reviewModal').classList.remove('open');
        _unlockBodyScroll();
        activeClaim = null; workingInvoices = [];
      }
      persistSessions();
    }
    persistData(true);
    if(currentPage === 'view' && viewState.type === 'claim' && viewState.id === id) goBack();
    renderAll(); showSuccess('تم','تم النقل');
  });
}

// providers.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let providerStatusFilter = 'all', providerSearch = '', editingProviderId = null;
const EGYPT_PROVINCES = ['القاهرة','الجيزة','الإسكندرية','الدقهلية','البحيرة','المنوفية','القليوبية','الشرقية','الغربية','كفر الشيخ','دمياط','بورسعيد','الإسماعيلية','السويس','شمال سيناء','جنوب سيناء','بني سويف','الفيوم','المنيا','أسيوط','سوهاج','قنا','الأقصر','أسوان','البحر الأحمر','مطروح','الوادي الجديد'];
let selectedProvinces = [];
let contractStatusFilter = 'all', contractSearch = '', editingContractId = null;
let tariffFilter = 'all', tariffSearch = '', editingTariffId = null;
let workingServices = [], workingIncreaseHistory = [], editingServiceIndex = null;
function bindProvidersControls(){
  $('#providerSearch').addEventListener('input', e => { providerSearch = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase(); renderProviders(); });
  $('#providerFilterChips').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const chip = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!chip) return;
    $$('#providerFilterChips .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    chip.classList.add('active'); providerStatusFilter = chip.dataset.pfilter; renderProviders();
  });
  $('#addProviderBtn').addEventListener('click', () => openProviderModal());
  $('#providersBody').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const ed = e.target.closest('[data-edit-provider]'); if(ed){ openProviderModal(ed.dataset.editProvider); return; }
    const op = e.target.closest('[data-open-contract]'); if(op){ navigateTo('contracts'); openContractModal(op.dataset.openContract); return; }
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-delete-provider]'); if(del){ deleteProviderWithDialog(del.dataset.deleteProvider); return; }
  });
  $$('[data-close-provider]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', closeProviderModal));
  $('#saveProvider').addEventListener('click', saveProvider);
  const ps = $('#pvProvinceSearch'); if(ps) ps.addEventListener('input', e => renderProvinceOptions(e.target.value));
  $('#deleteProvider').addEventListener('click', () => { if(editingProviderId) deleteProviderWithDialog(editingProviderId); });
}
function renderProviders(){
  const activeN = providers.filter(p => getEffectiveProviderStatus(p) === 'active').length;
  const expN = providers.filter(p => getEffectiveProviderStatus(p) === 'expiring').length;
  let tc = 0, tb = 0, ta = 0;
  providers.forEach(p => { const s = getProviderStats(p.name); tc+=s.claims; tb+=s.billed; ta+=s.approved; });
  const avg = tb ? ((tb-ta)/tb*100) : 0;
  $('#providerKpi').innerHTML = `
    <div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">الجهات</div><div class="kpi-val">${providers.length} <small>جهة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('building')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${activeN} نشط · ${expN} قريب</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k2"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">العقود</div><div class="kpi-val">${contracts.length} <small>عقد</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('contract')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${providers.length} جهة</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k3"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">المطالبات</div><div class="kpi-val">${fmt(tb)} <small>ج.م</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('link')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${tc} مطالبة</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k4"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">متوسط الاستقطاع</div><div class="kpi-val">${avg.toFixed(1)}<small>%</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('cut')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">عبر الجهات</div></div>`;
  const list = providers.filter(p => {
    if(providerStatusFilter !== 'all' && getEffectiveProviderStatus(p) !== providerStatusFilter) return false;
    if(providerSearch){ const q = providerSearch; return p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || p.city.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (p.contact||'').toLowerCase().includes(q); }
    return true;
  });
  const body = $('#providersBody');
  if(!list.length){ body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:36px;color:var(--muted)">لا جهات</td></tr>'; return; }
  body.innerHTML = list.map(p => {
    const eff = getEffectiveProviderStatus(p), cs = CONTRACT_STATUS[eff] || CONTRACT_STATUS.active;
    const st = getProviderStats(p.name);
    const c = getContractByProvider(p.id);
    return `<tr>
      <td data-label="الجهة"><div class="prov-cell"><div class="prov-avatar">${escapeHtml(p.name.charAt(0))}</div><div><div class="prov-name">${escapeHtml(p.name)}</div><div class="prov-city">${escapeHtml(p.city)}</div></div></div></td>
      <td data-label="النوع"><span class="badge ${TYPE_CLASS[p.type]||'info'}">${p.type}</span></td>
      <td data-label="التواصل" class="hide-sm">${escapeHtml(p.contact||'—')}</td>
      <td data-label="العقد"><span class="badge ${cs.cls}">${cs.label}</span>${c?`<div class="ctr-link" data-open-contract="${c.id}">${c.id}</div>`:''}</td>
      <td data-label="المطالبات"><div class="mono">${st.claims} مطالبة</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px">${fmtC(st.approved)}</div></td>
      <td class="act"><div style="display:flex;gap:5px;flex-wrap:nowrap;align-items:center"><button class="btn-xs" data-edit-provider="${p.id}">تعديل</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-delete-provider="${p.id}">×</button></div></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
}
function renderProvinceOptions(q){
  const box = $('#pvProvinceList'); if(!box) return;
  const term = (q||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = EGYPT_PROVINCES.filter(p => !term || p.toLowerCase().includes(term));
  box.innerHTML = list.map(p => `<label style="display:block;margin:2px 0"><input type="checkbox" value="${escapeHtml(p)}" ${selectedProvinces.includes(p)?'checked':''}> ${escapeHtml(p)}</label>`).join('');
  box.querySelectorAll('input[type=checkbox]').forEach(cb => {
    cb.addEventListener('change', () => {
      if(cb.checked){ if(!selectedProvinces.includes(cb.value)) selectedProvinces.push(cb.value); }
      else selectedProvinces = selectedProvinces.filter(x => x !== cb.value);
    });
  });
}
function openProviderModal(id){
  editingProviderId = id || null; const isEdit = !!id;
  const p = isEdit ? getProviderById(id) : null; if(isEdit && !p) return;
  $('#prvModalTitle').textContent = isEdit ? 'تعديل جهة' : 'إضافة جهة';
  $('#prvModalSub').textContent = isEdit ? p.name : 'بيانات جديدة';
  selectedProvinces = isEdit ? (Array.isArray(p.province) ? [...p.province] : (p.city ? p.city.split('، ').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean) : [])) : [];
  if(isEdit){
    $('#pvName').value = p.name; $('#pvType').value = p.type;
    $('#pvContact').value = p.contact||''; $('#pvPhone').value = p.phone||'';
    $('#pvEmail').value = p.email||''; $('#pvNotes').value = p.notes||'';
    (el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'block'; })($('#deleteProvider'));
  } else {
    ['pvName','pvContact','pvPhone','pvEmail','pvNotes'].forEach(x => { const el = $('#'+x); if(el) el.value = ''; });
    $('#pvType').value = 'مستشفى'; (el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'none'; })($('#deleteProvider'));
  }
  const s = $('#pvProvinceSearch'); if(s) s.value = '';
  renderProvinceOptions('');
  $('#providerModal').classList.add('open'); document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
}
function closeProviderModal(){ if(!$('#providerModal').classList.contains('open')) return; $('#providerModal').classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); editingProviderId = null; }
function saveProvider(){
  const name = $('#pvName').value.trim();
  if(!name){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل اسم الجهة'); return; }
  if(providers.find(p => p.name === name && p.id !== editingProviderId)){ showWarning('تنبيه','الاسم مستخدم'); return; }
  const email = $('#pvEmail').value.trim();
  if(!isValidProviderEmail(email)){ showWarning('تنبيه','بريد إلكتروني غير صالح'); return; }
  const phone = $('#pvPhone').value.trim();
  if(!isValidProviderPhone(phone)){ showWarning('تنبيه','رقم هاتف غير صالح'); return; }
  const provinceList = [...selectedProvinces];
  const data = { name, type:$('#pvType').value, province: provinceList, city: provinceList.join('، '), contact:$('#pvContact').value.trim(), phone:$('#pvPhone').value.trim(), email:$('#pvEmail').value.trim(), notes:$('#pvNotes').value.trim() };
  if(editingProviderId){ const p = getProviderById(editingProviderId); if(!p) return; const old = p.name; Object.assign(p, data); if(old !== name) renameProviderClaims(old, name); showSuccess('تم','تم التحديث'); }
  else { const maxN = providers.reduce((m,p)=>{ const n = parseInt(p.id.split('-')[1], 10); return Math.max(m, isNaN(n)?0:n); }, 0); const newId = 'PRV-'+String(maxN+1).padStart(3,'0'); providers.push({ id:newId, ...data }); showSuccess('تم','تمت الإضافة'); }
  closeProviderModal(); renderAll();
  if(document.getElementById('batchModal')?.classList.contains('open')){ const sel = document.getElementById('btProvider'); if(sel && providers.length){ sel.innerHTML = providers.map(p => `<option value="${escapeHtml(p.id)}">${escapeHtml(p.name)}${p.city?' — '+escapeHtml(p.city):''}</option>`).join(''); sel.value = providers[providers.length-1].id; } }
}
function deleteProviderWithDialog(id){
  const p = getProviderById(id); if(!p) return;
  if(providerHasClaims(p.name)){ showWarning('لا يمكن','مرتبطة بمطالبات'); return; }
  const lc = contracts.filter(c => c.providerId === id);
  const lb = batches.filter(b => b.providerId === id);
  showDelete('حذف الجهة', `سيتم نقل "${p.name}" و${lc.length+lb.length} عنصر.`, () => {
    addToTrash('provider','جهة', p.name, p);
    providers = providers.filter(x => x.id !== id);
    lc.forEach(c => addToTrash('contract','عقد', c.id, c));
    contracts = contracts.filter(c => c.providerId !== id);
    lb.forEach(b => addToTrash('batch','دفعة', b.id, b));
    batches = batches.filter(b => b.providerId !== id);
    tariffs.forEach(t => { t.linkedProviderIds = (t.linkedProviderIds||[]).filter(pid => pid !== id); });
    if($('#providerModal').classList.contains('open')) closeProviderModal();
    renderAll(); showSuccess('تم','تم النقل');
  });
}
function bindContractsControls(){

    const chip = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!chip) return;
    $$('#contractFilterChips .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    chip.classList.add('active'); contractStatusFilter = chip.dataset.cfilter; renderContracts();
  });
  $('#addContractBtn').addEventListener('click', () => openContractModal());

    const del = e.target.closest('[data-delete-contract]'); if(del) deleteContractWithDialog(del.dataset.deleteContract);
  });
  $$('[data-close-contract]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', closeContractModal));
  $('#saveContract').addEventListener('click', saveContract);
  const cs = $('#ctrServicesSearch'); if(cs) cs.addEventListener('input', e => renderContractServicesOptions(e.target.value));
  $('#deleteContract').addEventListener('click', () => { if(editingContractId) deleteContractWithDialog(editingContractId); });
}

// render-2.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function renderClaims(){
  const _prevSel = new Set($$('#claimsBody .claim-cb:checked').map(cb => cb.dataset.id));
  const list = claims.filter(c => {
    if(currentFilter === 'pa-required'){ if(!(c.priorApproval && c.priorApproval.required && c.priorApproval.status !== 'approved')) return false; }
    else if(currentFilter !== 'all' && c.status !== currentFilter) return false;
    if(currentSearch){
      const q = currentSearch;
      if(c.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.patient.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
         c.provider.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (c.receivedNumber||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)) return true;
      const lines = (c.invoices||[]).flatMap(inv => (inv.patients||[]).flatMap(p => p.lines||[]));
      if(lines.some(l => (l.code||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (l.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q))) return true;
      const nids = (c.invoices||[]).flatMap(inv => (inv.patients||[]).map(p => p.nationalId));
      if(nids.some(nid => (nid||'').toLowerCase().includes(q))) return true;
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
  const body = $('#claimsBody');
  if(!list.length){ body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="9" style="text-align:center;padding:36px;color:var(--muted)">لا مطالبات</td></tr>'; _resetClaimsBulk(); return; }
  body.innerHTML = list.map(c => {
    const st = STATUS[c.status] || STATUS.pending;
    let paBadge = '';
    if(c.priorApproval && c.priorApproval.required){
      if(c.priorApproval.status === 'approved') paBadge = ' <span class="badge pa-approved sm">✓</span>';
      else if(c.priorApproval.status === 'pending') paBadge = ' <span class="badge pa-pending sm">⏳</span>';
      else if(c.priorApproval.status === 'rejected') paBadge = ' <span class="badge pa-rejected sm">✗</span>';
      else paBadge = ' <span class="badge pa-req sm">⚠</span>';
    }
    return `<tr>
      <td style="width:36px"><input type="checkbox" class="claim-cb" data-id="${escapeHtml(c.id)}"></td>
      <td data-label="رقم / وارد"><span class="clm-id">${escapeHtml(c.id)}</span><div style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--teal);font-weight:700;margin-top:2px">وارد: ${escapeHtml(c.receivedNumber||'—')}</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px">${(c.invoices||[]).length} فاتورة</div></td>
      <td data-label="المريض">${escapeHtml(c.patient)}${paBadge}</td>
      <td data-label="الجهة" class="hide-sm">${escapeHtml(c.provider)}</td>
      <td data-label="التواريخ" class="hide-sm" style="font-size:11.5px;line-height:1.6"><div style="color:var(--teal);font-weight:800">وارد: ${escapeHtml(c.receivedDate||'—')}</div><div style="color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">مطالبة: ${escapeHtml(c.date||'—')}</div></td>
      <td data-label="المطالب" class="mono">${fmt(c.billed)}</td>
      <td data-label="المعتمد" class="mono big">${fmt(c.approved)}</td>
      <td data-label="الحالة"><span class="badge ${st.cls}">${st.label}</span></td>
      <td class="act"><div style="display:flex;gap:5px;flex-wrap:nowrap;align-items:center"><button class="btn-xs view" data-view="${escapeHtml(c.id)}">👁</button><button class="btn-xs" data-review="${escapeHtml(c.id)}">مراجعة</button><button class="btn-xs" data-add-memo="${escapeHtml(c.id)}" title="إضافة مذكرة خصم">+مذكرة</button><button class="btn-xs tl" data-timeline="${escapeHtml(c.id)}">📅</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-delete-claim="${escapeHtml(c.id)}">×</button></div></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
  $$('#claimsBody .claim-cb').forEach(cb => { if(_prevSel.has(cb.dataset.id)) cb.checked = true; });
  updateClaimsBulkBar();
}
function openView(type, id){
  if(currentPage === 'view'){
    navHistory.push({ page: 'view', viewType: viewState.type, viewId: viewState.id });
  } else {
    navigateTo('view');
  }
  viewState = { type, id };
  renderView();
}
function renderView(){
  const { type, id } = viewState;
  let html = '';
  if(type === 'claim'){
    const c = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(getClaimById(id)))
    if(!c){ $('#viewContent').innerHTML = '<div class="panel"><div class="panel-body" style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--muted)">غير موجود</div></div>'; return; }
    const st = STATUS[c.status] || STATUS.pending;
    const invoicesHtml = (c.invoices||[]).map(inv => {
      const t = INVOICE_TYPES[inv.type] || {label:inv.type};
      const tot = invoiceTotals(inv);
      const patHtml = (inv.patients||[]).map(p => {
        computePatient(p, c, inv.date, inv);
        return `<div style="margin-top:8px;padding-top:8px;border-top:1px dashed var(--line)">
          <div style="font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:6px">👤 ${escapeHtml(p.name)}${p.beneficiaryType==='retiree'?' <span class="badge retiree sm">متقاعد</span>':''}</div>
          ${(p.lines||[]).map(l => `<div class="view-line"><span class="mono" style="color:var(--navy-400);font-weight:700;min-width:70px">${escapeHtml(l.code)}</span><span style="flex:1">${escapeHtml(l.name)}</span><span class="mono" style="color:var(--muted)">${l.qty}×</span><span class="mono">${fmt(l.qty*l.billedUnit)}</span><span class="mono" style="color:var(--ok);font-weight:800;min-width:70px;text-align:left">${fmt(Number(l.approved)||0)}</span></div>`).join('')}
          <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;padding:6px 8px;background:#EFF6F6;border-radius:7px;margin-top:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">
            <span>إجمالي: <b>${fmtC(p._subtotal)}</b></span>
            ${p._capAmt?`<span style="color:var(--bad)">سقف: ${fmtC(p._capAmt)}</span>`:''}
            ${p._copayAmt?`<span style="color:var(--warn)">تحمل: ${fmtC(p._copayAmt)}</span>`:''}
            <span style="color:var(--ok)">المعتمد: ${fmtC(p._final)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>`;
      }).join('');
      return `<div class="view-invoice">
        <div class="view-invoice-head">
          <div><b>${escapeHtml(t.label||'غير محدد')}</b> <span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11.5px">· ${escapeHtml(inv.number||inv.id||'—')} · ${escapeHtml(inv.date||'')}</span></div>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700">
            <span style="color:var(--muted)">مطالب: ${fmtC(tot.billed)}</span>
            <span style="color:var(--ok)">معتمد: ${fmtC(tot.approved)}</span>
            <span style="color:var(--bad)">استقطاع: ${fmtC(tot.deduction)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        ${patHtml || '<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">لا مرضى</div>'}
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
    html = `
      <div class="view-header">
        <div><div class="view-title">${escapeHtml(c.patient)}</div><div class="view-sub">${escapeHtml(c.id)} • وارد ${escapeHtml(c.receivedNumber||'—')}</div></div>
        <div class="view-actions">
          <button class="btn-export pdf" data-export="viewClaim" data-format="pdf"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M14 2H6.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 4 4.5v15A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 6.5 22h11a2.5 2.5 0 0 0 2.5-2.5V8z"/><path d="M14 2v6h6"/><path d="M8 13h4M8 17h8"/></svg></button>
          <button class="btn-export print" data-export="viewClaim" data-format="print"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 9V2h12v7"/><path d="M6 18H4a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h16a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v5a2 2 0 0 1-2 2h-2"/><rect x="6" y="14" width="12" height="8" rx="1"/></svg></button>
          <button class="btn-sm" data-view-action="review" data-id="${escapeHtml(c.id)}">مراجعة</button>
          <button class="btn-xs danger" data-view-action="delete-claim" data-id="${escapeHtml(c.id)}">حذف</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="view-grid">
        <div class="view-card"><div class="view-card-head"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"><path d="M14 2H6.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 4 4.5v15"/></svg><h4>بيانات</h4></div>
          <div class="view-card-body">
            <div class="view-row"><span class="k">رقم</span><span class="v mono">${escapeHtml(c.id)}</span></div>
            <div class="view-row"><span class="k">وارد</span><span class="v mono" style="color:var(--teal)">${escapeHtml(c.receivedNumber||'—')}</span></div>
            <div class="view-row"><span class="k">وارد في</span><span class="v mono">${escapeHtml(c.receivedDate||'—')}</span></div>
            <div class="view-row"><span class="k">تاريخ</span><span class="v mono">${escapeHtml(c.date||'—')}</span></div>
            <div class="view-row"><span class="k">الجهة</span><span class="v">${escapeHtml(c.provider)}</span></div>
            <div class="view-row"><span class="k">نوع</span><span class="v">${c.type==='retiree'?'متقاعد':'عامل'}</span></div>
            <div class="view-row"><span class="k">الحالة</span><span class="v"><span class="badge ${st.cls}">${st.label}</span></span></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="view-card"><div class="view-card-head"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"><rect x="2.5" y="6" width="19" height="12" rx="2.5"/></svg><h4>مالية</h4></div>
          <div class="view-card-body">
            <div class="view-row"><span class="k">المطالب</span><span class="v mono" style="color:var(--navy);font-size:15px">${fmtC(c.billed)}</span></div>
            <div class="view-row"><span class="k">المعتمد</span><span class="v mono" style="color:var(--ok);font-size:15px">${fmtC(c.approved)}</span></div>
            <div class="view-row"><span class="k">الاستقطاع</span><span class="v mono" style="color:var(--bad)">${fmtC(c.billed-c.approved)}</span></div>
            <div class="view-row"><span class="k">النسبة</span><span class="v mono">${c.billed ? ((c.billed-c.approved)/c.billed*100).toFixed(1) : '0'}%</span></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="view-card" style="grid-column:1/-1;margin-bottom:18px">
        <div class="view-card-head"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"><path d="M14 2H6.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 4 4.5v15"/></svg><h4>الفواتير (${(c.invoices||[]).length})</h4></div>
        <div class="view-card-body">${invoicesHtml || '<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12.5px">لا فواتير</div>'}</div>
      </div>
    `;
  } else {
    html = '<div class="panel"><div class="panel-body" style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--muted)">قيد التطوير</div></div>';
  }
  $('#viewContent').innerHTML = html;
  $$('#viewContent [data-view-action]').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const a = btn.dataset.viewAction, cid = btn.dataset.id;
      if(a === 'review') openReview(cid);
      else if(a === 'delete-claim') deleteClaimWithDialog(cid);
    });
  });
}
let currentTimelineClaimId = null;
function openTimeline(claimId){
  const c = getClaimById(claimId); if(!c){ showWarning('خطأ','غير موجود'); return; }
  currentTimelineClaimId = claimId; navigateTo('timeline'); renderTimeline();
}
function timelineIcon(name){
  const icons = {
    inbox:'<path d="M21 15v4a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-4"/><path d="M17 8l-5-5-5 5"/><path d="M12 3v12"/>',
    check:'<path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"/>',
    x:'<path d="M18 6 6 18M6 6l12 12"/>',
    clock:'<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 7v5l3 2"/>',
    alert:'<path d="M10.29 3.86 1.82 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.71 3h16.94a2 2 0 0 0 1.71-3L13.71 3.86a2 2 0 0 0-3.42 0z"/><path d="M12 9v4M12 17h.01"/>',
    file:'<path d="M14 2H6.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 4 4.5v15"/><path d="M14 2v6h6"/>',
    calendar:'<rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><path d="M16 2v4M8 2v4M3 10h18"/>'
  };
  return `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24">${icons[name]||''}</svg>`;
}

// render-3.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function buildClaimTimeline(claim){
  const events = [];
  if(claim.receivedDate) events.push({ date: claim.receivedDate, type:'received', icon:'inbox', title:'استلام المطالبة', description:`من ${claim.provider} برقم ${claim.receivedNumber||'—'}` });
  (claim.invoices||[]).forEach(inv => {
    const t = INVOICE_TYPES[inv.type] || {label:inv.type};
    const tot = invoiceTotals(inv);
    events.push({ date: inv.date || claim.receivedDate || claim.date, type:'note', icon:'file', title:`فاتورة — ${t.label}`, description:`${(inv.patients||[]).length} مريض · ${allInvoiceLines(inv).length} بند · المطالب ${fmtC(tot.billed)}`, refNumber: inv.number||inv.id });
  });
  if(claim.priorApproval && claim.priorApproval.required){
    const pa = claim.priorApproval;
    if(pa.status === 'approved') events.push({ date: pa.date||claim.receivedDate, type:'pa-approved', icon:'check', title:'الموافقة صادرة', refNumber: pa.refNumber });
    else if(pa.status === 'rejected') events.push({ date: pa.date||claim.receivedDate, type:'pa-rejected', icon:'x', title:'رفض الموافقة', refNumber: pa.refNumber });
    else if(pa.status === 'pending') events.push({ date: pa.date||claim.receivedDate, type:'pa-pending', icon:'clock', title:'قيد الدراسة', refNumber: pa.refNumber });
    else events.push({ date: claim.receivedDate, type:'note', icon:'alert', title:'موافقة غير مطلوبة' });
  }
  // قرارات البنود
  (claim.invoices||[]).forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(pat => (pat.lines||[]).forEach(l => {
    if(l._decidedAt){
      const map = { 'manual-approved':'اعتماد بند', 'manual-reduced':'تخفيض بند', 'manual-rejected':'رفض بند', 'pa-pending':'بانتظار PA' };
      const title = map[l._decision] || l._decision || 'قرار بند';
      events.push({ date: l._decidedAt.slice(0,10), type:'note', icon:'file', title:`${title} — ${l.code}`, description: l._decisionNote || '' });
    }
  })));
  if(claim._overrideChecklist && claim._overrideChecklist.length){
    claim._overrideChecklist.forEach(o => events.push({ date: (o.at||'').slice(0,10), type:'note', icon:'alert', title:'تجاوز الفحوصات', description: o.reason }));
  }
  if(claim.status !== 'pending'){
    const sm = { approved:{type:'review-approved',icon:'check',title:'اعتماد كامل'}, partial:{type:'review-partial',icon:'file',title:'اعتماد جزئي'}, rejected:{type:'review-rejected',icon:'x',title:'رفض'} };
    const s = sm[claim.status];
    let desc = `المطالب: ${fmtC(claim.billed)} — المعتمد: ${fmtC(claim.approved)}`;
    if(claim.reason) desc += ` — السبب: ${claim.reason}`;
    events.push({ date: claim.reviewedDate||claim.date, type:s.type, icon:s.icon, title:s.title, description:desc });
  } else events.push({ date: claim.reviewedDate||claim.receivedDate||claim.date, type:'review-pending', icon:'clock', title:'قيد المراجعة' });
  if(claim.notes && claim.notes.trim()) events.push({ date: claim.reviewedDate||claim.date, type:'note', icon:'file', title:'ملاحظات', description: claim.notes });
  return events.sort((a,b)=> new Date(a.date) - new Date(b.date));
}
function renderTimeline(){
  if(!currentTimelineClaimId) return;
  const claim = getClaimById(currentTimelineClaimId); if(!claim) return;
  const _pd = document.getElementById('pageDesc'); if(_pd) _pd.textContent = `${claim.patient} • ${claim.provider}`;
  $('#tlHeaderTitle').textContent = `${claim.id} — ${claim.patient}`;
  $('#tlHeaderSub').textContent = claim.provider;
  const st = STATUS[claim.status] || STATUS.pending;
  let paBadge = '<span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">لا تتطلب</span>';
  if(claim.priorApproval && claim.priorApproval.required){ const ps = PA_STATUS[claim.priorApproval.status] || PA_STATUS['not-requested']; paBadge = `<span class="badge ${ps.cls}">${ps.label}</span>`; }
  const daysSince = daysBetween(claim.receivedDate, daysFromNow(0));
  $('#tlSummary').innerHTML = `
    <div class="tl-summary-item"><span>الوارد</span><b style="color:var(--teal)">${escapeHtml(claim.receivedNumber||'—')}</b></div>
    <div class="tl-summary-item"><span>تاريخ الوارد</span><b>${escapeHtml(claim.receivedDate||'—')}</b></div>
    <div class="tl-summary-item"><span>تاريخ المطالبة</span><b>${escapeHtml(claim.date||'—')}</b></div>
    <div class="tl-summary-item"><span>الحالة</span><b><span class="badge ${st.cls}">${st.label}</span></b></div>
    <div class="tl-summary-item"><span>الموافقة</span><b>${paBadge}</b></div>
    <div class="tl-summary-item"><span>منذ الاستلام</span><b>${daysSince>=0?daysSince+' يوم':'—'}</b></div>
  `;
  const events = buildClaimTimeline(claim);
  $('#tlCount').textContent = `${events.length} حدث`;
  $('#timelineList').innerHTML = events.map((ev,i) => `
    <div class="tl-event ${escapeHtml(ev.type)}" style="animation-delay:${i*60}ms">
      <div class="tl-dot ${escapeHtml(ev.type)}">${timelineIcon(ev.icon)}</div>
      <div class="tl-card">
        <div class="tl-date">${timelineIcon('calendar')} ${escapeHtml(ev.date||'—')}</div>
        <div class="tl-title">${escapeHtml(ev.title)}</div>
        <div class="tl-desc">${escapeHtml(ev.description||'')}</div>
        ${ev.refNumber?`<div class="tl-ref">مرجع: ${escapeHtml(ev.refNumber)}</div>`:''}
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}
let srchTerm = '', srchTariffId = 'all', srchFilter = 'all', srchPage = 1, srchPerPage = 12, srchDebounce = null;
function bindSearchControls(){
  $('#srchQuery').addEventListener('input', e => { clearTimeout(srchDebounce); srchDebounce = setTimeout(() => { srchTerm = e.target.value.trim(); srchPage = 1; renderSearch(); }, 220); });
  $('#srchTariff').addEventListener('change', e => { srchTariffId = e.target.value; srchPage = 1; renderSearch(); });
  $('#srchPerPage').addEventListener('change', e => { srchPerPage = parseInt(e.target.value, 10); srchPage = 1; renderSearch(); });
  $('#srchChips').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const chip = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!chip) return;
    $$('#srchChips .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    chip.classList.add('active'); srchFilter = chip.dataset.srch; srchPage = 1; renderSearch();
  });
  $('#srchPagination').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const btn = e.target.closest('.page-btn'); if(!btn || btn.disabled) return;
    const p = parseInt(btn.dataset.page, 10); if(!isNaN(p)){ srchPage = p; renderSearch(); window.scrollTo({top:120,behavior:'smooth'}); }
  });
}
function getSearchResults(){
  const results = []; const q = srchTerm.toLowerCase();
  const checkTariff = srchTariffId !== 'all';
  tariffs.forEach(t => {
    if(checkTariff && t.id !== srchTariffId) return;
    (t.services||[]).forEach(s => {
      if(srchFilter === 'covered' && !s.covered) return;
      if(srchFilter === 'pa' && !s.requiresPriorApproval) return;
      if(q && !s.code.toLowerCase().includes(q) && !s.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) && !s.category.toLowerCase().includes(q)) return;
      results.push({ ...s, tariffId:t.id, tariffName:t.name, tariffType:t.type });
    });
  });
  return results;
}
function renderSearch(){
  const sel = $('#srchTariff');
  const prev = sel.value;
  sel.innerHTML = '<option value="all">جميع اللوائح</option>' + tariffs.map(t => `<option value="${escapeHtml(t.id)}">${escapeHtml(t.name)} — ${escapeHtml(TARIFF_TYPES[t.type].label)}</option>`).join('');
  if(prev && [...sel.options].some(o=>o.value===prev)) sel.value = prev;
  const results = getSearchResults();
  const total = results.length;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(total/srchPerPage) || 1;
  if(srchPage > totalPages) srchPage = totalPages;
  if(srchPage < 1) srchPage = 1;
  const start = (srchPage-1) * srchPerPage;
  const pageResults = results.slice(start, start + srchPerPage);
  const cov = results.filter(r=>r.covered).length;
  const paN = results.filter(r=>r.requiresPriorApproval).length;
  $('#srchStatsBar').innerHTML = `<span>إجمالي: <b>${total}</b></span><span>مغطاة: <b>${cov}</b></span><span>موافقة: <b>${paN}</b></span>`;
  $('#srchCount').textContent = srchTariffId === 'all' ? `${total} خدمة` : `${total} خدمة في اللائحة`;
  if(!pageResults.length){ $('#srchResults').innerHTML = '<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--muted)">لا نتائج</div>'; $('#srchPagination').innerHTML = ''; return; }
  $('#srchResults').innerHTML = pageResults.map(s => {
    const tt = TARIFF_TYPES[s.tariffType];
    return `<div class="service-card">
      <div class="sc-head"><span class="sc-code">${escapeHtml(s.code)}</span>${s.requiresPriorApproval?'<span class="badge pa-req sm">⚠</span>':''}</div>
      <div class="sc-name">${escapeHtml(s.name)}</div>
      <div class="sc-category">${escapeHtml(s.category)}</div>
      <div class="sc-prices"><div class="sc-price-item"><span>أساسي</span><b>${fmt(s.basePrice)}</b></div><div class="sc-price-item current"><span>حالي</span><b>${fmt(s.currentPrice)}</b></div></div>
      <div class="sc-badges">${s.covered?'<span class="badge ok sm">مغطاة</span>':'<span class="badge bad sm">غير مغطاة</span>'}</div>
      <div class="sc-source"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M14 2H6.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 4 4.5v15"/></svg><div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><div style="font-size:10px;margin-bottom:2px">مصدر</div><b style="display:block;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis">${escapeHtml(s.tariffName)}</b></div><span class="badge ${tt.cls} sm">${escapeHtml(tt.label)}</span></div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
  if(totalPages > 1){
    const btns = [`<button class="page-btn" data-page="${srchPage-1}" ${srchPage===1?'disabled':''}>‹</button>`];
    const pages = [];
    for(let i=1;i<=totalPages;i++){ if(i===1 || i===totalPages || Math.abs(i-srchPage)<=2) pages.push(i); else if(pages[pages.length-1] !== '...') pages.push('...'); }
    pages.forEach(p => { if(p==='...') btns.push('<span class="page-info">...</span>'); else btns.push(`<button class="page-btn ${p===srchPage?'active':''}" data-page="${p}">${p}</button>`); });
    btns.push(`<button class="page-btn" data-page="${srchPage+1}" ${srchPage===totalPages?'disabled':''}>›</button>`);
    $('#srchPagination').innerHTML = btns.join('');
  } else $('#srchPagination').innerHTML = '';
}

// render-4.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function renderReports(){
  let base = claims;
  if(reportsSearchTerm){
    const q = reportsSearchTerm.toLowerCase();
    base = claims.filter(c => c.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.patient.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.provider.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    $('#reportsSearchInfo').innerHTML = `عرض ${base.length} من ${claims.length}`;
  } else $('#reportsSearchInfo').innerHTML = '';
  const s = statsFor(base);
  const vTotal = base.reduce((s,c)=> s + (c.violationDeduction||0), 0);
  $('#reportKpi').innerHTML = `
    <div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">صافي</div><div class="kpi-val">${fmt(s.approved)} <small>ج.م</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('check')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">من ${fmtC(s.billed)}</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k2"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">نسبة</div><div class="kpi-val">${s.rate.toFixed(1)}<small>%</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('cut')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">متوسط</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k3"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">الاستقطاع</div><div class="kpi-val">${fmt(s.deduction)} <small>ج.م</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('money')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">فروقات</div></div>
    <div class="kpi k4"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">مخالفة</div><div class="kpi-val">${fmt(vTotal)} <small>ج.م</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('shield')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">سقف + تحمل</div></div>`;
  const total = s.total || 1;
  const statusData = [{label:'معتمدة',n:s.approvedN,color:'#059669'},{label:'جزئياً',n:s.partialN,color:'#2563EB'},{label:'قيد',n:s.pending,color:'#D97706'},{label:'مرفوضة',n:s.rejectedN,color:'#DC2626'}];
  $('#statusBars').innerHTML = statusData.map(i => `<div class="bar-row"><div class="bar-top"><span>${i.label}</span><b>${i.n} مطالبة</b></div><div class="bar-track"><div class="bar-fill" style="width:${Math.max(3,i.n/total*100)}%;background:${i.color}"></div></div></div>`).join('');
  $('#summaryList').innerHTML = `
    <div class="info-row"><span class="k">المطالبات</span><span class="v">${s.total}</span></div>
    <div class="info-row"><span class="k">المطالب</span><span class="v">${fmtC(s.billed)}</span></div>
    <div class="info-row"><span class="k">المعتمد</span><span class="v" style="color:var(--ok)">${fmtC(s.approved)}</span></div>
    <div class="info-row"><span class="k">الاستقطاع</span><span class="v" style="color:var(--bad)">${fmtC(s.deduction)}</span></div>
    <div class="info-row"><span class="k">الاعتماد</span><span class="v" style="color:var(--teal)">${(100-s.rate).toFixed(1)}%</span></div>`;
  const rData = reasonsBreakdownFor(base);
  const rBox = $('#reasonBars2');
  if(!rData.length) rBox.innerHTML = '<p style="color:var(--muted);text-align:center;padding:10px">لا استقطاعات</p>';
  else { const max = Math.max(...rData.map(d=>d[1])); rBox.innerHTML = rData.map(([n,v]) => `<div class="bar-row"><div class="bar-top"><span>${escapeHtml(n)}</span><b>${fmtC(v)}</b></div><div class="bar-track"><div class="bar-fill" style="width:${Math.max(4,v/max*100)}%"></div></div></div>`).join(''); }
}

// render.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function kpiIcon(name){
  const svgs = {
    file:'<path d="M14 2H6.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 4 4.5v15A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 6.5 22h11a2.5 2.5 0 0 0 2.5-2.5V8z"/><path d="M14 2v6h6"/><path d="M8 13h8M8 17h5"/>',
    money:'<rect x="2.5" y="6" width="19" height="12" rx="2.5"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2.6"/><path d="M6 10v4M18 10v4"/>',
    cut:'<circle cx="6.5" cy="6.5" r="2.8"/><circle cx="6.5" cy="17.5" r="2.8"/><path d="M20 4 8.6 15.4M8.6 8.6 20 20"/>',
    check:'<path d="M22 11.1V12a10 10 0 1 1-5.9-9.1"/><path d="M22 4 12 14.1l-3-3"/>',
    building:'<path d="M3 21h18M4 21V10l8-6 8 6v11"/><path d="M9 21v-5h6v5"/><path d="M12 4v4M10 6h4"/>',
    contract:'<path d="M14 2H6.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 4 4.5v15A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 6.5 22h11a2.5 2.5 0 0 0 2.5-2.5V8z"/><path d="M14 2v6h6"/><path d="M9 13l2 2 4-4"/>',
    link:'<path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/>',
    tariff:'<path d="M12 2 L22 7 L22 17 L12 22 L2 17 L2 7 Z"/><path d="M12 8v4M12 16h.01"/>',
    inbox:'<path d="M21 15v4a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-4"/><path d="M17 8l-5-5-5 5"/><path d="M12 3v12"/>',
    shield:'<path d="M12 2 L20 6 V12 C20 17 16 20.5 12 22 C8 20.5 4 17 4 12 V6 Z"/><path d="M9 12l2 2 4-4"/>',
    trash:'<path d="M3 6h18M8 6V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h4a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v2"/><path d="M19 6v14a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H7a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V6"/><path d="M10 11v6M14 11v6"/>'
  };
  return `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">${svgs[name]||''}</svg>`;
}
let dashSearchTerm = '', reportsSearchTerm = '';
let currentFilter = 'all', currentSearch = '';
function renderKPIs(){
  const s = stats();
  const paCount = claims.filter(c => c.priorApproval && c.priorApproval.required && c.priorApproval.status !== 'approved').length;
  const cards = [
    {cls:'', icon:'file', label:t('kpi.totalClaims') || 'إجمالي المطالبات', val:s.total, unit:'مطالبة', foot:`${s.pending} قيد · ${paCount} تحتاج موافقة`},
    {cls:'k2', icon:'money', label:t('kpi.billed') || 'المطالب به', val:fmt(s.billed), unit:'ج.م', foot:'إجمالي'},
    {cls:'k3', icon:'cut', label:t('kpi.deductions') || 'الاستقطاعات', val:fmt(s.deduction), unit:'ج.م', foot:'الفروقات'},
    {cls:'k4', icon:'check', label:t('kpi.approved') || 'المعتمد', val:fmt(s.approved), unit:'ج.م', foot:`${(100-s.rate).toFixed(1)}% اعتماد`}
  ];
  $('#kpiGrid').innerHTML = cards.map(c => `<div class="kpi ${c.cls}"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">${c.label}</div><div class="kpi-val">${c.val} <small>${c.unit}</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon(c.icon)}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${c.foot}</div></div>`).join('');
}
function renderRecent(){
  let base = claims;
  if(dashSearchTerm){
    const q = dashSearchTerm.toLowerCase();
    base = claims.filter(c => c.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.patient.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.provider.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (c.receivedNumber||'').toLowerCase().includes(q));
    $('#dashSearchInfo').textContent = `${base.length} نتيجة`;
  } else $('#dashSearchInfo').textContent = 'آخر 5';
  const display = dashSearchTerm ? base.slice(0,10) : base.slice(0,5);
  if(!display.length){ $('#recentBody').innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align:center;padding:22px;color:var(--muted)">لا مطالبات بعد. ابدأ باستلام مطالبة وارد.</td></tr>'; return; }
  $('#recentBody').innerHTML = display.map(c => {
    const st = STATUS[c.status] || STATUS.pending;
    return `<tr style="cursor:pointer" data-view="${escapeHtml(c.id)}"><td data-label="رقم"><span class="clm-id">${escapeHtml(c.id)}</span><div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--teal);font-weight:700;margin-top:2px">${escapeHtml(c.receivedNumber||'—')}</div></td><td data-label="المريض">${escapeHtml(c.patient)}</td><td data-label="المطالب" class="mono">${fmt(c.billed)}</td><td data-label="المعتمد" class="mono big">${fmt(c.approved)}</td><td data-label="الحالة"><span class="badge ${st.cls}">${st.label}</span></td></tr>`;
  }).join('');
}
function renderDonut(){
  const s = stats(); const C = 2*Math.PI*66; const pct = Math.min(100,Math.max(0,s.rate)); const arc = C*pct/100;
  setTimeout(() => { $('#donutArc').setAttribute('stroke-dasharray', `${arc} ${C}`); }, 120);
  $('#donutPct').textContent = pct.toFixed(1)+'%';
  const total = s.total || 1;
  const items = [{label:'معتمدة',n:s.approvedN,color:'#059669'},{label:'جزئية',n:s.partialN,color:'#2563EB'},{label:'قيد',n:s.pending,color:'#D97706'},{label:'مرفوضة',n:s.rejectedN,color:'#DC2626'}];
  $('#legendBox').innerHTML = items.map(i => `<div class="legend-row"><span class="sw" style="background:${i.color}"></span><span class="lbl">${i.label}</span><span class="val">${i.n} (${Math.round(i.n/total*100)}%)</span></div>`).join('');
}
function renderReasonBars(sel){
  const data = reasonsBreakdown(); const box = $(sel); if(!box) return;
  if(!data.length){ box.innerHTML = '<p style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px;text-align:center;padding:10px">لا استقطاعات</p>'; return; }
  const max = Math.max(...data.map(d=>d[1]));
  box.innerHTML = data.map(([n,v]) => `<div class="bar-row"><div class="bar-top"><span>${escapeHtml(n)}</span><b>${fmtC(v)}</b></div><div class="bar-track"><div class="bar-fill" style="width:${Math.max(4,v/max*100)}%"></div></div></div>`).join('');
}
function updateClaimsBulkBar(){
  const n = $$('#claimsBody .claim-cb:checked').length;
  const cnt = document.getElementById('claimsBulkCount');
  if(cnt) cnt.textContent = n;
  const bar = document.getElementById('claimsBulkBar');
  if(bar) bar.classList.toggle('show', n > 0);
  const all = $$('#claimsBody .claim-cb');
  const sa = document.getElementById('claimsSelectAll');
  if(sa) sa.checked = n > 0 && n === all.length;
}
function bindClaimsControls(){
  $('#searchInput').addEventListener('input', e => { currentSearch = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase(); renderClaims(); });
  $('#filterChips').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const chip = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!chip) return;
    $$('#filterChips .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    chip.classList.add('active'); currentFilter = chip.dataset.filter; renderClaims();
  });
  const recentBody = document.getElementById('recentBody');
  if(recentBody) recentBody.addEventListener('click', e => { const vw = e.target.closest('[data-view]'); if(vw){ openView('claim', vw.dataset.view); } });
  $('#claimsBody').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const rv = e.target.closest('[data-review]'); if(rv){ openReview(rv.dataset.review); return; }
    const am = e.target.closest('[data-add-memo]'); if(am){ const cid = am.dataset.addMemo; openReview(cid); setTimeout(() => { try { openInvoiceEditor(); } catch(e){} }, 600); return; }
    const tl = e.target.closest('[data-timeline]'); if(tl){ openTimeline(tl.dataset.timeline); return; }
    const vw = e.target.closest('[data-view]'); if(vw){ openView('claim', vw.dataset.view); return; }
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-delete-claim]'); if(del){ deleteClaimWithDialog(del.dataset.deleteClaim); return; }
  });
  const updateBulk = updateClaimsBulkBar;
  $('#claimsSelectAll').addEventListener('change', () => { $$('#claimsBody .claim-cb').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = $('#claimsSelectAll').checked; }); updateBulk(); });
  $('#claimsBody').addEventListener('change', e => { if(e.target.classList.contains('claim-cb')) updateBulk(); });
  $('#claimsBulkReview').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const first = $$('#claimsBody .claim-cb:checked')[0];
    if(first) openReview(first.dataset.id);
  });
  $('#claimsBulkDelete').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const ids = $$('#claimsBody .claim-cb:checked').map(cb => cb.dataset.id);
    if(!ids.length) return;
    const linked = ids.filter(id => findSessionByClaim(id)).length;
    showDelete('حذف مطالبات', `سيتم نقل ${ids.length} مطالبة${linked?' و'+linked+' جلسة':''}.`, () => {
      ids.forEach(id => {
        const c = getClaimById(id); if(!c) return;
        const ls = findSessionByClaim(id);
        const toTrash = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(c));
        if(ls && Array.isArray(ls.workingInvoices) && ls.workingInvoices.length){
          toTrash.invoices = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ls.workingInvoices));
          toTrash.invoices.forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => computePatient(p, toTrash, inv.date, inv)));
          toTrash.billed = toTrash.invoices.reduce((s,inv) => s + (inv.patients||[]).reduce((a,p) => a + (p._subtotal||0), 0), 0);
          toTrash.approved = toTrash.invoices.reduce((s,inv) => s + (inv.patients||[]).reduce((a,p) => a + (p._final||0), 0), 0);
          if(ls.reviewFormState){
            if(ls.reviewFormState.reason) toTrash.reason = ls.reviewFormState.reason;
            if(ls.reviewFormState.notes) toTrash.notes = ls.reviewFormState.notes;
            if(toTrash.priorApproval && ls.reviewFormState.paStatus){
              toTrash.priorApproval.status = ls.reviewFormState.paStatus;
              toTrash.priorApproval.refNumber = ls.reviewFormState.paRefNumber || '';
              toTrash.priorApproval.date = ls.reviewFormState.paDate || '';
            }
          }
        }
        addToTrash('claim','مطالبة', c.id, toTrash);
        if(ls){
          workSessions = workSessions.filter(x => x.id !== ls.id);
          if(activeSessionId === ls.id){ activeSessionId = null; activeClaim = null; workingInvoices = []; }
        }
      });
      claims = claims.filter(c => !ids.includes(c.id));
      persistSessions(); persistData(true);
      renderAll(); showSuccess('تم', `تم نقل ${ids.length} مطالبة`);
    });
  });
  $('#claimsBulkClear').addEventListener('click', () => { $$('#claimsBody .claim-cb').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = false; }); updateBulk(); });
}
function _resetClaimsBulk(){
  const bar = document.getElementById('claimsBulkBar');
  if(bar) bar.classList.remove('show');
  const cnt = document.getElementById('claimsBulkCount');
  if(cnt) cnt.textContent = '0';
  const sa = document.getElementById('claimsSelectAll');
  if(sa) sa.checked = false;
}

  const st = document.getElementById('rvPageViewTimelineBtn');

  // نوع المطالبة

    markSessionDirty(); renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview();
  }));

    });
  }

    if(e.target.closest('[data-edit-invoice]')){ openInvoiceEditor(rvActiveInvoiceIdx); return; }
    if(e.target.closest('[data-delete-invoice]')){ const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx]; if(!inv) return; showDelete('حذف الفاتورة', `سيتم حذف "${inv.number||inv.id}".`, () => { markSessionDirty(); workingInvoices.splice(rvActiveInvoiceIdx,1); rvActiveInvoiceIdx = Math.max(0, rvActiveInvoiceIdx-1); rvActivePatientIdx = 0; renderReviewInvoices(); }); return; }
    if(e.target.closest('[data-edit-patient]')){ openPatientEditor(rvActiveInvoiceIdx, rvActivePatientIdx); return; }
    const patTab = e.target.closest('[data-pat-idx]'); if(patTab){ rvActivePatientIdx = Number(patTab.dataset.patIdx); renderActiveInvoice(); if(typeof updateCurrentReviewProgressPoint === 'function' && activeClaim) updateCurrentReviewProgressPoint(activeClaim.id, rvActiveInvoiceIdx, rvActivePatientIdx, 0); return; }
    const editL = e.target.closest('[data-edit-line]'); if(editL){ openLineEditor(rvActivePatientIdx, Number(editL.dataset.editLine)); return; }
    const delL = e.target.closest('[data-del-line]'); if(delL){ const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx]; const pat = inv&&inv.patients?inv.patients[rvActivePatientIdx]:null; if(!pat) return; const idx = Number(delL.dataset.delLine); showDelete('حذف البند','سيتم حذف هذا البند.', () => { markSessionDirty(); pat.lines.splice(idx,1); renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview(); }); return; }

    const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx];
    const pat = inv && inv.patients ? inv.patients[rvActivePatientIdx] : null;
    if(!pat) return;

    // تجاوزات المريض
    if(e.target.closest('[data-revert-cap]')){ markSessionDirty(); pat._capMode='reverted'; renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview(); return; }
    if(e.target.closest('[data-reset-cap]')){ markSessionDirty(); pat._capMode='auto'; pat._manualCapAmount=null; renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview(); return; }
    if(e.target.closest('[data-manual-cap-toggle]')){ markSessionDirty(); pat._capMode='manual'; pat._manualCapAmount = pat._capAmt||0; renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview(); return; }
    if(e.target.closest('[data-revert-copay]')){ markSessionDirty(); pat._copayMode='reverted'; renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview(); return; }
    if(e.target.closest('[data-reset-copay]')){ markSessionDirty(); pat._copayMode='auto'; pat._manualCopayAmount=null; renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview(); return; }
    if(e.target.closest('[data-manual-copay-toggle]')){ markSessionDirty(); pat._copayMode='manual'; pat._manualCopayAmount = pat._copayAmt||0; renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview(); return; }

    // تجاوزات البند
    const rvl = e.target.closest('[data-revert-line]'); if(rvl){ markSessionDirty(); pat.lines[Number(rvl.dataset.revertLine)]._overrideMode='reverted'; renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview(); return; }
    const rsl = e.target.closest('[data-reset-line]'); if(rsl){ markSessionDirty(); const l = pat.lines[Number(rsl.dataset.resetLine)]; l._overrideMode='auto'; l._manualValue=null; l._maxlabsForced = false; renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview(); return; }

    // إعادة تطبيق
    if(e.target.closest('[data-reapply-all]')){
      markSessionDirty();
      workingInvoices.forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => {
        p.lines.forEach(l => { if(l._overrideMode!=='manual' && l._overrideMode!=='reverted' && !l._maxlabsForced) l._overrideMode='auto'; });
        if(p._capMode!=='manual' && p._capMode!=='reverted') p._capMode='auto';
        if(p._copayMode!=='manual' && p._copayMode!=='reverted') p._copayMode='auto';
        computePatient(p, activeClaim, inv.date, inv);
      }));
      renderActiveInvoice(); recalcReview(); showSuccess('تم','تم إعادة التطبيق'); return;
    }

    const rb = e.target.closest('[data-rule]'); if(rb){ applyReviewRule(rb.dataset.rule); return; }
  }
  const _ip = document.getElementById('rvInvoicePanel');
  if(_ip && !_ip.dataset.bound){ _ip.dataset.bound='1'; _ip.addEventListener('click', _invoicePanelClick); }
  const _ipp = document.getElementById('rvPageInvoicePanel');
  if(_ipp && !_ipp.dataset.bound){ _ipp.dataset.bound='1'; _ipp.addEventListener('click', _invoicePanelClick); }
  function _invoicePanelChange(e){
    const internalCheck = e.target.closest('[data-internal-review]');
    if(internalCheck){ const inv=workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx]; if(!inv)return; if(!inv.internalReview)inv.internalReview={}; inv.internalReview[internalCheck.dataset.internalReview]=internalCheck.checked; markSessionDirty(); return; }
    if(e.target.matches('[data-internal-review-note]')){ const inv=workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx]; if(!inv)return; if(!inv.internalReview)inv.internalReview={}; inv.internalReview.note=e.target.value; markSessionDirty(); return; }
    const doc = e.target.closest('[data-doc]'); if(doc){
      const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx]; if(!inv) return;
      if(!inv.documents) inv.documents = {};
      inv.documents[doc.dataset.doc] = doc.checked;
      doc.closest('.rv-doc').classList.toggle('ok', doc.checked);
      doc.closest('.rv-doc').classList.toggle('miss', !doc.checked);
      markSessionDirty();
      return;
    }
    const pdoc = e.target.closest('[data-pat-doc]'); if(pdoc){
      const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx]; if(!inv) return;
      const pi = Number(pdoc.dataset.patIdx);
      const pat = inv.patients && inv.patients[pi]; if(!pat) return;
      if(!pat.documents) pat.documents = {};
      pat.documents[pdoc.dataset.patDoc] = pdoc.checked;
      pdoc.closest('.rv-doc').classList.toggle('ok', pdoc.checked);
      pdoc.closest('.rv-doc').classList.toggle('miss', !pdoc.checked);
      markSessionDirty();
      return;
    }
  }
  const _ipc = document.getElementById('rvInvoicePanel');
  if(_ipc && !_ipc.dataset.changeBound){ _ipc.dataset.changeBound='1'; _ipc.addEventListener('change', _invoicePanelChange); }

  // تعديل القيم يدوياً
  function _invoicePanelInput(e){

      rvActivePatientIdx = _patIdxIn; p._capMode='manual'; p._manualCapAmount = Math.max(0, Number(e.target.value)||0); recalcReview(); return;
    }
    if(e.target.classList.contains('rv-manual-input') && e.target.dataset.manualCopay !== undefined){
      const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx]; if(!inv) return;
      const p = inv.patients[_patIdxIn]; if(!p) return;
      rvActivePatientIdx = _patIdxIn; p._copayMode='manual'; p._manualCopayAmount = Math.max(0, Number(e.target.value)||0); recalcReview(); return;
    }
    if(!e.target.classList.contains('rv-line-approved')) return;
    const inv = workingInvoices[rvActiveInvoiceIdx]; if(!inv) return;
    const pat = inv.patients[_patIdxIn]; if(!pat) return;
    rvActivePatientIdx = _patIdxIn;
    const i = Number(e.target.dataset.lineIdx); const line = pat.lines[i]; if(!line) return;
    const billed = line.qty*line.billedUnit;
    let v = Number(e.target.value); if(isNaN(v)||v<0) v = 0; if(v>billed){ v = billed; e.target.value = v; }
    line.approved = v; line._overrideMode='manual'; line._manualValue = v; line._maxlabsForced = false;
    const row = e.target.closest('tr'); const diffCell = row.querySelector('.rv-diff');
    if(diffCell){ const d = billed-v; diffCell.textContent = fmt(d); diffCell.style.color = d>0?'var(--bad)':'var(--muted)'; }
    row.classList.toggle('rejected', v===0);
    recalcReview();
  }
  const _ipi = document.getElementById('rvInvoicePanel');
  if(_ipi && !_ipi.dataset.inputBound){ _ipi.dataset.inputBound='1'; _ipi.addEventListener('input', _invoicePanelInput); }
  const _ipip = document.getElementById('rvPageInvoicePanel');

    if (rvTabs.dataset.bound) return;
