  // binding exceptions
  const excBox = document.getElementById('rvExceptions');
  if (excBox && !excBox.dataset.bound) {
    excBox.dataset.bound = '1';
    excBox.addEventListener('click', e => {
      const reopen = e.target.closest('[data-exc-reopen]');
      if (reopen) {
        const key = reopen.dataset.excReopen;
        const [ii, pi, li] = key.split('-').map(Number);
        const inv = workingInvoices[ii]; const pat = inv && inv.patients ? inv.patients[pi] : null; const ln = pat && pat.lines ? pat.lines[li] : null;
        if (!ln) return;
        markSessionDirty();
        delete ln._decidedBy; delete ln._decidedAt; delete ln._decisionNote; delete ln._manualValue;
        ln._decision = 'needs-attention';
        ln._overrideMode = 'auto';
        ln._suggestedAction = ln._suggestedAction || { type: 'review', reason: (ln._checksFailed||[])[0] };
        workingInvoices.forEach(inv2 => (inv2.patients||[]).forEach(p => computePatient(p, activeClaim, inv2.date, inv2)));
        recalcReview();
        renderExceptions();
        renderActiveInvoice();
        return;
      }
      const btn = e.target.closest('[data-exc-act]'); if(!btn) return;
      const key = btn.dataset.excKey;
      const [ii, pi, li] = key.split('-').map(Number);
      const inv = workingInvoices[ii]; const pat = inv && inv.patients ? inv.patients[pi] : null; const ln = pat && pat.lines ? pat.lines[li] : null;
      if (!ln) return;
      const noteInput = excBox.querySelector(`[data-exc-note="${key}"]`);
      const note = noteInput ? noteInput.value.trim() : '';
      const act = btn.dataset.excAct;
      markSessionDirty();
      if (act === 'approve') {
        ln._decision = 'manual-approved';
        ln._overrideMode = 'manual';
        ln._manualValue = ln._contractCovered ? (ln.lineBaseAfterContract !== undefined ? ln.lineBaseAfterContract : ln.lineBase) : 0;
        ln.approved = ln._manualValue;
        ln._decidedBy = 'reviewer';
        ln._decidedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        ln._decisionReason = (ln._checksFailed||[])[0] || '';
        ln._decisionNote = note;
      } else if (act === 'reject') {
        if (!note) { showWarning('تنبيه','الملاحظة إلزامية للرفض'); return; }
        ln._decision = 'manual-rejected';
        ln._overrideMode = 'manual';
        ln._manualValue = 0;
        ln.approved = 0;
        ln._decidedBy = 'reviewer';
        ln._decidedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        ln._decisionReason = (ln._checksFailed||[])[0] || '';
        ln._decisionNote = note;
      } else if (act === 'reduce') {
        const reduceWrap = excBox.querySelector(`[data-exc-reduce="${key}"]`);
        if (reduceWrap) reduceWrap.style.display = '';
        const valInput = excBox.querySelector(`[data-exc-reduce-val="${key}"]`);
        if (!valInput) return;
        const v = Number(valInput.value);
        if (isNaN(v) || v < 0) { showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل مبلغًا صالحًا'); return; }
        if (v > (ln._contractCovered ? (ln.lineBaseAfterContract||ln.lineBase||0) : 0)) { showWarning('تنبيه','المبلغ يتجاوز المعتمد'); return; }
        if (v < (ln._contractCovered ? (ln.lineBaseAfterContract||ln.lineBase||0) : 0) && !note) { showWarning('تنبيه','الملاحظة إلزامية للتخفيض'); return; }
        ln._decision = 'manual-reduced';
        ln._overrideMode = 'manual';
        ln._manualValue = v;
        ln.approved = v;
        ln._decidedBy = 'reviewer';
        ln._decidedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        ln._decisionReason = (ln._checksFailed||[])[0] || '';
        ln._decisionNote = note;
      } else if (act === 'pa') {
        ln._decision = 'pa-pending';
        ln._overrideMode = 'auto';
        ln._decidedBy = 'reviewer';
        ln._decidedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        ln._decisionReason = (ln._checksFailed||[])[0] || '';
        ln._decisionNote = note;
      }
      // recalc
      workingInvoices.forEach(inv2 => (inv2.patients||[]).forEach(p => computePatient(p, activeClaim, inv2.date, inv2)));
      recalcReview();
      renderExceptions();
      renderActiveInvoice();
    });
  }

  // gating finalize button on checklist checkboxes
  const finalPanel = document.getElementById('rvTabFinal');
  if (finalPanel && !finalPanel.dataset.checkBound) {
    finalPanel.dataset.checkBound = '1';
    finalPanel.addEventListener('change', e => {
      if (!e.target.classList.contains('final-check')) return;
      const checks = finalPanel.querySelectorAll('.final-check');
      const all = Array.from(checks).every(c => c.checked);
      const btn = document.getElementById('finalizeReviewBtn');
      if (btn) btn.disabled = !all;
    });
  }

  const fh = document.getElementById('rvPageFinalizeHeaderBtn');
  if (fh && !fh.dataset.bound) { fh.dataset.bound='1'; fh.addEventListener('click', () => { const b = document.getElementById('rvPageFinalizeBtn') || document.getElementById('finalizeReviewBtn'); if(b) b.click(); }); }
  const finBtn = document.getElementById('finalizeReviewBtn') || document.getElementById('rvPageFinalizeBtn');
  if (finBtn && !finBtn.dataset.bound) {
    finBtn.dataset.bound = '1';
    finBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      // validation
      const excs = typeof collectExceptions === 'function' ? collectExceptions() : [];
      const paPending = (workingInvoices||[]).reduce((s,inv)=>s+(inv.patients||[]).reduce((a,p)=>a+(p.lines||[]).filter(l=>l._decision==='pa-pending').length,0),0);
      const billed = activeClaim ? (Number(activeClaim.billed)||0) : 0;
      let entered = 0;
      (workingInvoices||[]).forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => (p.lines||[]).forEach(l => { entered += (Number(l.qty)||0)*(Number(l.billedUnit)||0); })));
      // Keep reconciliation tied to the source-submitted amount after recalculation.
      const targetInfo = typeof getClaimReconciliationTarget === 'function'
        ? getClaimReconciliationTarget(activeClaim)
        : { amount: billed, source: 'legacy-billed' };
      const mismatch = Math.abs(targetInfo.amount - entered) > 1;
      const missingDocsInv = (workingInvoices||[]).reduce((s,inv)=>s+DOC_ITEMS.filter(d=>!inv.documents||!inv.documents[d.key]).length,0);
      const missingDocsPat = (workingInvoices||[]).reduce((s,inv)=>s+(inv.patients||[]).reduce((a,p)=>a+DOC_ITEMS.filter(d=>!(p.documents&&p.documents[d.key])).length,0),0);
      const missingDocs = missingDocsInv + missingDocsPat;
      const finalReasonEl = document.getElementById('rvFinalReason') || document.getElementById('rvPageFinalReason');
      const finalReason = finalReasonEl ? finalReasonEl.value : '';
      const decision = (document.querySelector('input[name="rvDecision"]:checked')||document.querySelector('input[name="rvPageDecision"]:checked')||{}).value || 'partial';
      const needsReason = (decision !== 'approved');
      const missing = [];
      if (excs.length) missing.push(`${excs.length} بند غير معالج`);
      if (paPending) missing.push(`${paPending} بند بانتظار PA`);
      if (mismatch) missing.push('الإجمالي غير مُطابق');
      if (missingDocs) missing.push(`${missingDocs} مستند ناقص`);
      if (needsReason && !finalReason) missing.push('سبب الاستقطاع العام');

      if (missing.length) {
        const msg = 'لا يمكن إتمام التوقيع — ' + missing.length + ' نقص:\n\n• ' + missing.join('\n• ') + '\n\n[العودة للمعالجة]   [تجاوز وتوقيع مع توثيق]';
        const ok = confirm(msg + '\n\nتجاوز وتوقيع؟');
        if (!ok) return;
        const overrideReason = prompt('سبب التجاوز (إلزامي):');
        if (!overrideReason || !overrideReason.trim()) { showWarning('تنبيه','سبب التجاوز إلزامي'); return; }
        if (!activeClaim._overrideChecklist) activeClaim._overrideChecklist = [];
        activeClaim._overrideChecklist.push({ by: (appSettings && appSettings.reviewer && appSettings.reviewer.name) || 'المراجع', at: new Date().toISOString(), reason: overrideReason.trim(), missing: missing.slice() });
      }

      // signature dialog
      const approved = (workingInvoices||[]).reduce((s,inv)=>s+(inv.patients||[]).reduce((a,p)=>a+(p._final||0),0),0);
      const ded = billed - approved;
      const sig = confirm(`توقيع المراجعة\n\nالمطالبة: ${activeClaim.id}\nالمطالب: ${fmt(billed)}\nالمعتمد: ${fmt(approved)}\nالاستقطاع: ${fmt(ded)}\nالقرار: ${decision}\nالسبب: ${finalReason||'—'}\n\nأقر بأنني راجعت هذه المطالبة.`);
      if (!sig) return;

      // apply decision to claim
      activeClaim._signedOff = true;
      activeClaim._reviewedBy = (appSettings && appSettings.reviewer && appSettings.reviewer.name) || 'المراجع';
      activeClaim._reviewedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      activeClaim._locked = true;
      activeClaim._decision = decision;
      activeClaim.reason = finalReason || activeClaim.reason || '';
      activeClaim.notes = ((document.getElementById('rvFinalNotes')||document.getElementById('rvPageFinalNotes')) ? ((document.getElementById('rvFinalNotes')||document.getElementById('rvPageFinalNotes')).value.trim()) : '') || activeClaim.notes || '';

      // sync rvReason select
      const rvReason = document.getElementById('rvReason') || document.getElementById('rvPageReason');
      if (rvReason && finalReason) rvReason.value = finalReason;

      saveReview();
    });
  }

  // تتبع التعديلات الحقيقية
  const rvModal = $('#reviewModal');
  if(rvModal){
    rvModal.addEventListener('input', markSessionDirty);
    rvModal.addEventListener('change', markSessionDirty);
  }
}

// review-open.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function _fillReviewPage(c){
  const set = (id, v) => { const el = document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.value = v == null ? '' : v; };
  const sub = document.getElementById('rvPageSub');
  if(sub) sub.textContent = `${c.id} • ${c.patient}`;
  const ctxBar = document.getElementById('rvPageContextBar');
  if (ctxBar) {
    const ctx = getContractContext(c);
    ctxBar.textContent = `مراجعة ${c.id} · ${c.provider} · عقد ${ctx.contract?ctx.contract.id:'—'} · لائحة ${ctx.tariff?ctx.tariff.name:'—'} · إجمالي ${fmt(c.billed||0)}`;
  }
  set('rvPageId', c.id); set('rvPageDate', c.date); set('rvPageReceivedNumber', c.receivedNumber||'—');
  set('rvPageReceivedDate', c.receivedDate||'—'); set('rvPagePatient', c.patient); set('rvPageProvider', c.provider);
  set('rvPageReason', c.reason||''); set('rvPageNotes', c.notes||'');
  const pab = document.getElementById('rvPagePriorApprovalBox');
  if(c.priorApproval && c.priorApproval.required){
    if(pab) pab.style.display = 'block';
    set('rvPagePaStatus', c.priorApproval.status || 'not-requested');
    set('rvPagePaRefNumber', c.priorApproval.refNumber || '');
    set('rvPagePaDate', c.priorApproval.date || '');
    if(typeof updatePAWarning === 'function') updatePAWarning();
  } else {
    if(pab) pab.style.display = 'none';
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('#page-review .rv-type-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.claimType === (c.type||'employee')));
}
function openReview(id){
  const c = getClaimById(id); if(!c) return;
  const existing = findSessionByClaim(id);
  const isAlreadyInThisSession = existing && activeSessionId === existing.id
                              && activeClaim && activeClaim.id === existing.claimId;
  if(existing && !isAlreadyInThisSession){
    if(activeSessionId && activeClaim){ upsertCurrentSession(); activeClaim = null; workingInvoices = []; }
    resumeSession(existing.id); return;
  }
  let baseInvoices = c.invoices || [];
  if (!Array.isArray(baseInvoices)) baseInvoices = [];
  activeClaim = c;
  workingInvoices = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(baseInvoices));
  rvActiveInvoiceIdx = 0; rvActivePatientIdx = 0;
  workingInvoices.forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => {
    if(p.coverageCapOverride === undefined) p.coverageCapOverride = null;
    if(p.retireeCopayOverride === undefined) p.retireeCopayOverride = null;
    if(p.beneficiaryType === undefined) p.beneficiaryType = c.type || 'employee';
    if(!Array.isArray(p.deductionReasons)) p.deductionReasons = [];
    const _emptyPatientDocs = { patientId: false, referral: false, medicalReport: false, priorApprovalDoc: false };
    const _looksInheritedFromInvoice = !!(p.documents && inv.documents && JSON.stringify(p.documents) === JSON.stringify(inv.documents));
    if(!p.documents || _looksInheritedFromInvoice) p.documents = { ..._emptyPatientDocs };
  }));
  if(typeof recalcClaim === 'function') recalcClaim(c);
  workingInvoices.forEach(inv => (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => { if(typeof computePatient === 'function') computePatient(p, c, inv.date, inv); }));
  _fillReviewPage(c);
  // try modal for backward compat
  try {
    const sub = document.getElementById('rvSub');
    if(sub) sub.textContent = `${c.id} • ${c.patient}`;
    const ctxBar = document.getElementById('rvContextBar');
    if (ctxBar) {
      const ctx = getContractContext(c);
      ctxBar.textContent = `مراجعة ${c.id} · ${c.provider} · عقد ${ctx.contract?ctx.contract.id:'—'} · لائحة ${ctx.tariff?ctx.tariff.name:'—'} · إجمالي ${fmt(c.billed||0)}`;
    }
    const set = (id, v) => { const el = document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.value = v == null ? '' : v; };
    set('rvId', c.id); set('rvDate', c.date); set('rvReceivedNumber', c.receivedNumber||'—');
    set('rvReceivedDate', c.receivedDate||'—'); set('rvPatient', c.patient); set('rvProvider', c.provider);
    set('rvReason', c.reason||''); set('rvNotes', c.notes||'');
    const pab = document.getElementById('priorApprovalBox');
    if(c.priorApproval && c.priorApproval.required){
      if(pab) pab.style.display = 'block';
      set('paStatus', c.priorApproval.status || 'not-requested');
      set('paRefNumber', c.priorApproval.refNumber || '');
      set('paDate', c.priorApproval.date || '');
      if(typeof updatePAWarning === 'function') updatePAWarning();
    } else {
      if(pab) pab.style.display = 'none';
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('#reviewModal .rv-type-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.claimType === (c.type||'employee')));
  } catch(e){}
  if(typeof renderReviewInvoices === 'function') renderReviewInvoices();
  if (typeof renderExceptions === 'function') { clearTimeout(window._excDebounce); window._excDebounce = setTimeout(renderExceptions, 120); }
  const s = upsertCurrentSession();
  if(s){ s.status = 'active'; activeSessionId = s.id; persistSessions(); if(typeof startSessionTimeTracker === 'function') startSessionTimeTracker(s); }
  clearSessionDirty();
  try { navigateTo('review', {noPush:false}); } catch(e){}
  try { renderReviewInvoices(); } catch(e){}
  updateSessionsBadge();
}
function closeReview(opts){
  const isModalOpen = $('#reviewModal') && $('#reviewModal').classList.contains('open');
  if(!isModalOpen && currentPage !== 'review') return;
  const discard = !!(opts && opts.discard === true);
  if(!discard && _hasUnsavedChanges()){
    openDialog({
      type:'warning', title:'تعديلات غير محفوظة',
      message:'هل تريد إغلاق المراجعة؟ سيتم حفظ الجلسة مؤقتاً لكن التعديلات لن تُطبَّق على المطالبة حتى تضغط «حفظ».',
      confirmText:'إغلاق', cancelText:'متابعة',
      onConfirm: () => _doCloseReview(false)
    });
    return;
  }
  _doCloseReview(discard);
}
function _doCloseReview(discard){
  if(activeClaim && !discard){
    const s = upsertCurrentSession();
    if(s){ s.status = 'paused'; persistSessions(); }
  }
  if(typeof stopSessionTimeTracker === 'function') stopSessionTimeTracker();
  activeSessionId = null;
  clearSessionDirty();
  if($('#reviewModal')) $('#reviewModal').classList.remove('open');
  try { _unlockBodyScroll(); } catch(e){}
  activeClaim = null; workingInvoices = [];
  rvActiveInvoiceIdx = 0; rvActivePatientIdx = 0;
  updateSessionsBadge();
  if(currentPage === 'sessions') renderSessions();
  try {
    if(currentPage === 'review'){
      if(typeof goBack === 'function') goBack(); else navigateTo('claims',{noPush:true});
    }
  } catch(e){}
}

// review-state.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let activeClaim = null;
let workingInvoices = [];
let _sessionDirty = false;
function markSessionDirty(){ _sessionDirty = true; try { const b = document.getElementById('rvPageDirtyBadge'); if(b) b.style.display = ''; } catch(e){} }
function clearSessionDirty(){ _sessionDirty = false; try { const b = document.getElementById('rvPageDirtyBadge'); if(b) b.style.display = 'none'; } catch(e){} }
let rvActiveInvoiceIdx = 0;
let rvActivePatientIdx = 0;
let rvEditInvoiceIdx = -1;
let rvEditPatientInvoiceIdx = -1;
let rvEditPatientIdx = -1;
let rvEditLineIdx = -1;
let lnApplicableTariffs = [];
let lnSelectedTariffId = null;
let lnDebounceTimer = null;
function newInvoiceId(){
  let m = 0;
  workingInvoices.forEach(inv => { const n = parseInt(String(inv.id).split('-').pop(), 10); if(!isNaN(n)) m = Math.max(m,n); });
  return activeClaim ? (activeClaim.id+'-'+(m+1)) : ('INV-'+Date.now());
}
function newPatientId(inv){
  let m = 0;
  (inv.patients||[]).forEach(p => { const n = parseInt(String(p.id).split('-P').pop(), 10); if(!isNaN(n)) m = Math.max(m,n); });
  return inv.id+'-P'+(m+1);
}
function _hasUnsavedChanges(){ return _sessionDirty === true; }

/* review-ui.js — واجهة تبويبات المراجعة */
function renderReviewTabsLayout() {
  const container = document.getElementById('reviewModalContainer');
  if (!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="rv-tabs" role="tablist">
      <button type="button" class="rv-tab active" data-tab="input" onclick="switchReviewTab('input')">
        📋 1. الإدخال والتدقيق الآلي
      </button>
      <button type="button" class="rv-tab" data-tab="exceptions" onclick="switchReviewTab('exceptions')">
        ⚠️ 2. الاستثناءات المعلقة (<span id="rvExcCountBadge">0</span>)
      </button>
      <button type="button" class="rv-tab" data-tab="final" onclick="switchReviewTab('final')">
        ⚖️ 3. القرار والتخريج القانوني النهائي
      </button>
    </div>
    <div id="rvTabContentArea" class="rv-tab-content"></div>
  `;
}

function switchReviewTab(tabName) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.rv-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
  const btn = document.querySelector(`[data-tab="${tabName}"]`);
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('active');
  const panels = document.querySelectorAll('.rv-tab-panel');
  panels.forEach(p => { p.style.display = 'none'; });
  const map = { input: 'rvTabInput', exceptions: 'rvTabExceptions', final: 'rvTabFinal' };
  const panel = document.getElementById(map[tabName] || '');
  if (panel) panel.style.display = '';
  if (tabName === 'exceptions' && typeof renderExceptions === 'function') renderExceptions();
  if (tabName === 'final' && typeof renderFinalSummary === 'function') renderFinalSummary();
}

// rule-wizard-2.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function openDrWizard(){
  wizState = { step:1, type:null, name:'', rate:0, from:'', to:'', notes:'', ctypes:['employee'], providerIds:[], contractIds:[], action:'apply_cap', conditions:{logic:'AND',rules:[]}, requires:{mode:'any',ruleIds:[]}, compositeConfig:{childRuleIds:[],applyMode:'atomic',requireAllMatch:true}, tiers:[], tiersEnabled:false };
  $('#drWizardModal').classList.add('open'); wizRenderStep();
}
function closeDrWizard(){ $('#drWizardModal').classList.remove('open'); }
function bindDrWizard(){
  $$('[data-close-drwiz]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', closeDrWizard));
  $('#wizBackBtn').addEventListener('click', wizBack);
  $('#wizNextBtn').addEventListener('click', wizNext);
  $$('[data-wiz-jump]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', () => wizGoTo(Number(el.dataset.wizJump))));
  $$('.wiz-type-card').forEach(card => card.addEventListener('click', () => {
    wizState.type = card.dataset.wizType;
    if(wizState.type === 'coverage_cap'){ wizState.action = 'apply_cap'; if(!wizState.ctypes.includes('employee')) wizState.ctypes.push('employee'); }
    else if(wizState.type === 'retiree_copay'){ wizState.action = 'apply_copay'; if(!wizState.ctypes.includes('retiree')) wizState.ctypes.push('retiree'); }
    else if(wizState.type === 'composite') wizState.action = 'composite';
    $$('.wiz-type-card').forEach(c => c.classList.toggle('active', c === card));
  }));
  $('#wizAction').addEventListener('change', e => { wizState.action = e.target.value; });
  $('#wizCompositeMode').addEventListener('change', e => { wizState.compositeConfig.applyMode = e.target.value; });
  $('#wizRequireAll').addEventListener('change', e => { wizState.compositeConfig.requireAllMatch = e.target.checked; });
  $('#wizRequiresMode').addEventListener('change', e => { wizState.requires.mode = e.target.value; });
  $('#wizRequiresClear').addEventListener('click', () => { wizState.requires.ruleIds = []; wizRenderRequires(); });
  $('#wizRatePresets').addEventListener('click', e => { const btn = e.target.closest('[data-wiz-rate]'); if(!btn) return; wizState.rate = Number(btn.dataset.wizRate); $('#wizRate').value = wizState.rate; });
  $('#wizPeriodPresets').addEventListener('click', e => { const btn = e.target.closest('[data-wiz-period]'); if(!btn) return; const p = WIZ_PERIOD_PRESETS[Number(btn.dataset.wizPeriod)]; wizState.from = p.from(); wizState.to = p.to(); $('#wizFrom').value = wizState.from; $('#wizTo').value = wizState.to; });
  $$('.wiz-ctype').forEach(cb => cb.addEventListener('change', () => { if(cb.checked){ if(!wizState.ctypes.includes(cb.value)) wizState.ctypes.push(cb.value); } else wizState.ctypes = wizState.ctypes.filter(x=>x!==cb.value); cb.closest('.scope-chk').classList.toggle('checked', cb.checked); }));
  $('#condLogic').addEventListener('change', e => { wizState.conditions.logic = e.target.value; wizRenderCondPreview(); });
  $('#addCondBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { wizState.conditions.rules.push({ field:'line.category', op:'eq', value:'مختبر' }); wizRenderConditions(); });
  $('#addGroupBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { wizState.conditions.rules.push({ logic:'OR', rules:[{ field:'line.billedUnit', op:'gt', value:500 }] }); wizRenderConditions(); });
  $('#drWizardModal').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const sa = e.target.closest('[data-wiz-selectall]');
    if(sa){ if(sa.dataset.wizSelectall === 'providers') wizState.providerIds = providers.map(p=>p.id); else wizState.contractIds = contracts.map(c=>c.id); wizRenderScope(); }
    const cl = e.target.closest('[data-wiz-clear]');
    if(cl){ if(cl.dataset.wizClear === 'providers') wizState.providerIds = []; else wizState.contractIds = []; wizRenderScope(); }
  });
  const addTierBtn = $('#wizAddTierBtn');
  if(addTierBtn) addTierBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { if(!wizState.tiers) wizState.tiers = []; wizState.tiers.push({from:0, to:null, rate:0}); wizState.tiersEnabled = true; const te = $('#wizTiersEnable'); if(te) te.checked = true; wizRenderTiers(); });
  const tiersEnable = $('#wizTiersEnable');
  if(tiersEnable) tiersEnable.addEventListener('change', e => { wizState.tiersEnabled = e.target.checked; if(!wizState.tiersEnabled) wizState.tiers = []; });
}

function wizConditionRowHTML(cond, idPrefix, inGroup, parentPath){
  parentPath = parentPath || '';
  const mode = cond.mode || 'expr';
  const scope = cond.scope || 'self';
  const scopeSel = inGroup ? '' : `<select class="sel-scope" data-cond-scope="${idPrefix}">${Object.entries(WIZ_SCOPES).map(([k,v])=>`<option value="${k}" ${scope===k?'selected':''}>${v.label}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
  const modeSel = inGroup ? '' : `<select class="sel-mode" data-cond-mode="${idPrefix}">${Object.entries(WIZ_MODES).map(([k,v])=>`<option value="${k}" ${mode===k?'selected':''}>${v.label}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
  const fSel = val => `<select data-cond-field="${idPrefix}">${WIZ_FIELDS.map(x=>`<option value="${x.path}" ${val===x.path?'selected':''}>${x.label}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
  const opOptions = (val) => WIZ_OP_GROUPS.map(g => `<optgroup label="${g.label}">${g.ops.map(k=>`<option value="${k}" ${val===k?'selected':''}>${(WIZ_OPS[k]||{}).label||k}</option>`).join('')}</optgroup>`).join('') + `<optgroup label="أخرى">${Object.keys(WIZ_OPS).filter(k=>!WIZ_OP_GROUPS.some(g=>g.ops.includes(k))).map(k=>`<option value="${k}" ${val===k?'selected':''}>${WIZ_OPS[k].label}</option>`).join('')}</optgroup>`;
  const oSel = (val,attr) => `<select data-cond-${attr}="${idPrefix}">${opOptions(val)}</select>`;
  const vInp = (val,attr,opKey) => { const o = cond[opKey]; if((WIZ_OPS[o]||{}).noValue) return ''; return `<input type="text" data-cond-${attr}="${idPrefix}" value="${val||''}" placeholder="قيمة">`; };
  let inner = '';
  if(mode === 'expr') inner = fSel(cond.field||'line.category') + oSel(cond.op||'eq','op') + vInp(cond.value,'value','op');
  else if(mode === 'any' || mode === 'all') inner = fSel(cond.subField||'line.code') + oSel(cond.subOp||'eq','subOp') + vInp(cond.subValue,'subValue','subOp');
  else if(mode === 'count') inner = fSel(cond.subField||'line.category') + oSel(cond.subOp||'eq','subOp') + vInp(cond.subValue,'subValue','subOp') + oSel(cond.compareOp||'gt','compareOp') + `<input type="number" data-cond-compareValue="${idPrefix}" value="${cond.compareValue||0}">`;
  else if(['sum','avg','max','min'].includes(mode)) inner = fSel(cond.aggField||'line.billedTotal') + oSel(cond.compareOp||'gt','compareOp') + `<input type="number" data-cond-compareValue="${idPrefix}" value="${cond.compareValue||0}">`;
  else if(mode === 'duplicate' || mode === 'unique') inner = fSel(cond.field||'line.code');
  else if(mode === 'frequency') inner = fSel(cond.field||'line.code') + oSel(cond.compareOp||'gt','compareOp') + '<input type="number" data-cond-compareValue="' + idPrefix + '" value="' + (cond.compareValue||1) + '" placeholder="حد">' + '<input type="number" data-cond-windowDays="' + idPrefix + '" value="' + (cond.windowDays||14) + '" placeholder="أيام">';
  else if(mode === 'bundle') inner = '<input type="text" data-cond-components="' + idPrefix + '" value="' + (cond.components||[]).join(',') + '" placeholder="أكواد مفصولة بفواصل">' + fSel(cond.field||'line.code');
  else if(mode === 'diagnosis_match') inner = fSel(cond.field||'claim.diagnosis') + oSel(cond.op||'in','op') + '<input type="text" data-cond-value="' + idPrefix + '" value="' + (cond.value||'') + '" placeholder="أكواد ICD-10 مفصولة بفواصل">';
  return `<div class="cond-item mode-${mode}" data-drag-path="${idPrefix}" data-drop-container="${parentPath}">
    <span class="cond-drag-handle" draggable="true" aria-grabbed="false" tabindex="0" title="سحب">⠿</span>
    ${scopeSel}${modeSel}${inner}
    <div class="cond-reorder-btns">
      <button type="button" data-move-up="${idPrefix}" title="أعلى">▲</button>
      <button type="button" data-move-down="${idPrefix}" title="أسفل">▼</button>
    </div>
    <button type="button" class="cond-del" data-cond-del="${idPrefix}">×</button>
  </div>`;
}
function renderNodeHTML(node, path, parentPath){
  parentPath = parentPath || '';
  if(node && node.logic && Array.isArray(node.rules)){
    const inner = node.rules.map((ic,iidx) => renderNodeHTML(ic, path + '_c' + iidx, path)).join('');
    return `<div class="cond-group" data-drag-path="${path}" data-drop-into="${path}" data-drop-container="${parentPath}">
      <div class="cond-group-head">
        <span class="cond-drag-handle" draggable="true" tabindex="0" title="سحب">⠿</span>
        <span>مجموعة</span>
        <select data-group-logic="${path}" class="cond-logic-sel" style="height:26px;font-size:10.5px"><option value="AND" ${node.logic==='AND'?'selected':''}>ALL</option><option value="OR" ${node.logic==='OR'?'selected':''}>ANY</option></select>
        <button type="button" class="del-grp" data-group-del="${path}">×</button>
        <div class="cond-reorder-btns">
          <button type="button" data-move-up="${path}" title="أعلى">▲</button>
          <button type="button" data-move-down="${path}" title="أسفل">▼</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="cond-list" data-drop-container="${path}" data-drop-target-end="true">${inner}</div>
      <div class="cond-actions">
        <button type="button" class="btn-apply out" data-group-add-cond="${path}" style="height:26px;padding:0 10px;font-size:10.5px;margin-top:4px">+ شرط</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn-apply out" data-group-add-group="${path}" style="height:26px;padding:0 10px;font-size:10.5px;margin-top:4px;margin-right:4px">+ مجموعة</button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }
  return wizConditionRowHTML(node, path, false, parentPath);
}
function wizRenderConditions(){
  const list = $('#condList'); const conds = wizState.conditions.rules || [];
  if(!conds.length) list.innerHTML = '<div class="cond-empty">لا شروط</div>';
  else list.innerHTML = conds.map((c,idx) => renderNodeHTML(c, (c.logic&&Array.isArray(c.rules)) ? 'g'+idx : 'c'+idx, '')).join('');
  wizRenderCondPreview(); wizBindConditionHandlers(); if(typeof initDragDrop === 'function') initDragDrop();
}
function wizRenderCondPreview(){
  const el = $('#condPreview'); if(!el) return;
  const conds = wizState.conditions.rules || [];
  if(!conds.length){ el.classList.remove('show'); return; }
  el.innerHTML = `سيتم التطبيق عند: ${conds.length} شرط`;
  el.classList.add('show');
}
function parsePath(id){
  if(!id) return null;
  const nums = String(id).match(/\d+/g);
  if(!nums) return null;
  return nums.map(Number);
}
function getNodeByPath(path){ let node = { rules: wizState.conditions.rules }; for(const i of path){ if(!node.rules || !node.rules[i]) return null; node = node.rules[i]; } return node; }
function getParentByPath(path){ if(path.length === 1) return { parent: { rules: wizState.conditions.rules }, idx: path[0] }; const p = getNodeByPath(path.slice(0,-1)); return p ? { parent: p, idx: path[path.length-1] } : null; }
function wizBindConditionHandlers(){
  const root = $('#condList');
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-scope]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condScope); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; n.scope = e.target.value; n.field = null; n.op = null; n.value = null; wizRenderConditions(); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-mode]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condMode); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; n.mode = e.target.value; n.field = null; n.op = null; n.value = null; wizRenderConditions(); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-field]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condField); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; const mode = n.mode || 'expr'; if(mode === 'expr' || mode === 'duplicate' || mode === 'unique' || mode === 'frequency' || mode === 'diagnosis_match') n.field = e.target.value; else if(mode === 'any' || mode === 'all' || mode === 'count') n.subField = e.target.value; else n.aggField = e.target.value; }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-op]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condOp); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; n.op = e.target.value; wizRenderConditions(); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-subOp]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condSubop); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; n.subOp = e.target.value; wizRenderConditions(); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-compareOp]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condCompareop); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; n.compareOp = e.target.value; }));
  ['value','subValue','compareValue'].forEach(attr => {
    root.querySelectorAll(`[data-cond-${attr}]`).forEach(el => {
      ['input','change'].forEach(evt => el.addEventListener(evt, e => {
        const key = 'cond' + attr.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + attr.slice(1);
        const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset[key]); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return;
        n[attr] = e.target.value;
      }));
    });
  });
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-windowDays]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condWindowdays); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; n.windowDays = Number(e.target.value)||0; }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-components]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const p = parsePath(e.target.dataset.condComponents); const n = getNodeByPath(p); if(!n) return; n.components = String(e.target.value||'').split(',').map(function(x){return x.trim();}).filter(Boolean); }));
    root.querySelectorAll('[data-cond-del]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', e => { const p = parsePath(e.currentTarget.dataset.condDel); if(!p) return; const g = getParentByPath(p); if(!g) return; showDelete('حذف شرط', 'سيتم حذف الشرط المحدد من القاعدة.', () => { const current=getParentByPath(p); if(current) current.parent.rules.splice(current.idx,1); wizRenderConditions(); }); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-group-del]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', e => { const path = parsePath(e.currentTarget.dataset.groupDel); if(!path) return; const g = getParentByPath(path); if(!g) return; showDelete('حذف مجموعة شروط', 'سيتم حذف المجموعة وكل الشروط التابعة لها.', () => { const current=getParentByPath(path); if(current) current.parent.rules.splice(current.idx,1); wizRenderConditions(); }); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-group-logic]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', e => { const path = parsePath(e.target.dataset.groupLogic); const g = getNodeByPath(path); if(g){ g.logic = e.target.value; } }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-group-add-cond]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', e => { const path = parsePath(e.target.dataset.groupAddCond); const g = getNodeByPath(path); if(g && g.rules) g.rules.push({ field:'line.category', op:'eq', value:'مختبر' }); wizRenderConditions(); }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-group-add-group]').forEach(el => el.addEventListener('click', e => { const path = parsePath(e.target.dataset.groupAddGroup); const g = getNodeByPath(path); if(g && g.rules) g.rules.push({ logic:'OR', rules:[{ field:'line.billedUnit', op:'gt', value:500 }] }); wizRenderConditions(); }));
  if(typeof bindReorderButtons === 'function') bindReorderButtons();
}

// rule-wizard-dnd-anim.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function createGhost(sourceEl, e){
  const clone = sourceEl.cloneNode(true);
  clone.classList.add('drag-ghost');
  clone.style.width = sourceEl.offsetWidth + 'px';
  const rect = sourceEl.getBoundingClientRect();
  clone.style.left = '0px';
  clone.style.top = '0px';
  clone.style.transform = `translate(${e.clientX+12}px, ${e.clientY+12}px)`;
  document.body.appendChild(clone);
  dragGhost = clone;
}

function updateGhost(e){
  if(!dragGhost) return;
  rafId = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    if(dragGhost) dragGhost.style.transform = `translate(${e.clientX+12}px, ${e.clientY+12}px)`;
  });
}

function showDropIndicator(targetEl, position){
  clearDropIndicators();
  if(position === 'before') targetEl.classList.add('drop-before');
  else if(position === 'after') targetEl.classList.add('drop-after');
  else if(position === 'into') targetEl.classList.add('drop-into');
  else if(position === 'before-group') targetEl.classList.add('drop-before-group');
  else if(position === 'after-group') targetEl.classList.add('drop-after-group');
}

function clearDropIndicators(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.drop-before,.drop-after,.drop-before-group,.drop-after-group,.drop-into,.drop-target-end,.drop-active').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.remove('drop-before','drop-after','drop-before-group','drop-after-group','drop-into','drop-target-end','drop-active');
  });
}

function recordFlipFirst(){
  flipFirst = {};
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-drag-path]').forEach(el => {
    flipFirst[el.dataset.dragPath] = el.getBoundingClientRect();
  });
}

function playFlip(){
  if(!flipFirst) return;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-drag-path]').forEach(el => {
    const first = flipFirst[el.dataset.dragPath];
    if(!first) return;
    const last = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    const dx = first.left - last.left;
    const dy = first.top - last.top;
    if(dx || dy){
      el.style.transform = `translate(${dx}px, ${dy}px)`;
      el.style.transition = 'none';
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        el.style.transition = 'transform .2s ease';
        el.style.transform = '';
      });
    }
  });
  flipFirst = null;
}

function flashMovedItem(path){
  const el = document.querySelector(`[data-drag-path="${path}"]`);
  if(el){ el.classList.add('flash'); setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('flash'), 400); }
}

function autoScroll(e){
  const container = document.getElementById('condList');
  if(!container) return;
  const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
  const threshold = 40;
  if(e.clientY < rect.top + threshold){ container.scrollTop -= 8; }
  else if(e.clientY > rect.bottom - threshold){ container.scrollTop += 8; }
}

FILE: /mnt/data/phase23work/TPA-Pro-main/js/rule-wizard-dnd-handlers.js

// rule-wizard-dnd-handlers.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function clearDragState(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.dragging,.drop-before,.drop-after,.drop-before-group,.drop-after-group,.drop-into,.drop-target-end,.drop-active').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.remove('dragging','drop-before','drop-after','drop-before-group','drop-after-group','drop-into','drop-target-end','drop-active');
  });
  if(dragGhost){ dragGhost.remove(); dragGhost = null; }
  document.body.style.userSelect = '';
  if(rafId){ cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); rafId = null; }
  if(autoScrollInterval){ clearInterval(autoScrollInterval); autoScrollInterval = null; }
  dragSrcPath = null; dragSrcParent = null; dragSrcIndex = -1;
  dropTarget = null; flipFirst = null; dragThresholdMet = false;
}

function handleDragStart(e){
  const handle = e.target.closest('.cond-drag-handle');
  if(!handle) return;
  const item = handle.closest('[data-drag-path]');
  if(!item) return;
  dragSrcPath = item.dataset.dragPath;
  const info = getPathInfo(dragSrcPath);
  if(!info) return;
  dragSrcParent = info.parentPath;
  dragSrcIndex = info.index;
  dragStartX = e.clientX; dragStartY = e.clientY;
  dragThresholdMet = false;
  e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
  e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', dragSrcPath);
  item.classList.add('dragging');
  item.setAttribute('aria-grabbed', 'true');
  document.body.style.userSelect = 'none';
  createGhost(item, e);
  recordFlipFirst();
  // Activate drop zones
  document.querySelectorAll('.cond-list').forEach(cl => cl.classList.add('drop-active'));
}

function handleDrag(e){
  if(!dragSrcPath) return;
  if(!dragThresholdMet){
    const dx = e.clientX - dragStartX, dy = e.clientY - dragStartY;
    if(Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy) < 5) return;
    dragThresholdMet = true;
  }
  updateGhost(e);
  autoScroll(e);
}

function handleDragOver(e){
  if(!dragSrcPath) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
  const target = e.target.closest('[data-drag-path],[data-drop-container]');
  if(!target) return;
  const isGroup = target.classList.contains('cond-group');
  const isItem = target.classList.contains('cond-item');
  const isContainer = target.hasAttribute('data-drop-container') && !target.hasAttribute('data-drag-path');
  // Determine position
  let position = 'after';
  let targetPath = target.dataset.dragPath || '';
  let containerPath = target.dataset.dropContainer || '';
  if(isGroup && e.target.closest('.cond-group-head')){
    // Dropping into group
    position = 'into';
    targetPath = target.dataset.dragPath;
    containerPath = targetPath;
  } else if(isItem){
    const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
    const midY = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
    position = e.clientY < midY ? 'before' : 'after';
  } else if(isContainer){
    position = 'end';
    containerPath = target.dataset.dropContainer || '';
  }
  // Validate: block self/descendant drop
  if(targetPath && (dragSrcPath === targetPath || isAncestor(dragSrcPath, targetPath))) {
    e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'none';
    clearDropIndicators();
    return;
  }
  // Update indicators
  clearDropIndicators();
  if(position === 'into'){
    target.classList.add('drop-into');
  } else if(position === 'before'){
    target.classList.add('drop-before');
  } else if(position === 'after'){
    target.classList.add('drop-after');
  } else if(position === 'end'){
    target.classList.add('drop-target-end');
  }
  dropTarget = { containerPath, targetPath, position };
}

function handleDragLeave(e){
  // small delay to avoid flicker
  setTimeout(() => {
    const t = e.target;
    if(t && t.classList) t.classList.remove('drop-before','drop-after','drop-into','drop-before-group','drop-after-group','drop-target-end');
  }, 50);
}

function handleDrop(e){
  if(!dragSrcPath || !dropTarget) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const { containerPath, targetPath, position } = dropTarget;
  // Validate
  if(dragSrcPath === targetPath) { clearDragState(); return; }
  if(isAncestor(dragSrcPath, targetPath)) { clearDragState(); return; }
  // Determine insertion index
  let insertIndex = 0;
  const targetArr = containerPath === '' ? wizState.conditions.rules : getPathInfo(containerPath)?.item?.rules;
  if(!targetArr){ clearDragState(); return; }
  if(position === 'into' || position === 'end'){
    insertIndex = targetArr.length;
  } else if(targetPath){
    const targetInfo = getPathInfo(targetPath);
    if(targetInfo){
      insertIndex = position === 'before' ? targetInfo.index : targetInfo.index + 1;
    }
  }
  // Adjust index if same container and src before target
  const srcInfo = getPathInfo(dragSrcPath);
  if(srcInfo && srcInfo.parentPath === containerPath && srcInfo.index < insertIndex){
    insertIndex--;
  }
  // Remove source
  const item = removeAtPath(dragSrcPath);
  if(!item){ clearDragState(); return; }
  // Insert at target
  insertAtPath(containerPath, insertIndex, item);
  // Re-render
  wizRenderConditions();
  // Flash moved item
  setTimeout(() => flashMovedItem(item.logic ? 'g' + wizState.conditions.rules.indexOf(item) : 'c' + wizState.conditions.rules.indexOf(item)), 50);
  // FLIP
  playFlip();
  clearDragState();
}

function handleDragEnd(e){
  clearDragState();
}

FILE: /mnt/data/phase23work/TPA-Pro-main/js/rule-wizard-dnd.js

// rule-wizard-dnd.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let dragSrcPath = null, dragSrcParent = null, dragSrcIndex = -1;
let dragGhost = null, dropTarget = null, flipFirst = null, rafId = null;
let dragThresholdMet = false, dragStartX = 0, dragStartY = 0;
let autoScrollInterval = null;
let undoStack = [];

function getPathInfo(path){
  if(!path) return null;
  const nums = String(path).match(/\d+/g);
  if(!nums) return null;
  const idxs = nums.map(Number);
  let parentArr = wizState.conditions.rules;
  let parentPath = '';
  for(let i=0;i<idxs.length-1;i++){
    const node = parentArr[idxs[i]];
    if(!node || !Array.isArray(node.rules)) return null;
    parentArr = node.rules;
    // rebuild parentPath
    if(i===0) parentPath = (node.logic?'g':'c')+idxs[i];
    else parentPath += '_' + (node.rules && node.rules[idxs[i+1]] && node.rules[idxs[i+1]].logic ? 'g' : 'c') + idxs[i+1];
  }
  const index = idxs[idxs.length-1];
  if(index < 0 || index >= parentArr.length) return null;
  return { parentArray: parentArr, index, item: parentArr[index], parentPath: idxs.length===1 ? '' : path.split('_').slice(0,-1).join('_') };
}

function getParentPath(path){
  if(!path) return '';
  const parts = String(path).split('_');
  return parts.length <= 1 ? '' : parts.slice(0,-1).join('_');
}

function isAncestor(srcPath, targetPath){
  if(!srcPath || !targetPath) return false;
  return String(targetPath).startsWith(String(srcPath)+'_');
}

function removeAtPath(path){
  const info = getPathInfo(path);
  if(!info) return null;
  const [removed] = info.parentArray.splice(info.index, 1);
  return removed;
}

function insertAtPath(containerPath, index, item){
  const targetArr = containerPath === '' ? wizState.conditions.rules : (() => {
    const info = getPathInfo(containerPath);
    return info ? info.item.rules : null;
  })();
  if(!targetArr) return false;
  const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(index, targetArr.length));
  targetArr.splice(idx, 0, item);
  return true;
}

function moveCondition(path, direction){
  const info = getPathInfo(path);
  if(!info) return;
  const arr = info.parentArray;
  const idx = info.index;
  const parentPath = info.parentPath;
  if(direction === 'up'){
    if(idx > 0){
      [arr[idx-1], arr[idx]] = [arr[idx], arr[idx-1]];
    } else if(parentPath !== ''){
      // move out of group: insert before the group in parent
      const item = arr.splice(idx, 1)[0];
      const groupInfo = getPathInfo(parentPath);
      if(groupInfo){
        const groupParent = groupInfo.parentArray;
        const groupIdx = groupInfo.index;
        groupParent.splice(groupIdx, 0, item);
      }
    }
  } else {
    if(idx < arr.length - 1){
      [arr[idx], arr[idx+1]] = [arr[idx+1], arr[idx]];
    } else if(parentPath !== ''){
      const item = arr.splice(idx, 1)[0];
      const groupInfo = getPathInfo(parentPath);
      if(groupInfo){
        const groupParent = groupInfo.parentArray;
        const groupIdx = groupInfo.index;
        groupParent.splice(groupIdx + 1, 0, item);
      }
    }
  }
  wizRenderConditions();
  setTimeout(() => flashMovedItem(path), 50);
}

function initDragDrop(){
  const root = document.getElementById('condList');
  if(!root || root.dataset.dndInit === '1') return;
  root.dataset.dndInit = '1';
  root.addEventListener('dragstart', handleDragStart);
  root.addEventListener('drag', handleDrag);
  root.addEventListener('dragover', handleDragOver);
  root.addEventListener('dragleave', handleDragLeave);
  root.addEventListener('drop', handleDrop);
  root.addEventListener('dragend', handleDragEnd);
  // window-level cleanup
  window.addEventListener('blur', clearDragState);
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if(document.hidden) clearDragState(); });
  // keyboard
  root.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if(!e.altKey) return;
    const handle = e.target.closest('.cond-drag-handle');
    if(!handle) return;
    const item = handle.closest('[data-drag-path]');
    if(!item) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    if(e.key === 'ArrowUp') moveCondition(item.dataset.dragPath, 'up');
    else if(e.key === 'ArrowDown') moveCondition(item.dataset.dragPath, 'down');
  });
}
// Bind reorder buttons (called from wizBindConditionHandlers or after render)

function bindReorderButtons(){
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-move-up]').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', e => { e.stopPropagation(); moveCondition(btn.dataset.moveUp, 'up'); });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-move-down]').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', e => { e.stopPropagation(); moveCondition(btn.dataset.moveDown, 'down'); });
  });
}

function wizSyncInputs(){
  if(wizState.step === 2){
    wizState.name = $('#wizName').value.trim(); wizState.notes = $('#wizNotes').value.trim();
    if(wizState.type !== 'composite') wizState.rate = Number($('#wizRate').value)||0;
    if(wizState.type === 'custom') wizState.action = $('#wizAction').value;
    else if(wizState.type === 'coverage_cap') wizState.action = 'apply_cap';
    else if(wizState.type === 'retiree_copay') wizState.action = 'apply_copay';
    else if(wizState.type === 'composite') wizState.action = 'composite';
    if(wizState.type === 'composite'){ wizState.compositeConfig.applyMode = $('#wizCompositeMode').value; wizState.compositeConfig.requireAllMatch = $('#wizRequireAll').checked; }
    const te = $('#wizTiersEnable'); if(te) wizState.tiersEnabled = te.checked;
    wizState.requires.mode = $('#wizRequiresMode').value;
  }
  if(wizState.step === 3){ wizState.from = $('#wizFrom').value; wizState.to = $('#wizTo').value; }
  if(wizState.step === 4) wizState.conditions.logic = $('#condLogic').value;
}
function wizValidate(step){
  if(step === 1){ if(!wizState.type){ showWarning('تنبيه','اختر'); return false; } return true; }
  if(step === 2){
    if(!wizState.name.trim()){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الاسم'); return false; }
    if(wizState.type === 'composite'){ if(!wizState.compositeConfig.childRuleIds.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','اختر'); return false; } }
    else if(!wizState.rate || wizState.rate <= 0){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل النسبة'); return false; }
    return true;
  }
  if(step === 3){
    if(!wizState.from){ showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل السريان'); return false; }
    if(wizState.to && wizState.to < wizState.from){ showWarning('تنبيه','النهاية بعد'); return false; }
    return true;
  }
  if(step === 4){
    const c = wizState.conditions || { logic:'AND', rules:[] };
    const rules = c.rules || [];
    for (const r of rules) {
      if (r.logic && Array.isArray(r.rules)) continue;
      if (!r.field || !r.op) { showWarning('تنبيه','أكمل شروط الخطوة 4 أو امسحها'); return false; }
    }
    return true;
  }
  return true;
}
function wizGoTo(step){
  if(step < wizState.step){ wizSyncInputs(); wizState.step = step; wizRenderStep(); return; }
  for(let s = wizState.step; s < step; s++){ wizSyncInputs(); if(!wizValidate(s)) return; }
  wizState.step = step; wizRenderStep();
}
function wizNext(){ wizSyncInputs(); if(!wizValidate(wizState.step)) return; if(wizState.step === 6){ wizSave(); return; } wizState.step++; wizRenderStep(); }
function wizBack(){ wizSyncInputs(); if(wizState.step > 1){ wizState.step--; wizRenderStep(); } }
function _wizBuildRule(){
  const y = new Date(wizState.from).getFullYear(), pre = 'DR-'+y+'-';
  let m = 0;
  deductionRules.forEach(x => { if(x.id.startsWith(pre)){ const n = parseInt(x.id.split('-').pop(), 10); if(!isNaN(n)) m = Math.max(m,n); } });
  return { id:pre+String(m+1).padStart(3,'0'), name:wizState.name, type:wizState.type, action:wizState.action, rate:wizState.type === 'composite' ? 0 : wizState.rate, isComposite:wizState.type === 'composite', compositeConfig:wizState.type === 'composite' ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(wizState.compositeConfig)) : null, requires:(wizState.requires.ruleIds && wizState.requires.ruleIds.length) ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(wizState.requires)) : null, scope:{ providerIds:[...wizState.providerIds], contractIds:[...wizState.contractIds], claimTypes:[...wizState.ctypes] }, conditions:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(wizState.conditions)), effectiveFrom:wizState.from, effectiveTo:wizState.to||'', active:true, enabled:true, notes:wizState.notes, tiers: (wizState.tiersEnabled && wizState.tiers && wizState.tiers.length) ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(wizState.tiers)) : null, tiersEnabled: !!wizState.tiersEnabled };
}
function _wizDoSave(){
  const rule = _wizBuildRule();
  deductionRules.push(rule); closeDrWizard(); renderDeductionRules(); showSuccess('تم', rule.id);
  if (typeof claims !== 'undefined') { claims.forEach(c => { if (typeof recalcClaim === 'function') recalcClaim(c); }); }
  if (typeof persistData === 'function') persistData(true);
  if (typeof renderAll === 'function') renderAll();
}
function wizSave(){
  wizSyncInputs();
  // tiers overlap check
  if (wizState.tiersEnabled && Array.isArray(wizState.tiers) && wizState.tiers.length > 1) {
    const sorted = [...wizState.tiers].sort((a,b) => (Number(a.from)||0) - (Number(b.from)||0));
    for (let i = 1; i < sorted.length; i++) {
      if ((Number(sorted[i].from)||0) < (Number(sorted[i-1].to)||Infinity)) { showWarning('تنبيه','تداخل في شرائح التسعير'); wizState.step = 2; wizRenderStep(); return; }
    }
  }
  for (const s of [1,2,3,4,5]) { if (!wizValidate(s)) { wizState.step = s; wizRenderStep(); return; } }
  // conflict detection
  const draftRule = _wizBuildRule();
  const cd = (typeof detectRuleConflicts === 'function') ? detectRuleConflicts(draftRule, deductionRules) : { hasConflicts:false, conflicts:[] };
  if(cd.hasConflicts){
    const msg = cd.conflicts.map(c => `• [${c.severity}] ${c.message}`).join('\n');
    openDialog({ type:'warning', title:'تعارضات محتملة في القاعدة', message: msg + '\n\nهل تريد المتابعة والحفظ رغم التعارضات؟', confirmText:'حفظ رغم ذلك', cancelText:'إلغاء', onConfirm: () => { _wizDoSave(); }, onCancel: () => {} });
    return;
  }
  _wizDoSave();
}
