// services.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let servicesSearchTerm = '', servicesFilter = 'all', servicesSort = 'name', servicesSortDir = 'asc';
let servicesAdv = { code:'', name:'', category:'', tariff:'', min:'', max:'' };
let servicesAdvOpen = false;
let editingServiceForm = {tariffId:null,index:null};
let servicesTariffFilter = 'all';

function getAllServicesFlat(){
  const out = [];
  (typeof tariffs !== 'undefined' ? tariffs : []).forEach(t => {
    (t.services||[]).forEach((s, idx) => { out.push({ code:s.code, name:s.name, nameEn:s.nameEn||'', category:s.category, tariff:t.name, tariffId:t.id, index:idx, base:s.basePrice, current:s.currentPrice, covered:!!s.covered }); });
  });
  return out;
}

function renderServicesTariffFilter(){
  const sel = $('#servicesTariffFilter'); if(!sel) return;
  const cur = sel.value || 'all';
  sel.innerHTML = '<option value="all">كل اللوائح</option>' + (tariffs||[]).map(t => `<option value="${escapeHtml(t.id)}">${escapeHtml(t.name)}</option>`).join('');
  sel.value = (tariffs||[]).some(t=>t.id===cur) ? cur : 'all';
}

function renderServices(){
  renderServicesTariffFilter();
  let list = getAllServicesFlat();
  if(servicesTariffFilter !== 'all') list = list.filter(s => s.tariffId === servicesTariffFilter);
  if(servicesSearchTerm){
    const q = servicesSearchTerm.toLowerCase();
    list = list.filter(s => (s.code||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (s.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (s.nameEn||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (s.category||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (s.tariff||'').toLowerCase().includes(q));
  }
  if(servicesFilter === 'covered') list = list.filter(s => s.covered);
  if(servicesFilter === 'uncovered') list = list.filter(s => !s.covered);
  if(servicesAdv.code){ const q=servicesAdv.code.toLowerCase(); list=list.filter(s=>(s.code||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)); }
  if(servicesAdv.name){ const q=servicesAdv.name.toLowerCase(); list=list.filter(s=>(s.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(s.nameEn||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)); }
  if(servicesAdv.category){ const q=servicesAdv.category.toLowerCase(); list=list.filter(s=>(s.category||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)); }
  if(servicesAdv.tariff){ const q=servicesAdv.tariff.toLowerCase(); list=list.filter(s=>(s.tariff||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)); }
  if(servicesAdv.min){ const n=Number(servicesAdv.min); list=list.filter(s=>(s.base||0)>=n); }
  if(servicesAdv.max){ const n=Number(servicesAdv.max); list=list.filter(s=>(s.base||0)<=n); }
  const dir = servicesSortDir === 'desc' ? -1 : 1;
  list.sort((a,b) => {
    let va, vb;
    switch(servicesSort){
      case 'code': va=a.code||''; vb=b.code||''; break;
      case 'base': va=a.base||0; vb=b.base||0; break;
      case 'current': va=a.current||0; vb=b.current||0; break;
      case 'tariff': va=a.tariff||''; vb=b.tariff||''; break;
      case 'category': va=a.category||''; vb=b.category||''; break;
      default: va=a.name||''; vb=b.name||'';
    }
    if(typeof va === 'string') return va.localeCompare(vb)*dir;
    return (va-vb)*dir;
  });

  const body = $('#servicesBody'); if(!body) return;
  if(!list.length){ body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;padding:36px;color:var(--muted)">لا خدمات</td></tr>'; }
  else body.innerHTML = list.map(s => `<tr>
    <td class="mono">${escapeHtml(s.code)}</td>
    <td>${escapeHtml(s.name)}${s.nameEn?`<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);direction:ltr;text-align:right">${escapeHtml(s.nameEn)}</div>`:''}</td>
    <td>${escapeHtml(s.category||'—')}</td>
    <td>${escapeHtml(s.tariff)}</td>
    <td class="mono">${fmt(s.base||0)}</td>
    <td class="mono">${fmt(s.current||0)}</td>
    <td>${s.covered?'<span class="badge ok sm">مغطاة</span>':'<span class="badge bad sm">غير مغطاة</span>'}</td>
    <td class="act"><div style="display:flex;gap:5px;align-items:center"><button class="btn-xs" data-edit-service="${s.tariffId}|${s.index}">تعديل</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-del-service="${s.tariffId}|${s.index}">حذف</button></div></td>
  </tr>`).join('');
  const kpi = $('#servicesKpi');
  if(kpi){ kpi.innerHTML = `<div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">الخدمات</div><div class="kpi-val">${list.length} <small>خدمة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('shield')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">من أصل ${getAllServicesFlat().length}</div></div>`; }
}

function showServiceFormPanel(tariffId, idx){
  if(typeof tariffs === 'undefined') return;
  if(!tariffs.length){
    const t = { id: nextTariffNumber(daysFromNow(0)), name:'خدمات عامة', type:'general', status:'active', effectiveDate:daysFromNow(0), expiryDate:'', basePriceLevel:100, services:[] };
    tariffs.push(t);
  }
  const sel = $('#servicesTariffFilter');
  const tid = tariffId || ((sel && sel.value !== 'all') ? sel.value : tariffs[0].id);
  const t = tariffs.find(x=>x.id===tid) || tariffs[0];
  // populate tariff select
  const ts = $('#sfTariff'); if(ts){ ts.innerHTML = tariffs.map(x=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(x.id)}">${escapeHtml(x.name)}</option>`).join(''); ts.value = t.id; }
  // reset or load
  const svc = (idx !== null && idx !== undefined && t.services && t.services[idx]) ? t.services[idx] : null;
  editingServiceForm = { tariffId: t.id, index: (svc? idx : null) };
  const tariffSelect=$('#sfTariff'); if(tariffSelect) tariffSelect.disabled=!!svc;
  const eff=$('#servicesEffectiveDate'); if(eff && (!eff.value || (t.effectiveDate && eff.value<=t.effectiveDate))){ eff.value=tariffDatePlusOne(t.effectiveDate||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)); }
  $('#sfCode').value = svc? svc.code : '';
  $('#sfCategory').value = svc? svc.category : 'استشارة';
  $('#sfName').value = svc? svc.name : '';
  $('#sfNameEn').value = svc? (svc.nameEn||'') : '';
  $('#sfBasePrice').value = svc? svc.basePrice : '';
  $('#sfCurrentPrice').value = svc? svc.currentPrice : '';
  $('#sfCovered').checked = svc? !!svc.covered : true;
  $('#sfRequiresPriorApproval').checked = svc? !!svc.requiresPriorApproval : false;
  const panel = $('#serviceFormPanel'); if(panel) panel.style.display = 'block';
}
function hideServiceFormPanel(){ const panel = $('#serviceFormPanel'); if(panel) panel.style.display = 'none'; editingServiceForm = {tariffId:null,index:null}; }
function saveServiceForm(){
  const tid=$('#sfTariff').value, t=tariffs.find(x=>x.id===tid); if(!t){showWarning('تنبيه','اختر اللائحة');return;}
  if(!t.services)t.services=[];
  const code=$('#sfCode').value.trim(),name=$('#sfName').value.trim(),cat=$('#sfCategory').value;
  const bp=Number($('#sfBasePrice').value)||0,cp=Number($('#sfCurrentPrice').value)||0,eff=$('#servicesEffectiveDate').value;
  if(!code){showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الكود');return;} if(!name){showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل الاسم');return;} if(bp<=0){showWarning('تنبيه','أدخل سعراً أساسياً صحيحاً');return;}
  if(!eff){showWarning('تنبيه','حدد تاريخ السريان');return;}
  const nameEn=$('#sfNameEn')?.value.trim()||'';const data={code,name,...(nameEn?{nameEn}:{}),category:cat,basePrice:bp,currentPrice:cp||bp,covered:$('#sfCovered').checked,requiresPriorApproval:$('#sfRequiresPriorApproval').checked};
  const idx=editingServiceForm.index;
  if(idx!==null && editingServiceForm.tariffId!==tid){showWarning('تنبيه','نقل الخدمة بين اللوائح غير متاح من شاشة التعديل؛ أضفها للائحة الجديدة ثم احذف القديمة بتاريخ سريان مستقل');return;}
  const at=idx!==null?idx:t.services.findIndex(x=>String(x.code).toLowerCase()===code.toLowerCase());
  if(idx===null && at>=0){showWarning('تنبيه','الكود مستخدم بالفعل في هذه اللائحة');return;}
  if(t.effectiveDate && eff<=t.effectiveDate){showWarning('تاريخ السريان','يجب أن يكون بعد تاريخ سريان النسخة الحالية حتى لا تتغير الأسعار التاريخية');return;}
  const before=at>=0?{...t.services[at]}:null, action=before?'service-update':'service-add';
  const snapshot={services:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.services)),priceVersions:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.priceVersions||[])),changeLog:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.changeLog||[])),effectiveDate:t.effectiveDate};
  archiveTariffVersion(t,eff,action,[{code,action:before?'update':'add',before,after:{...data}}]);
  if(before)t.services[at]=data;else t.services.push(data);
  const saved=typeof persistData==='function'?persistData(true):true;
  if(saved===false){t.services=snapshot.services;t.priceVersions=snapshot.priceVersions;t.changeLog=snapshot.changeLog;t.effectiveDate=snapshot.effectiveDate;showWarning('خطأ','تعذر الحفظ؛ تم التراجع عن التعديل');return;}
  hideServiceFormPanel();renderServices();renderTariffs();renderTariffChangeHistory();showSuccess('تم','تم حفظ الخدمة بتاريخ سريان مستقل');
}

function editServiceAt(tid, idx){
  const t = tariffs.find(x=>x.id===tid); if(!t || !t.services) return;
  if(typeof openServiceModal === 'function'){ openServiceModal(idx, t.id); }
}

function deleteServiceAt(tid,idx){
  const t=tariffs.find(x=>x.id===tid); if(!t||!t.services||!t.services[idx])return;
  const svc=t.services[idx],eff=$('#servicesEffectiveDate')?.value||'';
  if(!eff){showWarning('حدد السريان','أدخل تاريخ سريان الحذف');return;}
  if(t.effectiveDate&&eff<=t.effectiveDate){showWarning('تاريخ السريان','يجب أن يكون بعد تاريخ سريان النسخة الحالية');return;}
  showDelete('حذف الخدمة',`سيتم إيقاف الخدمة "${svc.name}" اعتبارًا من ${eff} مع الاحتفاظ بسعرها السابق في التاريخ.`,()=>{
    const snapshot={services:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.services)),priceVersions:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.priceVersions||[])),changeLog:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.changeLog||[])),effectiveDate:t.effectiveDate};
    archiveTariffVersion(t,eff,'service-delete',[{code:svc.code,action:'delete',before:{...svc},after:null}]);t.services.splice(idx,1);
    const saved=typeof persistData==='function'?persistData(true):true;
    if(saved===false){t.services=snapshot.services;t.priceVersions=snapshot.priceVersions;t.changeLog=snapshot.changeLog;t.effectiveDate=snapshot.effectiveDate;showWarning('خطأ','تعذر الحفظ؛ تم التراجع عن الحذف');return;}
    renderServices();renderTariffs();renderTariffChangeHistory();showSuccess('تم','تم الحذف بتاريخ سريان مع حفظ التاريخ');
  });
}


// Parse RFC-4180-style CSV, including quoted commas, quotes and embedded newlines.
function parseServicesCSV(text, limits = {}){
  const maxChars = limits.maxChars || 5 * 1024 * 1024;
  const maxRows = limits.maxRows || 10000;
  const maxColumns = limits.maxColumns || 32;
  const source = String(text == null ? '' : text).replace(/^\uFEFF/, '');
  if(source.length > maxChars) throw new Error('حجم الملف أكبر من الحد المسموح (5MB)');
  if(!source.trim()) throw new Error('الملف فارغ');
  const rows=[]; let row=[], cell='', quoted=false, afterQuote=false;
  for(let i=0;i<source.length;i++){
    const ch=source[i];
    if(quoted){
      if(ch==='"'){
        if(source[i+1]==='"'){ cell+='"'; i++; }
        else { quoted=false; afterQuote=true; }
      } else cell+=ch;
      continue;
    }
    if(afterQuote && ch!==',' && ch!=='\r' && ch!=='\n' && ch!==' ' && ch!=='\t') throw new Error('تنسيق CSV غير صالح: نص بعد إغلاق علامات الاقتباس');
    if(ch==='"'){
      if(cell.length || afterQuote) throw new Error('تنسيق CSV غير صالح: علامة اقتباس في موضع غير صحيح');
      quoted=true; continue;
    }
    if(ch===','){ row.push(cell); cell=''; afterQuote=false; if(row.length>=maxColumns) throw new Error('عدد أعمدة CSV يتجاوز الحد المسموح'); continue; }
    if(ch==='\r' || ch==='\n'){
      if(ch==='\r' && source[i+1]==='\n') i++;
      row.push(cell); cell=''; afterQuote=false;
      if(row.some(v=>v.trim()!=='')){ rows.push(row); if(rows.length>maxRows+1) throw new Error('عدد صفوف CSV يتجاوز 10000 صف'); }
      row=[]; continue;
    }
    if(!afterQuote || (ch!==' ' && ch!=='\t')) cell+=ch;
  }
  if(quoted) throw new Error('تنسيق CSV غير صالح: علامات اقتباس غير مغلقة');
  row.push(cell); if(row.some(v=>v.trim()!=='')) rows.push(row);
  if(rows.length>maxRows+1) throw new Error('عدد صفوف CSV يتجاوز 10000 صف');
  if(!rows.length) throw new Error('الملف لا يحتوي على بيانات');
  const width=rows[0].length;
  if(width>maxColumns) throw new Error('عدد أعمدة CSV يتجاوز الحد المسموح');
  if(rows.some(r=>r.length!==width)) throw new Error('عدد الأعمدة غير متطابق بين صفوف CSV');
  return rows;
}

async function parseServicesXLSX(file){
  if(typeof DecompressionStream==='undefined') throw new Error('متصفحك لا يدعم قراءة XLSX محليًا؛ احفظ الملف بصيغة CSV أو حدّث المتصفح');
  const buffer=await file.arrayBuffer(), view=new DataView(buffer), bytes=new Uint8Array(buffer);
  let eocd=-1;
  for(let i=Math.max(0,bytes.length-65558);i<=bytes.length-22;i++) if(view.getUint32(i,true)===0x06054b50) eocd=i;
  if(eocd<0) throw new Error('ملف Excel غير صالح أو ليس XLSX');
  const count=view.getUint16(eocd+10,true), cdOffset=view.getUint32(eocd+16,true);
  if(count>5000) throw new Error('ملف XLSX يحتوي على عدد كبير من المكونات');
  const entries=new Map(); let pos=cdOffset;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    if(view.getUint32(pos,true)!==0x02014b50) throw new Error('بنية ملف XLSX غير صالحة');
    const method=view.getUint16(pos+10,true), compSize=view.getUint32(pos+20,true), nameLen=view.getUint16(pos+28,true), extraLen=view.getUint16(pos+30,true), commentLen=view.getUint16(pos+32,true), localOffset=view.getUint32(pos+42,true);
    const name=new TextDecoder().decode(bytes.slice(pos+46,pos+46+nameLen));
    entries.set(name,{method,compSize,localOffset}); pos+=46+nameLen+extraLen+commentLen;
  }
  async function readEntry(name){
    const e=entries.get(name); if(!e) return null;
    const o=e.localOffset; if(view.getUint32(o,true)!==0x04034b50) throw new Error('مدخل XLSX غير صالح');
    const n=view.getUint16(o+26,true), x=view.getUint16(o+28,true), start=o+30+n+x;
    if(e.compSize>10*1024*1024 || start+e.compSize>bytes.length) throw new Error('مكوّن داخل XLSX يتجاوز الحجم المسموح أو تالف');
    const compressed=bytes.slice(start,start+e.compSize);
    if(e.method===0) return new TextDecoder().decode(compressed);
    if(e.method!==8) throw new Error('طريقة ضغط غير مدعومة داخل XLSX');
    const stream=new Blob([compressed]).stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('deflate-raw'));
    const reader=stream.getReader(), chunks=[]; let total=0;
    while(true){const part=await reader.read();if(part.done)break;total+=part.value.byteLength;if(total>20*1024*1024){await reader.cancel();throw new Error('مكوّن Excel بعد فك الضغط أكبر من 20MB');}chunks.push(part.value);}
    const output=new Uint8Array(total);let offset=0;for(const chunk of chunks){output.set(chunk,offset);offset+=chunk.length;}
    return new TextDecoder().decode(output);
  }
  const wb=await readEntry('xl/workbook.xml'); if(!wb) throw new Error('ملف Excel لا يحتوي على مصنف صالح');
  const rel=await readEntry('xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels'); if(!rel) throw new Error('تعذر قراءة علاقات أوراق Excel');
  const parser=new DOMParser(), wbDoc=parser.parseFromString(wb,'application/xml'), relDoc=parser.parseFromString(rel,'application/xml');
  if(wbDoc.getElementsByTagName('parsererror').length||relDoc.getElementsByTagName('parsererror').length) throw new Error('ملف Excel يحتوي على XML غير صالح');
  const sheet=wbDoc.getElementsByTagName('sheet')[0]; if(!sheet) throw new Error('لا توجد ورقة عمل في الملف');
  const rid=sheet.getAttribute('r:id')||sheet.getAttributeNS('http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships','id');
  const relationship=[...relDoc.getElementsByTagName('Relationship')].find(x=>x.getAttribute('Id')===rid);
  if(!relationship) throw new Error('تعذر تحديد ورقة العمل الأولى');
  let target=relationship.getAttribute('Target')||''; target=target.replace(/^\//,''); if(!target.startsWith('xl/')) target='xl/'+target;
  const sheetXml=await readEntry(target); if(!sheetXml) throw new Error('ورقة العمل الأولى غير موجودة');
  const sheetDoc=parser.parseFromString(sheetXml,'application/xml'); if(sheetDoc.getElementsByTagName('parsererror').length) throw new Error('بيانات ورقة Excel غير صالحة');
  const ssXml=await readEntry('xl/sharedStrings.xml');
  const shared=ssXml ? [...parser.parseFromString(ssXml,'application/xml').getElementsByTagName('si')].map(si=>[...si.getElementsByTagName('t')].map(t=>t.textContent).join('')) : [];
  const rows=[];
  for(const rowNode of [...sheetDoc.getElementsByTagName('row')]){
    const row=[];
    for(const cell of [...rowNode.getElementsByTagName('c')]){
      const ref=cell.getAttribute('r')||''; const letters=(ref.match(/[A-Z]+/i)||['A'])[0].toUpperCase();
      let col=0; for(const ch of letters) col=col*26+(ch.charCodeAt(0)-64); col=Math.max(0,col-1);
      const type=cell.getAttribute('t'); let value='';
      if(type==='inlineStr') value=[...cell.getElementsByTagName('t')].map(t=>t.textContent).join('');
      else { const v=cell.getElementsByTagName('v')[0]; value=v?v.textContent:''; if(type==='s') value=shared[Number(value)]??''; }
      row[col]=value;
    }
    rows.push(row.map(v=>v===undefined?'':String(v)));
    if(rows.length>10001) throw new Error('ملف Excel يتجاوز 10000 صف');
  }
  if(!rows.length) throw new Error('ورقة Excel فارغة');
  const width=Math.max(...rows.map(r=>r.length));
  if(width>32) throw new Error('ملف Excel يتجاوز 32 عمودًا');
  return rows;
}
let tariffImportRows=null, tariffImportMapping=null, tariffImportHeaderRow=0;
function normalizeImportHeader(v){return String(v||'').toLowerCase().replace(/[\s_\-ـ]/g,'').replace(/[أإآ]/g,'ا').replace(/[ة]/g,'ه').trim();}
function detectTariffImportLayout(rows){
  const aliases={price:['السعر','سعر','price','baseprice','سعراللائحه','السعرالاساسي'],currentPrice:['السعرالحالي','السعرالفعلي','سعرالتعاقد','السعرالمتفقعليه','currentprice','currentprice'],arabicName:['اسمالتحليل','اسمالخدمه','اسمالخدمه','اسم','arabicname','namear'],englishName:['englishname','اسمبالانجليزي','الاسمالانجليزي','نوعالتحليل'],category:['المجمع','المجموعه','الفئه','التصنيف','category','group'],code:['الكود','كود','كودالخدمه','code','م','رقم']};
  let best={row:0,score:-1};
  for(let r=0;r<Math.min(rows.length,30);r++){
    const cells=(rows[r]||[]).map(normalizeImportHeader); let score=0;
    for(const names of Object.values(aliases)) if(cells.some(c=>names.includes(c))) score++;
    if(score>best.score) best={row:r,score};
  }
  const header=(rows[best.row]||[]).map(normalizeImportHeader), mapping={price:-1,currentPrice:-1,arabicName:-1,englishName:-1,category:-1,code:-1};
  for(const key of Object.keys(aliases)){
    const matches=[]; header.forEach((h,i)=>{if(aliases[key].includes(h))matches.push(i);});
    if(matches.length) mapping[key]=matches[0];
  }
  // Some real-world sheets use misleading/duplicated headings; infer language by sample values.
  const samples=[]; for(let c=0;c<Math.max(...rows.slice(best.row+1,best.row+15).map(r=>r.length),0);c++){
    const vals=rows.slice(best.row+1,best.row+25).map(r=>String((r||[])[c]||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
    const numeric=vals.filter(v=>/^[-+]?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?$/.test(v.replace(/[,٬]/g,''))).length;
    const arabic=vals.filter(v=>/[\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(v)).length;
    const latin=vals.filter(v=>/[A-Za-z]/.test(v)).length;
    samples.push({c,vals,numeric,arabic,latin});
  }
  if(mapping.price<0){const x=samples.filter(x=>x.numeric>=2).sort((a,b)=>b.numeric-a.numeric)[0];if(x)mapping.price=x.c;}
  if(mapping.arabicName<0 || mapping.arabicName===mapping.category){const x=samples.filter(x=>x.arabic>=2&&x.c!==mapping.category&&x.c!==mapping.price).sort((a,b)=>b.arabic-a.arabic)[0];if(x)mapping.arabicName=x.c;}
  if(mapping.englishName<0 || mapping.englishName===mapping.arabicName){const x=samples.filter(x=>x.latin>=2&&x.c!==mapping.arabicName&&x.c!==mapping.price).sort((a,b)=>b.latin-a.latin)[0];if(x)mapping.englishName=x.c;}
  if(mapping.category<0){const x=samples.filter(x=>x.arabic>=1&&x.c!==mapping.arabicName&&x.c!==mapping.price).sort((a,b)=>b.arabic-a.arabic)[0];if(x)mapping.category=x.c;}
  // Prefer actual cell language/cardinality over misleading or duplicated headings.
  const arabicCol=samples.filter(x=>x.arabic>=2&&x.c!==mapping.price).sort((a,b)=>b.arabic-a.arabic)[0];
  if(arabicCol) mapping.arabicName=arabicCol.c;
  const englishCols=samples.filter(x=>x.latin>=2&&x.c!==mapping.price&&x.c!==mapping.arabicName).sort((a,b)=>new Set(b.vals).size-new Set(a.vals).size);
  if(englishCols.length) mapping.englishName=englishCols[0].c;
  // Repeated low-cardinality text (e.g. Hematology) is a group/category column.
  const categoryCol=samples.filter(x=>x.c!==mapping.price&&x.c!==mapping.arabicName&&x.c!==mapping.englishName&&x.numeric===0&&x.vals.length>=2).sort((a,b)=>new Set(a.vals).size-new Set(b.vals).size)[0];
  if(categoryCol) mapping.category=categoryCol.c;
  // A standalone ordinal column is a valid code source; do not mistake it for a price.
  if(mapping.code<0){const x=samples.find(x=>x.c!==mapping.price&&x.c!==mapping.arabicName&&x.c!==mapping.englishName&&x.c!==mapping.category&&x.numeric>=3);if(x)mapping.code=x.c;}
  if(mapping.currentPrice<0){const x=samples.filter(x=>x.c!==mapping.price&&x.c!==mapping.code&&x.numeric>=2).sort((a,b)=>b.numeric-a.numeric)[0];if(x)mapping.currentPrice=x.c;}
  return {headerRow:best.row,mapping,score:best.score};
}
function buildTariffImportRows(rows,mapping,headerRow){
  const result=[['code','name','category','basePrice','currentPrice','covered','requiresPriorApproval','nameEn']];let group='أخرى',serial=0;const usedCodes=new Set();
  const get=(r,k)=>mapping[k]>=0?String((r||[])[mapping[k]]??'').trim():'';
  for(let i=headerRow+1;i<rows.length;i++){
    const row=rows[i]||[], ar=get(row,'arabicName'), en=get(row,'englishName'), priceRaw=get(row,'price'), currentRaw=get(row,'currentPrice'), cat=get(row,'category'), codeRaw=get(row,'code');
    const hasPrice=priceRaw!==''&&Number.isFinite(Number(priceRaw.replace(/[٬,،\s]/g,'').replace(/[٠-٩]/g,d=>'٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩'.indexOf(d))));
    if(cat && !ar && !en && !hasPrice){group=cat;continue;}
    if(!ar&&!en&&!hasPrice)continue;
    if(!hasPrice)continue;
    const name=ar||en; if(!name)continue;
    serial++; const category=cat||group;let code=codeRaw||`IMP-${String(serial).padStart(5,'0')}`;if(usedCodes.has(code.toLowerCase())){const prefix=category.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]+/g,'').slice(0,8).toUpperCase()||'IMP';code=`${prefix}-${code}`;let suffix=2;while(usedCodes.has(code.toLowerCase()))code=`${prefix}-${codeRaw||serial}-${suffix++}`;}usedCodes.add(code.toLowerCase());
    const price=priceRaw;
    result.push([code, ar||en, category, price, currentRaw||price, '', '', en]);
  }
  return result;
}
function excelImportColumnLabel(index){let n=index+1,label='';while(n){const rem=(n-1)%26;label=String.fromCharCode(65+rem)+label;n=Math.floor((n-1)/26);}return label;}
function renderTariffImportMapping(rows,layout){
  tariffImportRows=rows;tariffImportMapping={...layout.mapping};tariffImportHeaderRow=layout.headerRow;
  const panel=$('#tariffImportMappingPanel');if(!panel)return;
  const width=Math.max(...rows.map(r=>r.length));const options='<option value="-1">— بدون ربط —</option>'+Array.from({length:width},(_,i)=>`<option value="${i}">عمود ${excelImportColumnLabel(i)} — ${escapeHtml(String((rows[layout.headerRow]||[])[i]||'بدون عنوان'))}</option>`).join('');
  const fields=[['price','السعر الأساسي *'],['currentPrice','السعر الحالي / التعاقد (اختياري)'],['arabicName','اسم الخدمة بالعربية'],['englishName','الاسم الإنجليزي'],['category','المجموعة / التصنيف'],['code','الكود / الرقم (اختياري)']];
  panel.innerHTML=`<div class="panel" style="padding:12px;margin-top:12px"><b>خريطة ربط الأعمدة</b><p class="hint">تم اكتشاف صف العناوين ${layout.headerRow+1}. راجع الربط قبل الاستيراد. الكود غير الموجود يُنشأ تلقائيًا، وصفوف المجموعات تُستخدم كتصنيف للخدمات التالية.</p><div class="form-grid">${fields.map(([key,label])=>`<div class="fg"><label>${label}</label><select data-import-map="${key}">${options}</select></div>`).join('')}</div><div id="tariffImportPreview" style="overflow:auto;margin-top:10px"></div></div>`;
  fields.forEach(([key])=>{const el=panel.querySelector(`[data-import-map="${key}"]`);el.value=String(tariffImportMapping[key]);el.addEventListener('change',()=>{tariffImportMapping[key]=Number(el.value);renderTariffImportPreview();});});
  panel.style.display='block';renderTariffImportPreview();
}
function renderTariffImportPreview(){
  const box=$('#tariffImportPreview');if(!box||!tariffImportRows)return;
  const mapped=buildTariffImportRows(tariffImportRows,tariffImportMapping,tariffImportHeaderRow).slice(1,6);
  box.innerHTML=`<div class="hint">معاينة أول ${mapped.length} خدمات مكتشفة</div><table class="data-table" style="min-width:500px"><thead><tr><th>الكود</th><th>الخدمة</th><th>المجموعة</th><th>الأساسي</th><th>الحالي</th></tr></thead><tbody>${mapped.map(r=>`<tr>${[r[0],r[1],r[2],r[3],r[4]].map(v=>`<td>${escapeHtml(v)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`;
}
function importServicesRows(rows, options={}){
  try{
    if(!Array.isArray(rows)||rows.length<2) throw new Error('الملف لا يحتوي على صفوف خدمات');
    const header=rows[0].map(h=>String(h||'').trim().toLowerCase());
    const idx=(names)=>{ for(const n of names){ const i=header.indexOf(n); if(i>=0) return i; } return -1; };
    const iCode=idx(['code','الكود','كود','كود الخدمة','service code']), iName=idx(['name','اسم الخدمة','اسم','service','الخدمة']), iNameEn=idx(['nameen','english name','الاسم الانجليزي','اسم بالانجليزي']);
    const iCat=idx(['category','الفئة','فئة']), iBase=idx(['baseprice','base price','السعر الأساسي','السعر الاساسي','سعر اللائحة','الأساسي','base']);
    const iCur=idx(['currentprice','current price','السعر الحالي','الحالي','سعر الخدمة','current','السعر']);
    const iCov=idx(['covered','مغطى','التغطية']), iPA=idx(['requirespriorapproval','requires pa','موافقة مسبقة','يتطلب موافقة']);
    if(iCode<0||iName<0||iBase<0) throw new Error('الأعمدة المطلوبة: الكود، اسم الخدمة، السعر الأساسي');
    let tariff=options.createTariff ? null : tariffs.find(t=>t.id===options.tariffId); if(!options.createTariff && !tariff) throw new Error('اختر اللائحة المراد تحديثها');
    const eff=String(options.effectiveDate||(options.createTariff&&options.createTariff.effectiveDate)||'').slice(0,10); if(!eff) throw new Error('حدد تاريخ سريان التحديث');
    if(tariff && tariff.effectiveDate && eff<=tariff.effectiveDate) throw new Error('يجب أن يكون تاريخ السريان بعد تاريخ النسخة الحالية لحفظ الأسعار التاريخية');
    const seen=new Set(), incoming=[];
    const val=(r,i)=>i<0?'':String(r[i]??'').trim();
    const num=(raw)=>Number(String(raw).replace(/[٬,،\s]/g,'').replace(/[٠-٩]/g,d=>'٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩'.indexOf(d)));
    for(let r=1;r<rows.length;r++){
      const row=rows[r]||[]; if(!row.some(x=>String(x||'').trim())) continue;
      const code=val(row,iCode), name=val(row,iName), baseText=val(row,iBase), currentText=val(row,iCur);
      if(!code||!name) throw new Error(`الكود أو اسم الخدمة مفقود في الصف ${r+1}`);
      const key=code.toLowerCase(); if(seen.has(key)) throw new Error(`الكود ${code} مكرر في الملف (صف ${r+1})`); seen.add(key);
      const base=num(baseText), current=currentText?num(currentText):base;
      if(!Number.isFinite(base)||base<=0||!Number.isFinite(current)||current<0) throw new Error(`السعر غير صالح في الصف ${r+1}`);
      const cov=val(row,iCov).toLowerCase(), pa=val(row,iPA).toLowerCase();
      incoming.push({code,name,...(iNameEn>=0&&val(row,iNameEn)?{nameEn:val(row,iNameEn)}:{}),category:val(row,iCat)||'أخرى',basePrice:base,currentPrice:current,covered:!(['false','0','no','لا','غير مغطى','غير مغطاة'].includes(cov)),requiresPriorApproval:['true','1','yes','نعم','مطلوب'].includes(pa)});
    }
    if(!incoming.length) throw new Error('لا توجد خدمات صالحة للاستيراد');
    if(options.createTariff){
      const nm=String(options.createTariff.name||'').trim(); if(!nm) throw new Error('أدخل اسم اللائحة الجديدة');
      if(tariffs.some(t=>t.name.toLowerCase()===nm.toLowerCase())) throw new Error('اسم اللائحة مستخدم بالفعل');
      tariff={id:nextTariffNumber(eff),name:nm,type:options.createTariff.type||'general',description:'مستوردة من Excel',effectiveDate:eff,expiryDate:'',status:'active',basePriceLevel:100,services:[],priceVersions:[],changeLog:[]};
    }
    const oldServices=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tariff.services||[]));
    const nextServices=oldServices.map(x=>({...x})); const changes=[];
    incoming.forEach(s=>{ const at=nextServices.findIndex(x=>String(x.code).toLowerCase()===s.code.toLowerCase());
      if(at>=0){ const before=nextServices[at]; if(JSON.stringify(before)!==JSON.stringify(s)){ changes.push({code:s.code,action:'update',before:{...before},after:{...s}}); nextServices[at]=s; } }
      else { changes.push({code:s.code,action:'add',before:null,after:{...s}}); nextServices.push(s); }
    });
    if(!changes.length) throw new Error('الملف مطابق للأسعار الحالية؛ لا توجد تغييرات لتطبيقها');
    const snapshot={services:oldServices,priceVersions:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tariff.priceVersions||[])),changeLog:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tariff.changeLog||[])),effectiveDate:tariff.effectiveDate};
    if(options.createTariff){ tariff.services=nextServices; tariff.changeLog.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),effectiveDate:eff,action:'excel-create',actor:(appSettings.reviewer&&appSettings.reviewer.name)||'المستخدم',changes}); tariffs.push(tariff); }
    else { archiveTariffVersion(tariff,eff,'excel-import',changes); tariff.services=nextServices; }
    const saved=typeof persistData==='function'?persistData(true):true;
    if(saved===false){ if(options.createTariff) tariffs=tariffs.filter(t=>t.id!==tariff.id); else {tariff.services=snapshot.services; tariff.priceVersions=snapshot.priceVersions; tariff.changeLog=snapshot.changeLog; tariff.effectiveDate=snapshot.effectiveDate;} throw new Error('تعذر الحفظ؛ تم التراجع عن الاستيراد'); }
    try { renderServices(); if(typeof renderTariffs==='function') renderTariffs(); renderTariffChangeHistory(); } catch(uiErr){ console.warn('Tariff import committed; UI refresh will retry on navigation',uiErr); }
    showSuccess('تم الاستيراد',`تم تحديث ${changes.length} خدمة اعتبارًا من ${eff}`);
    return {ok:true,count:changes.length};
  }catch(err){ showWarning('تعذر الاستيراد',err&&err.message?err.message:'ملف غير صالح'); return {ok:false,error:err&&err.message?err.message:'Import failed'}; }
}
function importServicesCSV(text, options={}){ try { return importServicesRows(parseServicesCSV(text),options); } catch(err){ showWarning('تعذر الاستيراد',err&&err.message?err.message:'CSV غير صالح'); return {ok:false,error:err.message}; } }
async function importServicesFile(file, options={}){
  if(!file) return; if(file.size>10*1024*1024) { showWarning('تعذر الاستيراد','حجم الملف أكبر من 10MB'); return; }
  try{
    const ext=(file.name.split('.').pop()||'').toLowerCase();
    if(ext==='xlsx'){ const rows=await parseServicesXLSX(file); return importServicesRows(rows,options); }
    if(ext==='csv'||ext==='txt'){ const text=await file.text(); return importServicesCSV(text,options); }
    throw new Error('الصيغ المدعومة: XLSX وCSV. صيغة XLS القديمة غير مدعومة');
  }catch(err){ showWarning('تعذر الاستيراد',err&&err.message?err.message:'تعذرت قراءة الملف'); return {ok:false,error:err.message}; }
}
function openTariffImportModal(){
  const target=$('#tariffImportTarget'); if(!target)return;
  target.innerHTML='<option value="new">إنشاء لائحة جديدة من الملف</option>'+tariffs.map(t=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(t.id)}">تحديث: ${escapeHtml(t.name)} (${escapeHtml(t.id)})</option>`).join('');
  $('#tariffImportEffective').value=tariffDatePlusOne(new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10));
  $('#tariffImportName').value=''; $('#tariffImportType').value='general'; $('#tariffImportFile').value=''; $('#tariffImportNewFields').style.display='block'; $('#confirmTariffImport').textContent='بدء الاستيراد';
  const panel=$('#tariffImportMappingPanel');if(panel){panel.style.display='none';panel.innerHTML='';}tariffImportRows=null;tariffImportMapping=null;
  target.onchange=()=>{ $('#tariffImportNewFields').style.display=target.value==='new'?'block':'none'; const t=tariffs.find(x=>x.id===target.value); if(t){$('#tariffImportEffective').value=tariffDatePlusOne(t.effectiveDate||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10));} };
  const file=$('#tariffImportFile');file.onchange=async()=>{const f=file.files&&file.files[0];if(!f)return;try{const ext=(f.name.split('.').pop()||'').toLowerCase();const rows=ext==='xlsx'?await parseServicesXLSX(f):parseServicesCSV(await f.text());const layout=detectTariffImportLayout(rows);renderTariffImportMapping(rows,layout);$('#confirmTariffImport').textContent='اعتماد الخريطة والاستيراد';}catch(err){showWarning('تعذر تحليل الملف',err.message||'تعذر قراءة الأعمدة');}};
  $('#tariffImportModal').classList.add('open');
}
async function confirmTariffImport(){
  const file=$('#tariffImportFile').files&&$('#tariffImportFile').files[0]; if(!file){showWarning('اختر ملفًا','حدد ملف Excel أو CSV أولًا');return;}
  const target=$('#tariffImportTarget').value, eff=$('#tariffImportEffective').value;
  if(!eff){showWarning('حدد السريان','أدخل تاريخ السريان');return;}
  if(!tariffImportRows||!tariffImportMapping){showWarning('راجع خريطة الأعمدة','اختر الملف وانتظر ظهور معاينة الربط');return;}
  if(tariffImportMapping.price<0||tariffImportMapping.arabicName<0&&tariffImportMapping.englishName<0){showWarning('خريطة غير مكتملة','يجب ربط عمود السعر وعمود اسم الخدمة على الأقل');return;}
  const rows=buildTariffImportRows(tariffImportRows,tariffImportMapping,tariffImportHeaderRow);
  if(rows.length<2){showWarning('لا توجد خدمات','لم يتم اكتشاف صفوف خدمات بأسعار صالحة');return;}
  const opts=target==='new'?{createTariff:{name:$('#tariffImportName').value.trim(),type:$('#tariffImportType').value,effectiveDate:eff},effectiveDate:eff}:{tariffId:target,effectiveDate:eff};
  const result=importServicesRows(rows,opts); if(result&&result.ok){$('#tariffImportModal').classList.remove('open');renderAll();tariffImportRows=null;tariffImportMapping=null;}
}
function renderTariffChangeHistory(){
  const box=$('#tariffChangeHistory'); if(!box) return;
  const entries=[]; (tariffs||[]).forEach(t=>(t.changeLog||[]).forEach(x=>entries.push({...x,tariffId:t.id,tariffName:t.name})));
  entries.sort((a,b)=>String(b.at).localeCompare(String(a.at)));
  if(!entries.length){ box.innerHTML='<div style="padding:12px;color:var(--muted)">لا توجد تغييرات مسجلة حتى الآن</div>'; return; }
  box.innerHTML=entries.map(e=>`<div class="panel" style="padding:10px;margin:8px 0"><b>${escapeHtml(e.tariffName)} (${escapeHtml(e.tariffId)})</b><div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted)">${escapeHtml(e.action)} · سريان ${escapeHtml(e.effectiveDate||'—')} · ${escapeHtml(e.actor||'—')} · ${escapeHtml(e.at||'')}</div><div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:5px">${(e.changes||[]).map(c=>`${escapeHtml(c.action)}: ${escapeHtml(c.code)}`).join('، ')}</div></div>`).join('');
}

  }); }

// sessions-2.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function bindSessionsControls(){
  const s = document.getElementById('sessionsSearch');
  const c = document.getElementById('sessionsFilterChips');
  const n = document.getElementById('newSessionBtn');
  const l = document.getElementById('sessionsList');
  const sw = document.getElementById('rvSessionSwitcher');
  if(s){ let t; s.addEventListener('input', e => { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(() => { sessionsSearch = e.target.value.trim(); renderSessions(); }, 180); }); }
  if(c){ c.addEventListener('click', e => { const chip = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!chip) return; $$('#sessionsFilterChips .chip').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('active')); chip.classList.add('active'); sessionsFilter = chip.dataset.sfilter; renderSessions(); }); }
  if(n){ n.addEventListener('click', () => { navHistory = []; navigateTo('claims', {noPush:true}); showToast('اختر مطالبة واضغط «مراجعة»'); }); }
  if(l){
    l.addEventListener('click', e => {
      const r = e.target.closest('[data-resume-session]');
      if(r){ const ss = workSessions.find(x => x.id === r.dataset.resumeSession); if(ss){ const info = getSessionClaimInfo(ss); if(info.stale){ deleteSessionWithDialog(ss.id); return; } if(activeSessionId && activeSessionId !== ss.id){ upsertCurrentSession(); activeClaim = null; workingInvoices = []; } resumeSession(ss.id); } return; }
      const d = e.target.closest('[data-delete-session]'); if(d) deleteSessionWithDialog(d.dataset.deleteSession);
    });
  }
  if(sw){ sw.addEventListener('change', e => { const sid = e.target.value; if(!sid) return; upsertCurrentSession(); activeClaim = null; workingInvoices = []; $('#reviewModal').classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); rvActiveInvoiceIdx = 0; rvActivePatientIdx = 0; activeSessionId = null; resumeSession(sid); }); }
}
function deleteSessionWithDialog(id){
  const s = workSessions.find(x => x.id === id); if(!s) return;
  const info = getSessionClaimInfo(s);
  showDelete('حذف الجلسة', `"${s.claimId} — ${info.patient}".`, () => {
    workSessions = workSessions.filter(x => x.id !== id);
    if(activeSessionId === id){ activeSessionId = null; $('#reviewModal').classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); activeClaim = null; workingInvoices = []; }
    persistSessions(); updateSessionsBadge(); renderSessions(); showSuccess('تم','تم');
  });
}

// sessions.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let workSessions = [];
let _sessionsStorageState = 'uninitialized';
let activeSessionId = null;
let sessionsFilter = 'all';
let sessionsSearch = '';
let currentSessionTimer = null;
let lastUserActivityTimestamp = Date.now();
let activeSessionTimer = null;
let lastActivityTime = Date.now();
function genSessionId(){ return 'SES-'+Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase()+'-'+Math.floor(Math.random()*1000); }
function loadSessions(){
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SESSIONS_KEY);
    if(raw === null){ _sessionsStorageState = 'empty'; return; }
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if(!Array.isArray(parsed) || !parsed.every(s => s && typeof s === 'object' && !Array.isArray(s))) throw new Error('بنية جلسات المراجعة غير صالحة');
    workSessions = parsed.map(s => ({ ...s, status:'paused' }));
    _sessionsStorageState = 'loaded';
  } catch(e){
    _sessionsStorageState = 'corrupt';
    console.error('loadSessions rejected stored sessions; original value preserved', e);
  }
}
function persistSessions(){
  if(_sessionsStorageState === 'corrupt') { console.error('persistSessions blocked: corrupted session storage requires recovery'); return false; }
  try { localStorage.setItem(SESSIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(workSessions)); _sessionsStorageState = 'loaded'; return true; }
  catch(e){ console.error('persistSessions failed', e); return false; }
}
function findSessionByClaim(claimId){ return workSessions.find(s => s.claimId === claimId); }
// Compare the live claim with the immutable baseline captured when this review session began.
// Older sessions without a baseline remain resumable but cannot be conflict-checked.
function getSessionConflictState(session, liveClaim){
  if(!session || !session.baseClaimSnapshot || !liveClaim) return 'unknown';
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(session.baseClaimSnapshot) === JSON.stringify(liveClaim) ? 'current' : 'conflict';
  } catch(e){ return 'unknown'; }
}
function getSessionClaimInfo(s){
  const live = getClaimById(s.claimId);
  const snap = s.claimSnapshot || {};
  return { id:s.claimId, patient:(live&&live.patient)||snap.patient||'—', provider:(live&&live.provider)||snap.provider||'—', billed:(live&&live.billed)||snap.billed||0, approved:(live&&live.approved)||snap.approved||0, stale:!live };
}
/**
 * التقاط حالة الجلسة الحالية (لحفظ/استئناف).
 * @returns {Object|null} حالة الجلسة
 */
function captureCurrentSessionState(){
  if(!activeClaim) return null;
  const ex = findSessionByClaim(activeClaim.id);
  return {
    id:(ex&&ex.id)||activeSessionId||genSessionId(),
    claimId:activeClaim.id,
    claimSnapshot:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(activeClaim)),
    baseClaimSnapshot:(ex && ex.baseClaimSnapshot) ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ex.baseClaimSnapshot)) : JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(getClaimById(activeClaim.id) || activeClaim)),
    sessionSchemaVersion:2,
    workingInvoices:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(workingInvoices||[])),
    rvActiveInvoiceIdx:rvActiveInvoiceIdx||0,
    rvActivePatientIdx:rvActivePatientIdx||0,
    reviewFormState:{ reason:($('#rvReason')&&$('#rvReason').value)||'', notes:($('#rvNotes')&&$('#rvNotes').value)||'', paStatus:($('#paStatus')&&$('#paStatus').value)||'not-requested', paRefNumber:($('#paRefNumber')&&$('#paRefNumber').value)||'', paDate:($('#paDate')&&$('#paDate').value)||'', claimType:activeClaim.type||'employee' },
    createdAt:(ex&&ex.createdAt)||new Date().toISOString(),
    lastActiveAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    status:'paused',
    name:(ex&&ex.name)||null,
    elapsedSeconds: (ex && typeof ex.elapsedSeconds === 'number') ? ex.elapsedSeconds : 0,
    lastSavedPoint: (ex && ex.lastSavedPoint) ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ex.lastSavedPoint)) : { invoiceIdx: rvActiveInvoiceIdx||0, patientIdx: rvActivePatientIdx||0, lineIdx: 0 }
  };
}
function upsertCurrentSession(){
  const s = captureCurrentSessionState(); if(!s) return null;
  const i = workSessions.findIndex(x => x.id === s.id);
  if(i >= 0) workSessions[i] = s; else workSessions.push(s);
  persistSessions(); updateSessionsBadge(); return s;
}
function updateSessionsBadge(){
  const b = document.getElementById('sessionsCount');
  if(!b) return;
  const n = workSessions.length;
  if(n > 0){ b.textContent = n; b.style.display = 'grid'; } else b.style.display = 'none';
  renderSessionSwitcher();
}
function renderSessionSwitcher(){
  const sel = document.getElementById('rvSessionSwitcher'); if(!sel) return;
  const others = workSessions.filter(s => s.id !== activeSessionId);
  if(!others.length){ sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">لا جلسات أخرى</option>'; sel.disabled = true; return; }
  sel.disabled = false;
  sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">⇄ تبديل...</option>' + others.map(s => { const info = getSessionClaimInfo(s); return `<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}">${escapeHtml(s.claimId)} — ${escapeHtml(info.patient.substring(0,22))}</option>`; }).join('');
}
function resumeSession(sessionId){
  const s = workSessions.find(x => x.id === sessionId); if(!s){ showWarning('خطأ','غير موجودة'); return false; }
  let live = getClaimById(s.claimId);
  const conflictState = live ? getSessionConflictState(s, live) : 'missing';
  if(conflictState === 'conflict'){
    showWarning('تعارض في بيانات المطالبة', 'تغيرت المطالبة بعد إنشاء جلسة المراجعة. أوقفنا الاستئناف حتى لا تستبدل الجلسة تعديلات أحدث. احتفظ بنسخة احتياطية وافحص المطالبة والجلسة قبل المتابعة.');
    return false;
  }
  if(!live){
    live = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(s.claimSnapshot || {}));
    if(!live.id || !live.patient){ showWarning('خطأ','لا يمكن'); return; }
    claims.push(live); showToast(`تم استرداد ${live.id}`);
  }
  activeClaim = live;
  if(s.claimSnapshot && Array.isArray(s.claimSnapshot.invoices)) activeClaim.invoices = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(s.claimSnapshot.invoices));
  workingInvoices = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(s.workingInvoices || []));
  rvActiveInvoiceIdx = s.rvActiveInvoiceIdx || 0;
  rvActivePatientIdx = s.rvActivePatientIdx || 0;
  activeSessionId = s.id;
  s.status = 'active'; s.lastActiveAt = new Date().toISOString(); persistSessions();
  if(s.lastSavedPoint){ rvActiveInvoiceIdx = s.lastSavedPoint.invoiceIdx || rvActiveInvoiceIdx || 0; rvActivePatientIdx = s.lastSavedPoint.patientIdx || rvActivePatientIdx || 0; }
  if(typeof startSessionTimeTracker === 'function') startSessionTimeTracker(s);
  $('#rvSub').textContent = `${activeClaim.id} • ${activeClaim.patient}`;
  $('#rvId').value = activeClaim.id; $('#rvDate').value = activeClaim.date||'';
  $('#rvReceivedNumber').value = activeClaim.receivedNumber||'—'; $('#rvReceivedDate').value = activeClaim.receivedDate||'—';
  $('#rvPatient').value = activeClaim.patient||''; $('#rvProvider').value = activeClaim.provider||'';
  $('#rvReason').value = s.reviewFormState.reason||''; $('#rvNotes').value = s.reviewFormState.notes||'';
  if(s.reviewFormState.claimType) activeClaim.type = s.reviewFormState.claimType;
  $$('.rv-type-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.claimType === activeClaim.type));
  if(activeClaim.priorApproval && activeClaim.priorApproval.required){
    (el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'block'; })($('#priorApprovalBox'));
    $('#paStatus').value = s.reviewFormState.paStatus || activeClaim.priorApproval.status || 'not-requested';
    $('#paRefNumber').value = s.reviewFormState.paRefNumber || activeClaim.priorApproval.refNumber || '';
    $('#paDate').value = s.reviewFormState.paDate || activeClaim.priorApproval.date || '';
    updatePAWarning();
  } else (el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'none'; })($('#priorApprovalBox'));
  renderReviewInvoices();
  clearSessionDirty();
  try { navigateTo('review', {noPush:false}); } catch(e){ if($('#reviewModal')) { $('#reviewModal').classList.add('open'); document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'; } }
  updateSessionsBadge(); renderSessions();
  return true;
}
function formatTimeAgo(iso){
  if(!iso) return '—';
  const diff = Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime())/1000);
  if(diff < 30) return 'الآن';
  if(diff < 3600) return `قبل ${Math.floor(diff/60)} د`;
  if(diff < 86400) return `قبل ${Math.floor(diff/3600)} س`;
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('ar-SA');
}
function renderSessionCard(s){
  const info = getSessionClaimInfo(s);
  const inv = (s.workingInvoices||[]).length;
  const pat = (s.workingInvoices||[]).reduce((a,i)=>a+(i.patients||[]).length,0);
  const lines = (s.workingInvoices||[]).reduce((a,i)=>a+(i.patients||[]).reduce((b,p)=>b+(p.lines||[]).length,0),0);
  const billed = (s.workingInvoices||[]).reduce((a,i)=>a+(i.patients||[]).reduce((b,p)=>b+(p.lines||[]).reduce((c,l)=>c+(l.qty*l.billedUnit),0),0),0);
  const appr = (s.workingInvoices||[]).reduce((a,i)=>a+(i.patients||[]).reduce((b,p)=>b+(p.lines||[]).reduce((c,l)=>c+(Number(l.approved)||0),0),0),0);
  const rate = billed ? ((billed-appr)/billed*100) : 0;
  let pc = 'paused', pt = 'موقوفة';
  if(info.stale){ pc = 'stale'; pt = 'مفقودة'; }
  else if(s.status === 'active'){ pc = 'active'; pt = 'نشطة'; }
  return `<div class="session-card ${s.id===activeSessionId?'active-session':''} ${info.stale?'stale':''}">
    <div class="sc-head"><div class="sc-claim"><div class="sc-claim-id">${escapeHtml(s.claimId)}</div><div class="sc-patient">${escapeHtml(info.patient)}</div><div class="sc-provider">${escapeHtml(info.provider)}</div></div><span class="sc-status-pill ${pc}">${pt}</span></div>
    <div class="sc-stats"><div><span>فواتير</span><b>${inv}</b></div><div><span>مرضى</span><b>${pat}</b></div><div><span>بنود</span><b>${lines}</b></div></div>
    <div class="sc-money"><span>مطالب: <b>${fmt(billed)}</b></span><span style="color:var(--ok)">معتمد: <b>${fmt(appr)}</b></span><span style="color:var(--bad)">استقطاع: <b>${rate.toFixed(1)}%</b></span></div>
    <div class="sc-foot"><div class="sc-time">🕐 ${formatTimeAgo(s.lastActiveAt)}</div><div class="sc-actions"><button class="btn-xs" data-resume-session="${escapeHtml(s.id)}">${info.stale?'⚠ حذف':'استئناف'}</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-delete-session="${escapeHtml(s.id)}">×</button></div></div>
  </div>`;
}
function renderSessions(){
  const box = document.getElementById('sessionsList'); if(!box) return;
  const activeN = workSessions.filter(s=>s.status==='active').length;
  const pausedN = workSessions.filter(s=>s.status==='paused').length;
  const kpi = document.getElementById('sessionsKpi');
  if(kpi){
    kpi.innerHTML = `
      <div class="kpi"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">الجلسات</div><div class="kpi-val">${workSessions.length} <small>جلسة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('file')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${activeN} نشطة · ${pausedN} موقوفة</div></div>
      <div class="kpi k2"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">قيد العمل</div><div class="kpi-val">${activeN} <small>جلسة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('shield')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">${pausedN} بانتظار</div></div>
      <div class="kpi k3"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">مطالبات</div><div class="kpi-val">${workSessions.length} <small>مطالبة</small></div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('money')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">غير مكتملة</div></div>
      <div class="kpi k4"><div class="kpi-top"><div><div class="kpi-label">آخر نشاط</div><div class="kpi-val" style="font-size:13px">${workSessions.length?formatTimeAgo(workSessions.reduce((a,s)=> (s.lastActiveAt>a?s.lastActiveAt:a), workSessions[0].lastActiveAt)):'—'}</div></div><div class="kpi-ico">${kpiIcon('check')}</div></div><div class="kpi-foot">تحديث تلقائي</div></div>`;
  }
  const filtered = workSessions.filter(s => {
    if(sessionsFilter === 'active' && s.status !== 'active') return false;
    if(sessionsFilter === 'paused' && s.status !== 'paused') return false;
    if(sessionsSearch){ const info = getSessionClaimInfo(s), q = sessionsSearch.toLowerCase(); return s.claimId.toLowerCase().includes(q) || info.patient.toLowerCase().includes(q) || info.provider.toLowerCase().includes(q); }
    return true;
  }).sort((a,b) => new Date(b.lastActiveAt) - new Date(a.lastActiveAt));
  if(!filtered.length){
    box.innerHTML = `<div class="session-empty"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="2.5" y="5" width="19" height="14" rx="2.5"/><path d="M6.5 3v4M17.5 3v4M2.5 10h19"/></svg><p>${workSessions.length?'لا نتائج':'لا جلسات'}</p><span>${workSessions.length?'جرّب آخر':'ابدأ بمراجعة'}</span></div>`;
  } else box.innerHTML = `<div class="sessions-grid">${filtered.map(renderSessionCard).join('')}</div>`;
  updateSessionsBadge();
}

/* ---------- تتبع النشاط الفعلي والحفظ التلقائي ---------- */
(function(){
  const syncActivity = () => { lastUserActivityTimestamp = Date.now(); lastActivityTime = Date.now(); };
  ['click', 'keydown', 'mousemove', 'scroll'].forEach(evt => {
    try { window.addEventListener(evt, syncActivity, { passive: true }); } catch(e){}
  });
})();

function startSessionTimeTracker(session) {
  if (currentSessionTimer) clearInterval(currentSessionTimer);
  if (activeSessionTimer) clearInterval(activeSessionTimer);
  if (!session) return;
  if (!session.elapsedSeconds) session.elapsedSeconds = 0;
  if (!session.lastSavedPoint) session.lastSavedPoint = { invoiceIdx: 0, patientIdx: 0, lineIdx: 0 };
  currentSessionTimer = setInterval(() => {
    const isUserActive = (Date.now() - lastUserActivityTimestamp) < 60000;
    const isWindowFocused = !document.hidden;
    if (isUserActive && isWindowFocused && session.status === 'active') {
      session.elapsedSeconds += 1;
      updateSessionTimerUI(session.elapsedSeconds);
      updateTimerUI(session.elapsedSeconds);
    }
  }, 1000);
  activeSessionTimer = currentSessionTimer;
}
function initActiveSessionTracker(session){ return startSessionTimeTracker(session); }

function stopSessionTimeTracker() {
  if (currentSessionTimer) { clearInterval(currentSessionTimer); currentSessionTimer = null; }
  if (activeSessionTimer) { clearInterval(activeSessionTimer); activeSessionTimer = null; }
}
function stopActiveSessionTracker(){ return stopSessionTimeTracker(); }

function updateCurrentReviewProgressPoint(claimId, invoiceIdx, patientIdx, lineIdx) {
  let session = workSessions.find(s => s.claimId === claimId && s.status === 'active');
  if (!session) return;
  session.lastSavedPoint = {
    invoiceIdx: invoiceIdx || 0,
    patientIdx: patientIdx || 0,
    lineIdx: lineIdx || 0,
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
  };
  session.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  persistSessions();
}
function saveProgressPoint(claimId, invoiceIdx, patientIdx, lineIdx) {
  return updateCurrentReviewProgressPoint(claimId, invoiceIdx, patientIdx, lineIdx);
}

function resumeSessionFromLastPoint(sessionId) {
  const session = workSessions.find(s => s.id === sessionId);
  if (!session) return;
  workSessions.forEach(s => { if (s.status === 'active') s.status = 'paused'; });
  session.status = 'active';
  activeSessionId = session.id;
  try { openReview(session.claimId); } catch(e){}
  if (session.lastSavedPoint) {
    rvActiveInvoiceIdx = session.lastSavedPoint.invoiceIdx || 0;
    rvActivePatientIdx = session.lastSavedPoint.patientIdx || 0;
    try { renderReviewInvoices(); } catch(e){}
  }
  startSessionTimeTracker(session);
  try { showSuccess('تم استئناف الجلسة', `الوقت المستغرق حتى الآن: ${formatElapsedTime(session.elapsedSeconds || 0)}`); } catch(e){}
}

function formatElapsedTime(seconds) {
  seconds = seconds || 0;
  const hrs = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const mins = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
  const secs = seconds % 60;
  if (hrs > 0) return `${hrs} ساعة و ${mins} دقيقة`;
  return `${mins} دقيقة و ${secs} ثانية`;
}

function updateSessionTimerUI(seconds) {
  const timerEl = document.getElementById('rvSessionTimerDisplay');
  if (timerEl) {
    const hrs = String(Math.floor(seconds / 3600)).padStart(2, '0');
    const mins = String(Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
    const secs = String(seconds % 60).padStart(2, '0');
    timerEl.textContent = `⏱️ وقت المراجعة الفعلي: ${hrs}:${mins}:${secs}`;
  }
}
function updateTimerUI(seconds) {
  const el = document.getElementById('rvSessionTimerDisplay');
  if (el) {
    const m = String(Math.floor((seconds||0) / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
    const s = String((seconds||0) % 60).padStart(2, '0');
    el.textContent = `⏱️ وقت العمل الفعلي: ${m}:${s}`;
  }
  updateSessionTimerUI(seconds);
}

// shortcuts.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function bindShortcuts(){
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    const ctrl = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
    if(ctrl && e.key === 'k'){ e.preventDefault(); navHistory=[]; navigateTo('search',{noPush:true}); const inp = document.getElementById('srchQuery')||document.getElementById('searchInput'); if(inp) setTimeout(()=>inp.focus(),50); return; }
    if(ctrl && e.key === 'n'){ e.preventDefault(); if(currentPage!=='intake') navigateTo('intake',{noPush:true}); setTimeout(()=>{ const b=document.getElementById('addBatchBtn'); if(b) b.click(); },50); return; }
  if(ctrl && e.key === 's'){ e.preventDefault(); if(typeof saveReview === 'function' && document.getElementById('reviewModal')?.classList.contains('open')) saveReview(); else if(typeof saveLineEditor === 'function' && document.getElementById('lineModal')?.classList.contains('open')) saveLineEditor(); return; }
    if(e.key === 'Escape'){
      const openModal = document.querySelector('.modal.open, .dialog-overlay.open');
      if(openModal){
        if(openModal.id === 'unifiedDialog'){ const cb = dialogState.onCancel; closeDialog(); if(cb) cb(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'reviewModal'){ closeReview(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'batchModal'){ closeBatchModal(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'providerModal'){ closeProviderModal(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'contractModal'){ closeContractModal(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'tariffModal'){ closeTariffModal(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'invoiceModal'){ closeInvoiceEditor(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'patientModal'){ closePatientEditor(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'lineModal'){ closeLineEditor(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'serviceModal'){ closeServiceModal(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'drWizardModal'){ closeDrWizard(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'drModal'){ closeDrModal(); }
        else if(openModal.id === 'paProcModal'){ $('#paProcModal').classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); }
        else { openModal.classList.remove('open'); _unlockBodyScroll(); }
        return;
      }
      if(window.innerWidth <= 900) closeSidebar();
      return;
    }
    if(e.key === '?' && !ctrl){ const a=document.activeElement; if(a && (a.tagName==='INPUT'||a.tagName==='TEXTAREA'||a.tagName==='SELECT')) return; e.preventDefault(); navHistory=[]; navigateTo('help',{noPush:true}); return; }
  });
}

================================================================================
FILE: /mnt/data/phase23work/TPA-Pro-main/js/storage.js
================================================================================
let _persistTimer = null;
// Storage state is deliberately fail-closed: malformed persisted data must not
// be silently replaced by demo/default state during the next autosave.
let _dataStorageState = 'uninitialized'; // uninitialized | empty | loaded | corrupt | unsupported
let _allowCorruptStorageReplace = false;
let _dataStorageError = null;
const PERSISTED_ARRAY_KEYS = ['providers','contracts','tariffs','batches','claims','trash','deductionRules','paProcedures'];
function isPersistedRecordArray(value){
  return Array.isArray(value) && value.every(item => item && typeof item === 'object' && !Array.isArray(item));
}
function persistData(immediate){
  if(_persistTimer) { clearTimeout(_persistTimer); _persistTimer = null; }
  const doIt = () => {
    if((_dataStorageState === 'corrupt' || _dataStorageState === 'unsupported') && !_allowCorruptStorageReplace){
      console.error('persistData blocked: stored data requires recovery', _dataStorageError || _dataStorageState);
      return false;
    }
    try {
      const payload = { version: DATA_VERSION, savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        data: { providers, contracts, tariffs, batches, claims, trash, deductionRules, paProcedures } };
      localStorage.setItem(DATA_STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload));
      _dataStorageState = 'loaded'; _dataStorageError = null;
      return true;
    } catch(e){ console.error('persistData failed', e); return false; }
  };
  if(immediate) return doIt();
  _persistTimer = setTimeout(() => { _persistTimer = null; doIt(); }, 250);
  return true;
}
function loadPersistedData(){
  let raw;
  try { raw = localStorage.getItem(DATA_STORE_KEY); }
  catch(e){ _dataStorageState = 'corrupt'; _dataStorageError = 'تعذر قراءة التخزين المحلي'; console.error('loadPersistedData read failed', e); return false; }
  if(raw === null){ _dataStorageState = 'empty'; return false; }
  try {
    const p = JSON.parse(raw);
    if(!p || typeof p !== 'object' || !p.data || typeof p.data !== 'object' || Array.isArray(p.data)) throw new Error('بنية ملف البيانات غير صالحة');
    if(Number.isFinite(p.version) && p.version > DATA_VERSION){ _dataStorageState = 'unsupported'; _dataStorageError = `إصدار بيانات أحدث (${p.version})`; return false; }
    const d = p.data;
    // Validate the complete envelope before assigning anything to live state.
    for(const key of PERSISTED_ARRAY_KEYS){
      if(d[key] !== undefined && !isPersistedRecordArray(d[key])) throw new Error(`حقل ${key} ليس قائمة سجلات صالحة`);
    }
    const next = {};
    PERSISTED_ARRAY_KEYS.forEach(key => { next[key] = d[key] === undefined ? null : d[key]; });
    if(next.claims) next.claims.forEach(c => {
      if(!c.invoices) c.invoices = [];
      if(!Array.isArray(c.invoices)) throw new Error('قائمة فواتير مطالبة غير صالحة');
      if(!c.priorApproval) c.priorApproval = {required:false,status:'not-requested',refNumber:'',date:''};
      if(typeof c.priorApproval !== 'object' || Array.isArray(c.priorApproval)) throw new Error('بيانات الموافقة المسبقة غير صالحة');
    });
    // Commit only after every recognized collection has passed validation.
    if(next.providers !== null) providers = next.providers;
    if(next.contracts !== null) contracts = next.contracts;
    if(next.tariffs !== null) tariffs = next.tariffs;
    if(next.batches !== null) batches = next.batches;
    if(next.claims !== null) claims = next.claims;
    if(next.trash !== null) trash = next.trash;
    if(next.deductionRules !== null) deductionRules = next.deductionRules;
    if(next.paProcedures !== null) paProcedures = next.paProcedures;
    _dataStorageState = 'loaded'; _dataStorageError = null;
    return true;
  } catch(e){
    _dataStorageState = 'corrupt'; _dataStorageError = e && e.message ? e.message : 'بيانات تالفة';
    console.error('loadPersistedData rejected stored data; original value preserved', e);
    return false;
  }
}
function getDataStorageHealth(){ return { state:_dataStorageState, error:_dataStorageError }; }
function trackBackup(){ try { localStorage.setItem(LAST_BACKUP_KEY, String(Date.now())); } catch(e){} }
function getLastBackupAgo(){
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(LAST_BACKUP_KEY);
    if(!raw) return null;
    const ms = Date.now() - parseInt(raw, 10);
    if(ms < 60000) return 'الآن';
    if(ms < 3600000) return `قبل ${Math.floor(ms/60000)} دقيقة`;
    if(ms < 86400000) return `قبل ${Math.floor(ms/3600000)} ساعة`;
    return `قبل ${Math.floor(ms/86400000)} يوم`;
  } catch(e){ return null; }
}
function hasRecentBackup(maxAgeMs){
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(LAST_BACKUP_KEY);
    if(!raw) return false;
    return (Date.now() - parseInt(raw, 10)) < (maxAgeMs || 86400000);
  } catch(e){ return false; }
}
function downloadJSON(obj, filename){
  const blob = new Blob(['\ufeff', JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2)], {type:'application/json;charset=utf-8'});
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url; a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1500);
}
loadPersistedData();

// Read-only relationship diagnostics. Legacy/stale sessions are reported, not deleted.
function inspectDataRelationships(data, sessions){
  const source = data || {};
  const issues = [];
  const checkUnique = (rows, label) => {
    if(!Array.isArray(rows)) return;
    const seen = new Set();
    rows.forEach((row, index) => {
      if(!row || typeof row !== 'object') return;
      const id = row.id;
      if(id === undefined || id === null || String(id).trim() === ''){
        issues.push({severity:'warning', code:'missing-id', collection:label, index});
      } else if(seen.has(String(id))){
        issues.push({severity:'error', code:'duplicate-id', collection:label, id:String(id), index});
      } else seen.add(String(id));
    });
  };
  ['providers','contracts','tariffs','batches','claims','deductionRules','paProcedures'].forEach(k => checkUnique(source[k], k));
  const claimRows = Array.isArray(source.claims) ? source.claims : [];
  const claimIds = new Set(claimRows.filter(c=>c && c.id != null).map(c=>String(c.id)));
  const sessionRows = Array.isArray(sessions) ? sessions : (Array.isArray(source.workSessions) ? source.workSessions : []);
  checkUnique(sessionRows, 'workSessions');
  sessionRows.forEach((s, index) => {
    if(!s || typeof s !== 'object') return;
    if(s.claimId === undefined || s.claimId === null || String(s.claimId).trim() === ''){
      issues.push({severity:'error', code:'session-missing-claim-id', collection:'workSessions', index});
      return;
    }
    const claimKey = String(s.claimId);
    if(s.claimSnapshot && s.claimSnapshot.id != null && String(s.claimSnapshot.id) !== claimKey){
      issues.push({severity:'error', code:'session-snapshot-id-mismatch', collection:'workSessions', index, claimId:claimKey, snapshotId:String(s.claimSnapshot.id)});
    } else if(!claimIds.has(claimKey)){
      const recoverable=!!(s.claimSnapshot && String(s.claimSnapshot.id)===claimKey);
      issues.push({severity:'warning', code:'orphan-session', collection:'workSessions', index, claimId:claimKey, recoverableFromSnapshot:recoverable});
    }
  });
  return {ok:!issues.some(x=>x.severity==='error'), issues, counts:{claims:claimRows.length,sessions:sessionRows.length}};
}

// translate-dict-4.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
Object.assign(window.TRANSLATE_DICT = window.TRANSLATE_DICT || {}, {

// DOM-walking translation: translates all text nodes and attributes
const _i18nOrig = new WeakMap();
function translateDOM(root){
  if(!root) root = document.body;
  if(!root) return;
  const lang = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined') ? currentLang : 'ar';
  if(lang === 'ar') return;
  if(!window.TRANSLATE_DICT) return;

  const dict = window.TRANSLATE_DICT;

  // Walk text nodes
  const walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT, null, false);
  const nodes = [];
  let node;
  while(node = walker.nextNode()){ nodes.push(node); }

  nodes.forEach(n => {
    if(!_i18nOrig.has(n)) _i18nOrig.set(n, n.nodeValue);
    const original = n.nodeValue;
    if(!original || !original.trim()) return;
    let text = original;
    // Normalize non-breaking spaces for matching
    const normalized = text.replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/&nbsp;/g, ' ').trim();
    // Try exact match first
    if(dict[normalized]){
      n.nodeValue = original.replace(text.trim(), dict[normalized]);
      return;
    }
    const trimmed = text.trim();
    if(dict[trimmed]){
      n.nodeValue = original.replace(trimmed, dict[trimmed]);
      return;
    }
    // Exact match only (partial matching causes mixed-language artifacts)
    // Already handled above
    if(text !== original){
      n.nodeValue = text;
    }
  });

  // Translate placeholders
  root.querySelectorAll('[placeholder]').forEach(el => {
    if(!el.hasAttribute('data-orig-ph')) el.setAttribute('data-orig-ph', el.getAttribute('placeholder'));
    const ph = el.getAttribute('data-orig-ph');
    if(ph && dict[ph]){
      el.setAttribute('placeholder', dict[ph]);
    } else if(ph){
      // Partial match for placeholder
      let t = ph;
      const keys = Object.keys(dict).sort((a,b) => b.length - a.length);
      for(const key of keys){
        if(key.length < 2) continue;
        if(t.includes(key)){ t = t.split(key).join(dict[key]); }
      }
      if(t !== ph) el.setAttribute('placeholder', t);
    }
  });

  // Translate titles
  root.querySelectorAll('[title]').forEach(el => {
    const t = el.getAttribute('title');
    if(t && dict[t]){
      el.setAttribute('title', dict[t]);
    }
  });
}

function restoreDOM(root){
  if(!root) root = document.body;
  if(!root) return;
  const walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT, null, false);
  const nodes = [];
  let node;
  while(node = walker.nextNode()){ nodes.push(node); }
  nodes.forEach(n => {
    const orig = _i18nOrig.get(n);
    if(orig !== undefined) n.nodeValue = orig;
  });
  root.querySelectorAll('[placeholder]').forEach(el => {
    const orig = el.getAttribute('data-orig-ph');
    if(orig !== null) el.setAttribute('placeholder', orig);
  });
}
// Auto-translate is triggered by callers (initApp, applyPage, setLang)

// Run translateDOM multiple times to handle nested/partial translations
function translateDOMMulti(){
  if(typeof translateDOM !== 'function') return;
  const lang = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined') ? currentLang : 'ar';
  if(lang === 'ar') return;
  translateDOM(document.body);
  setTimeout(() => translateDOM(document.body), 100);
  setTimeout(() => translateDOM(document.body), 500);
  setTimeout(() => translateDOM(document.body), 1500);
}

// Periodic translation to catch re-rendered content
setInterval(function(){
  if(document.hidden) return;
  const lang = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined') ? currentLang : 'ar';
  if(lang === 'ar') return;
  if(typeof translateDOM === 'function') translateDOM(document.body);
}, 8000);

// MutationObserver: translate new content as it's added
(function(){
  if(typeof MutationObserver === 'undefined') return;
  const observer = new MutationObserver(function(mutations){
    const lang = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined') ? currentLang : 'ar';
    if(lang === 'ar') return;
    mutations.forEach(function(m){
      m.addedNodes.forEach(function(node){
        if(node.nodeType === 1){ // Element — skip; 8s ticker + initial walk already cover this
          return;
        } else if(node.nodeType === 3){ // Text node
          const text = node.nodeValue.trim();
          if(text && window.TRANSLATE_DICT && window.TRANSLATE_DICT[text]){
            node.nodeValue = node.nodeValue.replace(text, window.TRANSLATE_DICT[text]);
          }
        }
      });
    });
  });
  if(document.body){
    observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } else {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){
      observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    });
  }
})();

// trash.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
let trashFilter = 'all', trashSearch = '', trashDebounce = null;
function bindTrashControls(){
  $('#trashSearch').addEventListener('input', e => { clearTimeout(trashDebounce); trashDebounce = setTimeout(() => { trashSearch = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase(); renderTrash(); }, 200); });
  $('#trashFilterChips').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const chip = e.target.closest('.chip'); if(!chip) return;
    $$('#trashFilterChips .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    chip.classList.add('active'); trashFilter = chip.dataset.trf; renderTrash();
  });
  $('#emptyTrashBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { if(!trash.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','سلة المحذوفات فارغة'); return; } showDelete('إفراغ سلة المحذوفات', `سيتم حذف ${trash.length} عنصر نهائياً. لا يمكن التراجع عن هذا الإجراء.`, () => { trash = []; renderAll(); showSuccess('تم','تم إفراغ السلة'); }); });
  $('#trashBulkRestore').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const ids = $$('#trashList .trash-cb:checked').map(cb => cb.dataset.id);
    if(!ids.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','حدد'); return; }
    let r = 0; ids.forEach(id => { if(restoreTrashItem(id)) r++; });
    trash = trash.filter(t => !ids.includes(t.id));
    renderAll(); showSuccess('تم', `${r}`);
  });
  $('#trashBulkDelete').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const ids = $$('#trashList .trash-cb:checked').map(cb => cb.dataset.id);
    if(!ids.length){ showWarning('تنبيه','حدد'); return; }
    showDelete('حذف نهائي', `سيتم حذف ${ids.length} عنصر نهائياً. لا يمكن التراجع عن هذا الإجراء.`, () => { trash = trash.filter(t => !ids.includes(t.id)); renderAll(); showSuccess('تم',`تم حذف ${ids.length} عنصر`); });
  });
  $('#trashSelectAll').addEventListener('change', e => { $$('#trashList .trash-cb').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = e.target.checked; }); $$('#trashList .trash-row').forEach(r => r.classList.toggle('selected', e.target.checked)); updateTrashBulkBar(); });
  $('#trashList').addEventListener('change', e => { if(e.target.classList.contains('trash-cb')){ e.target.closest('.trash-row').classList.toggle('selected', e.target.checked); updateTrashBulkBar(); } });
  $('#trashList').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const res = e.target.closest('[data-restore-id]');
    if(res){ const id = res.dataset.restoreId; if(restoreTrashItem(id)){ trash = trash.filter(t => t.id !== id); renderAll(); showSuccess('تم','تمت الاستعادة'); } else showWarning('تعذّر','لا يمكن استعادة هذا العنصر (قد يكون مكرراً)'); return; }
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-permanent-delete-id]');
    if(del){ const id = del.dataset.permanentDeleteId; const item = trash.find(t => t.id === id); if(!item) return; showDelete('حذف نهائي', `سيتم حذف "${item.name}" نهائياً. لا يمكن التراجع عن هذا الإجراء.`, () => { trash = trash.filter(t => t.id !== id); renderAll(); showSuccess('تم','تم الحذف'); }); }
  });
}
function updateTrashBulkBar(){ const n = $$('#trashList .trash-cb:checked').length; $('#trashBulkCount').textContent = n; $('#trashBulkBar').classList.toggle('show', n > 0); }
function restoreTrashItem(id){
  const item = trash.find(t => t.id === id); if(!item) return false;
  switch(item.type){
    case 'claim': { const c = {...item.data}; if(typeof recalcClaim==='function') recalcClaim(c); claims.push(c); return true; }
    case 'provider': if(providers.find(p => p.id === item.data.id)) return false; providers.push({...item.data}); return true;
    case 'contract': if(contracts.find(c => c.id === item.data.id)) return false; contracts.push({...item.data}); return true;
    case 'tariff': if(tariffs.find(t => t.id === item.data.id)) return false; tariffs.push({...item.data}); return true;
    case 'batch': if(batches.find(b => b.id === item.data.id)) return false; batches.push({...item.data}); return true;
    case 'service': { const t = tariffs.find(x => x.id === item.data.tariffId); if(!t) return false; if(!t.services) t.services = []; t.services.push({...item.data.service}); return true; }
  }
  return false;
}
function getTrashItemDetails(item){
  switch(item.type){
    case 'provider': return `${item.data.type||''} — ${item.data.city||''}`;
    case 'contract': { const p = getProviderById(item.data.providerId); return `${p?p.name:'—'} — ${item.data.discountRate||0}%`; }
    case 'tariff': return `${TARIFF_TYPES[item.data.type]?.label||''} — ${item.data.services?.length||0}`;
    case 'batch': { const p = getProviderById(item.data.providerId); return `${p?p.name:'—'} — ${formatMonth(item.data.month)}`; }
    case 'claim': return `${item.data.patient} — ${fmtC(item.data.billed||0)}`;
    default: return '';
  }
}
function renderTrash(){
  const total = trash.length;
  const cc = trash.filter(t => t.type === 'claim').length;
  const pc = trash.filter(t => t.type === 'provider').length;
  const ctc = trash.filter(t => t.type === 'contract').length;
  const tc = trash.filter(t => t.type === 'tariff').length;
  const bc = trash.filter(t => t.type === 'batch').length;
  $('#trashInfo').textContent = `${total} عنصر`;
  const list = trash.filter(item => {
    if(trashFilter !== 'all' && item.type !== trashFilter) return false;
    if(trashSearch){ const q = trashSearch; return item.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || item.typeLabel.toLowerCase().includes(q) || getTrashItemDetails(item).toLowerCase().includes(q); }
    return true;
  }).sort((a,b) => new Date(b.deletedAt) - new Date(a.deletedAt));
  const box = $('#trashList');
  if(!list.length){ box.innerHTML = `<div class="trash-empty"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M3 6h18M8 6V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h4a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v2"/><path d="M19 6v14a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H7a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V6"/></svg><p>${trash.length?'لا نتائج':'فارغة'}</p></div>`; updateTrashBulkBar(); return; }
  box.innerHTML = list.map(item => {
    const d = new Date(item.deletedAt).toISOString().slice(0,10);
    return `<div class="trash-row" data-trash-id="${item.id}"><input type="checkbox" class="trash-cb" data-id="${item.id}"><span class="badge trash-${item.type}">${item.typeLabel}</span><div class="trash-info"><div class="trash-name">${escapeHtml(item.name)}</div><div class="trash-details">${escapeHtml(getTrashItemDetails(item))}</div></div><div class="trash-date">${d}</div><div class="trash-actions"><button class="btn-xs restore" data-restore-id="${item.id}">استعادة</button><button class="btn-xs danger" data-permanent-delete-id="${item.id}">حذف</button></div></div>`;
  }).join('');
  updateTrashBulkBar(); updateTrashBadge();
}

// utils-2.js — وحدة من تطبيق TPA Pro (مراجعة مالية للمطالبات الطبية)
function levenshtein(a, b){
  a = String(a||''); b = String(b||'');
  if(!a.length) return b.length; if(!b.length) return a.length;
  const m = Array.from({length:a.length+1}, () => Array(b.length+1).fill(0));
  for(let i=0;i<=a.length;i++) m[i][0]=i;
  for(let j=0;j<=b.length;j++) m[0][j]=j;
  for(let i=1;i<=a.length;i++) for(let j=1;j<=b.length;j++)
    m[i][j] = a[i-1]===b[j-1] ? m[i-1][j-1] : 1+Math.min(m[i-1][j],m[i][j-1],m[i-1][j-1]);
  return m[a.length][b.length];
}
function levenshteinSimilarity(a, b){
  const ml = Math.max(String(a||'').length, String(b||'').length);
  if(ml === 0) return 100;
  return Math.round((1 - levenshtein(a,b)/ml) * 100);
}
